




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

namak‍kārapāḷi

1.

 sugataṃ  sugataṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, kusalaṃkusalaṃ jahaṃ|

amataṃ amataṃ santaṃ, asamaṃ asamaṃ dadaṃ||

saraṇaṃ saraṇaṃ laukaṃ, araṇaṃ araṇaṃ karaṃ|

abhayaṃ abhayaṃ ṭhānaṃ, nāyakaṃ nāyakaṃ namai||

2.

 nayanasubhagakāyaṅgaṃ,

madhuravarasaraupaitaṃ|

amitaguṇagaṇādhāraṃ,

dasabalamatulaṃ vandai||

3.

 yau buddhau dhitimāñ‍ñadhārakau,

saṃsārai anubhausi kāyikaṃ|

dukkhaṃ caitasikañ‍ca laukatau,

taṃ vandai naradaivamaṅgalaṃ||

4.

 bāttiṃsatilakkhaṇacitradaihaṃ,

daihaj‍jutiniggatapaj‍jalantaṃ|

pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇaughavindaṃ,

vandai munimantimajātiyuttaṃ||

5.

 pātaudayaṃ bāladivākaraṃva,

majjhai yatīnaṃ lalitaṃ sirīhi|

puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhaṃ anaijaṃ,

vandāmi sabbañ‍ñumahaṃ munindaṃ||

6.

 upaitapuñ‍ñau  varabaudhimūlai,

sasainamāraṃ sugatau jinitvā|

abaujjhi baudhiṃ aruṇaudayamhi,

namāmi taṃ mārajinaṃ abhaṅgaṃ||

7.

 rāgādichaidāmalañāṇakhaggaṃ,

satīsamañ‍ñāphalakābhigāhaṃ|

sīlaughalaṅkāravibhūsitaṃ taṃ,

namāmibhiñ‍ñāvaramiddhupaitaṃ||

8.

 dayālayaṃ sabbadhi duk‍karaṃ karaṃ,

bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamamaggataṃ gataṃ|

tilaukanāthaṃ susamāhitaṃ hitaṃ,

samantacakkhuṃ paṇamāmi taṃmitaṃ||

9.

 tahiṃ tahiṃ pāramisañ‍cayaṃ cayaṃ,

gataṃ gataṃ sabbhi sukhappadaṃ padaṃ|

narānarānaṃ sukhasambhavaṃ bhavaṃ,

namānamānaṃ jinapuṅgavaṃ gavaṃ||

10.

 maggaṅganāvaṃ munidakkhanāvikau,

īhāphiyaṃ ñāṇakaraina gāhakau|

āruyha yau tāya bahū bhavaṇṇavā,

tāraisi taṃ buddhamaghappahaṃ namai||

11.

 samatiṃsatipāramisambharaṇaṃ,

varabaudhidumai catusac‍cadasaṃ|

varamiddhigataṃ naradaivahitaṃ,

tibhavūpasamaṃ paṇamāmi jinaṃ||

12.

 satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikaṃ virajaṃ,

gaganūpamadhiṃ dhitimairusamaṃ|

jalajūpamasītalasīlayutaṃ,

pathavīsahanaṃ paṇamāmi jinaṃ||

13.

 yau  buddhau sumati divai divākarauva,

saubhantau ratijananai silāsanamhi|

āsīnau sivasukhadaṃ adaisi dhammaṃ,

daivānaṃ tamasadisaṃ namāmi nic‍caṃ||

14.

 yau pādapaṅkajamuduttalarājikaihi,

laukaihi tīhivikalaihi nirākulaihi|

sampāpuṇai nirupamaiyyatamaiva nāthau,

taṃ sabbalaukamahitaṃ asamaṃ namāmi||

15.

 buddhaṃ narānarasamausaraṇaṃ dhitattaṃ,

pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā vihatandhakāraṃ|

atthābhikāmanaradaivahitāvahaṃ taṃ,

vandāmi kāruṇikamaggamanantañāṇaṃ||

16.

 akhilaguṇanidhānau yau munindaupagantvā,

vanamisipatanavhaṃ sañ‍ñatānaṃ nikaitaṃ|

tahimakusalachaidaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattau,

tamatulamabhikantaṃ vandanaiyyaṃ namāmi||

17.

 suciparivāritaṃ surucirappabhāhi rattaṃ,

sirivisarālayaṃ gupitamindriyaihupaitaṃ|

ravisasimaṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaupacittaṃ,

suranarapūjitaṃ sugatamādaraṃ namāmi||

18.

 maggauḷumpaina muhapaṭighāsādiul‍laulavīciṃ,

saṃsāraughaṃ tari tamabhayaṃ pārapattaṃ pajānaṃ|

tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ asamasaraṇaṃ aikatitthaṃ patiṭṭhaṃ,

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ paramasukhadaṃ dhammarājaṃ namāmi||

19.

 kaṇḍambaṃmūlai parahitakarau yau munindau nisin‍nau,

acchairaṃ sīghaṃ nayanasubhagaṃ ākulaṇṇaggijālaṃ|

duj‍jāladdhaṃsaṃ munibhijahitaṃ pāṭihairaṃ akāsi,

vandai taṃ saiṭṭhaṃ paramaratijaṃ iddhidhammaihupaitaṃ||

20.

 munindak‍kau  yvaikau dayudayaruṇau ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbau,

vinaiyyappāṇaughaṃ kamalakathitaṃ dhammaraṃsīvaraihi|

subaudhaisī suddhai tibhavakuharai byāpitak‍kittinañ‍ca,

tilaukaikac‍cakkhuṃ dukhamasahanaṃ taṃ mahaisiṃ namāmi||

21.

 yau jinau anaikajātiyaṃ saputtadāramaṅgajīvitampi,

baudhipaimatau alaggamānasau adāsiyaiva atthikassa|

dānapāramiṃ tatau paraṃ apūri sīlapāramādikampi,

tāsamiddhiyaupayātamaggataṃ tamaikadīpakaṃ namāmi||

22.

 daivādaivātidaivaṃ nidhanavapudharaṃ mārabhaṅgaṃ abhaṅgaṃ,

dīpaṃ dīpaṃ pajānaṃ jayavarasayanai baudhipattaṃdhipattaṃ|

brahmābrahmāgatānaṃ varagirakathikaṃ pāpahīnaṃ pahīnaṃ,

laukālaukābhirāmaṃ satatamabhinamai taṃ munindaṃ munindaṃ||

23.

 buddhau nigraudhabimbau mudukaracaraṇau brahmaghausaiṇijaṅghau,

kausacchādaṅgajātau punarapi sugatau suppatiṭṭhitapādau|

mūdaudātuṇṇalaumau athamapi sugatau brahmujuggattabhāvau,

nīlakkhī dīghapaṇhī sukhumamalachavī thaumyarasaggasaggī||

24.

 cattālīsaggadantau samakalapanajau antaraṃsappapīnau,

cak‍kainaṅkitapādau aviraḷadasanau mārajussaṅkhapādau||

tiṭṭhantau  naunamantaubhayakaramudunā jaṇṇukānāmasantau,

vaṭṭakkhandhau jinau gautaruṇapakhumakau sīhapubbaḍḍhakāyau||

25.

 sattappīnau ca dīghaṅguli matha sugatau laumakūpaikalaumau,

sampan‍naudātadāṭhau kanakasamatacau nīlamuddhaggalaumau||

sambuddhau thūlajivhau atha sīhahanukau jālikappādahatthau,

nāthau uṇhīsasīsau itiguṇasahitaṃ taṃ mahaisiṃ namāmi||

26.

 buddhaubuddhautighausau atidulabhatarau kā kathā buddhabhāvau,

laukai tasmā vibhāvī vividhahitasukhaṃ sādhavau patthayantā||

iṭṭhaṃ atthaṃ vahantaṃ suranaramahitaṃ nibbhayaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ,

laukānaṃ nandivaḍḍhaṃ dasabalamasamaṃ taṃ namassantu nic‍caṃ||

27.

 puñ‍ñainaitaina sauhaṃ nipuṇamati satau samparāyai ca tittau,

dakkhau diṭṭhuj‍jupañ‍ñau avikalavīriyau bhaugavā saṃvibhāgī||

tikkhau sūrau dhitattau saparahitacarau dīghajīvī araugau,

dhañ‍ñau vaṇṇau yasassī atibalavadharau kittimā khantupaitau||

28.

 saddhau  dātaṅgupaitau paramasiridharau diṭṭhadhammai virattau,

laj‍jī kalyāṇamittau abhiratakusalau pañ‍casīlādirakkhau||

appicchau appakaudhau ativujuhadayau iddhimā appamaiyyau,

pāsaṃsau paimavācau sujanaguṇavidū māmakau sau bhavaiyyaṃ||

29.

 itthaṃ asaṅkhayai nātha, guṇai lakkhaṇadīpitai|

gāthāsu sūcakāsvaika, gāthampi saratai budhau||

30.

 caturāpāyamuttau sau, sādhakatthadvayassa ca|

hatūpaddavajālau ca, lābhī hitasukhassa ca||

31.

 adhipau naradaivānaṃ, catudīpissaraupi vā|

bhavaiyya antimai daihai, tamañ‍ñaṃ saitachattakaṃ||

32.

 bhāvanāyānamāruyha, samamaissati subbatau|

imasmiṃ attabhāvaipi, araugau dīghajīvikau||

33.

 pūjitau sabbalaukaihi, bhāvanābhiratīmanau|

janappiyau manāpau ca, kā kathākhiladhāraṇai||

namak‍kārapāḷi niṭṭhitā|

namak‍kāraṭīkā

|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

1.

 sugatantyādivaṇṇaihi, pasatthañ‍ca sukhaddadaṃ|

vandantānaṃ jinaṃ natvā, dhammaṃ suddhaṃ gaṇuttamaṃ||

2.

 namak‍kāraṃ hitatthīhi, paurāṇācariyaihi ca|

likhitvā ṭhapitaṃ puñ‍ña, buddhiyā taina vandinaṃ||

3.

 tassatthavaṇṇanaṃ kassaṃ, gambhīratthasubaudhanaṃ|

mauvipunāmaina santaina, sīlupaitaina yācitau||

4.

 vaṇṇantaupi ca tassatthaṃ, taṃ taṃ aṭṭhakathādisu|

vuttañāyaina kassāmi, saddhā suṇātha sādhavau||

5.

 namanaṃ namatītī vā, namatī taina vā namau|

tassa kāraunti tāhīti, namak‍kārauti vuc‍cati||

6.

 tañ‍ca buddhaghausācari, yaina pubbasutaṃ kataṃ|

buddhaghausuppattiyañ‍ca, taṃ taṃ pana kathādisu||

7.

 na diṭṭhaṃ katakāraṇaṃ, tasmā hitatthikaihi ca|

paurāṇācariyaihī ca, ṭhapitanti mayā vuttaṃ|

taṃ kāraṇaṃ viñ‍ñūhī ca, vicāraitvā vijānitanti||

1.

 sugataṃ sugataṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, kusalaṃkusalaṃ jahaṃ|

amataṃ amataṃ santaṃ, asamaṃ asamaṃ dadaṃ||

saraṇaṃ saraṇaṃ laukaṃ, araṇaṃ araṇaṃ karaṃ|

abhayaṃ abhayaṃ ṭhānaṃ, nāyakaṃ nāyakaṃ namai||

1. [ka] idāni  paṭhamaṃ tāva sugatantyādīhi aṭṭhārasahi guṇaihi thaumaitvā buddhassa vanditukāmaina sugatantiādi gāthādvayaṃ āraddhaṃ| ayaṃ pana sabbapādādiyamakapathyā vattagāthāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| taṃ pana yamakaṃ subaudhālaṅkārai kiliṭṭhadausanti vuttaṃ|

vuttañhi tattha –

‘‘yaṃ kiliṭṭhapadaṃ mandā, bhidhaiyyaṃ yamakādikaṃ|

kiliṭṭhapadadausaiva, tampi antau karīyatī’’ti||

ayaṃ panaittha patītasaddaracitattāyaiva pasādaguṇa saṅkhātaina saddālaṅkāraina saṃyuttā hauti| tasmā taṃ yamakaṃ suvuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ parāsu yamakagāthāsu| taina vuttaṃ punā laṅkārai –

‘‘patītasaddaracitaṃ, siliṭṭhapadasandhikaṃ|

pasādaguṇasaṃyuttaṃ, yamakaṃ matamaidisa’’nti||

tattha sugatanti saubhanagamanattā sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ gatattā sammā gatattā ca sugatau, sau hi saubhanaṃ ariyamaggaina gacchatīti sugatauti vuc‍cati, supubbau gamudhātu ta| susaddau saubhanatthau| ariyamaggagamanaina parisuddhaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ khaimaṃ disaṃ asaj‍jamānau gatauti atthau| tañ‍ca gamanaṃ duvidhaṃ hauti kāyagamanaṃ, ñāṇagamanañ‍cāti| tattha bhagavatau vainaiyyajanupasaṅkamanaṃ aikantaina taisaṃ hitasukhassa nipphāda natau saubhanaṃ kāyaina gamanaṃ kāyagamanaṃ nāma| sayambhū ñāṇaina pana sakalampi laukaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayavasaina parijānanatau ñāṇaina gamanaṃ ñāṇagamanaṃ nāma| idha pana ñāṇagamanaṃ adhippaitaṃ| aṅguttaraṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘saubhanaṃ gataṃ gamanaṃ aitassāti sugatau’’ti vuttaṃ| sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ gacchatīti sugatauti ca vuc‍cati, ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina amataṃ nibbānaṃ gatauti  atthau| sammā aviparītaṃ caisa gacchatīti sugatauti vuc‍cati| susaddau sammāsaddatthau| taina taina maggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti atthau| atha vā sammā āgacchatīti sugatauti ca vuc‍cati, dīpaṅkarapādamūlatau pabhuti yāva baudhimaṇḍā tāva samatiṃsapāramipūrikāya sammāpaṭipattiyā sabbalaukassa hitasukhamaiva karauntau sassataṃ ucchaidaṃ kāmasukhaṃ attakilamathañ‍cāti imai cantai anupagacchantau āgatauti atthau| taṃ sugataṃ buddhaṃ namai namāmīti sambandhau|

puna sugatanti sammā gadattā sugatau| sau hi sammā gadati bhāsatīti sugatauti vuc‍cati| supubbagadadhātu bhāsāyaṃ viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ vā, ta, dassa tau| chasu vācāsu yuttaṭṭhānai yuttamaiva dvivācaṃ bhāsatīti atthau| vuttañhi majjhimapaṇṇāsapāḷiyaṃ abhayarājakumārasuttai –

1

. ‘‘rājakumāra yaṃ tathāgatau taṃ vācaṃ jānāti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, na taṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ bhāsati| 2. yampi tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā| tampi tathāgatau vācaṃ na bhāsati| 3. yañ‍ca khau tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ appiyā amanāpā, tatrakālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassā vācāya vaiyyākaraṇāya| 4. yaṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti abhūtaṃ atacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, na taṃ tathāgatau vācaṃ bhāsati| 5. yampi tathāgatau jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā, tampi tathāgatau vācaṃ na bhāsati| 6. yañ‍ca khau tathāgatau vācaṃ jānāti bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ, sā ca paraisaṃ piyā manāpā| tatra kālañ‍ñū tathāgatau hauti tassā vācāya vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti|

aittha pāḷiyaṃ panaisā sukhaggahaṇatthaṃ saṅkhaipaina vaiditabbā| kathaṃ haisā abhūtā anatthasaṃhitā appiyā vācā ca, bhūtā anatthasaṃhitā appiyā vācā ca, bhūtā attha saṃhitā appiyā vācā ca, abhūtā anatthasaṃhitā piyā vācā ca, bhūtā anatthasaṃhitā piyā vācā ca, bhūtā atthasaṃhitā piyā vācā cāti chabbidhā hautīti| tattha acauraṃyaiva caurau ayantiādivasaina vattabbavācā paṭhamavācā nāma| cauraṃyaiva caurau ayantiādivasaina vattabbavācā dutiyavācā nāma| akatapuñ‍ñatāya duggatau dubbaṇṇau appaisakkhau, idha pana ṭhatvāpi puna puñ‍ñaṃ na karauti, dutiyacittavārai kathaṃ catūhi apāyaihi muc‍cissatīti aivaṃ mahājanassa atthapuraikkhāraina dhammapuraikkhāraina anusāsanīpuraikkhāraina vattabbavācā tatiyavācā nāma| aikau dhuttau āha-mayhaṃ bhau mātu mayi kucchigatai kapiṭṭhaphaladauhaḷau ahausi| sā añ‍ñaṃ kapiṭṭhahārakaṃ alabhamānā maṃyaiva paisaisi| ahaṃ gantvā rukkhaṃ abhiruhituṃ asak‍kauntau attanāva attānaṃ pādai gahaitvā muggaraṃ viya rukkhassa upari khipiṃ| atha sākhatau sākhaṃ vicarantau phalāni gahaitvā autarituṃ asak‍kauntau gharaṃ gantvā nissaiṇiṃ āharitvā auruyha mātu santikai gantvā phalāni mātuyā adāsiṃ| tāni pana mahantāni haunti cāṭippa māṇāni| tatau mai mātarā aikāsanai nisin‍nāya samasaṭṭhiphalāni khāditānīti| aivamādivasaina vattabbavācā catutthavācā nāma| āmisahaitu cāṭukamyatādivasaina nānappakārakaṃ paraisaṃ thaumanā vācā caiva rājakathaṃ caurakathantiādinayappavattā tiracchānakathā ca pañ‍camavācā nāma| ariyasac‍casan‍nissitā dhammakathā chaṭṭhamavācā nāma| taṃ pana vassasatampi suṇantā paṇḍitā naiva tittiṃ gacchanti| tāsu pana bhagavā tatiyachaṭṭhamā dvivācāaiva bhāsati| tasmā sappurisaihi sāyaiva dvivācā vattabbāti| ayaṃ tadaṭṭhakathāyaṃ āgatau saṅkhaipanayau| visuddhimagga mahāṭīkāyaṃ pana sāratthadīpanīṭīkāyañ‍ca tadañ‍ñākāraina vaṇṇitau| tāsu pana taṃ aulaukaitvā gahaitabbanti| ayaṃ panaittha saṅgahagāthā –

‘‘yau bhūtamatthasaṃhītaṃ, paraisamappiyā vācaṃ|

bhūtamatthasahītañ‍ca, paraisaṃ piyavācanti|

dvivācaṃ yuttaṭṭhānaiva, vadatīti sugatauti|

vuc‍catī ca dakārassa, katvā takāraviñ‍ñunā’’ti||

saiṭṭhanti guṇamahantattā pasatthataraṃ| sau hi pasatthānaṃ visaisaina pasatthauti saiṭṭhauti vuc‍cati| pasatthapadaṃ, visaisataddhitai iṭṭhapac‍cayau| pasatthasaddassa sau, pasatthānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhāriyasāvakādīnaṃ sīlasamādhipañ‍ñādi guṇaihi visaisaina pasatthauti atthau| atha vā sundarai satipaṭṭhānādibhaidai dhammai aisati gavaisati aisiṃ gavaisinti vā saiṭṭhau, buddhau| supubbauaisa gavaisanai ta| tassa raṭṭhau| susaddau sundaratthau| santaihi sappurisaihi aisitabbau gavaisitabbauti vā saiṭṭhau| santasaddūpapadau isa gavaisanai ta, santasaddassa sau, ikārassai, tassa ṭṭhau| santaihi sappurisaihi icchiyati kantiya tīti vā saiṭṭhau| santasaddūpapadau isu icchākantīsu ta| atha vā santānaṃ sappurisānaṃ hitasukhaṃ icchatīti saiṭṭhau, buddhau| kattusādhanauyaṃ| sundarai satipaṭṭhānā dibhaidai dhammai aisaiti buddhaitīti vā saiṭṭhau| haitu kattusādhanauyaṃ| supubbaaisadhātu buddhiyaṃ ta| taṃ saiṭṭhaṃ|

kusalaṃkusalaṃ jahanti aittha kusalaṃ akusalaṃ jahanti padavibhāgau kātabbau| kucchitaṃ pāpadhammaṃ salayati calayati  kampaiti viddhaṃsaitīti kusalaṃ, catubhūmikakusalaṃ labbhati| kupubbau saladhātu a| tattha kāmāvacarakusalaṃ tadaṅgappahānaina pāpadhammaṃ salayati calayati, mahaggata kusalaṃ vikkhambhanappahānaina salayati kampaiti, laukuttara kusalaṃ samucchaidappahānaina salayati viddhaṃsaitīti atthau|

atha vā kucchitaṃ apāyadvāraṃ salanti saṃvaranti pidahanti sādhavau aitaināti kusalaṃ, karaṇasādhanauyaṃ| idha pana jahanti vuttattā taibhūmakakusalamaivādhippaitaṃ| taṃ pana anavaj‍jasukhavipākalakkhaṇaṃ, akusalaviddhaṃsanarasaṃ, vaudāna pac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ, iṭṭhavipākapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ vā, yaunisaumanasikāra padaṭṭhānaṃ| na kusalaṃ akusalaṃ, mittapaṭipakkhau amittau viya pahāyakapahātabbabhāvaina kusalapaṭipakkhanti atthau| taṃ pana sāvaj‍jadukkhavipākalakkhaṇaṃ, sāvaj‍jāniṭṭhavipāka lakkhaṇaṃ vā, anatthajananarasaṃ, aniṭṭhavipākapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ, ayaunisaumanasikārapadaṭṭhānaṃ| puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhāraānaiñ‍jā bhisaṅkhārasaṅkhātaṃ kusalaṃ apuñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārasaṅkhātaṃ akusalañ‍ca kusalaṃkusalaṃ| taṃ pana padaṃ jahantipadai kammaṃ|

jahanti maggakkhaṇai samucchaidappahānaina phalakkhaṇai paṭippassambhanappahānaina pajahantaṃ viddhaṃsaintanti atthau| tañhi arahattamaggañāṇaina jahati pajahati, phalañāṇaina jahiṃ pajahiṃ paṭippassambhinti vā jahau, buddhau| hādhātu cāgai a| kiñ‍cāpi panaittha kusalaṃ maggaina pahātabbadhammai na vuttaṃ, vaṭṭamūlabhūtai avij‍jātaṇhādikilaisai pana arahattamagga ñāṇaina jahitai laukiyakusaluppattiyā mūlabhūtāya avij‍jātaṇhāya abhāvā puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārau saṃsārai puna paṭisandhiyā nibbattaituṃ na sak‍kauti, tasmā vaṭṭamūlabhūtai kilaisai jahitai phalūpacāraina kusalampi jahitaṃ nāma hauti| rūpaṃ bhikkhavai na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajahathātiādīsu viya| arahantaina hi abhisamācārikavattaṃ katampi kusalanti na vuttaṃ, kriyāti pana vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

vuttañhi nidānavaggasaṃyuttaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘khīṇāsavaina hi kataṃ kammaṃ naiva kusalaṃ hauti nākusalaṃ, avipākaṃ hutvā kriyamattai tiṭṭhatī’’ti|

aṅguttaraṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘arahattamaggau ca kusalākusala kammakkhayāya saṃvattatīti aivaṃ taina taṃ bhij‍jatī’’ti vuttaṃ|

taina vuttaṃ cūḷaniddaisapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘arahattamaggañāṇaina abhisaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina yai uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, aitthaitai nirujjhanti vūpasamanti atthaṃ gacchanti paṭippassambhantī’’ti|

amatanti maraṇavirahitaṃ| sau jinau hi na matau amatau, maraṇadhammassa nibbattakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ arahatta maggaina samucchinditattā puna maraṇatau virahitauti vā amatau| atha vā nibbānai ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina adhigatai natthi mataṃ maraṇaṃ yassa jinassāti amatauti vuc‍cati| jinaina hi khandhamac‍cumāraṃ ajitaupi kilaisamāraṃ jayitvā taisaṃ avassaṃ jiyamānattā phalūpacāraina amatauti vuc‍catīti vaiditabbaṃ|

puna amatanti amatanibbānasaman‍nāgataṃ| natthi mataṃ maraṇaṃ aitthāti amataṃ, nibbānaṃ| taṃ pana santilakkhaṇaṃ, ac‍cutarasaṃ, animittapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ, nissaraṇapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ vā, nibbānassa pana padaṭṭhānaṃ na labbhati|

vuttañhi milindapañhai nāgasainatthairaina ‘‘na kainaci kāraṇaina nibbānaṃ uppaj‍jatī’’ti|

taṃ assa atthīti amatau, jinau| saddhāditau ṇāti suttaina assathyatthai ṇapac‍cayau| saddhau pañ‍ñautiādīsu viya| assa jinassa catumaggañāṇaina sacchikataṃ amataṃ nibbānaṃ atthīti atthau, taṃ amataṃ|

santanti sabbakilaisadarathaihi ca vaṭṭadukkhaihi ca suvūpasantaṃ| sau hi buddhau sabbakilaisadarathavaṭṭadukkhaihi samittha vūpasamitthāti santauti vuc‍cati| samudhātu upasamai ta| dhātvantassa laupaṃ tapac‍cayassantauādaisaṃ katvā padasiddhi vaiditabbā| saṃsāravaṭṭai darathakārakānaṃ kilaisānaṃ baudhimaṇḍai arahattamaggañāṇaina samucchinditattā sītibhūtau santakāyacittauti vuttaṃ hauti, taṃ santaṃ|

asamanti sīlādiguṇaihi kainaci puggalaina asadisaṃ, sau hi buddhau natthi sīlādiguṇaihi samau sadisau aitassāti asamauti vuc‍cati| imasmiṃ sattalaukai sīla samādhipañ‍ñādiguṇaihi buddhaina sadisau kauci manussau vā daivau vā brahmā vā natthīti atthau| bhagavatau hi asīti anubyañ‍janapaṭimaṇḍita bāttiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi vicitrarūpakāyau ca sabbākāraihi parisuddhasīlakkhandhādiguṇa ratanasamiddhau dhammakāyau ca puñ‍ñamahattathāmamahatta iddhimahattayasamahattapañ‍ñāmahattādiguṇaihi ca paramuk‍kaṃsagatau ca atthi, tasmā bhagavā sīlādiguṇaihi kainaci asamau asadisauti vuttaṃ hauti|

asamaṃ dadanti laukiyadhammaina asadisaṃ maggaphalanibbāna dhammaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ sattānaṃ daisanāñāṇaina dāyakaṃ| aittha ca asamanti natthi aittha aitaisaṃ vā samau sadisau laukiyadhammauti asamau, maggaphalanibbānaṃ labbhati| atha vā asamanti añ‍ñaisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggabhāvatau catubbidhaina ariya maggaina sacchikātabbaṃ nibbānaṃ vuc‍cati| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘yāvatā bhikkhavai dhammā saṅkhatā vā asaṅkhatā vā, virāgau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti| tassa pana padassa dadantipadaina  kammabhāvaina sambandhau| dadātīti dadau, buddhau| dādhātu a, sau kainaci laukiyadhammaina asamaṃ asadisaṃ maggaphalanibbānasaṅkhātaṃ laukuttaradhammaṃ mahākaruṇāppadhānaina amatādhigamapaṭipattidassanaina daisanāñāṇaina vainaiyyānaṃ sattānaṃ dātāti atthau| taṃ asamaṃ dadaṃ|

(kha) dutiyagāthāyaṃ pana saraṇanti sabbasattalaukassa paṭisaraṇabhūtau parāyaṇau laiṇau tāṇauti vuttaṃ hauti| sau hi buddhau saranti paṭisaranti aittha sattalaukāti saraṇauti vuc‍cati| saradhātu yu| atha vā saranti ausaranti samausaranti aittha sattalaukāti saraṇauti vuc‍cati| saranti cintainti aittha saddhā pasan‍nā daivamanussā saraṇamidanti saraṇauti ca| saranti paṭivijjhanti aitthupagatā daivamanussā saddhammanti saraṇauti ca vuc‍cati| sara cintāyaṃ yu| saraṇagamanaṭṭhānai pana sarati hiṃsati bhayaṃ santāpaṃ dukkhaṃ kilaisañ‍cāti saraṇaṃ| ratanatta yanti aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ| taṃ saraṇaṃ|

puna saraṇaṃ laukanti sattasaṅkhāraukāsasaṅkhataṃ laukaṃ sarantaṃ jānantaṃ| aittha hi laukayati patiṭṭhayati aittha puñ‍ñāpuñ‍ñañ‍ca tabbipākau cāti laukau, satta laukau labbhati| laukadhātu patiṭṭhāyaṃ ṇa| luj‍jati paluj‍jatīti laukau, saṅkhāralaukau| lujadhātuṇa| laukiyati vicittākāraina dissatīti laukau, laukiyanti patiṭṭhiyanti aittha vā jaṅgamā ca thāvarā cāti laukau, cak‍kavāḷasaṅkhatau aukāsalaukau labbhati| laukadhātuṇa| aittha ca sassatau laukauti vā asassatau laukauti vāti āgataṭṭhānai indriyabaddhānaṃ rūpādīnaṃ khandhānaṃ samūhau santānau ca sattauyaiva laukau sattalaukauti vacanatthaina sattalaukauti vaiditabbau| aikau laukau sabbai sattā āhā raṭṭhitikāti  āgataṭṭhānai indriyabaddhānaṃ vā anindriyabaddhānaṃ vā khandhānaṃ samūhau santānau ca pac‍cayaihi saṅkhariyantīti saṅkhārā, saṅkhārāaiva laukau saṅkhāralaukauti vacanatthaina saṅkhāralaukauti|

‘‘yāvatā candimasūriyā pariharanti,

disā bhanti viraucamānā|

tāva sahassadhā laukau,

aittha tai vattati vasau’’ti||

āgataṭṭhānai anindriyabaddhānaṃ rūpādīnaṃ samūhau santānau ca avakasanti patiṭṭhahanti aitthāti aukāsau, sauyaiva laukau aukāsalaukauti vacanatthaina aukāsa laukauti vaiditabbau, taṃ laukaṃ| sarati jānāti ajānīti vā saraṇau, buddhau| sara cintāyaṃ yu| laukaṃ saraṇanti sambandhau| buddhau pana sattalaukaṃ, saṅkhāralaukaṃ, aukāsalaukaṃ, kilaisalaukaṃ, bhavalaukaṃ, indriya laukaṃ, khandhalaukaṃ, āyatanalaukaṃ dhātulaukañ‍cātiādimanaikalaukaṃ anāvaraṇañāṇaina paṭivijjhatīti vuttaṃ hauti|

araṇanti nik‍kilaisaṃ, kilaisasaṅkhātaraṇavirahitaṃ vā| raṇasaddau ca kilaisaisu ca yuddhai cuṇṇavicuṇṇakaraṇai ca dissati| tathā hi saraṇā dhammā araṇā dhammātiādīsu kilaisaisu dissati| tai hi raṇanti kandanti sattā aitaihīti raṇāti vuc‍canti|

‘‘dhanuggahau asadisau, rājaputtau mahabbalau|

sabbāmittai raṇaṃ katvā, saṃyamaṃ ajjhupāgamī’’ti||

asadisajātakai yuddhai dissati| raṇaṃ katvāti hi yuddhaṃ katvā, saṃyamanti saṃyataṃ isipabbaj‍jaṃ ajjhupāgamīti atthau| tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca raṇaṃ karauntau, dhāviṃsu tai aṭṭhadisā  samantatauti chaddantajātakai cuṇṇavicuṇṇakaraṇai dissati| raṇaṃ karauntauti hi cuṇṇaṃ vicuṇṇaṃ karauntau| taiti sabbai aṭṭhasahassanāgāti atthau| idha pana kilaisai daṭṭhabbau| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau| raṇanti sattai rāgādayau cuṇṇainti pīḷaintīti raṇā, raṇanti sattā aitaihi kandanti paridaivantīti vā raṇā, kilaisā| raṇadhātu saddai a| natthi raṇā kilaisā aitassāti araṇauti vuc‍cati| jinassa kilaisamārassa baudhimūlai jiyamānattā raṇā kilaisā na santīti atthau| taṃ araṇaṃ|

araṇaṃ karanti vainaiyyasantānai raṇasaṅkhatakilaisassa abhāvakaraṇaṃ| aittha ca raṇanti aitaṃ yaihi abhibhūtā sattā kandanti paridavanti aitaisaṃ rāgādīnaṃ adhivacanaṃ| taisaṃ abhāvaṃ vainaiyyasantānai daisanāñāṇaina karauti atthau| atha vā karautīti karau, buddhau| taṃ karaṃ|

abhayanti jātibhayādīnaṃ abhāvatau nibbhayaṃ| aittha hi bhayaṃ cittutrāsabhayaṃ, ñāṇabhayaṃ, ārammaṇabhayaṃ, auttappa bhayañ‍cāti catubbidhaṃ hauti| tattha bhāyati cittaināti bhayaṃ, cittutrāsabhayaṃ| ñāṇaina tīraitvā bhāyatīti bhayaṃ, ñāṇaṃ| bhāyati aitasmāti bhayaṃ, ārammaṇabhayaṃ| bhāyati aitaina pāpatauti bhayaṃ, auttappabhayaṃ| bhīdhātu bhayai ṇa|

mahāniddaisapāḷiyaṃ pana āgatāni jātibhayaṃ, jarābhayaṃ, byādhibhayaṃ, maraṇabhayaṃ, rājabhayaṃ, caurabhayaṃ, aggibhayaṃ, udakabhayaṃ, attānuvādabhayaṃ, parānuvādabhayaṃ, daṇḍabhayaṃ, duggatibhayaṃ, ūmibhayaṃ, kumbhīḷabhayaṃ, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukabhayaṃ, ājīvikabhayaṃ, asilaukabhayaṃ, parisayasāraj‍jabhayaṃ, madana bhayaṃ, duggatibhayantiādīni gahitāni| natthi bhayāni aitassāti abhayau, buddhau| tassa bhayānaṃ kāraṇa bhūtānaṃ  sabbakilaisānaṃ arahattamaggaina samucchinditattā sabbabhayaṃ natthīti atthau|

puna abhayaṃ ṭhānaṃ nāyakanti aittha abhayantipadaṃ ṭhānantipadai visaisanaṃ| ṭhānantipadaṃ nāyakantipadai kammaṃ| bhayasaddassa atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| natthi sabbabhayaṃ aittha nibbānaiti abhayaṃ, nibbānaṃ| buddhapac‍caikabuddha arahantā ṭhanti uppaj‍janti aitthāti ṭhānaṃ, nibbānaṃ| vainaiyyasattai naitīti nāyakau, buddhau| nīdhātu nayanai ṇvu| sau buddhau bhabbai kulaputtai maggādhi gamappattiyā dhammadaisanāya maggaphalañāṇaṃ adhigamaintau nibbānaṭṭhānaṃ naitīti atthau, taṃ nāyakaṃ|

puna nāyakaṃ namaiti nāyakaṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ namai namāmīti sambandhau| tattha nāyakanti vainaiyyasattai saṃsāraṇṇavatau nibbānapāraṃ naitīti nāyakau, buddhau| sau hi yathā nāvikau janai nāvāya aurimatīratau pārimatīraṃ naiti, aivaṃ sattai maggaṅganāvāya saṃsāraṇṇavatau nibbāna pāraṃ naiti pāpuṇaitīti atthau| atha vā nāyakanti tiṇṇaṃ laukānaṃ jaiṭṭhabhāvagamanattā nāyakau jaiṭṭha saiṭṭhavuḍḍhatarau| sau hi jaiṭṭhabhāvaṃ naiti gacchati pāpuṇātīti nāyakauti vuc‍cati| nīdhātu gatimhi ṇvu| sau pana idha laukai avij‍jaṇḍagatānaṃ sabbapajānaṃ paṭhamataraṃ avij‍jaṇḍakausaṃ arahattamaggaina padālaitvā ariyāya jātiyā jātattā sabbasattānaṃ saiṭṭhajaiṭṭhavuḍḍhatarabhāvaṃ gatauti atthau| taṃ nāyakaṃ| namaiti ahaṃ tīhi dvāraihi sak‍kac‍caṃ namāmīti atthau| aittha hi vattamānamivibhattiyā k‍vacidhātutyādisuttaina aiādaisau hauti| ayaṃ panaittha vāc‍catthādinayau| namaitipadassa ākhyātapadattā vacanatthau na kātabbau| vuttañhi saddavidūhi –

‘‘ruḷhīkhyātaṃ nipātañ‍cu, pasaggālapanaṃ tathā|

sabbanāmantimaitaisu, na katau viggahau chasū’’ti||

taṃ  pana padaṃ namudhātuvattamānaamhayaugaparassapadamivibhatyantaṃ ākhyātapadaṃ| namudhātuyā vāc‍catthau namanakriyā saṅkhātau kusalacittuppādau padhānavasaina labbhati| mivibhattiyā vāc‍catthau taina cittuppādaina upalakkhitau uppāda vantau tadākārau puggalau appadhānavasaina labbhati| padasamudāyassa pana vāc‍catthau kattukammabhāvaisu kattubhūtau atītādikālaisu pac‍cuppan‍nakālabhūtau dhātvattha kiriyāvisaisauva labbhati| ayamaittha saṅkhaipau|

vitthārau panaivaṃ vaiditabbau| namudhātuyā vac‍caṃ sugatādi buddhaguṇaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā vandanākārappavattau kāyavacī viñ‍ñattisamuṭṭhāpakau saddhācaitanāpañ‍ñāpadhānau kusala cittuppādau ca dvai viñ‍ñattiyau asamuṭṭhāpaitvā manaudvārai vandanākārappavattau kusalacittuppādau ca mukhyaina labbhati| buddhaguṇānussaraṇavasaina pavattau yaunisaumanasikā rādipuraicarānucarakusalacittuppādau phalūpacāraina| diṭṭhadhammi kasamparāyikasaṅkhātaṃ pacchimapacchimaphalaṃ raugādiantarāyānaṃ abhāvañ‍ca vā saggamaukkhasampattisukhañ‍ca kāraṇūpacāraina, purai purai vandanā sadisyūpacāraina, vandanassa nāmapañ‍ñatti ṭhānūpacāraina, vatthudvārārammaṇaṃ ṭhānūpacāraina, vandana guṇaina lakkhitaṃ puggaladabbaṃ guṇūpacāraina| uk‍kaṭṭhamajjha muduttamattabhāvau guṇyūpacāraina, avayavabhūtā vandanā aikadaisūpacāraina| samūhabhūtā vandanā aikadaisyūpacāraina, pubbapubbakusalacittuppādau samīpūpacāraina, pacchimapacchima kusalacittuppādau samīpyūpacāraina, paṇāmakusalacittuppādabhūtānaṃ paramatthadhammānaṃ nibyāpārabhāvau taddhammūpacāraina| taisaṃ aivaṃ dhammatābhāvau ataddhammūpacāraina, vandanākārau abhaidabhaidūpacāraina| vandanasatti bhaidaabhaidūpacāraina labbhati| mivibhattiyā vāc‍caṃ taṃmukhyādīhi lakkhitau kattu kriyāvisaisaina ca vattamānavibhattisaṅkhātaina vibhattivisai saina  ca parassapadasaṅkhātaina padavisaisaina ca uttamapurisa visaisaina ca amhayaugavisaisaina ca sabbadhātuvisaisaina ca aikavacanavisaisaina ca pac‍cuppan‍nakālavisaisaina ca dhātvatthakriyāvisaisaina ca aliṅga visaisaina ca bhūvādigaṇa visaisaina ca nauvikaraṇavisaisaina ca bhāvakammakattūsukattuvisaisaina ca lakkhitau ahaṃsaṅkhātau khandhasaṅkhatau santānau appadhānaina labbhati| kriyāpadassa kriyāya padhānaina vācakattā dhātvatthauva padhānaina labbhati| apac‍cayassa vāc‍caṃ bhūvādigaṇajautakā rūpasiddhimattā pada siliṭṭhatā labbhati|

atha vā yau ahaṃ namāmīti sau namai| sāmañ‍ñattha viggahauyaṃ| vāc‍catthau pana vuttanayaina vaiditabbau| namanañ‍caittha buddhassa sīlādianantaguṇaisu nin‍napabbhāratāvasaina paṇāmakriyābhinipphādikā kusalacaitanā| sā ca vandanaiyya vandanakānaṃ khaittajjhāsayasampadāhi diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyabhūtā yathāladdhasampattinimittakassa purimakammassa anubalappadāna vasaina tan‍nibbattitavipākasantatiyā antarāyakarāni upapīḷakaupacchaidakakammāni paṭibāhitvā pavattikālai raugādiantarāyānamappavattiṃ sādhaiti| samparāyai ca upapaj‍javaidanīyabhūtā saggamaukkhasampattisukhaṃ nibbattaitīti|

ayaṃ panaittha dassanatthanayau| namaitiiminā buddhassa sugatādiguṇaṃ ārabbha pavattā tividhā paṇāmakusala caitanā mukhyaina dassaiti| taṃ dassanaina tassā kāraṇabhūtā yaunisaumanasikārādayau ca catucak‍kasampattiñ‍ca buddhuppādanavamakhaittañ‍ca pubbabhāgai pavattaṃ saddhāpañ‍ñāpadhānaṃ kusalacittuppādañ‍ca phalūpacāraina dīpaiti| taddīpanaina tassā phalabhūtaṃ diṭṭhadhammaraugādiantarāyavisausanañ‍ca saggamaukkha sukhañ‍ca paṭhamakāraṇūpacāraina pakāsaiti| tappakāsanaina buddhassa paṇāmārahabhāvaṃ dutiyakāraṇūpacāraina vibhāvaiti| taṃvibhāvanaina vandakassa mai khaittaṅgatabhāvañ‍ca paṇāmai tabbabhāvañ‍ca tatiyakāraṇūpacāraina anubhāvaiti|

ayaṃ panaittha alaṅkāranayau| namaitivācakapadassa vāc‍cabhūtā tividhā buddhapaṇāmakusalacaitanā samaṭṭhāyī bhāvau nāma| kasmā, sarīrattā| atha vā tividhāya buddhapaṇā makusalacaitanāya sampayuttau samādhippadhānau mahā kusalacittuppādau samaṭṭhāyībhāvau nāma| kasmā, buddhassa sugatādiguṇārammaṇai aikaggattā samāhitacittattā vā| sauyaiva pana santarasau nāma| kasmā, buddhapaṇāmacaitanā kusalaṅgaina akusalaṅgassa pahīnattā| buddhantipāṭhasaisa padassa vāc‍cabhūtau buddhadabbau ārammaṇavibhāvau nāma| kasmā, tassa paṇāmakusalacittuppādassa ārammaṇapac‍cayaina upakārattā| buddhassa sugatantyādiguṇau uddīpanavibhāvau nāma| kasmā, paṇāmassa kāraṇattā| kāraṇabhūtaṃ taṃ samaṭṭhāyībhāvaṃ upādāya jānitabbau diṭṭhadhammaraugā diantarāyābhāvau ca samparāyai saggamaukkhasukhasampatti ca ānubhāvau nāma| kasmā, ānisaṃsaphalattā|

tattha hi kiñ‍cāpi ānubhāvadīpakaṃ padaṃ natthi| kāraṇassa pana phalaina vinābhāvatau kāraṇassa phalaṃ viñ‍ñaiyyanti| atha vā namaitipadassa sāmatthiyatau ānubhāvau dassitauyaivāti daṭṭhabbau| āvibhūtau ussukau ca buddhapaṇāmassa ānisaṃsapajānanā mati ca alasābhāvau ca cittappasādau ca samādhityādi ca byabhicārībhāvau nāma| laumahaṃsādibhāvau satvaṃkābhāvau nāmāti alaṅkāra nayau vaiditabbau| aivaṃ pacchimapacchimagāthāsūti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| sugataṃ saubhanagataṃ sundaranibbānaṭṭhānaṃ gataṃ vā sammā gataṃ vā, sugataṃ sammā vacanaṃ gadaṃ bhāsanaṃ, saiṭṭhaṃ pasatthataraṃ satipaṭṭhānādibhaidai dhammai  gavaisantaṃ vā santaihi gavaisitabbaṃ vā sattānaṃ hitasukhaṃ icchitaṃ vā, kusalaṃkusalaṃ kusalaṃakusalaṃ, jahaṃ jahitaṃ, amataṃ maraṇavirahitaṃ amataṃ amatanibbānasaman‍nā gataṃ, santaṃ kilaisadarathavūpasamaṃ, asamaṃ laukiyadhammaina asadisaṃ maggaphalanibbānadhammaṃ| dadaṃ dātaṃ dāyakaṃ vā, saraṇaṃ sabbasattalaukassa paṭisaraṇabhūtaṃ| laukaṃ sattasaṅkha raukāsasaṅkhataṃ tilaukaṃ, saraṇaṃ jānantaṃ, araṇaṃ nik‍kilaisaṃ, kilaisasaṅkhātaraṇavirahitaṃ vā| araṇaṃ sattaisu kilaisarajassābhāvaṃ, karaṃ daisanāñāṇaina kataṃ, abhayaṃ nibbhayaṃ, abhayaṃ nibbhayaṭṭhānaṃ nibbānadisaṃ, nāyakaṃ sattai nibbānapāraṃ naintaṃ, nāyakaṃ tilaukānaṃ jaiṭṭhaṃ pāmaukkhaṃ buddhaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ tīhi dvāraihi ahaṃ namai namāmīti|

paṭhamavandanagāthādvayavaṇṇanā samattā|

2.

 nayanasubhagakāyaṅgaṃ,

madhuravarasaraupaitaṃ|

amitaguṇagaṇādhāraṃ,

dasabalamatulaṃ vandai||

2. aivaṃ paṭhamaṃ gāthādvayaina buddhassa namassitvā idāni nayanasubhagakāyaṅgantyādīhi pañ‍cahi guṇaihi thaumitvā buddhaṃ vandituṃ nayanasubhagakāyaṅgantiādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana na na magaṇayuttattā navakkharaihi lakkhitā bhujagasu sugāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘bhujagasusumatā nāmau’’ti|

ayaṃ panaittha yaujanā| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ nā ca dvai nagaṇāmau ca magaṇau ca yadi siyā| sā bhujagasusu gāthāti matā ñātabbāti| tattha pana nayanasubhagakāyaṅganti sabbasattānaṃ cakkhussa saubhanaṃ gataina kāyaṅgainasaman‍nāgataṃ| aittha  hi nayananti samavisamaṃ dassaintaṃ attabhāvaṃ naitīti nayanaṃ, cakkhu labbhati| nīdhātu yu| subhaṃ saubhanaṃ gacchatīti subhagaṃ, kāyaṅgaṃ labbhati| subhasaddūpapadau gamudhātu k‍vi| kāyassa aṅgaṃ avayavaṃ kāyaṅgaṃ| bhagavatau kāyaṅgaṃ labbhati| chaṭṭhītappurisasamāsauyaṃ| nayanassa subhagaṃ nayanasubhagaṃ, taṃ kāyaṅgaṃ yassa buddhassāti nayanasubhaga kāyaṅgau, buddhau| sau hi rūpakāyassa dassanā nuttariyaguṇayaugatau sabbasattānaṃ pasādacakkhussa saubhanaṃ gataina dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi ca asītyā nubyañ‍janalakkhaṇaihi ca lakkhitaina kāyaṅgaina saman‍nāgatauti atthau|

madhuravarasaraupaitanti piyapaimanīyauttamasaddaina saman‍nāgataṃ, sau hi bhagavā madhiyati piyati paimiyati vāti madhurau| madhadhātu urau| vuttañhi majjhimapaṇṇāsaṭīkāyaṃ ‘‘madhurauti piyau paimanīyau apalibuddhau’’ti| atha vā madhiyati assādiyatīti madhurau, madhati sabbasattānaṃ pītiṃ undaiti vaḍḍhaitīti madhurau, madhadhātu undai urau| taina vuttaṃ saddanītiyaṃ ‘‘madha undai’’ti| varitabbau icchitabbau patthitabbau vāti varau, sarau| varadhātu icchāyaṃ a| sarati gacchati sautaviñ‍ñāṇārammaṇabhāvanti sarau, saddau| saradhātu gatiyaṃ a| sarati gacchati suyyamānatanti vā sarau, sariyati suṇiyatīti vā sarau, bhagavatau aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau saddau labbhati| upasampaj‍jatīti upaitau, upapubbaidhātu ta| madhurau ca varau ca sau sarau cāti madhuravarasarau, taina upaitau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatauti madhuravarasaraupaitauti vuc‍cati| bhagavatau pana hadaya vatthunau suvisuddhattā pittasaimhādīhi apalibuddhattā daiva manussaihi madhuraina piyaina manāpiyaina varaina icchitapatthi taina aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataina saraina saddaina upaitau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatauti vuttaṃ hauti|

aittha  ca kiñ‍cāpi saraupaitanti sāmañ‍ñavasaina vuttaṃ, bhagavatau sarau pana visaṭṭhau ca, viñ‍ñaiyyau, mañ‍ju, savanīyau, bindu, avisārī, gambhīrau, nin‍nādi cāti visaisaina aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatauti atthau daṭṭhabbau| vuttañhi majjhima paṇṇāsai brahmāyusuttai ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau khau panassa gautamassa mukhatau ghausau niccharati visaṭṭhau ca, viñ‍ñaiyyau ca, mañ‍ju ca, savanīyau ca, bindu ca, avisārī ca, gambhīrau ca, nin‍nādi ca| yathāparisaṃ khau pana sau bhavaṃ gautamau saraina viñ‍ñāpaiti, na cassa bahiddhā parisāya ghausau na niccharatī’’ti|

tassāṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana visaṭṭhauti siniddhau apalibuddhau| viñ‍ñaiyyauti viñ‍ñāpanīyau pākaṭau| visaṭṭhattāyaiva caisa viñ‍ñaiyyau hauti| mañ‍jūti madhurau| savanīyauti sautasukhau, madhurattāyaiva caisa savanīyau hauti| bindūti sampiṇḍitau| avisārīti avisaṭau| binduttāyaiva caisa avisārī hauti| gambhīrauti gambhīrasamuṭṭhitau| nin‍nādīti nin‍nadavā| gambhīrattāyaiva caisa nin‍nādi hauti| yathāparisanti cak‍kavāḷapariyantāpi aikabaddhaṃ parisaṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti| bahiddhāti aṅgulimattampi parisatau bahiddhā na gacchati| kasmā| sau aivarūpau madhurasarau akāraṇā mā nassīti| iti bhagavatau ghausau parisāya mattakainaiva caratīti vaṇṇitauti| abhidhānappadīpikāyañ‍ca vuttā saṅgahagāthā –

‘‘visaṭṭhamañ‍juviñ‍ñaiyyā, savanīyāvisārinau|

bindugambhīranin‍nādi, tyaivamaṭṭhaṅgikau sarau’’ti||

yathā hi brahmunau sarau pittasaimhaihi apalibuddhattā visuddhau madhurau hauti| yathā ca sīhabyagghādīhi vāḷamigaihi anubandhā dhāvantā khuddakamigā karavīkasakuṇassa vikūjamānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā sarassa madhurattā maraṇabhayaṃ hitvā adhāvitvā ukkhittapādaṃ  anikkhipitvā tiṭṭhantā suṇanti, aivaṃ karavīkassa saddau dhammatābhāvaina madhurau piyau manāpau hauti| aivaṃ bhagavatā kataṃ kammaṃ vatthuṃ saudhaiti, vatthussa suddhattā pittasaimhaihi ca apalibuddhattā bhagavatau nābhitau samuṭṭhahantau saraupi aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau suvisuddhau atimadhurau atipiyau atimanāpau hauti| sā panaisā hīnūpamā hauti, bhagavatau hi sarau taihi saddaihi satabhāgaina sahassabhāgaina atimadhurau atipiyau atimanāpau hauti, tathā hi dhammāsau karañ‍ñau asandhimittā daivī karavīkasakuṇassa madhurasaddaṃ sutvā cintaisi ‘‘imassa tiracchānassa madhurau saddau, kau nāma sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasiriṃ pattassa bhagavatau atimadhurau saddau’’ti, pītiṃ uppādaitvā taṃ pītiṃ avijahitvā sattahi jaṅghasataihi saddhiṃ sautāpattiphalai patiṭṭhāti, tasmā bhagavatau madhuravarasaraupaitanti thaumaitīti|

amitaguṇagaṇādhāranti aparimiṇitabbasīlādiguṇasamūhānaṃ patiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ| aittha ca samitauti parimiṇitabbauti mitau, mādhātu parimāṇai ta, na mitau amitau| guṇauti sīlādayau dhammā| tai hi guṇainti taṃsamaṅgīpuggalaṃ pakāsaintīti guṇāti vuc‍canti| guṇadhātu pakāsanai a| maṇisāramañ‍jūsāṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘guṇanti attanau ādhāraṃ pākaṭaṃ karauntīti guṇā’’ti vuttaṃ|

saddanītidhātumālāyañ‍ca ‘‘guṇa āmantanai, guṇauti sīlādayau dhammā, kainaṭṭhaina tai guṇā, gauṇāpiyati āmantāpiyati attani patiṭṭhitau puggalau daṭṭhuṃ sautuṃ pūjituñ‍ca icchantaihi janaihīti guṇau| aittha kiñ‍cāpi sīlādi dhammānaṃ āmantāpanaṃ natthi, tathāpi taṃhaitu āmantanaṃ nimantanañ‍ca taiyaiva karaunti nāmāti aivaṃ vuttaṃ| añ‍ñai pana guñ‍jantai abyayantai iti guṇāti atthaṃ vadanti| tadanurūpaṃ pana dhātusaddaṃna passāma| guṇa āmantanai ic‍caiva passāma| vicāraitvā gahaitabba’’nti vuttaṃ| amitau guṇau amitaguṇau, taisaṃ gaṇau samūhau amitaguṇagaṇau, taisaṃ| ādhārati tiṭṭhati aitthāti amitaguṇagaṇādhārau, jinau labbhati| sau pana samuddaudakaṃ nāḷipattaina aparimitaṃ viya kainaci aparimitānaṃ sīlādiguṇasamūhānaṃ ādhārau patiṭṭhānabhūtauti atthau|

dasabalanti añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇaṃ tathāgatadasabalasampan‍naṃ| aittha hi balanti kaihici viruddhaihi kāraṇai hi balanti na kampainti thirantīti balaṃ, dasa balāni yassa sauti dasabalau, buddhau| taṃ pana duvidhaṃ hauti tathā gatassa dasabalaṃ kāyadasabalañ‍ca, ñāṇadasabalañ‍cāti| taisu hi kāyabalaṃ hatthikulānusāraina vaiditabbaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ paurāṇaihi –

‘‘kāḷā vakañ‍ca gaṅgaiyyaṃ, paṇḍaraṃ tambapiṅgalaṃ|

gandha maṅgalahaimañ‍ca, upausathaṃ chaddantimai dasā’’ti||

imāni hi dasa hatthikulāni| tattha kāḷāvakanti pakatihatthikulaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ purisānaṃ kāyabalaṃ, taṃ aikassa kāḷāvakahatthinau| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ kāḷāvakānaṃ balaṃ, taṃ aikassa gaṅgaiyyassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ gaṅgaiyyānaṃ, taṃ aikassa paṇḍarassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ paṇḍarānaṃ, taṃ aikassa tambassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ tambānaṃ, taṃ aikassa piṅgalassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ piṅgalānaṃ, taṃ aikassa gandhahatthinau| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ gandhahatthīnaṃ, taṃ aikassa maṅgalassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ maṅgalānaṃ, taṃ aikassa haimavatassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ haimavatānaṃ, taṃ upausathassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ upausathānaṃ, taṃ aikassa chaddantassa| yaṃ dasan‍naṃ chaddantānaṃ, taṃ aikassa tathāgatassa, nārāyanasaṅghātabalantipi idamaiva vuc‍cati| tadaitaṃ pakatihatthīnaṃ gaṇanāya hatthikauṭisahassānaṃ| purisagaṇanāya dasan‍naṃ purisakauṭisahassānaṃ balaṃ hauti| idaṃ tathāgatassa kāyabalaṃ hauti| idaṃ pana tāsu tāsu pāḷīsu na āgataṃ| aṭṭhakathāsuyaiva āgataṃ| taina vuttaṃ dasaka nipāta aṅguttaraṭīkāyaṃ ‘‘dasa balañāṇaṃ pana pāḷiyaṃ āgatamaiva| na kāyabalaṃ viya aṭṭha kathārūḷhamaivāti adhippāyau’’ti| aittha ca nārāyana saṅghāta balanti aittha nārā vuc‍canti rasmiyau, tā bahū nānāvidhā itau uppaj‍jantīti nārāyanaṃ, vajiraṃ| tasmā nārāyanasaṅghāta balanti vajirasaṅghātabalanti atthauti|

ñāṇadasabalaṃ pana vibhaṅgapāḷiyañ‍ca mūlapaṇṇāsai mahā sīhanādasuttai dasakanipātaaṅguttarapāḷiyañ‍ca vitthāraina āgatamaiva| sukhaggahaṇatthaṃ pana saṅkhaipaina dassayissāmi| saiyyathidaṃ| ṭhānāṭṭhānañāṇañ‍ca, kammasamādānānaṃ vipākañāṇaṃ, sabbatthagāminipaṭipadāñāṇaṃ, anaikadhātu nānādhātu lauka ñāṇaṃ, sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikataṃ ñāṇaṃ, parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparaupariyattiñāṇaṃ, jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ ñāṇaṃ, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇaṃ, sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ ñāṇaṃ, āsavānaṃ khayañāṇañ‍cāti| ayaṃ pana kamau abhidhammavibhaṅgapāḷiyaṃ āgatak‍kamau|

cutūpapātañāṇaṭṭhānai pana mūlapaṇṇāsapāḷiyaṃ mahāsīhanādasuttai ca dasakanipātaaṅguttarapāḷiyaṃ dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ āgataṃ| atthatau pana dibbacakkhuñāṇaina cutūpapātassa passitattā sadisamaivāti| tattha hi ṭhānāṭṭhānañāṇanti kāraṇākāraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ jānanañāṇaṃ| kāraṇañhi yasmā tattha phalaṃ tiṭṭhati tadāyattavuttitāya uppaj‍jati caiva pavattati ca, tasmā ṭhānanti vuc‍cati| kammasamādānānaṃ vipākañāṇanti samādiyitvā katānaṃ atītānāgata pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kusalākusalakammānaṃ sugatiduggatipaṭisandhisaṅkhātaṃ vipākaṃ nibbānaphalañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ| sabbattha gāminipaṭipadaṃ  ñāṇanti sabbagatigāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ| tattha ca paṭipadanti paṭipaj‍jati pāpuṇāti aitaināti paṭipadaṃ, maggaṃ| gatigāmininti nirayādipañ‍cagatigāminiṃ| agatigāmininti nibbānagāminiṃ| vuttañhi ‘‘nibbānañ‍cāhaṃ sāriputta pajānāmi nibbānagāminiñ‍ca paṭipada’’nti|

anaikadhātunānādhātulaukaṃ ñāṇanti aittha anaika dhātūti cakkhudhātuādīhi kāmadhātūhi vā bahudhātuṃ| nānādhātūti tāsaṃyaiva dhātūnaṃ vilakkhaṇattā visadisa sabhāvattā nānāppakāradhātuṃ| laukanti khandhāyatana dhātulaukaṃ| ñāṇanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ| sattānaṃ adhimuttikataṃ ñāṇanti sattānaṃ hīnādīhi adhimuttīhi nānādhimuttikabhāvaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ| parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparaupariyattiñāṇanti aittha parasattānanti padhānasattānaṃ| parapuggalānanti tatau paraisaṃ hīnasattānaṃ| aikatthamaiva vā aitaṃ padadvayaṃ vainaiyyavasaina dvaidhā vuttaṃ| indriyaparaupariyattiñāṇanti saddhādīnaṃ indriyānaṃ parabhāvaṃ vaḍḍhañ‍ca aparabhāvaṃ hāniñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ|

jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ ñāṇanti aittha jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnanti paṭhamādīnaṃ catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ, rūpī rūpāni passatītiādīnaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ vimaukkhānaṃ, savitak‍kasavicārādīnaṃ tiṇṇaṃ samādhīnaṃ, paṭhamajjhāna samāpattiādīnañ‍ca navan‍naṃ anupubbasamāpattīnaṃ| saṃkilaisanti hānabhāgiyadhammaṃ| vaudānanti visaisabhāgiyadhammaṃ| vuṭṭhānanti yaina kāraṇaina jhānādīhi vuṭṭhahanti, taṃ kāraṇaṃ| ñāṇanti yathābhūtaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ| pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇanti pubbai nivutthakkhandhānussaraṇasaṅkhātaṃ pajānanañāṇaṃ| sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ ñāṇanti sattānaṃ cutiñ‍ca upapātañ‍ca pajānanaṃ dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ| āsavānaṃ khayañāṇanti kāmāsavādīnaṃ khayasaṅkhātaṃ āsavaniraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānana ñāṇanti| imāni pana ñāṇāni añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇāni tathāgatassa dasabalañāṇānīti daṭṭhabbāni|

haunti caittha –

‘‘ṭhānāṭṭhānaṃ vipākañ‍ca, ñāṇaṃ paṭipadaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

anaikadhātulaukañ‍ca, sattānaṃ adhimuttikaṃ|| [sā panaisā saitavavipulāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā|]

indriyaparāparañ‍ca, jhānādinaṃ saṃkilaisaṃ|

vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ ñāṇaṃ, pubbainivāsānussatiṃ|

sattānaṃ cutūpapātaṃ, āsavakkhayaṃ ñāṇanti|

dasimāni buddhassa cā, sādhāraṇāni ñāṇānī’’ti|| [sā panaisā sāmañ‍ñagāthāti daṭṭhabbā|]

nanu dasabalañāṇaṃ nāma pāṭiyaik‍kaṃ natthi, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇamaiva hauti, atha kasmā taṃ ñāṇaṃ dasabhaidaina vuttanti| vuc‍catai| añ‍ñamaiva hi dasabalañāṇaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ, dasabalañhi sakasakakic‍camaiva jānāti| sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ pana tampi tatau avasaisampi pajānāti| dasabalañāṇaisu hi paṭhamaṃ kāraṇākāraṇamaiva jānāti| dutiyaṃ kammantara vipākantaramaiva| tatiyaṃ kammaparicchaidamaiva| catutthaṃ dhātu nānattakāraṇamaiva| pañ‍camaṃ sattānaṃ ajjhāsayādhimuttimaiva| chaṭṭhaṃ indriyānaṃ tikkhamudubhāvamaiva| sattamaṃ jhānādīhi saddhiṃ taisaṃ saṃkilaisādimaiva| aṭṭhamaṃ pubbai nivutthakhandhasantatimaiva| navamaṃ sattānaṃ cutipaṭisandhimaiva| dasamaṃ sac‍caparicchaidamaiva| sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ pana aitaihi jānitabbañ‍ca tatau uttariñ‍ca pajānāti| aitaisaṃ pana kic‍caṃ na sabbaṃ karauti| tañhi jhānaṃ hutvā appaituṃ na sak‍kauti| iddhi hutvā vikubbituṃ na sak‍kauti| maggau hutvā kilaisai khaipaituṃ na sak‍kauti| aivaṃ taisaṃ ñāṇadvayānaṃ visaisauti| taṃ pana idha ñāṇadvayaṃ adhippaitanti|

atulanti  sīlādīhi guṇaihi kainaci puggalaina asadisaṃ| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau| tulanaṃ ummānaṃ tulā, tula sadisyaṃ| saddanītiyañhi ‘‘tula ummānai’’ti vuttaṃ| atha vā tuliyati pamiyati aitāyāti tulā, pakatitulā| tulā viyāti tulā, ñāṇaṃ| tāya sammitau tulau| sammita taddhitai apac‍cayau| tulasadisañāṇaina sammitau puggalau| na tulau atulau| kammadhārayamissakatappurisa samāsauyaṃ| sīlādiguṇaihi kainaci puggalaina asadisau bhagavā labbhati| atha vā natthi tulau sadisau puggalau aitassāti atulau, bhagavā| atagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇauyaṃ bahubbīhi| sīlādiguṇaihi kainaci asadisau aggapuggalauti atthau| sadaivakai laukai aggapuggalabhāvatau| yathāha ‘‘yāvatā bhikkhavai sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati, virāgau saiṭṭhau dhammānaṃ dvipadānañ‍ca cakkhumā’’ti| taṃ atulaṃ buddhanti sambandhau| vandaiti buddhassa nayanasubhagakāyaṅgādiguṇaṃ anussaritvā tīhi dvāraihi sak‍kac‍caṃ ahaṃ vandāmīti atthau| aittha ca mivibhattiyā mahāvisayasuttaina aikārā daisauti daṭṭhabbau| vandanā pana tividhā hauti kāyavacīmanauvandanāvasaināti| yathāha ‘‘tissau imā bhikkhavai vandanā| katamā tissau| kāyaina vandati, vācāya vandati, manasā vandatī’’ti|

tattha yaunisau manasikāracittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaitvā jāṇudvayakapparadvayanalāṭasaṅkhātāni pañ‍ca aṅgāni bhūmiyaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā vandanaiyyānaṃ abhimukhaṃ nipatantau kāyaina vandati nāma| yaṃ sandhāya ‘‘pañ‍capatiṭṭhitaina vanditvā’’ti aṭṭha kathādīsu vuttaṃ| buddhaguṇapadāni vācāya pavattaintau vācāya vandati nāma| vipassissa namatthu, sikhissāpi namatthu, vaissa bhussa namatthu, kakusandhassa namatthu, kauṇāgamanassa namatthu, kassapassa namatthu, aṅgīrassassa namatthu tyādīsupi vācāvandanāyaiva| buddhādi guṇāni anussarantau manasā vandati nāmāti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| nayanasubhagakāyaṅgaṃ sabbasattānaṃ cakkhussa saubhanaṃ gataina kāyaṅgaina saman‍nāgataṃ, madhuravarasaraupaitaṃ piyapaimanīyaina assādi yaina uttamasaddaina saman‍nāgataṃ, amitaguṇagaṇādhāraṃ aparimiṇitabbasīlādiguṇasamūhānaṃ patiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ, dasabalaṃ anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaina tathāgatassa kāyañāṇadasa balaina saman‍nāgataṃ, atulaṃ sīlādiguṇaihi kainaci asadisaṃ buddhaṃ tīhi dvāraihi sak‍kac‍caṃ ahaṃ vandai vandāmīti|

dutiyavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

3.

 yau buddhau dhitimāñ‍ñadhārakau,

saṃsārai anubhausi kāyikaṃ|

dukkhaṃ caitasikañ‍ca laukatau,

taṃ vandai naradaiva maṅgalaṃ||

3. aivaṃ tatiyagāthāya buddhassa vanditvā idāni samādhi ādīhi catūhi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau yau buddhau dhitimāñ‍ñadhārakau tiādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana masajagaṇa garuyuttattā dasakkharaihi lakkhitā suddhavirā jitagāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai-msā jgā suddhavirā jitaṃ matanti| tassatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ msā masagaṇā ca jgā jagaṇagarukā ca cai siyyuṃ, taṃ vuttaṃ suddhavirājitanti mataṃ ñātabbanti| dhitimā añ‍ñadhārakau yau buddhau laukatau saṃsārai kāyikaṃ dukkhañ‍ca caitasikaṃ dukkhañ‍ca anubhausi naradaivamaṅgalaṃ, taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ vandaiti sambandhau|

tattha  hi yauti aniyamaniddaisau| yau yādisauti atthau| tassa ca aniyamavācaka sabbanāmapadattā vacanatthau na kātabbau| tassa pana ya auti padavibhāgau kātabbau| taisu ca yasaddassa vāc‍caṃ aniyamau buddhadabbau appadhānaina labbhati, tassa yādisayuttajac‍canāmagauttasīlavihārī gaucarasaṅkhātaṃ aniyamasāmañ‍ñākārattaguṇamaiva padhānaina labbhati| paṭhamāsivibhattiyā kāriyabhūtassa aukārassa vāc‍caṃ pakatūpanissayasaṅkhātaṃ kattukārakaikavacanasaṅkhyaṃ labbhati| atha vā sabbanāmabhāvaṃ yāti gacchatīti yau, aniyamavācakabhāvaṃ yāti gacchatīti vā yau, sabbanāma bhāvaina yāti pavattatīti vā yau, sabbanāmabhāvaina yātabbau ñātabbauti vā yau, yātabbau ñātabbau aitaina vā aniyamatthauti yau|

taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘nānākāraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca, dhātupac‍cayaviggahaṃ|

cintakānaṃ pabhāvauva, padhānaṃ hauti cintanai’’ti||

buddhauti paṭividdhacatusac‍cadhammau| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau| bujjhati cattāri sac‍cānīti buddhau, bhagavā| suddhakattusādhanauyaṃ| budhadhātu ta| baudhaitā pajāyāti vā buddhau, haitu kattusādhanauyaṃ| añ‍ñaisaṃ cattāri sac‍cāni baudhaitīti atthau| atha vā anañ‍ñabaudhitau hutvā savāsanāya sammauhaniddāya bujjhati jāgarautīti buddhau, dinakarakiraṇa samāgamaina paramarucirasirīsaubhaggappattiyā vikasitamiva padumaṃ aggamaggañāṇasamāgamaina aparimitaguṇagaṇālaṅkata sabbañ‍ñutañāṇappattiyā bujjhati vikasatīti vā buddhau, nibbāna saṅkhātaṃ aikāyanamaggaṃ bujjhati gacchatīti vā buddhau| budhasaddau hi ñāṇādīsu catūsvatthaisu vattati|

taina  vuttaṃ –

‘‘ñāṇai vikasanai caiva, gamanai cāpi jāgarai|

catūsvaitaisu atthaisu, budhasaddau pavattatī’’ti||

atha vā sabbañaiyyadhammai bujjhati jānātīti buddhau, pārami bhāvitāya pañ‍ñāya sabbai saṅkhatāsaṅkhatadhammai bujjhati abujjhi bujjhissatīti vā buddhau, sammāsambuddhau vata sau bhagavāti adhigatañāṇaguṇavisaisaihi bujjhitabbauti vā buddhau, bhagavatā bujjhitabbaṃ ñātabbanti buddhaṃ| kiṃ taṃ, sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇaṃ, tadassatthīti buddhau, bhagavā| atthatau pana pāramitā bhāvitau sayambhūñāṇaina saha vāsanāya vihataviddhasta niravasaisak‍laisau mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañāṇādiapari maiyyaguṇagaṇādhārau khandhasantānau buddhau| yathāha ‘‘buddhauti yau sau bhagavā sayambhū anācariyakau pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu sāmaṃ sac‍cāni abhisambujjhi, tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau, balaisu ca vasībhāva’’nti|

aittha ca buddhau nāma sabbañ‍ñubuddha, pac‍caikabuddha, catusac‍cabuddha, suta buddhavasaina catubbidhau hauti| tattha kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni samatiṃsapāramiyau pūraitvā sambaudhippattau sabbañ‍ñubuddhau nāma| kappasatasahassādhikāni dvai asaṅkhyaiyyāni pāramiyau pūraitvā sayambhūpattau pac‍caikabuddhau nāma| avasaisā khīṇāsavā catusac‍cabuddhau nāma| bahussutau sutabuddhau nāma| idha pana sabbañ‍ñubuddhau adhippaitau|

dhitimāñ‍ñadhārakauti aittha dhitimā añ‍ñadhārakauti padacchaidau kātabbau| dhitimāti samādhivantau, sampayutta dhammai aikārammaṇai dhāraitīti dhiti, samādhi| dhādhātuti| ākārassa ikārādaisau| dhiyati ṭhapaiti aikārammaṇai sampayuttadhammaiti dhiti, cittaina yaugainaivaisau aikā rammaṇai  patiṭṭhāpanavasaina cittassa ārammaṇai nic‍calabhāvaina patiṭṭhāpaitīti atthau| dhiti assa atthīti dhitimā, buddhau| sau hi yathā mahāmairu catūhi disāhi bhusaṃ āgataihi vātaihi na samiñ‍jati, aivaṃ paṭipakkhaihi micchāvāda vātaihi ca iṭṭhāniṭṭhārammaṇaihi ca lābhālābha yasāyasa nindā pasaṃsa sukhadukkhasaṅkhātaihi laukadhammaihi ca na samiñ‍jati, samādhivantauyaiva hautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

añ‍ñadhārakauti arahattaphalahārakau| vimuttisaṅkhātaṃ arahattaphalasaitacchattaṃ dhāraintau vā| jānāti paṭhama maggaina diṭṭhasac‍cassa āmariyādaṃ anatik‍kamitvā vāti añ‍ñaṃ, arahattaphalaṃ| āpubbañādhātu k‍vi| āsaddau mariyādatthau| saṃyaugaparattā ākārassa rassau, taṃ dhāraitīti añ‍ñadhārakau| añ‍ñasaddūpapadadharadhātu ṇvu| saṃsāraiti khandhadhātuāyatanānaṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ pavattamānai saṃsāravaṭṭai|

vuttañhi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ –

‘‘khandhānañ‍ca paṭipāṭi, dhātuāyatanāna ca|

abbaucchin‍naṃ vattamānā, saṃsārauti pavuc‍catī’’ti||

khandhadhātuāyatanānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñasambandhaina saṃpunappunaṃ sarati gacchati pavattatīti saṃsārau| saṃpubbasaradhātu gatimhi ṇa| atha vā khandhāyatanadhātuyau saṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ saranti gacchanti pavattanti aitthāti saṃsārauti vacanatthau kātabbau, tasmiṃ| anubhausīti anupunappunaṃ abhuñ‍ji, avaidayīti atthau| kāyikanti kāyai sarīrai pavattaṃ kāyikaṃ| caitasikanti caitasi pavattaṃ caitasikaṃ| taṃ pana padadvayaṃ dukkhantipadai visaisanaṃ| dukkhanti vaṭṭadukkhaṃ| dukkhanaṃ dukkhaṃ, atha vā dukkhayatīti dukkhaṃ, dukkhadhātu a| idaṃ pana nibbacanaṃ kāritavasaina vuttaṃ| yassa uppaj‍jati, taṃ dukkhitaṃ karautīti vuttaṃ hauti| laukatauti sattalaukahaitu| laukiyanti  pavattanti aittha puñ‍ñāpuñ‍ñāni tabbipākau cāti laukau| laukadhātu ṇa| atha vā kusalādīni lāti gaṇhātīti laukau| lādhātu aukapac‍cayau| satta laukauva labbhati| dhitimā añ‍ñadhārakau yau buddhau saṃsāravaṭṭai nimuggānaṃ sattānamanudayāya saṃsāravaṭṭadukkhatau pamaucaitukāmau kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni atiduk‍karāni samatiṃsapāramiyau pañ‍ca mahā paric‍cāgai ca karauntau sattalaukahaitu kāyikaṃ caitasikaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭadukkhaṃ anubhavasīti vuttaṃ hauti| tanti tādisaṃ buddhaṃ| vandaiti vandāmi|

naradaivamaṅgalanti naradaivānaṃ maṅgalabhūtaṃ| ayaṃ panaittha vacanattau| nirayaṃ naiti pāpuṇātīti narau, manussau| nidhātu pāpunanai arau| ariyamanussau pana bhūtapubbagatika nayaina narau nāmāti| atha vā saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ naitīti narau, uttamaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ naitīti atthau| vimānavatthuaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana jaiṭṭhabhāvaṃ naitīti narau| puttabhātubhūtaupi hi puggalau mātujaiṭṭhabhaginīnaṃ pituṭṭhānai tiṭṭhati, pagaiva bhattubhūtau itarāsanti vuttaṃ| visuddhimaggamahāṭīkāyaṃ pana naratīti narau, purisau| yathā hi paṭhamapakatibhūtau sattau itarāya pakatiyā saiṭṭhaṭṭhaina puriuc‍cai ṭhānai saiti pavattatīti purisauti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ nayanaṭṭhaina narauti vuc‍cati| puttabhātu bhūtaupi hi puggalau mātujaiṭṭhabhaginīnaṃ pituṭhānai tiṭṭhati| pagaiva itarā itarāsanti vuttaṃ| tasmā idha pana naranti sāmañ‍ñaina vuttampi visaisaina purisanaranti viñ‍ñāyati padhānanayaina gahaṇassa yuttattā| dibbanti kāmaguṇa jhānābhiñ‍ñācittissariyādīhi kīḷantīti daivā| divudhātu ṇa| atha vā sarīrālaṅkārajutiyā dibbantīti daivā| daivā ca nāma tividhā haunti sammutidaivā, upapattidaivā, visuddhidaivā cāti| tattha mahāsammatakālatau paṭṭhāya laukaina  daivāti sammatattā rājānau daiviyau rājakumārā ca sammutidaivā nāma| daivalaukai upapan‍nā upapattidaivā nāma| buddhapac‍caikabuddhakhīṇāsavā visuddhidaivā nāma| idha pana upapattidaivā adhippaitā|

maṅgalanti maṅgalabhūtaṃ| tantipadai visaisanaṃ| maṅganti vaḍḍhainti sabbasampattīhi sattānaṃ aitaināti maṅgalau, buddhau| maṅgala dhātu vaḍḍhanai alau| atha vā maṅgaṃ vuc‍cati pāpaṃ, taṃ vainaiyyānaṃ daisanāya lunāti chindatīti maṅgalau, buddhau| maṅgasaddūpapadaludhātu k‍vi| narā ca daivā ca naradaivā, taisaṃ maṅgalau naradaivamaṅgalau, buddhau| sau pana manussadaivabrahmānaṃ pūjanīyattā ca saṅkhai, cak‍kaṃ, puṇṇakumbhau, garā, sirī, saitacchattaṃ, aṅkusau, dhajaṃ, sauvattikañ‍cāti aṭṭhahi laukiyamaṅgalaihi saiṭṭhattā uttamattā ca maṅgalau nāmāti vuc‍cati, tasmā buddhassa naradaivamaṅgalanti thaumaitīti adhippāyauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| dhitimā samādhivantau añ‍ñadhārakau arahattaphaladhārakau arahattaphalasai tacchattaṃ dhāraintau vā, yau yādisau buddhau, saṃsārai saṃsāravaṭṭai, kāyikaṃ kāyai pavattaṃ dukkhañ‍ca vaṭṭadukkhañ‍ca caitasi pavattaṃ dukkhañ‍ca vaṭṭadukkhañ‍ca, laukatau laukahaitu, anubhausi punappunaṃ abhavasi avaidayi, naradaivamaṅgalaṃ naradaivānaṃ maṅgalabhūtaṃ, taṃ tādisaṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ vandai vandāmīti|

tatiyavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

4.

 bāttiṃsatilakkhaṇacitradaihaṃ,

daihaj‍jutiniggatapaj‍jalantaṃ|

pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇaughavindaṃ,

vandai munimantimajātiyuttaṃ||



4. aivaṃ  tatiyagāthāya buddhassa natvā idāni bāttiṃsatilakkhaṇacitradaihantyādīhi chahi guṇaihi thaumitvā muninau vanditukāmau bāttiṃsati lakkhaṇa citra daiha ntyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ta ja ja gaṇa garudvaya yuttattā aikādasakkharaihi lakkhitā upaṭṭhitā gāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai tjā jgā garunāyamupaṭṭhitā sā’’ti| tassatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ tjā tajagaṇā ca jgājagaṇagarū ca garunā pādantagarunā cai yuttā, sā ayaṃ gāthā upaṭṭhitā nāmāti| aittha ca aika garukai satipi dvigarukaipi lakkhaṇasamānattā upaṭṭhitā gāthā nāmāti daṭṭhabbā| tattha bāttiṃsati lakkhaṇa citra daihanti dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi vicitraṃ sarīravantaṃ| aittha ca dvīhi adhikaṃ tiṃsaṃ bāttiṃsati| dvisaddassa bādaisau| lakkhiyati lakkhitabbaṃ buddhasarīraṃ aitaināti lakkhaṇaṃ| dihati upacayati vaḍḍhati vā aittha kusalā kusalanti daihaṃ, sarīraṃ| dihadhātu upacayai vaḍḍhanai vā ṇa| bāttiṃsati mahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi citraṃ vicitraṃ daihaṃ sarīraṃ yassa sauti bāttiṃsatilakkhaṇacitradaihau, muni labbhati|

idaṃ panaittha dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇasarūpassa saṅkhaipa dassanaṃ| suppatiṭṭhitapādau ca, āyatapaṇhi, dīghaṅguli, muduta luṇahatthapādau, jālahatthapādau, ussaṅkhapādau, aiṇijaṅghau, ṭhitakauva anaunamantau ubhauhi pāṇitalaihi jāṇu kāni parimasati parimaj‍jati, kausauhitavatthaguyhau, suvaṇṇa vaṇṇau kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau, sukhumacchavī, aikaikalaumau, uddhaggalaumau, brahmujugattau, sattussadau, sīhapubbaddha kāyau, citantaraṃsau, nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau, samavaṭṭakkhandhau, rasaggarasī, sīhahanu, cattālīsadantau, samadantau, aviraḷadantau, susuk‍kadāṭhau, pahūtajivhau, brahmassarau karavīkabhāṇi, atinīlanaittau, gaupakhumau, uṇṇā bhamu kantarai  jātau, uṇhīsasīsau cāti dvattiṃsavidhāni haunti mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni|

taina vuttaṃ pāthaiyyavaggai lakkhaṇasuttai ‘‘katamāni tāni bhikkhavai dvattiṃsamahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvai gatiyau bhavanti, anañ‍ñā| sacai agāraṃ ajjhāvasati rājā hauti cak‍kavatti…pai॰… sacai khaupana agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati, arahaṃ hauti sammā sambuddhau laukai vivaṭacchadau 1. idha bhikkhavai mahā purisau suppatiṭṭhitapādau hauti| yampi bhikkhavai mahā purisau suppatiṭṭhitapādau hauti| idampi bhikkhavai mahā purisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati 2. puna caparaṃ bhikkhavai mahāpurisassa haiṭṭhāpādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātāni haunti sahassārāni sanaimikāni sanābhikāni sabbā kāraparipūrāni| idampi bhikkhavai mahāpurisassa mahāpurisa lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati 3. puna caparaṃ bhikkhavai mahāpurisau āyatapaṇhī hauti 4. dīghaṅgulī hauti 5. mudutaluṇa hatthapādau hauti 6. jālahatthapādau hauti 7. ussaṅkhapādau hauti 8. aiṇijaṅghau hauti 9. ṭhitakauva anaunamantau ubhauhi pāṇitalaihi jāṇukāni parimasati parimaj‍jati 10. kausauhitavatthaguyhau hauti 11. suvaṇṇavaṇṇau kañ‍canasan‍nibhattacau 12. sukhumacchavī hauti sukhumattā chaviyā rajaujal‍laṃ kāyai na upalimpati 13. aikaikalaumau hauti, aikaikāni laumāni laumakūpaisu jātāni 14. uddhaggalaumau hauti| uddhaggāni laumāni jātāni nīlāni añ‍janavaṇṇāni kuṇḍalā vattāni padakkhiṇāvattakajātāni 15. brahmujugattau hauti 16. sattussadau hauti 17. sīhapubbaddha kāyau hauti 18. citantaraṃsau hauti 19. nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau hauti, yāvatak‍vassa byāmau, tāva tak‍vassa  kāyau 20. samavaṭṭakkhandhau hauti 21. rasagga saggī hauti 22. sīhahanu hauti 23. cattālīsadantau hauti 24. samadantau hauti 25. aviraḷadantau hauti 26. susuk‍kadāṭhau hauti 27. pahūtajivhau hauti 28. brahmassarau hauti karavīkabhāṇi 29. atinīlanaittau hauti 30. gaupakhumau hauti 31. uṇṇā bhamukantarai jātā hauti audātā mudutūla san‍nibhā| yampi bhikkhavai mahāpurisassa uṇṇā bhamukantarai jātā hauti audātā mudutūlasan‍nibhā| idampi bhikkhavai mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati 32. puna caparaṃ bhikkhavai mahāpurisau uṇhīsasīsau hauti| yampi bhikkhavai mahāpurisau uṇhīsasīsau hauti| idampi bhikkhavai mahāpurisassa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ bhavati| imāni khau tāni bhikkhavai dvattiṃsamahāpurisassa mahāpurisa lakkhaṇānī’’ti| aitaisaṃ pana dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇānaṃ atthau pacchā āvibhavissatīti|

daihaj‍jutiniggatapaj‍jalantanti bhagavatau rūpakāyatau nikkhantaihi chabbaṇṇaraṃsīsaṅkhātaihi aubhāsaihi parisamantatau vij‍jautavantaṃ| javati dibbatīti juti| judhātuti| nigacchati niccharatīti niggatā, rasmi| nipubbagamudhātu ta| pakāraina jalatai dibbatai paj‍jalantaṃ| aittha ca daihaniggatajutīti vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ padavipariyāyaina daihaj‍jutiniggatanti vuttaṃ| tasmā daihatau niggatā jutīti samāsau daṭṭhabbau| daihatau rūpakāyatau niggataihi niccharaihi jutīhi raṃsīhi paj‍jalantaṃ yassa sauti daihaj‍juti niggatapaj‍jalantau, muni labbhati| muninau hi puratthimakāyatau chabbaṇṇarasmiyau uṭṭhahitvā asītihatthaṭṭhānaṃ agga haisi| pacchimakāyatau dakkhiṇahatthatau vāmahatthatau chabbaṇṇā rasmiyau uṭṭhahitvā asītihatthaṭṭhānaṃ aggahaisi| upari kaisantatau paṭṭhāya sabbakaisavattaihi mauragīvavaṇṇā rasmi  uṭṭhahitvā gaganatalai asītihatthaṭṭhānaṃ aggahaisi| haiṭṭhāpādatalaihi pavāḷavaṇṇā rasmi uṭṭhahitvā ghanapathaviyaṃ asītihatthaṭṭhānaṃ aggahaisi, aivaṃ samantā asītihatthamattaṃ ṭhānaṃ chabbaṇṇā buddharasmiyau vij‍jautamānā vipphandamānā kañ‍canadaṇḍadīpikāhi niccharitvā ākāsaṃ pakkhandajālā viya catuddīpika mahāmaighatau nikkhantavij‍julatā viya vidhāviṃsūti vuttaṃ hauti| tasmā daihaj‍jutiniggatapaj‍jalantanti thaumaitīti|

pañ‍ñādhiti sīla guṇaughavindanti aittha pañ‍ñāti sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇaṃ| dhitīti jhānamaggaphalasampayuttasamādhi| sīlanti pac‍caika buddhāriyasāvakaihi asādhāraṇaṃ suvisuddhaṃ catupārisuddhisīlaṃ| aughanti samūhaṃ| vindanti laddhaṃ| pakāraina yathāsabhāvaṃ sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ vā jānāti paṭivijjhatīti pañ‍ñā| sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇaṃ| papubbañādhātu a| sā panaisā pajānanalakkhaṇā paṭivaidhalakkhaṇā aubhāsanalakkhaṇā ca, visayaubhāsa rasā, asammauhapac‍cupaṭṭhānā, samādhipadaṭṭhānā| dhitisaddassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| sīlatīti sīlaṃ| kāyavacīkammāni sammā dahati sammā ṭhapaitīti atthau| atha vā sīlati samādhiyati kāyakammādīnaṃ susilya bhāvaina na vippakiratīti sīlaṃ, sīlanti samādahanti cittaṃ aitaināti vā sīlaṃ, catupārisuddhisīlaṃ| sīladhātu a| idaṃ pana patiṭṭhāpanalakkhaṇaṃ, dussilyaviddhaṃsanarasaṃ anavaj‍jarasaṃ vā, saucaiyyapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ, hiriauttappapadaṭṭhānaṃ| guṇasaddassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| uhanati samūhaṃ karauti aittha avayavanti aughau, samūhau| upubbahanadhātu ṇa| ussa au| hanassa ghau| abhidhānappadīpikāṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘avayavaṃ byapiyati gacchatīti aughau’’ti vuttaṃ| vindanaṃlabhanaṃ vindaṃ, vindiyittha labbhitthāti vā vindaṃ, pañ‍ñā ca dhiti ca sīlañ‍ca taṃ guṇañ‍cāti pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇā, taisaṃ aughau samūhauti tathā, taṃ vindaṃ labhanaṃ yassāti pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇaughavindau, muni| atha vā vindaṃ labhitabbaṃ pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇaughaṃ yaināti pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇaughavindau| tatiyābahubbīhi samāsauyaṃ| vindānaṃ labhitabbānaṃ pañ‍ñādhitisīla saṅkhātānaṃ guṇānaṃ samūhau atthīti atthau| taṃ|

vandaiti ahaṃ vandāmi| muninti buddhaṃ| munāti jānāti paricchindati vā hitāhitanti muni, buddhau| munadhātu ñāṇai i| atha vā munāti jānāti ubhau atthaiti muni, khandhādi laukai tulaṃ āraupaitvā minantau viya imai ajjhattikā khandhā imai bāhirātiādinā nayaina idhalaukatthañ‍ca paralaukatthañ‍ca bhagavā jānātīti atthau| sau panaisa agāriyamuni, anāgāriyamuni, saikhamuni, asaikhamuni, pac‍caikamuni, munimuni cāti anaikavidhau| tattha agāriyamunīti gihipi āgataphalau viñ‍ñātasāsanau| anāgāriyamunīti tathārūpauva pabbajitau| saikhamunīti satta saikkhā| asaikhamunīti khīṇāsavau| pac‍caikamunīti pac‍caikabuddhau| munimunīti sammāsambuddhau| taisu pana idha munimuni adhippaitau, taṃ muniṃ| antimajātiyuttanti antimaina carimabhavaina yuttaṃ| avasānaṃ amati gacchatīti antaṃ| amadhātu ta| massa nau| antai bhavau antimau, carimabhavau| jānanaṃ nibbattanaṃ jāti, khandhānaṃ pavatti| kammaina janiyati nibbattiyatīti vā jāti| upapattibhavau labbhati| jānidhātuti| dhātvanta laupau| antimajātiyā yuttauti antimajātiyuttau, muni| āyatiñhi tīsu bhavaisu paṭisandhijanakānaṃ sabba kilaisānaṃ baudhipal‍laṅkaiyaiva aggamaggaina samucchinditattā bhagavatau anāgatai punabbhavau natthi, tasmā ayaṃ ariyajāti pacchimattabhāvayuttauti vuttaṃ hauti|

taina vuttaṃ dhammacak‍kapavattanasuttai –

‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca maidassanaṃ udapādi, akuppāmai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthidāni punabbhavau’’ti|

ayaṃ  panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| bāttiṃsatilakkhaṇacitraṃ daihaṃ dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi vicitraṃ rūpakāyavantaṃ, daihaj‍jutiniggata paj‍jalantaṃ rūpakāyatau niggataihi chabbaṇṇaraṃ sīhi parisamantatau vij‍jautavantaṃ, pañ‍ñādhitisīlaguṇau ghavindaṃ pañ‍ñādhitisīlasaṅkhatānaṃ guṇānaṃ samūhaṃ labhanavantaṃ, vinditabbānaṃ pañ‍ñādhitisīlasaṅkhātānaṃ guṇānaṃ samūhavantaṃ vā, antimajātiyuttaṃ carimabhavaina carimattabhāvaina vā yuttaṃ muniṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ tīhi dvāraihi sak‍kac‍caṃ vandai vandāmīti|

catutthavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

5.

 pātaudayaṃ bāladivākaraṃva,

majjhai yatīnaṃ lalitaṃ sirīhi|

puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhaṃ anaijaṃ,

vandāmi sabbañ‍ñumahaṃ munindaṃ||

5. aivaṃ catutthagāthāya buddhaṃ vanditvā idāni sirīhi lalitantyādīhi catūhi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau pātaudayantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ta ta ja gaṇadvigarūhi yuttattā aikādasakkharaihi lakkhitā indavajirā gāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘indādikā tā vajirā jagāgau’’ti| tassatthau, yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ tā dvaita gaṇā ca jagā jagaṇa garu ca gau garu ca cai bhavanti, sā indā dikā indasaddaādikā vajirā vajirāgāthā nāmāti|

tattha pātaudayanti aittha pātau udayanti padacchaidau kātabbau| pagai pubbaṇhasamayai pācīnalaukadhātutau uggatanti atthau| pātau pagai pācīnalaukadhātutau udaiti uggacchatīti pātaudayau, sūriyau| pātaupubba udadhātu uggamanai ayau, taṃ pātaudayaṃ| bāladivā karaṃ  vāti bāladivākaraṃ ivātipadacchaidau| bāladivākaraṃ vāti niggatābhinavasūriyaṃ viya| aittha ca bālauti sampati jātau taruṇadārakau pautakau vā| sau hi balati assasitapassasitamattaina jīvati na saiṭṭhaina pañ‍ñājīvitaināti bālau, taruṇadārakau| baladhātu pāṇanai jīvāyaṃ vā| sūriyaraṃsinā divati dibbatīti divā| divudhātu a| atha vā dīyati khīyati andhakārau aitaināti divā| dīdhātu khayai a, vāgamau| taṃ karautīti divākarau, divā divasai vā karau ābhā yassāti divākarau, sūriyau| bālasadisau divākarau bāladivākarau| atha vā bālau iva divākarauti bāladivākarau, navuggatasūriyau| sau pana ākāsai nakkhattatārānaṃ majjhai sahassaraṃsīhi lalati vilāsati viyāti atthau|

yatīnanti bhikkhūnaṃ| tai hi yatanti uṭṭhahanti ghaṭanti vāyamanti kusalai dhammaiti yatīti vuc‍canti| yata dhātu patiyatanai vāyāmai vā i| atha vā yamanti uparamanti akusalai dhammaiti yatīti vuc‍canti| yamudhātu uparamai i| dhātvantalaupau| taisaṃ yatīnaṃ| lalitanti vilāsitaṃ| lalati vilāsaitīti lalitau, muni| laladhātu vilāsai ta| taṃ| sirīhīti bhagavatau kāyaṅgasaubhaggapattaihi sirīhi, bāttiṃsalakkhaṇāsītyā nubyañ‍janachabbaṇṇaraṃsīsaṅkhataihi sampattisirīhi vā| katapuñ‍ñai nissayatīti sirī| sidhātu saivāyaṃ rapac‍cayau itthi liṅgajautakaīpac‍cayau ca| katapuñ‍ñaihi saiviyatīti vā sirī, sampatti| mūlaṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘sirīti pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñāna maitaṃ adhivacana’’nti vuttaṃ| yatīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ majjhai sirīhi lalitaṃ vilāsitanti sambandhau| aittha ca idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti, yathā pātau uditau uggatau sūriyau ākāsai nakkhattatārānaṃ  majjhai sahassaraṃsīhi lalati vilāsati, aivaṃ muni laukai yatīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ majjhai bāttiṃsalakkhaṇā sītyānubyañ‍janachabbaṇṇaraṃsīhi alaṅkataihi sarīrasaubhagga pattīhi sampattisirīhi lalati vilāsatīti|

puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhanti puṇṇacandaina sadisaṃ ānanasampan‍naṃ| yathā hi candau kāḷapakkhai pāṭipadadivasatau paṭṭhāya dinai dinai ūnau hauti, aivaṃ na muninau mukhaṃ ānanaṃ ūnaṃ| bhagavatau pana mukhaṃ nic‍caṃ paripuṇṇacandamaṇḍalaṃ viya anūnaṃ hutvā saubhati, tasmā puṇṇasaddaina visaisaitvā puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhanti thaumaiti| sauḷasakalaihi pūraitīti puṇṇaṃ| pūradhātu ta, tassa ṇṇau| idati nakkhattatārānaṃ issariyaṃ karautīti indu, ididhātu issariyaṃ u| puṇṇaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ indu puṇṇindu, candau| saṅkāsanti sadisaṃ| saṅkāsasaddau hi upamāvācakau nipātau| vuttañhi

subaudhālaṅkārai –

‘‘ivādi iva vā tulya, samānanibhasan‍nibhā|

yathā saṅkāsa tulita, ppakāsa patirūpakā’’tyādi||

puṇṇacandaṃ viya mukhati saubhatīti mukhaṃ, ānanaṃ| mukhadhātu a| puṇṇindunā saṅkāsaṃ sadisaṃ mukhaṃ ānanaṃ yassa sauti puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhau, munindau| taṃ| munindassa pana puṇṇa candassa sahassaraṃsīhi sassirikau viya bhikkhūnaṃ majjhai bāttiṃsa mahāpurisa lakkhaṇāsītyānubyañ‍jana paṭimaṇḍitaihi sirīhi ca byāmappabhāparikkhittaihi āvaiḷāvaiḷā yamakāyamakā gumbāgumbā hutvā viniccharantīhi ghanabuddharasmisaṅkhātaihi alaṅkitau sassirikamukhau hautīti vuttaṃ hauti| tasmā puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhanti thaumaiti|

anaijanti nittaṇhaṃ| lābhālābhādiṃ paṭic‍ca aijati kampatīti aijā, taṇhā| aijadhātu kampanai a, natthi aijā yassāti  anaijau, munindau| vuttañhi mahāniddaisapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘lābhālābha yasāyasa nindāninda sukhadukkhaihi na iñ‍jatīti anaijau, bhagavā labbhatī’’ti|

sabbañ‍ñunti paṭividdha sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ| saṅkharavikāralakkhaṇanibbāna pañ‍ñattivasaina pañ‍cavidhaṃ sabbaṃ ñaiyyadhammaṃ jānāti paṭivijjhati sīlaināti sabbañ‍ñū, munindau| sabbasaddūpapada ñādhātu rū| aittha ca sabbai saṅkhatadhammā saṅkharā nāma sakasakapac‍cayaihi saṅkhariyantīti katvā| viñ‍ñattidvayaṃ lahutādittayañ‍ca vikārau nāma catūhi mahābhūtaihi virūpaṃ kariyatīti katvā| viñ‍ñattidvayassa yathāk‍kamaṃ cittajavāyaudhātu cittajapathavī dhātūnaṃ visaisaguṇattā, lahutādittayassa ca cittau tvāhārajanipphan‍narūpānaṃ visaisaguṇattā| phassādidhammānaṃ phusanādilakkhaṇañ‍ca pathavīdhātvādīnaṃ kakkhaḷādilakkhaṇañ‍ca, cittassa ārammaṇavijānanalakkhaṇañ‍ca, nibbānassa santilakkhaṇañ‍ca, rūpassa ruppanalakkhaṇañ‍ca, nāmassa namanalakkhaṇañ‍ca, anic‍catādittayāni ca upacayādicattāri lakkhaṇarūpāni ca lakkhaṇāni nāma lakkhiyanti aitaina lakkhitabbā dhammāti katvā| asaṅkhatadhātunibbānaṃ nāma vānaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā, tatau nikkhantanti katvā| paricchaidarūpaṃ itthipurisādighaṭapaṭādayau sabba vauhāratthā ca pañ‍ñatti nāma pañ‍ñāpiyati pakāraina ñāpiyatīti katvā| aittha ca saṅkhāranibbānāni paramatthadhammā haunti| vikārādīsu pana vikāralakkhaṇāni pañ‍ñattivisaisattā visuṃ vuttāni| imaihi dvīhi vinimuttā pana paricchaidarūpā sabbavauhāratthā pañ‍ñattīti vuttāti vaiditabbā| apadāna aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘sabbaṃ atītādibhaidaṃ hatthāmalakaṃ viya jānātīti sabbañ‍ñū’’ti vuttaṃ|

sabbañ‍ñūti ca kamasabbañ‍ñū, sakiṃ sabbañ‍ñū, satatasabbañ‍ñū, satti sabbañ‍ñū, ñātasabbañ‍ñū cāti pañ‍cavidhā hauti sabbañ‍ñū| tattha yathāk‍kamaṃ sabbaṃ jānanadhammau kamasabbañ‍ñū nāma| aikāvaj‍janaina javanavāraina  jānitabbaṃ catusac‍cadhammaṃ jānanadhammau sakiṃsabbañ‍ñū nāma| cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ niyatārammaṇattaṃ jānanadhammau satatasabbañ‍ñū nāma| aikappahāraina sabbadhammaṃ jānanasamatthau dhammau sattisabbañ‍ñū nāma| āvaj‍janābhaidaina jānitabbaṃ sabbadhammaṃ jānanadhammau ñātasabbañ‍ñū nāma| taisu ca sakiṃ sabbañ‍ñū sattisabbañ‍ñūvasaina dvai dhammā maggañāṇāniyaiva| tadavasaisā sabbañ‍ñutañāṇāni haunti| taṃ sabbañ‍ñuṃ|

munindanti aittha munisaddassa atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| munīnaṃ indau issarau munindau, taṃ munindaṃ ahaṃ vandāmīti sambandhauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| pātaudayaṃ pagaiva udayaṃ uggataṃ, bāladivākaraṃva navuggatasūriyamāṇavauiva, yatīnaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ majjhai sirīhi bāttiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇā sītyānubyañ‍janasampattisaṅkhātaihi sirīhi lalitaṃ vilāsitaṃ, puṇṇindusaṅkāsamukhaṃ puṇṇacandaina sadisaṃ ānanavantaṃ, anaijaṃ nittaṇhaṃ, laukadhammaihi aniñ‍janaṃ vā, sabbañ‍ñuṃ sabbadhamma vijānantaṃ, munindaṃ buddhaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ tīhi dvāraihi ahaṃ vandāmīti|

pañ‍camavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

6.

 upaitapuñ‍ñau varabaudhimūlai,

sasainamāraṃ sugatau jinitvā|

abaujjhi baudhiṃ aruṇaudayamhi,

namāmi taṃ mārajinaṃ abhaṅgaṃ||

6. aivaṃ pañ‍camagāthāya munindaṃ vanditvā idāni mārajinaguṇaina thaumitvā vanditukāmau upaitapuñ‍ñau tyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ja ta ja gaṇa dvigaruyuttattā aikādasakkharaihi lakkhitā upaindavajirāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘upādikā  sāva jatā jagā gau’’ti| tassatthau, yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ jatā jagaṇa tagaṇā ca jagā jagaṇagarū ca gau garu ca cai siyā, sāva sāgāthāaiva upādikā upasaddādikā indavajirāgāthā nāmāti|

upaitapuñ‍ñau yau sugatau varabaudhimūlai sasaina māraṃ jinitvā aruṇaudayamhi baudhiṃ abaujjhi mārajinaṃ abhaṅgaṃ, taṃ sugataṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau| tattha upaitapuñ‍ñauti sampan‍nadānasīlādipāramīpuñ‍ñavā| dānasīlādi pāramīpuñ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau vā| dānasīlādipāramīpuñ‍ñaina upaitau samupaitau upagatau samupagatau sampan‍nau saman‍nāgatauti atthau| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau, upan‍nau sampan‍nau hutvā aiti gacchati pavattatīti upaitaṃ, puñ‍ñaṃ| upapubbaidhātu gatiyaṃ ta| punāti attanau kārakanti puñ‍ñaṃ, pāramīpuñ‍ñaṃ labbhati| pudhātu pīṇanai saudhanai vā ṇyau| tassa ñau| tattha attanau kārakanti punappunaṃ pavattiyā sacittasantatiñ‍ca saṃjanitapaṇītarūpavisaṭaina sanissaya rūpasantatiñ‍ca punāti pīṇaiti saudhaiti vāti atthau| ayaṃ paṭisambhidāmaggagaṇṭhipadai vuttau| pūraiti ajjhāsayanti vā puñ‍ñaṃ| pūradhātu ṇya| yau puggalau puñ‍ñaṃ karauti tassa ajjhāsayaṃ pūraitīti atthau|

atha vā puj‍jañ‍ca bhavaṃ nibbattaitīti puñ‍ñaṃ| nibbattitaddhitāyaṃ| pūja padaṃ ṇyapac‍cayau| yau puñ‍ñaṃ karauti, tassa pūjitabbaṃ bhavaṃ pavattaitīti atthau| taina vuttaṃ sammauhavinaudanī aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘punāti attanau kārakaṃ pūraiti cassa ajjhāsayaṃ puj‍jañ‍ca bhavaṃ nibbattaitīti puñ‍ñau’’ti| mūlaṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘punātīti saudhaiti apuñ‍ñaphalatau dukkhasaṃkilaisatau ca hitajjhāsayaina puñ‍ñaṃ karautīti taṃ nipphādanaina kārakassajjhāsayaṃ pūraitīti puñ‍ñau| pūrakau puj‍janibbattakau ca niruttilakkhaṇaina puñ‍ñauti vaiditabbau’’ti vuttaṃ| visuddhimaggamahāṭīkāyañ‍ca- ‘‘punāti attanau santānaṃ apuñ‍ñaphalatau dukkhasaṃkilaisatau ca visaudhaitīti puñ‍ñaṃ| hitasukhajjhāsayaina puñ‍ñaṃ karautīti taṃnipphādanaina kārakassa ajjhāsayaṃ pūraiti, puj‍jabhavaṃ nibbattaitīti vā puñ‍ñaṃ| iti pūrakau puj‍janibbattakau ca niruttinayaina puñ‍ñanti vaiditabbau’’ti vuttaṃ| upaitaṃ sampan‍naṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pāramīpuñ‍ñaṃ yassa sauti upaitapuñ‍ñau, sugatau labbhati|

varabaudhimūlaiti uttamassa baudhirukkhassa samīpai| tattha varauti varitabbau patthitabbau icchitabbauti vā varau| baudhimūlaiti aittha baudhīti vuc‍cati rukkhaupi, maggaupi, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇampi, nibbānampi| tattha hi baudhirukkhamūlai paṭhamābhi sambuddhauti ca, antarā ca baudhiṃ antarā ca gayanti ca āgataṭṭhānai rukkhau| baudhīti vuc‍cati catūsu maggaisu ñāṇanti āgataṭṭhānai maggau| pappauti baudhiṃ varabhūri maidhasauti āgataṭṭhānai sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| patvāna baudhiṃ amataṃ asaṅkhatanti āgataṭṭhānai nibbānaṃ baudhīti vuc‍cati| idha pana baudhirukkhauti adhippaitau| ayaṃ pana taisaṃ vacanatthau| bujjhati abujjhi bujjhissati cattāri ariyasac‍cāni sabbaṃ ñaiyyadhammampi aitthāti baudhi, baudhirukkhau| budhadhātu ṇi| bujjhati cattāri sac‍cānīti baudhi, maggau| bujjhati sabbañaiyyadhammanti baudhi, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| ariyaina bujjhitabbanti baudhi, nibbānaṃ labbhati| varau uttamau baudhirukkhauti varabaudhi, tassa mūlaṃ samīpaṃ varabaudhimūlaṃ, tasmiṃ|

sasainamāranti saha saināya pavattaṃ mārarājaṃ| tattha siyati bandhiyatīti sainā| sidhātu bandhanaiyu| tāya saha pavattatīti sasainau, mārarājā| anatthai niyaujaintau guṇamāraṇaina sattai māraitīti mārau| maradhātu ṇa| sattānaṃ kusalaṃ māraiti vināsaitīti vā mārau| sasainau ca sau mārau cāti sasainamārau, mārarājā| taṃ| jinitvāti padai kammaṃ| upaitapuñ‍ñau yau sugatauti sambandhau| sugatasaddassa atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva|

jinitvāti visākhapuṇṇamiyaṃ buddhadinai anatthaṅgataiyaiva sūriyai pāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina vijayitvāti atthau| abaujjhīti paṭivijjhi adhigami vā| baudhinti anuttaraṃ sammā sambaudhiṃ, arahattamaggasabbañ‍ñutañāṇanti atthau| sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇapadaṭṭhānañhi maggañāṇaṃ, maggañāṇapadaṭṭhānañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ baudhīti vuc‍cati| taṃ baudhiṃ|

aruṇaudayamhīti aruṇaudayamhīti padacchaidau| aruṇuggamanavailāyanti atthau| tattha āsamantatau ālaukaṃ karauntau unati gacchatīti aruṇau| āpubbaunadhātu gatimhi ṇa| ākārassa rassau| rakārāgamau| nassa ṇau| sūriyassa udayatau pubbai uggataraṃsi aruṇau nāmāti atthau| vuttañhi dasakanipāta aṅguttarapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘sūriyassa bhikkhavai udayatau aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ yadidaṃ aruṇuggaṃ, aivamaiva khau bhikkhavai kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ aitaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ aitaṃ pubbanimittaṃ yadidaṃ sammādiṭṭhī’’ti| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ abhidhānappadīpikāyaṃ ‘‘sūrassaudayatau pubbu, ṭṭhitaraṃsi siyāruṇau’’ti| abhidhānappadīpikāṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘aruṇavaṇṇatāya arati gacchatīti aruṇau’’ti vuttaṃ| udaiti uggacchatīti udayau, sūriyau| udidhātu a| udayassa sūriyassa aruṇau uggataraṃsīti aruṇaudayau| tassaṃ aruṇuggamanavailāyaṃ| baudhiṃ abaujjhīti sambandhau|

bhagavā hi visākhapuṇṇamiyaṃ anatthaṅgataiyaiva sūriyai mārabalaṃ vidhamaitvā cīvarūpari patamānaihi baudhirukkhaṅkaraihi rattapavāḷapal‍lavaihi viya pūjayamānau rattiyā paṭhamayāmai pubbainivāsaṃ anussaritvā majjhimayāmai dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhaitvā pacchimayāmai paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ manasikaritvā ānāpānacatutthajjhānaṃ nibbattaitvā tadaiva pādakaṃ katvā mahāvajirañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ  vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhaitvā maggapaṭipāṭiyā adhigataina catutthamaggañāṇaina sabbakilaisai khaipaitvā yadā aruṇau uggamissati, tadā sabbañ‍ñutañāṇādisabba buddhaguṇai paṭivijjhi adhigamīti vuttaṃ hauti|

mārajinanti pañ‍camārānaṃ vijitāviṃ| daivaputta kilaisābhi saṅkhārakhandhamac‍cusaṅkhātai pañ‍camārai jināti ajini jinissatīti mārajinau, sugatau| daivaputtakilaisābhisaṅkharasaṅkhataṃ mārattayaṃ jināti ajini, khandhamac‍cusaṅkhataṃ māradvayaṃ avassaṃ jinissatīti atthau| yadi aivaṃ kasmā vijitāvinti vuttanti| aviraddhaṃ aikantaina jiyamānattāti| abhaṅganti sasainamārassa bhañ‍jituṃ viddhaṃsituṃ asamatthaṃ| yassa kassaci bhañ‍jituṃ viddhaṃsituṃ asamatthaṃ vā| tatrāyaṃ vacanatthau| māraina yaina kainaci bhañ‍jituṃ na sak‍kautīti abhaṅgau, taina vā na bhañ‍jiyati na viddhaṃsiyatīti abhaṅgau, sugatau| napubba bhanjadhātu ta| tassa dhātvantaina gakārādaisau| atha vā bhanjati viddhaṃsatīti bhaṅgau, mārau| yau kauci vā| natthi bhaṅgau mārau yau kauci vā yassāti abhaṅgau, sugatau| taṃ abhaṅgaṃ| tanti sugataṃ| mārajinaṃ abhaṅgaṃ taṃ sugataṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau|

imissaṃ pana gāthāyaṃ aikanipātajātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ, apadānaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca āgatanayaina buddhassa māravijayādiṃ sautujanānaṃ bahussutatthaṃ saṅkhaipaina vaiditabbaṃ| baudhi sattau hi mahāsampattiṃ anubhavitvā hatthagataṃ cak‍kavattiraj‍jaṃ khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ viya anapaikkhau chaṭṭaitvā aikūnatiṃsavassai āsaḷhipuṇṇamāya uttarasaḷhanakkhattaina yuttai mahābhinikkhamaṃ nikkhamitvā anaumanadītīrai ghaṭīkārabrahmunā din‍naṃ arahatta dhajabhūtaṃ aṭṭhaparikkhāraṃ paṭiggahaitvā nivāsaitvā samaṇapabbaj‍jaṃ katvā chabbassāni duk‍karakārikaṃ caritvā nigraudharukkhamūlai sainanigamai sainakuṭumbikassa dhītarāya sujātāya saha satasahassagghiyā  suvaṇṇapātiyā din‍naṃ sabbaṃ appaudakaṃ madhupāyāsaṃ aikaṭṭhitālapak‍kappamāṇaṃ aikūnapañ‍ñāsālaupaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā sāyanhasamayai daivatāhi alaṅkataina maggaina baudhirukkhābhimukhau gacchantai antaramaggai sautthiyaina nāma tiṇahārakaina din‍naṃ aṭṭhamuṭṭhikatiṇaṃ gahaitvā baudhimaṇḍaṃ āruyha idaṃ ṭhānaṃ sabbabuddhānaṃ avijahitaṃ acalaṭṭhānaṃ kilaisabhañ‍janaṭṭhānanti ñatvā tāni tiṇāni aggai gahaitvā cālaisi| tāvadaiva cuddasahatthau pal‍laṅkau ahausi| tānipi tiṇāni tathārūpaina saṇṭhānaina santhariṃsu saṇṭhahiṃsu vā| yathārūpaṃ sukusalaupi vicittakārau vā pautthatārau vā ālikhitumpi samatthau natthi| baudhi sattau baudhikhandhaṃ piṭṭhitau katvā puratthābhimukhau daḷhamānasau hutvā –

‘‘kāmaṃ tacau ca nhāru ca, aṭṭhi ca avasussatu|

upasussatu nisaisaṃ, sarīrai maṃsalauhita’’nti||

na tvaiva sammāsambaudhiṃ apatvā imaṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ bhindissāmīti asanisatasan‍nipātainapi abhaij‍jarūpaṃ taṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ abhañ‍jitvā nisīdi| tasmiṃ samayai mārau daivaputtau siddhatthakumārau ‘‘mayhaṃ vasaṃ atik‍kamitukāmau, na dāni assa atik‍kamituṃ dassāmī’’ti mārabalassa santikaṃ gantvā aitamatthaṃ āraucaitvā māraghausanaṃ nāma ghausāpaitvā mārabalaṃ ādāya nikkhami| sā mārasainā mārassa puratau dvādasa yaujanā haunti| dakkhiṇatau ca vāmatau ca dvādasa yaujanā| pacchatau cak‍kavāḷapariyantaṃ katvā ṭhitā, uddhaṃ navayaujanubbaidhā| yassā un‍nādantiyā un‍nādasaddau yaujanasahassatau paṭṭhāya pathavīundriyasaddau viya suyyati| atha mārau daivaputtau diyaḍḍhayaujanasatikaṃ girimaikhalaṃ nāma hatthiṃ abhirūhitvā bāhusahassaṃ māpaitvā nānāvudhāni aggahaisi| avasaisāyapi māraparisāya dvai janā aikasadisaṃ  āvudhaṃ na gaṇhiṃsu| nānappakāravaṇṇā nānappakāra mukhā hutvā mahāsattaṃ ajjhauttharamānā āgamaṃsu|

dasasahassacak‍kavāḷadaivatā pana mahāsattassa thuti yau vadamānā aṭṭhaṃsu| sak‍kau daivarājā vijayuttarasaṅkhaṃ dhamamānau aṭṭhāsi| sau kira saṅkhau vīsahatthasatikau hauti| sakiṃ vātaṃ gāhāpaitvā dhamantau cattārau māsai saddaṃ karitvā nissaddau hauti| mahākāḷanāga rājā atiraikapadasataina vaṇṇaṃ vadantau aṭṭhāsi| mahābrahmā saitacchattaṃ dhārayamānau aṭṭhāsi| māra balai pana baudhimaṇḍalaṃ upasaṅkamantai taisaṃ aikaupi ṭhātuṃ nāsakkhi| sammukha sammukhaṭṭhānainaiva palāyiṃsu| kāḷau nāma nāgarājā pathaviṃ nimuj‍jitvā pañ‍cayaujanasatikaṃ mantirakanāgabhavanaṃ gantvā ubhauhi hatthaihi mukhaṃ pidahitvā nipan‍nau| sak‍kau vijayuttarasaṅkhaṃ piṭṭhiyaṃ katvā cak‍kavāḷamukha vaṭṭiyaṃ aṭṭhāsi| mahābrahmā saitacchattaṃ kauṭiyaṃ gahaitvā brahmalaukamaiva agamāsi| aikadaivatāpi ṭhātuṃ samatthau nāma nāhausi| mahāpurisau aikakauva nisīdi| māraupi attanau parisaṃ āha ‘‘tāta suddhaudanaputtaina siddhatthaina sadisau añ‍ñau purisau nāma natthi, mayaṃ sammukhā yuddhaṃ dātuṃ na sakkhissāma, pacchā bhāgaina dassāmā’’ti| mahāpurisaupi tīṇi passāni aulaukaitvā sabbadaivatānaṃ palāyitattā suñ‍ñāni addasa| puna uttarapassaina mārabalaṃ ajjhauttharamānaṃ disvā ‘‘ayaṃ aittakau janau maṃ aikakaṃ sandhāya mahantaṃ vāyāmaṃ karauti, imasmiṃ ṭhānai mayhaṃ mātā vā pitā vā bhātā vā añ‍ñau kauci ñātakau vā natthi, imā pana dasa pāramiyauva mayhaṃ dīgharattaṃ puttaparijanasadisā, tasmā mayā pāramiyauva phalakaṃ katvā pāramisatthainaiva paharitvā ayaṃ balakāyau mayā viddhaṃsaituṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti dasa pāramiyau āvaj‍jamānau nisīdi|

atha  mārau daivaputtau aitainaiva siddhatthaṃ palāpaissāmīti vātamaṇḍalaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| taṅkhaṇañ‍ñaiyaiva puratthimā dibhaidā vātā samuṭṭhahitvā aḍḍhayaujanaaikayaujana dviyaujanatiyaujanappamāṇāni pabbatakūṭāni padālaitvā vanagaccharukkhādīni uddhaṃ mūlāni katvā sāmantā gāmanigamai cuṇṇavicuṇṇaṃ kātuṃ samatthāpi mahāpurisassa puñ‍ñataijaina mahānubhāvaina baudhisattaṃ patvā cīvarakaṇṇamattampi cālaituṃ nāsakkhiṃsu| tatau udakaina ajjhauttharitvā māraissāmāti mahāvassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| tassānubhāvaina uparūpari satapaṭalasahassapaṭalādibhaidā vaḷāhakā uṭṭhahitvā vassiṃsu| vuṭṭhidhārāvaigaina pathavī bhin‍nā ahausi| vanarukkhādīnaṃ uparibhāgaina mahāmaighau āgantvā mahā sattassa cīvarai ussāvabinduggahaṇamattampi taimaituṃ nāsakkhi| tatau pāsāṇavassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| mahantāni pabbatakūṭāni dhūmāyantāni paj‍jalantāni ākāsaināgantvā baudhisattassa pādamūlai dibbamālāguḷabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu| tatau paharavassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| aikataudhārā ubhataudhārā asisattikhurappādayau dhūmāyantā paj‍jalantā ākāsaināgantvā baudhisattaṃ patvā dibbapupphāni ahaisuṃ| tatau aṅgāravassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| kiṃsukavaṇṇā aṅgārā ākāsaināgantvā baudhisattassa pādamūlai dibba pupphāni hutvā vikiriṃsu| tatau kuk‍kuḷavassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| ac‍cuṇhau aggivaṇṇau kuk‍kuḷau ākāsaināgantvā baudhisattassa pādamūlai dibbacandanacuṇṇaṃ hutvā nipatati| tatau vāḷukavassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| atisukhumavāḷikā dhūmāyantā paj‍jalantā ākāsaināgantvā baudhisattassa pādamūlai dibbapupphāni hutvā nipatiṃsu| tatau kalalavassaṃ samuṭṭhāpaisi| taṃ kalalavassaṃ dhūmāyantaṃ paj‍jalantaṃ ākāsai nāgantvā baudhisattassa pādamūlai dibbavilaipanaṃ hutvā patati| tatau iminā bhiṃsaitvā siddhatthaṃ palāpaissāmīti andhakāraṃ  samuṭṭhāpaisi| taṃ caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ andhakāraṃ viya mahātamaṃ hutvā baudhisattaṃ patvā sūriyappabhā vihataṃ viya antaradhāyati|
