aivaṃ mārau imāhi navahi vātavassapāsāṇapaharaaṅgāra kuk‍kuḷavāḷuka kalalaandhakāravuṭṭhīhi baudhisattaṃ palāpaituṃ asak‍kauntau ‘‘kiṃ bhaṇai tiṭṭhatha imaṃ siddhatthakumāraṃ gaṇhatha hanatha palāpaithā’’ti parisaṃ āṇāpaitvā sayampi girimaikhalassa hatthinau khandhai nisin‍nau cak‍kāvudhaṃ ādāya baudhisattaṃ upasaṅkamitvā ‘‘siddhattha uṭṭhāhi aitasmā pal‍laṅkā, nāyaṃ tumhākaṃ pāpuṇāti, mayhaṃaiva pāpuṇātī’’ti āha| mahāsattau tassa vacanaṃ sutvā avauca ‘‘māra naiva tayā dasapāramiyau pūritā, na upapāramiyau na paramattha pāramiyau nāpi pañ‍camahāparic‍cāgā paric‍cattā na ñātatthacariyā na laukatthacariyā na buddhatthacariyā pūritā, sabbā tā mayāyaiva pūritā, tasmā nāyaṃ pal‍laṅkau tuyhaṃ pāpuṇāti, mayhamaiva pāpuṇātī’’ti| mārau kuddhau kaudhavaigaṃ asahantau mahāpurisassa cak‍kāvudhaṃ vissaj‍jaisi| taṃ tassa dasapāramiyau āvaj‍jantassa uparibhāgai mālāvitānaṃ hutvā aṭṭhāsi| taṃ kira khuracak‍kāvudhaṃ añ‍ñadā taina kuddhaina vissaṭṭhaṃ aikaghanapāsāṇa thambhai vaṃsakaḷīrai viya chindantaṃ gacchati| idāni pana tasmiṃ mālāvitānaṃ hutvā ṭhitai avasaisā māraparisā idāni pal‍laṅkatau vuṭṭhāya palāyissatīti mahantamahantāni sailakūṭāni vissaj‍jaisuṃ| tānipi mahāpurisassa pāramiyau āvaj‍jantassa mālāguḷabhāvaṃ āpaj‍jitvā bhūmiyaṃ patiṃsu|

daivatā cak‍kavāḷamukhavaṭṭiyaṃ ṭhitā gīvaṃ pasāraitvā sīsaṃ ukkhipitvā ‘‘naṭṭhau vata bhau siddhatthakumārassa rūpaggappattau attabhāvau, kiṃ nu khau karissatī’’ti aulaukainti| tatau  mahāpurisau pūritapāramīnaṃ baudhisattānaṃ abhi sambujjhanadivasai pattapal‍laṅkau mayhaṃva pāpuṇātīti vatvā ṭhitaṃ māraṃ āha ‘‘māra tuyhaṃ dānassa din‍nabhāvai kau sakkhī’’ti| mārau ‘‘aittakā janā sakkhinau’’ti māra balābhimukhaṃ hatthaṃ pasāraiti| tasmiṃ khaṇai māraparisāya ‘‘ahaṃ sakkhī ahaṃ sakkhī’’ti pavattasaddau pathavīundriyasaddasadisau ahausi| atha khau mārau mahāpurisaṃ āha ‘‘siddhattha tuyhaṃ dānassa din‍nabhāvai kau sakkhī’’ti| mahāpurisau ‘‘tuyhaṃ tāva dānassa din‍nabhāvai sacaitanā sakkhinau| mayhaṃ pana imasmiṃ ṭhānai sacaitanau kauci sakkhī nāma natthi| tiṭṭhatu tāva mai avasaisattabhāvaisu din‍nadānaṃ, vaissantarattabhāvai pana ṭhatvā mayhaṃ sattasattaka mahādānassa din‍nabhāvai acaitanāpi ghanamahāpathavī sakkhī’’ti cīvaragabbhantaratau dakkhiṇahatthaṃ nīharitvā vaissantarattabhāvai mayhaṃ sattasattakamahādānassa din‍nabhāvai tvaṃ sakkhi na sakkhīti mahāpathaviyābhimukhaṃ hatthaṃ pasāraisi| mahā pathavī ‘‘ahaṃ tai dānassa sakkhī’’ti viravasahassaina viravasatasahassaina mārabalaṃ avattharamānā viya un‍nadi|

tatau mahāpurisai din‍naṃ tai siddhattha mahādānaṃ uttama dānanti vaissantaradānaṃ sammasantai diyaḍḍhayaujanasatikau girimaikhalau hatthī jāṇukaihi patiṭṭhāsi| māraparisā disāvidisā palāyiṃsu| dvai aikamaggaina gatā nāma natthi| sīsābharaṇāni caiva vatthābharaṇāni ca pahāya sammukha sammukhaṭṭhānai disāvidisāhiyaiva palāyiṃsu| tatau daivasaṅghau palāyamānaṃ mārabalaṃ disvā mārassa parājayau jātau| siddhatthakumārassa jayau| jayapūjaṃ karissāmāti nāgā nāgānaṃ, supaṇṇā supaṇṇānaṃ, daivatā daivatānaṃ, brahmānau brahmānaṃ paisaitvā gandhamālādi hatthā mahāpurisassa santikaṃ baudhipal‍laṅkaṃ āgamaṃsu| aivaṃ gataisu ca panaitaisu –

‘‘jayau  hi buddhassa sirīmatau ayaṃ,

mārassa ca pāpimatau parājayau|

ugghausisuṃ baudhimaṇḍai pamauditā,

jayaṃ tadā nāgagaṇā mahaisinau||

jayau hi buddhassa sirīmatau ayaṃ,

mārassa ca pāpimatau parājayau|

ugghausisuṃ baudhimaṇḍai pamauditā,

jayaṃ tadā supaṇṇasaṅghā mahaisinau||

jayau hi buddhassa sirīmatau ayaṃ,

mārassa ca pāpimatau parājayau|

ugghausisuṃ baudhimaṇḍai pamauditā,

jayaṃ tadā daivagaṇā mahaisinau||

jayau hi buddhassa sirīmatau ayaṃ,

mārassa ca pāpimatau parājayau|

ugghausisuṃ baudhimaṇḍai pamauditā,

jayaṃ tadā brahmagaṇāpi tādinau’’ti||

avasaisaisu dasasu cak‍kavāḷasahassaisu daivatā mālāgandhavilaipanaihi ca pūjayamānā nānappakāra thutiyau ca vadamānā aṭṭhaṃsu| aivaṃ anatthaṅgataiyaiva sūriyai mahāpurisau mārabalaṃ vidhamaitvā cīvarūpari pata mānaihi baudhirukkhaṅkuraihi rattapavāḷapal‍lavaihi viya pūjayamānau paṭhamayāmai pubbainivāsañāṇaṃ anussaritvā majjhimayāmai dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhaitvā pacchimayāmai paṭic‍ca samuppādai ñāṇaṃ autāraisi| athassa dvādasapadikaṃ pac‍cayā kāraṃ vaṭṭavivaṭṭavasaina anulaumatau paṭilaumatau sammasantassa dasasahassilaukadhātu udakapariyantaṃ katvā dvādasakkhattuṃ sampakampi| mahāpurisaina pana dasasahassi laukadhātuṃ un‍nādaitvā aruṇuggamanavailāyaṃ sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇaṃ  paṭivijjhīti vuttaṃ hauti| idha pana vuttāvasaisaṃ tāsu ca aulaukaitvā gahaitabbanti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| upaitapuñ‍ñau sampan‍na dānādipāramīpuñ‍ñavā dānādipāramīpuñ‍ñaina sampan‍nau vā, yau sugatau varabaudhimūlai uttamassa baudhirukkhassa samīpai, sasainamāraṃ saināya saha pavattaṃ mārarājaṃ, jinitvā pāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina vijayitvā, aruṇaudayamhi visākhapuṇṇamiyaṃ aruṇuggamanavailāyaṃ, baudhiṃ arahattamaggañāṇa sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ baudhiṃ, abaujjhi paṭivijjhi adhigami vā, mārajinaṃ pañ‍camārānaṃ vijitāviṃ, abhaṅgaṃ sasainamārau yau kauci vā bhindituṃ asamatthaṃ taṃ sugataṃ ahaṃ namāmīti|

chaṭṭhamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

7.

 rāgādi chaidāmalañāṇakhaggaṃ,

satīsamañ‍ñāphalakābhigāhaṃ|

sīlaughalaṅkāravibhūsitaṃ taṃ,

namāmibhiñ‍ñāvaramiddhupaitaṃ||

7. aivaṃ chaṭṭhamagāthāya sugataṃ vanditvā idāni arahatta maggañāṇādīhi catūhi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau rāgādityādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana indavajira upaindavajira gāthādvayassa lakkhaṇassa missakattā aikādasakkharaihi lakkhitā upajātigāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘anantaraudīritalakkhaṇā cai, pādāvimissā upajātiyau tā’’ti| tassatthau| yassā vuttiyā anantarau dīritalakkhaṇā anantarai udīritaṃ vuttaṃ indavajiraupainda vajiragāthānaṃ lakkhaṇavantā pādā vimissā saṃsaṭṭhā cai bhavanti, tā vuttiyau upajātiyau nāmāti|

tattha  rāgādichaidāmala ñāṇakhagganti aittha rāgaādichaida amalañāṇakhaggantipadacchaidau| rāgādikilaisaṃ chindantaṃ malavira hitaṃ arahattamaggañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ asivantanti atthau| tatrāyaṃ vacanatthau| rūpādīsu rañ‍jatīti rāgau| ranjadhātu rāgai ṇa| rāgau ādi yaisantaiti rāgādayau, kilaisā| tai chindatīti rāgādichaidaṃ, arahattamaggañāṇaṃ| apāyaṃ malati dhāraitīti malaṃ, rāgādi| atha vā malati saṃkilissati aitaināti malaṃ| maladhātu saṃkilaisanai a| malatau virahitaṃ amalaṃ, ñāṇaṃ| cattāri sac‍cāni jānāti paṭivijjhatīti ñāṇaṃ, arahattamaggañāṇaṃ| rāgādichaidañ‍ca amalañ‍ca taṃ ñāṇañ‍cāti rāgādichaidāmalañāṇaṃ| rukkhādiṃ khaggati chindati aitaināti vā khaggaṃ, pakatiasi| khaggaṃ viyāti khaggaṃ, ñāṇaṃ| rāgādichaidāmalañāṇasaṅkhataṃ khaggaṃ yassa sauti rāgādichaidāmalañāṇakhaggau, buddhau| rāgādikilaisaṃ chindantaina malavira hitaina aggamaggañāṇasaṅkhataina asinā saman‍nāgatauti atthau| taṃ|

satīsamañ‍ñā phalakābhigāhanti aittha satisamañ‍ñāphalaka abhigāhantipadacchaidau| sammāsatināmikaṃ phalakaṃ daḷhagāha kanti atthau| tatrāyaṃ vacanatthau| saraṇaṃ sati, sarati sampayuttadhammā aitāyāti, saratīti vā sati| saradhātuti| atha vā pamādaṃ sarati hiṃsatīti sati, sammāsati| saradhātu hiṃsāyaṃti| sammā ājānāti aitāyāti samañ‍ñā, satiiti samañ‍ñā satisamañ‍ñā, asiādisatthaṃ phalati nivāraitīti phalakaṃ, khaiṭakaṃ| phaladhātu nivāraṇai ṇvu| satisamañ‍ñaṃ phalakaṃ abhidaḷhaṃ gaṇhatīti satisamañ‍ñāphala kābhigāhau, buddhau labbhati| yathā hi saināya phalakaṃ asiādisatthaṃ rakkhati, aivaṃ satisaṃvarau cakkhādi dvāraisu rūpādiārammaṇaṃ pavisituṃ adatvā rakkhati| tasmā bhagavā satidhammau sabbākusalānaṃ rakkhakattā sabbakusala dhammānaṃ upakārattā sabbatthānaṃ sādhakattā ca satiphalakaṃ daḷhaṃ  gaṇhatīti vuttaṃ hauti| vuttañhi ‘‘yai kaici kusalā dhammā, sabbai tai appamādamūlakā appamādasamausaraṇā, appamādau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti ca| ‘‘satiṃ khvāhaṃ bhikkhavai sabbatthikaṃ vadāmī’’ti ca|

sīlaughalaṅkāravibhūsitanti sīlaaughaalaṅkāra vibhū sitanti padacchaidau| visuddhasīlānaṃ samūhasaṅkhātaina alaṅkāraina vibhūsitanti atthau| tattha sīlanti laukuttara sīlaṃ| tassa atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| taṃ pana caitanā sīlaṃ, caitasikaṃ sīlaṃ, saṃvarau sīlaṃ, avītik‍kamau sīlañ‍cāti catubbidhaṃ hauti| tattha caitanā sīlaṃ nāma pāṇātipātā dīhi vā viramantassa vattapaṭipattiṃ vā pūraintassa caitanā| caitasikasīlaṃ nāma pāṇātipātādīhi viramantassa virati| saṃvarau sīlanti aittha saṃvarau pātimaukkhasaṃvarau, sati saṃvarau, ñāṇasaṃvarau, khantisaṃvarau, vīriyasaṃvarau cāti pañ‍cavidhau hauti, sabbamaitaṃ saṃvarau sīlaṃ nāma| avītik‍kamau sīlaṃ nāma samādin‍nasīlassa kāyikavācasikau anatik‍kamauti| vuttañhaitaṃ paṭisambhidāyaṃ ‘‘kiṃsīlanticaitanā sīlaṃ, caitasikaṃ sīlaṃ, saṃvarau sīlaṃ, avītik‍kamau sīla’’nti| idha pana avītik‍kamau sīlaṃ nādhippaitaṃ| kasmā| bhagavatau samādin‍nasikkhāpadassa abhāvatau| buddhānañhi sikkhāpadaṃ nāma natthīti| aughauti samūhau| aughasaddau hi samūhavācakau| taina vuttaṃ abhidhānappadīpikāyaṃ –

‘‘samūhau gaṇasaṅghātā, samudāyau ca sañ‍cayau|

sandauhau nivahau aughau, visarau nikarau cayau’’tyādi||

avahanati gacchati pavattati avayavaṃ aitthāti aughau, samūhau| avapubbahanadhātu gatimhi ṇau| avassa au, hanassa ghau| abhidhānappadīpikāṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘avayavaṃ katvā  byāpiyati gacchatīti aughau’’ti vuttaṃ| sīlānaṃ aughau samūhau sīlaughau| alaṅkārauti piḷandhanau| alaṃ vibhusaṃ taṃ samaṅgīpuggalaṃ karauti anaināti alaṅkārau, sīlaṃ| alaṃsaddūpapadakaradhātu ṇau| sīlaughau aiva alaṅkārau sīlaughālaṅkārau, taina vibhū siyatīti sīlaughalaṅkāravibhūsitau, buddhau| taina pana añ‍ñaihi asādhāraṇaina laukuttarasīlālaṅkāraina vibhūsitau| na cīvarādinālaṅkāraināti atthau| sīlālaṅkāraina hi sadisau alaṅkārau nāma natthi| taṃ sīlaughalaṅkāravibhūsitaṃ|

namāmibhiñ‍ñāvaramiddhupaitanti aittha namāmi abhiñ‍ñāvaraṃ iddhi upaitanti padacchaidau| tattha namāmīti vandāmi| abhiñ‍ñāvaramiddhupaitanti abhiñ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya uttamāya iddhiyā saman‍nāgatanti atthau| abhivisaisaina jānātīti abhiñ‍ñā, chabbidhaṃ ñāṇaṃ| tañhi iddhividhadibbasauta paracitta vijānana pubbainivāsānussati cutūpapāta āsavakkhaya ñāṇavasaina chabbidhaṃ hauti| tattha iddhividhañāṇanti aikaupi hutvā bahudhā hautītiādinayappavattai iddhividhai jānana ñāṇaṃ| dibbasautañāṇanti dūrasantikādibhaidasaddārammaṇāya dibbasautadhātuyā jānanañāṇaṃ| paracittavijānanañāṇanti parasattānaṃ cittaparicchaidassa vijānanañāṇaṃ| pubbainivāsā nussatiñāṇanti attasantānai vutthavasaina caiva gaucara nivāsavasaina ca pubbai atītabhavaisu khandhādīnaṃ anussaraṇa satiyā sampayuttaṃ ñāṇaṃ| cutūpapātañāṇanti sattānaṃ cutiyañ‍ca upapātai ca jānanañāṇaṃ| aittha ca aṭṭhakathāsu cutūpapātañāṇaṭṭhānai tassa sādhakattā dibbacakkhuñāṇanti vuttaṃ| āsavakkhayañāṇanti sac‍caparicchaidassa jānanaṃ arahatta maggañāṇanti| varanti uttamaṃ| iddhīti ijjhanaṃ samijjhanaṃ| ijjhati samijjhatīti vā iddhi| idhadhātuti| abhiñ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya varāya  iddhiyā upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatauti abhiñ‍ñāvaramiddhupaitau, buddhau| taṃ abhiñ‍ñāvaramiddhupaitaṃ| taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| rāgādichaidāmalañāṇa khaggaṃ rāgādikilaisaṃ chindantaṃ malavirahitaṃ arahattamaggañāṇa saṅkhātaṃ asivantaṃ, satīsamañ‍ñāphalakābhigāhaṃ sammā satināmikaṃ phalakaṃ daḷhagahaṇaṃ, sīlaughalaṅkāravibhūsitaṃ sīlānaṃ samūhasaṅkhātaina alaṅkāraina vibhūsitaṃ, abhiñ‍ñā varamiddhupaitaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya varāya uttamāya iddhiyā upaitaṃ samupaitaṃ saman‍nāgataṃ taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti|

sattamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

8.

 dayālayaṃ sabbadhi duk‍karaṃ karaṃ,

bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamamaggataṃ gataṃ|

tilaukanāthaṃ susamāhitaṃ hitaṃ,

samantacakkhuṃ paṇamāmi taṃmitaṃ||

8. aivaṃ sattamagāthāya buddhaṃ vanditvā idāni mahākaruṇā dīhi navahi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau dayālayantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ja ta ja ragaṇaihi yuttattā dvādasakkharaihi lakkhitā catupādantayamakā vaṃsaṭṭhagāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘vadanti vaṃsaṭṭhamidaṃ jatā jarā’’ti| tassatthau, yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ jatā jatagaṇā ca jarā jaragaṇā ca cai bhavanti, idaṃ vuttaṃ vaṃsaṭṭhaṃ vaṃsaṭṭhagāthā nāmāti munayau isayau vadantīti|

tattha dayālayanti mahākaruṇāya ca, maittāya ca ādhāraṃ| dayāsaddau hi karuṇāya ca, maittāya ca vattati| adayāpan‍nauti aittha hi karuṇāya vattati| nik‍karuṇikataṃ  āpan‍nauti atthau| dayāpan‍nauti aittha maittāya maittacittataṃ āpan‍nauti atthau| idha pana tadubhayampi vaṭṭati| tasmā dayā ca dayā ca dayāti saddasarūpaikasaisau kātabbau| vacanatthau panaittha aivaṃ vaiditabbau| dayati hiṃsati kāruṇikanti dayā, mahākaruṇā| dayadhātu hiṃsāyaṃ a| yā karuṇā yāva yathādhippaitaṃ parassa hitanipphattiṃ na pāpuṇāti, tāva kāruṇikaṃ hiṃsatīti atthau| dayati hiṃsati apanaiti paradukkhanti vā dayā| atha vā dayati attanau sukhampi pahāya khaidaṃ gaṇhāti saj‍janau aitāyāti dayā, dayati anuggaṇhāti pāpajanampi aitāyāti vā dayā, dayanti gaṇhanti baudhisattā sammāsambaudhiṃ aitāyāti vā dayā, baudhisattā buddhabhāvāya abhinīhārakaraṇa kālai hatthagatampi aggaphalaṃ chaṭṭaitvā mahākaruṇāya saṃsārasāgaratau sattai samuddharitukāmā anassāsa karaṃ mahantaṃ saṃsāradukkhañ‍ca pacchimabhavai saha amata dhātupaṭilābhaina anaikaguṇasamalaṅkataṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇañ‍ca gaṇhantīti atthau| dayati dadāti sattānaṃ abhayaṃ aitāyāti dayā, maittā| dayadhātu dānai a| dayati gacchati vibhāgaṃ akatvā sattaisu samaṃ pavattatīti dayā| yathā udakaṃ sabbasattaisu sītaina samaṃ pharati rajaumalañ‍ca pavāhaiti, aivaṃ maittā pāpakalyāṇajanaisu vibhāgaṃ akatvā samaṃ pavattatīti atthau|

aparau nayau| dayanti anurakkhanti sattai aitāya, sayaṃ vā anuddayati anuddayamattamaiva vā aitanti dayā, karuṇāmaittā labbhanti| tāya ālayati patiṭṭhati aitthāti dayālayau, buddhau| bhagavā hi daivadattādīsu viraudhi sattaisu ca aviraudhisattaisu aurasai rāhulai ca samaṃ nic‍caṃ hitajjhāsayatāya mahākaruṇāya maittāya ca ṭhitau, tasmā dayālayanti thaumaitīti vuttaṃ hauti| vuttañhaitaṃ apadānapāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘vadhakai daivadattai ca, caurai aṅgulimālakai|

dhanapālai rāhulai ca, samacittau mahāmunī’’ti||

sabbadhīti sabbasmiṃ attabhāvai| buddhabhāvāya abhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya sabbattabhāvaisūti atthau| duk‍karanti dukkhaina karitabbaṃ pañ‍camahāparic‍cāgādikammaṃ dukkhaina karitabbanti duk‍karaṃ, pañ‍camahāparic‍cāgādikammaṃ labbhati| aittha ca pañ‍camahā paric‍cāgauti dhanaparic‍cāgau, aṅgaparic‍cāgau, puttaparic‍cāgau, bhariyaparic‍cāgau, jīvitaparic‍cāgau cāti pañ‍cavidhau hauti| tattha hatthiassa dāsidāsādi saviñ‍ñāṇakassa dhanassa vā muttāmaṇirajatādi aviñ‍ñāṇakassa dhanassa vā paric‍cāgau dhanaparic‍cāgau nāma| hatthapādacakkhukaṇṇanāsādisarīrāva yavassa paric‍cāgau aṅgaparic‍cāgau nāma| puttassa ca dhītuyā ca paric‍cāgau puttaparic‍cāgau nāma| bhariyāya paric‍cāgau bhariyaparic‍cāgau nāma| jīvitindriyapaṭibaddhassa sarīrassa paric‍cāgau jīvitaparic‍cāgau nāma| kiñ‍cāpi pana dhanaparic‍cāgā dayau dānapāramiyaṃyaiva antaugadhā, tathāpi paric‍cāga visaisabhāvadassanatthañ‍caiva suduk‍karabhāvadassanatthañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgavasaina visuṃ katanti adhippāyau| mahāpadānasuttaṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘aṅgaparic‍cāgau, nayanaparic‍cāgau, attaparic‍cāgau, raj‍japaric‍cāgau, puttadāraparic‍cāgauti imai pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgā| tatthapi kāmaṃ aṅgaparic‍cāgādayaupi dānapāramīyaiva| tathāpi paric‍cāgavisaisabhāvadassanatthañ‍caiva suduk‍karabhāvadassanatthañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgānaṃ visuṃ gahaṇaṃ| tatauyaiva ca aṅgaparic‍cāgataupi visuṃ nayanaparic‍cāgaggahaṇaṃ| paric‍cāgabhāvasāmañ‍ñaipi raj‍japaric‍cāga puttadāraparic‍cāgaggahaṇaṃ kata’’nti vuttaṃ| taṃ duk‍karaṃ| idaṃ pana karanti aitassa kammapadaṃ| karanti karauntaṃ, karautīti karau, buddhau| taṃ|

bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamamaggatanti  aittha bhavaaṇṇavaatik‍kamaṃ aggatanti padacchaidau| bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamanti kāmarūpaarūpabhavasaṅkhatā aṇṇavā samuddatau atik‍kantaṃ| bhavanti sattā aitthāti bhavau| aṇṇau vuc‍cati udakaṃ| taṃ aitasmiṃ atthīti aṇṇavā, samuddau| bhavasaṅkhātau aṇṇavauti bhavaṇṇavā, mahāsamuddau| bahūhi ūmivaigādīhi bhayaihi aukiṇṇau viya tividhaupi bhavau bahūhi jātijarāmaraṇādīhi dukkhabhayaihi aukiṇṇau hauti, tasmā bhavaṇṇavāti vuc‍cati| taṃ atik‍kamittha uttaritthāti bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamau, buddhau| tassa hi gambhīrai mahāudakakkhandhai mahāsamuddai udakaṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ pavattati sandatiiva tibhavasaṅkhatai saṃsārai paṭisandhicutiyau abbaucchin‍naṃ nibbattakānaṃ diyaḍḍhasahassakilaisānaṃ baudhimaṇḍai arahattamaggañāṇaina asaisaṃ viddhaṃsitattā tīsu bhavaisu punabbhavau natthi| tasmā buddhaṃ bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamanti abhitthavituṃ arahatīti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamaṃ|

aggatanti sabbasattānaṃ saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ| ayaṃ panaittha aggasaddau ādikauṭikauṭṭhāsasaiṭṭhaisu dissati| tathā haisa aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gatantiādīsu ādimhi dissati| tainaiva aṅgulaggaina taṃ aṅgulaggaṃ parāmasaiyyātiādīsu kauṭiyaṃ| anujānāmi bhikkhavai vihāraggaina vā parivaiṇaggaina vā bhājaituntiādīsu kauṭṭhāsai| aggau hamasmi laukassātiādīsu saiṭṭhai| svāyamidhapi sauyaiva daṭṭhabbau| daivamanussānaṃ saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ ajati gacchatīti aggau, buddhau| asadisaṭṭhaina guṇavisiṭṭhaṭṭhaina asamasamaṭṭhaina ca aggau saiṭṭhau nāmāti atthau| atha vā dul‍labhapātu bhāvatau acchariyamanussabhāvatau bahujanahitasukhāvahatau adutiyaasahāyādibhāvatau ca bhagavā laukai aggau saiṭṭhau nāmāti atthau| vuttañhi aṅguttarapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘yāvatā bhikkhavai sattā apadā vā dvipadā  vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyatī’’ti| aggassa bhāvau aggataṃ| taṃ pana gatantiaitassa kammapadaṃ| gatanti pattaṃ, pāpuṇantanti atthau|

tilaukanāthanti kāmarūpaarūpasaṅkhatānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ laukānaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ| aittha ca laukasaddassa atthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| tayau laukā tilaukā| nāthasaddau pana nipphan‍nānipphan‍navasaina duvidhau hauti| tattha anipphan‍nanāthasaddau paṭisaraṇatthau hauti saṅkaitavasaina| tathā hi vuttaṃ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘saṅkaitavacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, laukasammutikāraṇa’’nti| tilaukānaṃ nāthau paṭisaraṇauti tilaukanāthau, buddhau| sabbānatthaparihāra pubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāyaniratisayāya payaugasampattiyā ca sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantupakāritāya aparimitanirupamappabhāvaguṇavisaisasamaṅgitāya ca sabbasattuttamau bhagavā aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ aikapaṭisaraṇau nāmāti atthau|

nipphan‍nanāthasaddau pana yācanai, upatāpai, issariyai, āsīsai cāti catūsvatthaisu vattati| tattha yācanai nāthati yācatīti nāthau, buddhau| nāthadhātu a| sau pana vainaiyyasattai attanau hitakaraṇai yācitvāpi niyaujaitīti atthau| atha vā bhagavā sādhu bhikkhavai bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhaiyyātiādinā sattānaṃ taṃ taṃ hitapaṭipattiṃ yācitvāpi karuṇāya samussāhitau tai tattha niyaujaiti| tasmā nāthau nāmāti atthau| upatāpai nāthati vainaiyyagatai kilaisai upatāpaitīti nāthau, buddhau| dhammadaisanāya vainaiyyasantānai kilaisai viddhaṃsāpaitīti atthau| issariyai nāthati issariyati sattaiti nāthau| paramaina pana  cittissariyaina saman‍nāgatau bhagavā sabbasattai dhammaina isati abhibhavatīti atthau| āsīsai pana nāthati āsīsatīti nāthau| vainaiyyānaṃ hitasukhaṃ maittāya navasaina patthaitīti atthau| tilaukānaṃ nāthau tilaukanāthau, buddhau| taṃ tilaukanāthaṃ|

susamāhitanti lābhālābhādilaukadhammaihi acala mānattā sundarasamādhivantaṃ| saṃsamaṃ ādhiyati aikārammaṇai sampayuttadhammai ṭhapaiti aitaināti samādhi| saṃpubba dhā dhātu i| sundarau pasatthau vā samādhi susamādhi| taṃ assa atthīti susamāhitau, buddhau| assathyatthai tau| pabbatautiādīsu viya| dhassa hau| catūhi disāhi āgataihi vātaihi acalamānau mahāmairupabbatau viya bhagavā lābhālābhādilaukadhammaihi ca micchāvāda vātaihi ca acalamānaina samādhinā saman‍nāgatauti atthau| taṃ susamāhitaṃ|

hitanti manussadaivabrahmasaṅkhātānaṃ sabbasattānaṃ payaujana dhāraintaṃ| kāraṇānurūpaṃ hinauti pavattatīti hitaṃ, payaujanaṃ| hidhātu gatiyaṃ ta| taṃ dhāraitīti hitau, buddhau| kitanta samāsauyaṃ| hitadharanti vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇatthāya uttara padalaupaina hitanti vattabbaṃ| yathā samaj‍janī padīpauti| atha vā dukkhaṃ hiṃsatīti hitaṃ| hiṃsadhātu hiṃsāyaṃ tapac‍cayau| yathā ca byādhiyā ausamaṃ raugīnaṃ hitanti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ dukkhahananatau hitaṃ nāma hauti| sukhaṃ vidadhātīti vā hitaṃ| dhādhātu dhāraṇai ta| yathā ca rudhiramaṃsādi dhātu vaḍḍhanaṃ rasāyatanaṃ kissānaṃ hitanti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ sukhavahanatau hitanti vuc‍cati| payaujanaṃ taṃ dhāraitīti hitau, buddhau| sau pana dhammadaisanāya vainaiyyajanānaṃ dukkhāpanayanampi ñāṇaparipācanampi katvā laukiyalaukuttarahitasukhaṃ dhāraitīti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ hitaṃ|

samantacakkhunti  aittha cakkhu nāma buddhacakkhu, dhammacakkhu, ñāṇa cakkhu, samantacakkhu, dibbacakkhūti pañ‍cavidhaṃ hauti| tattha addasaṃ khau ahaṃ bhikkhavai buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintauti idaṃ buddhacakkhu nāma| virajaṃ vītamalaṃ dhammacakkhunti idaṃ haiṭṭhimamaggatta yasaṅkhātaṃ dhammacakkhu nāma| cakkhuṃ udapādīti idaṃ ñāṇacakkhu nāma| samantacakkhu vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañāṇanti idaṃ samantacakkhu nāma| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ bhikkhavai dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaināti idaṃ dibbacakkhu nāma| idha pana samantacakkhu adhippaitaṃ| parisamantatau sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ passantaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇavantanti atthau| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau| samantatau sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ cakkhati passatīti samantacakkhu, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| cakkhadhātu u| taina vuttaṃ mahāniddaisapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘samantacakkhu vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañāṇa’’nti| samantacakkhu assa atthīti samantacakkhu, buddhau| vuttañhaitaṃ paṭisambhidāmaggai –

‘‘na tassa addiṭṭhamidhatthi kiñ‍ci,

athau aviñ‍ñātamajānitabbaṃ|

sabbaṃ abhiñ‍ñāsi yadatthi naiyyaṃ,

tathāgatau taina samantacakkhū’’ti||

tassatthau| tassa tathāgatassa addiṭṭhaṃ apassantaṃ kiñ‍ci dhammajātaṃ idhalaukai na atthi| athau aviñ‍ñātaṃ aviditaṃ apākaṭaṃ vā ajānitabbaṃ dhammajātaṃ na atthi| naiyyaṃ ñātabbaṃ yaṃ dhammajātaṃ atthi| sabbaṃ taṃ dhammajātaṃ tathāgatau abhiñ‍ñāsi paṭivijjhi| taina tasmā tathāgatau samantacakkhu nāmāti| taṃ samantacakkhuṃ|

paṇamāmi taṃmitanti aittha paṇamāmi taṃ amitanti padacchaidau| amitaṃ taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ paṇamāmīti sambandhau| tattha amitanti aparimaiyyaṃ ñāṇaina aparicchin‍naṃ vā, sīlasamādhi pañ‍ñādiguṇavantaṃ| amitabbaṃ ñāṇaina aparicchinditabbanti vā amitaṃ, sīlādiguṇaṃ| napubba mādhātu parimāṇai ta| taṃ assa atthīti  amitau, buddhau| yathā hi mahāsamuddai na sukaraṃ udakassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ aittakāni udakāḷhakānīti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasatānīti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasahassānīti vā, aittakāni udakāḷhakasatasahassānīti vāti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāudakakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati, aivamaiva buddhassa na sukaraṃ sīlādiguṇassa pamāṇaṃ gaṇaituṃ aittakaṃ sīlaguṇaṃ, aittakau samādhiguṇau, aittakā pañ‍ñāguṇātiādināti| atha khau asaṅkhyaiyyau appamaiyyau mahāguṇakkhandhautvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| tainavuttaṃ apadānapāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘buddhaupi buddhassa bhaṇaiyya vaṇṇaṃ,

kappampi cai añ‍ñamabhāsamānau|

khīyaitha kappau ciradīghamantarai,

vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatāna’’nti ca||

‘‘asaṅkhyaiyyāni nāmāni, saguṇaina mahaisinau|

guṇaina nāmamuddhaiyyaṃ, api nāmasahassatau’’ti ca||

taina buddhaṃ amitanti thaumaitīti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| dayālayaṃ mahākaruṇāya maittāya ādhāraṃ| sabbadhi sabbasmiṃ attabhāvai, duk‍karaṃ dukkhaina karitabbaṃ pañ‍camahāparic‍cāgādikammaṃ karaṃ karauntaṃ, bhavaṇṇavātik‍kamaṃ tibhavasaṅkhātasamuddatau atik‍kantaṃ, aggataṃ sabbasattānaṃ saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ, gataṃ pattaṃ, tilaukanāthaṃ kāmarūpa arūpasaṅkhātassa tilaukassa paṭisaraṇaṃ, susamāhitaṃ sundara samādhivantaṃ, hitaṃ sabbasattānaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasaṅkhātaṃ atthadhāraṇaṃ, samantacakkhuṃ parisamantatau sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ passantaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇavantaṃ, amitaṃ aparimaiyyaṃ sīlādiguṇavantaṃ, taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ paṇamāmi vandāmīti|

aṭṭhamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

9.

 tahiṃ  tahiṃ pāramisañ‍cayaṃ cayaṃ,

gataṃ gataṃ sabbhi sukhappadaṃ padaṃ|

narānarānaṃ sukhasambhavaṃ bhavaṃ,

namānamānaṃ jinapuṅgavaṃ gavaṃ||

9. aivaṃ aṭṭhamagāthāya buddhaṃ vanditvā idāni pāramisañ‍cayaṃ cayantyādīhi pañ‍cahi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau tahiṃ tahiṃ pāramisañ‍cayaṃcayantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana jatajara gaṇaihi yuttattā dvādasakkharaihi lakkhitā sabbapādādyantayamakā vaṃsaṭṭhagāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘vadanti vaṃsaṭṭhamidaṃ jatā jarā’’ti| tassatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva|

tattha tahiṃ tahinti tasmiṃ tasmiṃ bhavai attabhāvai vā, buddhavaraṃ paṇidhānatau paṭṭhāya taisu taisu bhavaisu attabhāvaisu vāti atthau| pāramisañ‍cayanti dānādipā ramīnaṃ samūhaṃ| aittha ca pāramī nāma dānasīlanaikkhammapañ‍ñā vīriya khantisac‍caadhiṭṭhānamaittāupaikkhāvasaina dasavidhā hauti| vuttañ‍ca buddhavaṃsai –

‘‘dānaṃ sīlañ‍ca naikkhammaṃ, pañ‍ñā vīriyaina pañ‍camaṃ|

khantisac‍camadhiṭṭhānaṃ, maittupaikkhāti tai dasā’’ti||

ayaṃ pana pāramīsaddassa vacanatthau| dānasīlādiguṇa visaisayaugaina sattuttamatāya mahābaudhisattau paramau| tassa ayaṃ bhāvau kammanti vā pāramī, dānādi kriyāva labbhati| santakabhāvakammataddhitai ṇapac‍cayau| itthiliṅgajautaka īpac‍cayau ca| atha vā dānādīni pūraitīti paramau| dānādiguṇānaṃ pūrakau pālakau ca baudhisattau| pūradhātu amapac‍cayau| parai sattai mavati attani bandhati guṇavisaisayaugaināti vā paramau, baudhisattau| parasaddūpapada mudhātu bandhanai k‍vi| paraṃ atiraikaṃ maj‍jati kilaisamalatauti vā paramau| parasaddūpa pada  maj‍jadhātu saṃsuddhiyaṃ k‍vi| parasaddau atiraikatthau| paraṃ saiṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ visaisaina mayati gacchatīti vā paramau| parasaddūpapada mayadhātu gatiyaṃ k‍vi| dhātvantalaupau| para saddau saiṭṭhatthau| paraṃ laukaṃ pamāṇabhūtaina ñāṇa visaisaina idhalaukamiva munāti paricchindatīti vā paramau| parasaddūpa pada munadhātu paricchindanai k‍vi| paraṃ ati viya sīlādiguṇagaṇaṃ attanau santānai minauti pakkhipatīti vā paramau| parasaddūpapada midhātu pakkhipanai k‍vi| parasaddau atiraikavācakau| paraṃ attabhūtadhammakāyatau añ‍ñaṃ paṭipakkhaṃ vā tadanatthakaraṃ kilaisacauragaṇaṃ mināti hiṃsatīti vā paramau, mahābaudhisattau| parasaddūpapada midhātu hiṃsāyaṃ k‍vi| parasaddau añ‍ñavācakau, paṭipakkhavācakau vā| paramassa ayantiādinā vuttanayaina pāramīti| api ca pārai nibbānai maj‍jati sujjhatīti pāramī, mahābaudhi sattau| suddhakattusādhanauyaṃ| pārasaddūpapada majadhātu saṃsuddhiyaṃ k‍vi| ī ca| pārai nibbānai sattai maj‍jati saudhaitīti vā pāramī, haitukattusādhanauyaṃ|

pārainibbānai sattai mavati bandhati yaujaitīti vā pāramī| pāraṃ nibbānaṃ mayati gacchati sattai ca māyaiti gamaiti vā pāramī| pāraṃ nibbānaṃ yāthāvatau munāti paricchindatīti vā pāramī| pārai nibbānai sattai minauti pakkhipatīti vā pāramī| midhātu pakkhipanai ī| pārai nibbānai sattānaṃ mināti hiṃsatīti vā pāramī, mahābaudhisattau| tassa ayaṃ bhāvau kammanti dānādikriyā pāramīti| iminā vuttanayaina pāramīsaddassa vacanatthau vaiditabbau| kiñ‍cāpi pana pāramīsaddassa nibbacanaṃ saṅkhaipaina vuttaṃ| sautūnaṃ pana niruttinayakausal‍latthaṃ vitthāraina vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

sā pana dasapāramī, dasaupapāramī, dasaparamatthapāramī cāti tividhā hauti| tattha saviñ‍ñāṇāviñ‍ñāṇakaṃ dhanaṃ paric‍cāgaṃ katvā  pūritā baudhisambhārā dasapāramiyau nāma| hattha pāda kaṇṇa nāsādiaṅgapac‍caṅgaparic‍cāgaṃ katvā pūritā baudhisambhārā dasaupapāramiyau nāma| jīvitindriyaparic‍cāgaṃ katvā pūritā baudhisambhārā dasa paramatthapāramiyau nāmāti| dānādipāramīnaṃ pana atthau cariyapiṭakaaṭṭhakathādīsu gahaitabbau| avayavaṃ saṅgahavasaina cayati gacchati pavattatīti sañ‍cayau, saṃpubba cidhātu gatiyaṃ ṇa| sañ‍cinauti avayavanti vā sañ‍cayau, samūhau| pāramīnaṃ sañ‍cayau samūhauti pāramisañ‍cayau, taṃ pāramisañ‍cayaṃ| taṃ pana cayanti aitassa kammaṃ| cayanti upacitaṃ, parisambhūtanti atthau| cayati upacinātīti cayau, ciyittha upaciyitthāti vā cayau, buddhau| cidhātu upacayai ṇa| taṃ cayaṃ| sabbhigatanti sambandhau|

tattha sabbhīti santaihi sappurisaihi| rāgādikilaisai samainti upasamaintīti santā, sappurisā| samudhātu upasamaita, antapac‍cayau vā| santaihīti vattabbai smāhismiṃnaṃ mhā bhimhivāti suttaina hivibhattiyā bhikāraṃ santasaddassa sau bhai bau cantaiti suttaina bhakārai parai santasaddassa sakāraṃ katvā antai ca bakārāgamaṃ katvā sabbhīti vuttaṃ| taihi sabbhi| taṃ pana gatanti aitassa avuttakattā| gatanti upagamitabbaṃ| tañhi daivamanussaihi gamiyati upagamiyati upasaṅkamiyatīti gatauti vuc‍cati| taṃ buddhaṃ atthakāmaihi sappurisaihi daivamanussaihi sīlādianantaguṇikattā saraṇanti abhimānā payirupāsitabbauti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ gataṃ| sukhappadaṃ padaṃ gatanti sambandhau| tattha sukhappadanti ac‍cantasukhassa patiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ| sukhanaṃ sukhaṃ, tassa paj‍jati tiṭṭhati aitthāti sukhappadaṃ, nibbānaṃ| padanti nibbānaṃ| tañhi buddhādīhi ariyaihi paj‍jiyati gamiyatīti padanti vuc‍cati| gatanti agaminti gatau, buddhau| dukkhādiasammissassa aikantasukhassa patiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ nibbānaṃ ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina apāpuṇinti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ gataṃ|

narānarānanti  naraanarānanti padacchaidau| manussadaivānanti atthau| pabbaj‍jādisaiṭṭhabhāvaṃ narati naitīti narau, narīyati sakaina kammaina niyatīti vā narau, manussau, sattau vā| naradhātu nayanai a| na narau anarau, daivau| narau ca anarau ca narānarau, taisaṃ narānarānaṃ| taṃ pana sukhasambhavanti padai sambandhau|

sukhasambhavanti laukiyalaukuttarasukhassa kāraṇaṃ| tividhasukhassa labhanakāraṇabhūtaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ vā| sukhayatīti sukhaṃ| tividhasukhaṃ| yassa uppaj‍jati, taṃ sukhitaṃ karautīti atthau| atha vā suṭṭhu dukkhaṃ khanatīti sukhaṃ| supubbakhanudhātu avadhāraṇai a| sambhavati sukhasaṅkhātaṃ phalaṃ anaina puñ‍ñaināti sambhavaṃ, puñ‍ñakaraṇaṃ| saṃpubbabhūdhātu sattāyaṃṇa| sukhassa sambhavaṃ sukhasambhavaṃ, taṃ pana bhavantipadai kammaṃ| bhavanti nibbattaintaṃ| taṃ sukhasambhavaṃ bhāvaiti nibbattaitīti bhavau, buddhau| taṃ bhavaṃ| sau hi mahākaruṇāpubbaṅgamāya dhammadaisanāya daivamanussānaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasukhassa labhanakāraṇabhūtaṃ dānasīlabhāvanāsaṅkhātaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ kāraiti, tasmā bhagavatau narānarānaṃ sukhasambhavaṃ bhavanti thaumaitīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

namānamānanti padassa namai anamānanti padacchaidau| namaiti ahaṃ namāmīti atthau| aittha ca mivibhattiyā aikārādaisauti vaiditabbau| anamānanti anamaintānaṃ avandantānaṃ anādarānanti atthau| taṃ pana gavantiaitassa visaisanaṃ| gavanti gauṇasadisānaṃ bālānaṃ| gavanti hi chaṭṭhībahuvacanantanāmapadaṃ| tatau namaṃ patimhāluttai ca samāsaiti suttai samabhiniviṭṭhaina caggahaṇaina naṃvacanassa aṃ ādaisañ‍ca gauic‍caitassa aukārassa avādaisaṃ katvā rūpasiddhi vaiditabbā| gacchantīti gāvau, gauṇā| gāvau viyāti gāvau, taisaṃ gavaṃ bālānaṃ| taṃ pana jinapuṅgavantipadai chaṭṭhīkammaṃ| jinapuṅgavanti jayantapurisuttamaṃ buddhaṃ namai namāmīti sambandhau| aittha ca puṅgava saddau  kammadhārayasamāsai saiṭṭhatthavācakau| munipuṅgavautiādīsu viya| vuttañ‍ca amarakausai –

‘‘uttarasmiṃ padai byaggha, puṅgavausabhakuñ‍jarā|

sīhasaddūlanāgādyā, pumai saiṭṭhatthagaucarā’’ti||

tassatthau| kammadhārayasamāsai uttarapadai vattamānā byagghapuṅgavausabhakuñ‍jarā byagghapuṅgavausabhakuñ‍jarasaddā ca sīha saddūlanāgādyā sīha saddūla nāgāsaddā ca pumai saiṭṭhattha vācakāti| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau| gau viya buddhaguṇai ajānantai anamantai añ‍ñadiṭṭhikai bālai jināti ajinīti vā jinau| pakaṭṭhabhāvaṃ padhānabhāvaṃ vā uddhaṃ gacchatīti puṅgavau| paupubba gamudhātu gatiyaṃ avapac‍cayau| niggahitā gamau| tassa kavaggantaṃ dhātvantalaupañ‍ca yadādinā katvā rūpasiddhi vaiditabbā| jinau ca sau puṅgavau cāti jinapuṅgavau, buddhau| sau pana gun‍naṃ viya buddhānaṃ sīlādikai appamaiyyaguṇai ajānantānaṃ anamantānaṃ agāravantānaṃ avandantānaṃ añ‍ña diṭṭhigatikānaṃ bālānaṃ asatthaina pāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina ajinīti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ jinapuṅgavanti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| tahiṃ tahiṃ tasmiṃ tasmiṃ bhavai, attabhāvai vā, pāramisañ‍cayaṃ dānādipāramīnaṃ samūhaṃ, cayaṃ upacitaṃ parisambhūtaṃ, sabbhi santaihi sappurisaihi, gataṃ upagamitabbaṃ payirupāsitabbaṃ, sukhappadaṃ ac‍cantasukhassa patiṭṭhāna bhūtaṃ, padaṃ nibbānaṃ gataṃ pattaṃ agamiṃ, narānarānaṃ manussadaivānaṃ, sukhasambhavaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasukhassa kāraṇaṃ tividha sukhassa labhanakāraṇabhūtaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ vā bhavaṃ nibbattaintaṃ, anamānaṃ anamantānaṃ anādarānaṃ avandantānaṃ vā, gavaṃ gauṇasadisānaṃ bālānaṃ jinapuṅgavaṃ jayantapurisuttamaṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ namai namāmīti|

navamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

10.

 maggaṅganāvaṃ  munidakkhanāvikau,

īhāphiyaṃ ñāṇakaraina gāhakau|

āruyha yau tāya bahū bhavaṇṇavā,

tāraisi taṃ buddhamaghappahaṃ namai||

10. aivaṃ navamagāthāya buddhaṃ natvā idāni bahū sattai tibhavasaṅkhātā saṃsārasamuddatau tāraintaina guṇaina ca sattānaṃ dukkhappahānaina guṇaina ca thaumitvā buddhaṃ vanditu kāmau maggaṅganāvantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana tata jara gaṇaihi racitattā dvādasakkharaihi yuttā indavaṃsa gāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘sāyindavaṃsā khalu yattha tā jarā’’ti|

tassatthau| yattha yassaṃ gāthāyaṃ tā tagaṇadvayañ‍ca jarā jagaṇa ragaṇā ca cai siyuṃ, sā gāthā khalu aikantaṃ indavaṃsagāthā nāmāti|

tattha yau munidakkhanāvikauti sambandhau| yau buddha saṅkhātau chaikau nāvājaiṭṭhakauti atthau| tattha munīti ubhau laukai munāti jānātīti muni, bhagavā| munadhātu ñāṇai i| atha vā maunaṃ vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ, taṃ assa atthīti muni, bhagavā| assathyatthai ipac‍cayau| vuttañhi aikanipātai vaṇṇapathajātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘munīti maunaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ| kāya maunaiyyādīsu vā añ‍ñataraina saman‍nāgatattā puggalau munīti vuc‍cati| sau panaisa agāriyamuni, anagāriyamuni, saikkhamuni, asaikkhamuni, pac‍caikamuni, munimunīti anaikavidhau| tattha agāriyamunīti gihi āgataphalau viñ‍ñātasāsanau| anagāriyamunīti tathārūpauva pabbajitau| saikkhamunīti sattasaikkhā| asaikkhamunīti khīṇāsavau| pac‍caikamunīti pac‍caikasambuddhau| munimunīti sammāsambuddhau’’ti| idha pana munimuni adhippaitau|

dakkhauti  chaikau| samuddataraṇai dakkhati kusalattaṃ gacchatīti dakkhau, nāvikau| dakkhadhātu gatiyaṃ a| atha vā dakkhati kusalakammai añ‍ñasmiñ‍ca kic‍cākic‍cai adandhatāya sīghaṃ gacchatīti dakkhau, yaukauci kusalau puggalau| dakkhadhātu sīghatthai a| dakkhau viyāti dakkhau, buddhau| nītatthaina pana buddhau sattānaṃ saṃsārasamuddatau uddhāraṇassa kāraṇaṃ dakkhati paricchindati ñāṇaināti dakkhauti vuc‍cati| nāvikauti samuddatīraṃ nāvāya taratīti nāvikau, nāvājaiṭṭhakau| taratitaddhitai ṇikau| nāvikau viyāti nāvikau, buddhau| nītatthaina pana buddhau nibbānaṃ pāraṃ aṭṭhamaggaṅganāvāya taratīti nāvikauti vuc‍cati| dakkhau nāvikau dakkhanāvikau, muni ca sau dakkhanāvikaucāti munidakkhanāvikau, kammadhārayasamāsauyaṃ| atha vā dakkhanāvikau viya dakkhau munīti munidakkhanāvikau, buddhau| upamākammadhārayasamāsauyaṃ| yathā hi samuddataraṇai chaikau nāvikau manussai samuddapāraṃ naiti, aivaṃ buddhau vainaiyyasattai nibbānapāraṃ naitīti vuttaṃ hauti|

maggaṅganāvaṃ āruyhāti sambandhau| maggaṅganāvanti aṭṭhamaggaṅga saṅkhātaṃ nāvaṃ| āruyhāti ārauhitvāti atthau| kilaisai māraintau ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina nibbānaṃ gacchatīti maggau| mārasaddūpapada gamudhātu k‍vi| mārainta gauti vattabbai niruttinayaina maggauti vuttaṃ| atha vā nibbānaṃ maggati gavaisatīti maggau| maggadhātu gavaisanai a| nibbānatthikaihi maggiyati gavaisiyatīti vā maggau| aikantatau jāti jarābyādhimaraṇadukkhādīhi pīḷitaihi sattaihi dukkhakkhayaṃ nibbānaṃ pāpuṇatthāya maggitabbau gavaisitabbauti maggau, catumaggau| vuttañhi ‘‘maggauti kainaṭṭhaina nibbānaṃ magganaṭṭhaina nibbānatthikaihi magganīyaṭṭhaina cā’’ti| aṅgati gacchati phalanibbānaṃ aitaināti aṅgaṃ| agidhātu gamanai a| maggassa aṅgaṃ kāraṇanti maggaṅgaṃ, sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappa sammāvācā sammākammanta sammāājīva sammāvāyāma  sammāsati sammāsamādhivasaina aṭṭhamaggaṅgaṃ labbhati| nuyati thavīyatīti nāvā| nudhātu thaviyaṃ avau| atha vā auratau pāraṃ naitīti nāvā, bhāraṃ nayanti aitāyāti vā nāvā| nidhātu nayanai avau| maggaṅgasaṅkhātā nāvā maggaṅganāvā| atha vā nāvā viya maggaṅganti maggaṅga nāvā| yathā hi nāvā attani āruḷhai manussai samudda nadīpāraṃ naiti, aivaṃ aṭṭhamaggaṅgadhammā attānaṃ paṭipaj‍jantai nibbāna pāraṃ naintīti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ maggaṅganāvaṃ|

īhāphiyanti vīriyasaṅkhātaṃ dabbisārakaṃ| vuttañhi suttani pātaaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘phiyaināti dabbisārakainā’’ti| īhati ārabbhatīti īhā| īhati vāyamati caitayati aitaināti vā īhā, vīriyaṃ| saddanītiyaṃ pana ‘‘īha caitāya’’nti vuttaṃ| phāti vaḍḍhaiti udakaṃ aitaināti phiyaṃ| phādhātu vaḍḍhiyaṃ iyau| phāti ghaṭṭaiti udakanti vā phiyaṃ| atha vā phāyati gacchati nāvaṃ aitaināti phiyaṃ| dabbisārakaṃ| phāyi dhātu gatiyaṃ a| ākārassa i| īhāsaṅkhātaṃ phiyanti īhāphiyaṃ| phiyaṃ viya īhāti īhāphiyaṃ| yathā hi phiyaina nāvā icchitaṭṭhānaṃ gacchati, aivaṃ īhāsaṅkhātaina vīriyaina taṃ taṃ kic‍caṃ sādhaiti, icchitaṃ nibbānaṭṭhānaṃ gacchatīti adhippāyau| taṃ pana gāhakauti padai kammaṃ| ñāṇakaraināti sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasaṅkhātaina hatthaina| sabbaṃ ñaiyyadhammaṃ jānātīti ñāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| taṃ taṃ kammaṃ karautīti karau, hatthau| ñāṇasaṅkhātau karau ñāṇa karau, karaṃ viya ñāṇanti vā ñāṇakarau, taina ñāṇakaraina| gāhakauti daḷhagaṇhamānau hutvā| tāyāti aṭṭhamaggaṅga saṅkhātāya tāya nāvāya, bahūti anaikai vainaiyyasattai| bhavaṇṇavāti tibhavasaṅkhātā saṃsāravaṭṭasaṅkhātā vā mahāsamuddatau| tassa pana vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva|

tāraisīti autiṇṇaisi, atik‍kamaisīti atthau| buddhanti paṭividdhacatusac‍cadhammaṃ| aghappahanti sattānaṃ dukkhappaja hantaṃ| dukkhaviddhaṃsaintanti atthau| atha vā aghappahanti sattānaṃ kilaisappajahantaṃ| kilaisaviddhaṃsaintanti atthau| aittha hi aghasaddau dukkhai kilaisai ca vattati| tattha hi dukkhaṃ aghayati dukkhayatīti aghanti ca, aghayati dukkhākāraina pavattatīti vā aghanti ca vuc‍cati| kilaisau pana aghayanti pāpaṃ karaunti sattā aitaināti aghauti vuc‍cati| aghadhātu pāpakaraṇai a| aghaṃ pajahati viddhaṃsaitīti aghappahau, buddhau| aghasaddūpapadapapubbahādhātu cāgai| buddhau vainaiyyasattānaṃ dukkhañ‍ca kilaisañ‍ca dhammadaisanāya apanaitīti atthau| aittha ca paṭhamavikappai dukkhassa appahātabbadhammattā maggaina appahātabbampi phalūpacāraina dukkhappajahantanti vuttaṃ| tanti munidakkhanāvikaṃ ahaṃ namai namāmīti sambandhauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| yau munidakkhanāvikau yau buddhasaṅkhātau chaikau nāvājaiṭṭhakau, maggaṅganāvaṃ aṭṭhamaggaṅgasaṅkhātaṃ nāvaṃ āruyha ārauhitvā īhāphiyaṃ vīriyasaṅkhātaṃ dabbisārakaṃ, ñāṇakaraina sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasaṅkhātaina hatthaina, gāhakau daḷhaṃ gaṇhamānau hutvā tāya aṭṭhamaggaṅganāvāya, bahū anaikai vainaiyyasattai bhavaṇṇavā tibhavasaṅkhātamahāsamuddatau, tāraisi autāraisi, buddhaṃ paṭividdhacatusac‍cadhammaṃ aghappahaṃ sattānaṃ dukkhaviddhaṃsaintaṃ kilaisa viddhaṃsaintaṃ vā taṃ munidakkhanāvikaṃ ahaṃ namai namāmīti|

dasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

11.

 samatiṃsatipāramisambharaṇaṃ,

varabaudhidumai catusac‍cadasaṃ|

varamiddhigataṃ naradaivahitaṃ,

tibhavūpasamaṃ paṇamāmi jinaṃ||

11. aivaṃ  dasamagāthāya buddhaṃ vanditvā idāni samatiṃ satipāramisambharaṇādīhi pañ‍cahi guṇaihi thaumitvā jinaṃ vanditukāmau samatiṃsatipāramisambharaṇantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana catūhi sagaṇaihi racitattā dvādasakkharaihi yuttā tauṭakagāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘iha tauṭaka mambudhisaihi mita’’nti| tassatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ ambudhisaihi catūhi sagaṇaihi mitaṃ pamitaṃ racitaṃ lakkhitaṃ vā vuttaṃ idha jagatichandai tauṭakaṃ nāmāti|

tattha pana samatiṃsatipāramisambharaṇanti dasapāramiyau, dasa upapāramiyau, dasa paramatthapāramiyau cāti samatiṃsapāramiyau sambhūtaṃ, paripūritanti atthau| tāsaṃ pana visaisau pāramisaddassa ca vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| sambharittha paripūrayitthāti sambharaṇau, jinau| saṃpubbabharadhātu pūraṇai yu| samatiṃsapāramiyau sambharaṇauti samatiṃsatipāramisambharaṇau, jinau| dīpaṅkarabuddhassa pādamūlai laddhabyākaraṇatau paṭṭhāya kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni samatiṃsapāramiyau pūrayitthāti atthau| taṃ samatiṃsatipāramisambharaṇaṃ| varabaudhidumaiti uttamabaudhirukkhamūlai| aittha ca varabaudhisaddassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| davati gacchati mūlakhandhatacasākha viṭapa pal‍lava phalaihi vuḍḍhiṃ virūḷiṃ vaipul‍laṃ rajata suvaṇṇamaṇi santā rucīhi atisaubhanaṃ vā pāpuṇātīti dumau, baudhisattassa vijātadivasaiyaiva baudhisattaina saddhiṃ saha jātau satahatthubbaidhau asatthadumarājā labbhati| dudhātu gatimhi amau| apadānaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘dunāti kampatīti dumau| dumati pūraiti ākāsatalanti vā dumau’’ti vuttaṃ| varau baudhi varabaudhi, varabaudhi ca sau dumau cāti varabaudhidumau, tasmiṃ|

catusac‍cadasanti cattāri ariyasac‍cāni maggañāṇaina paṭivijjhitaṃ| aittha ca sac‍casaddau anaikaisvatthaisu dissati| saiyyathidaṃ| sac‍caṃ  bhaṇai na kujjhaiyyātiādīsu vācāsac‍cai| sac‍cai ṭhitā samaṇabrāhmaṇātiādīsu viratisac‍cai| kasmā nu sac‍cāni vadanti nānā pavādiyāsai kusalāvadānātiādīsu diṭṭhisac‍cai| aikañhi sac‍caṃ na dutiyamatthītiādīsu paramatthasac‍cai nibbānai caiva maggai ca, catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ kati kusalātiādīsu ariyasac‍cai| svāyamidhāpi ariyasac‍cai vattatīti vaiditabbau| sathanaṃ sac‍caṃ, avitathaṃ| sathadhātu avitathai ṇyau| yathā hi aggīnaṃ sabhāvau aikantaṃ uṇhauyaiva hauti, na sītalau, aivaṃ dukkhādīnaṃ pīḷanādi sabhāvaṃ aikantaṃ sac‍caṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ anañ‍ñathaṃ hauti| taṃ pana catubbidhaṃ hauti dukkhasac‍caṃ, samudayasac‍caṃ, niraudhasac‍caṃ, maggasac‍cañ‍cāti| tattha dukkhasac‍canti dukkhacchitaṃ khaṃ tucchanti dukkhaṃ| kammadhārayasamāsauyaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ sac‍cavibhaṅgaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘duiti ayaṃsaddau kucchitai dissati| kucchitañhi puttaṃ duputtauti vadanti| khaṃsaddau pana tucchai| tucchañhi ākāsaṃ khanti vuc‍cati| idañ‍ca paṭhamasac‍caṃ kucchitaṃ| anaikaupaddavādhiṭṭhānatau, tucchaṃbālajanaparikappitadhuvasubhasukhattabhāvavirahitatau| tasmā kucchitattā tucchattā ca dukkhanti vuc‍catī’’ti| dukkhañ‍ca taṃ sac‍cañ‍cāti dukkhasac‍caṃ| taibhūmikavaṭṭaṃ labbhati|

samudayasac‍canti ayati pavattati dukkhaphalaṃ aitaināti ayau, laubhau| ayadhātu gatimhi a| saṃ avasaisa pac‍cayasaṃyaugai sati u dukkhuppattiyā ayau kāraṇauti samudayau| chaṭṭhītappurisasamāsauyaṃ| vuttaiñhaitaṃ sac‍ca vibhaṅgaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘saṃiti ca ayaṃsaddau samāgamau samaitantiādīsu saṃyaugaṃ dīpaiti| uiti ayaṃsaddau uppan‍naṃ uditantiādīsu uppattiṃ| ayasaddau pana kāraṇaṃ dīpaiti| idañ‍cāpi dutiyasac‍caṃ avasaisapac‍cayasamāyaugai sati dukkhassuppattikāraṇaṃ| iti dukkhassa saṃyaugai uppattikāraṇattā dukkhasamudayanti vuc‍catī’’ti| samudayau ca sau sac‍cañ‍cāti samudayasac‍caṃ, laubhasaṅkhātā taṇhā labbhati| niraudhasac‍canti raudhati carati pavattatīti raudhau, taibhūmikavaṭṭadhammau| raudhadhātu caraṇai ṇa| natthi taibhūmikavaṭṭadhammassa raudhau caraṇaṃ aitthāti niraudhau, nibbānaṃ| atha vā nirujjhati saṃsāradukkhaṃ aitthāti niraudhau, nibbānaṃ| rudhidhātu āvaraṇai ṇa| vuttañhaitaṃ sac‍cavibhaṅgaṭṭha kathāyaṃ ‘‘tatiyasac‍caṃ pana yasmā nisaddau abhāvaṃ| raudha saddau cārakaṃ dīpaiti| tasmā abhāvau aittha saṃsāra cārakasaṅkhātassa dukkharaudhassa sabbagatisuñ‍ñattā| samadhi gatai vā tasmiṃ saṃsāracārakasaṅkhātassa dukkharaudhassa abhāvau hauti tappaṭipakkhattātipi dukkhaniraudhanti vuc‍catī’’ti| dukkhassa vā anuppādaniraudhapac‍cayattā dukkhaniraudhanti| niraudhau ca sau sac‍cañ‍cāti niraudhasac‍caṃ| nibbānaṃ| maggasac‍canti dukkhaniraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ maggati gacchati aitaināti maggau| aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| maggadhātu gatiyaṃ a| vuttañhaitaṃ sac‍ca vibhaṅgaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘catutthasac‍caṃ pana yasmā aitaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ gacchati ārammaṇavasaina tadabhimukhabhūtattā paṭipadā ca hauti dukkhaniraudhappattiyā| tasmā dukkhaniraudhagāminipaṭipadāti vuc‍catī’’ti| maggau ca sau sac‍cañ‍cāti maggasac‍caṃ| aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau labbhati| yasmā panaitāni buddhādayau ariyā paṭivijjhanti, tasmā ariyasac‍cānītipi vuc‍canti| atha vā buddhādīhi ariyaihi paṭivijjhiyanti, tasmā ariyasac‍cānītipi vuc‍canti|

taisaṃ pana lakkhaṇādīni aivaṃ vaiditabbāni| aittha hi bādhanalakkhaṇaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, santāpanarasaṃ, pavattipac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ, pabhavalakkhaṇaṃ samudayasac‍caṃ, anupacchaidakaraṇarasaṃ, palibaudhapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| santilakkhaṇaṃ niraudhasac‍caṃ, ac‍cutirasaṃ, animittapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| niyyānalakkhaṇaṃ maggasac‍caṃ, kilaisappahāna karaṇarasaṃ, vuṭṭhānapac‍cupaṭṭhānanti| aitaisu pana catūsu sac‍caisu paṭhamatatiyasac‍cāni phalāni haunti| dutiya catutthasac‍cāni haitūni haunti| purimāni dvaisac‍cāni dudda sattā gambhīrāni| pacchimāni dvaisac‍cāni gambhīrattā duddasāni|

api  ca khau pana dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ gambhīrañ‍caiva duddasañ‍cāti vaiditabbaṃ| aikaikassa pana sac‍cassa catun‍naṃ catun‍naṃ atthānaṃ vasaina sauḷasa sac‍caṭṭhā haunti| taina vuttaṃ paṭisambhidā maggapāḷiyaṃ ‘‘dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau saṅkhataṭṭhau santāpaṭṭhau vipariṇāmaṭṭhau, imai cattārau dukkhassa dukkhaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| aivaṃ dukkhaṃ tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| samudayassa āyūhanaṭṭhau nidānaṭṭhau saṃyaugaṭṭhau palibaudhaṭṭhau, imai cattārau samudayassa samudayaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā| aivaṃ samudayau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| niraudhassa nissaraṇaṭṭhau vivaikaṭṭhau asaṅkhataṭṭhau amataṭṭhau, imai cattārau niraudhassa niraudhaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā, aivaṃ niraudhau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍caṃ| maggassa niyyānaṭṭhau haitvaṭṭhau dassanaṭṭhau adhipataiyyaṭṭhau, imai cattārau maggassa maggaṭṭhā tathā avitathā anañ‍ñathā, aivaṃ maggau tathaṭṭhaina sac‍ca’’nti|

dassanaṃ dasaṃ, disati passatīti vā dasaṃ, catumaggañāṇaṃ| disadhātu apaikkhanai a| catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ dassanaṃ assāti catusac‍cadasau, jinau| atha vā cattāri sac‍cāni adassi apassīti catusac‍cadasau, jinau| sau cattāri sac‍cāni catumaggañāṇaina appaṭivijjhīti atthau| taṃ catusac‍cadasaṃ| catusac‍cañāṇaṃ pana duvidhaṃ hauti anubaudhañāṇañ‍ca, paṭivaidha ñāṇañ‍cāti| tattha anubaudhañāṇaṃ laukiyaṃ anussavādivasaina niraudhai maggai ca pavattati, paṭivaidhañāṇaṃ laukuttaraṃ niraudhamārammaṇaṃ katvā kic‍catau cattāripi sac‍cāni paṭivijjhati| yathāha yau bhikkhavai dukkhaṃ passati, dukkhasamudayampi sau passati| dukkhaniraudhampi passati, dukkhaniraudhagāminipaṭi padampi passatīti sabbaṃ vattabbaṃ| catusac‍capaṭivaidhañāṇaṃ pana anubaudhañāṇaina vinā na pavattati, tasmā anubaudha ñāṇatthāya pubbai catusac‍cakammaṭṭhānaṃ paṇḍitaina samārambhi tabbanti| nanu ca ariyasāvakā cattāri sac‍cāni paṭivijjhanti| atha kasmā jinauyaiva catusac‍cadasanti thaumitauti| bhagavā pana ariyasāvakaihi visiṭṭhaina anañ‍ñaupadaisaina arahattamaggañāṇaina cattāri sac‍cāni paṭivijjhanti| tasmā bhagavāyaiva catusac‍cadasanti abhitthavitauti| tattha pana ṭhapaitvā taṇhañ‍ca nibbānañ‍ca avasaisadhammā dukkhasac‍caṃ nāma| aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritā samudayasac‍caṃ nāma| nibbānaṃ niraudha sac‍caṃ nāma| aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau maggasac‍caṃ nāma| vuttaṃ pana abhidhammatthasaṅgahai –

‘‘dukkhaṃ taibhūmakaṃ vaṭṭaṃ, taṇhā samudayau bhavai|

niraudhau nāma nibbānaṃ, maggau laukuttarau matau’’ti||

varamiddhigatanti varaṃ iddhigataṃ| uttamaṃ atītabuddhānaṃ iddhiṃ pattanti atthau| atha vā uttamānaṃ iddhīnaṃ patiṭṭhānabhūtanti atthau| ijjhanaṃ samijjhanaṃ iddhi, ijjhati samijjhati nippaj‍jatīti vā iddhi| iddhividhañāṇañ‍ca arahattamaggau ca labbhati| vuttañhi visuddhimaggai ‘‘tathā naikkhammaṃ ijjhatīti iddhīti ca arahattamaggau ijjhatīti iddhī’’ti ca| atha vā ijjhanti sattā aitāya iddhā vuddhā uk‍kaṃsagatā hauntīti iddhi, aṭṭhasamāpattiyau ca cattārau maggā ca labbhanti| idhadhātu vuḍḍhiyanti pac‍cayau| atha vā tapac‍cayau itthiliṅgajautakau īpac‍cayau ca| iddhiṃ agaminti iddhigatau, jinau| atha vā gacchati patiṭṭhahati aitthāti gatau, varānaṃ uttamānaṃ iddhīnaṃ gatau patiṭṭhānabhūtauti varamiddhigatau, jinau| taṃ varamiddhigataṃ| kiñ‍cāpi pana ariyasāvakā iddhippattā haunti, buddhā pana visaisatau aitāya abhiñ‍ñāya iddhā samiddhā vuddhā atiuk‍kaṃsagatā haunti, tasmā buddhauyaiva varamiddhigatanti abhitthavituṃ arahatīti vaiditabbau|

naradaivahitanti manussadaivānaṃ laukiyalaukuttara saṅkhātaṃ hitaṃ payaujanaṃ dhāraṇaṃ| taisaṃ pana atthau haiṭṭhā  vuttauyaiva| tibhavūpasamanti tibhavaupasamaṃ| punabbhavābhinibbattiyā abhāvatau kāmarūpaarūpasaṅkhātaihi tīhi bhavaihi tīsu bhavaisu punabbhavābhinibbattitau vā upasamitaṃ nibbutaṃ niruddhanti atthau| bhavanti sattā aitthāti bhavau, tayau bhavā tibhavā, taihi upasamitthāti tibhavūpasamau, jinau| taṃ tibhavūpasamaṃ| bhagavatau pana baudhipal‍laṅkaiva kāmarūpārūpasaṅkhātaisu tīsu bhavaisu paṭisandhijanakānaṃ avij‍jātaṇhāmūlakānaṃ puñ‍ñādisaṅkhārānaṃ arahattamaggañāṇaina niravasaisaṃ pahīnattā punabbhavābhinibbatti natthi| tasmā bhagavantaṃyaiva visaisaina tibhavūpasamanti thaumaitīti vuttaṃ hauti| jinanti pañ‍camārajayantaṃ buddhaṃ| paṇamāmīti ahaṃ ñāṇasampayuttacittaina ādaraṃ vandāmīti yaujanā|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| samatiṃsati sambharaṇaṃ samatiṃsapāramiyau sambhūtaṃ paripūritaṃ, varabaudhidumai uttamabaudhirukkhamūlai, catusac‍cadasaṃ cattāri ariyasac‍cāni maggañāṇaina passantaṃ paṭivijjhantaṃ, varamiddhigataṃ uttamaṃ atīta buddhānaṃ iddhipattaṃ uttamānaṃ iddhīnaṃ patiṭṭhānabhūtaṃ vā, naradaivahitaṃ manussadaivānaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasaṅkhātaṃ payaujanaṃ dhāraṇaṃ, tibhavūpasamaṃ kāmarūpaarūpasaṅkhātaihi tīhi bhavaihi upasamitaṃ, kāmarūpārūpasaṅkhātaisu tīsu bhavaisu punabbhavā bhinibbattitau upasamitaṃ nibbutaṃ vā, jinaṃ pañ‍camārajayantaṃ buddhaṃ paṇamāmi ahaṃ ñāṇasampayuttacittaina ādaraṃ vandāmīti|

aikādasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

12.

 satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikaṃ virajaṃ,

gaganūpamadhiṃ dhitimairusamaṃ|

jalajūpamasītalasīlayutaṃ,

pathavīsahanaṃ paṇamāmi jinaṃ||

12. aivaṃ  aikādasamagāthāya jinaṃ vanditvā idāni satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikantyādīhi chahi guṇaihi thaumitvā jinaṃ vanditu kāmau satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana catūhi sa gaṇaihi racitattā dvādasakkharaihi yuttā tauṭakagāthāti daṭṭhabbā| sādhakaṃ pana anantaragāthāvaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttamaiva|

tattha satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikanti anaikapāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina jātaina dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaina sampan‍naṃ| aittha ca satasaddau anaikasaṅkhyāvācakau| satataijau sahassaraṃ sītiādīsu viya| sataina anaikaina pāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina jātanti satapuñ‍ñajaṃ, lakkhiyati lakkhitabbaṃ buddhadabbaṃ aitaihīti lakkhaṇaṃ, dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇādi| lakkhadhātu dassanaṅkaisuyu| satapuñ‍ñajañ‍ca taṃ lakkhaṇañ‍cāti satapuñ‍ñaja lakkhaṇaṃ| taṃ assa atthīti satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikau, jinau| taṃ| bhagavā hi anantaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu sabbai sattā aikaikaṃ puñ‍ñakammaṃ satakkhattuṃ karaiyyuṃ| aittakaihi janaihi kataṃ puñ‍ñakammaṃ baudhisattau aikauva aikaikaṃ sataguṇaṃ katvā nibbattaina pāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina jātaihi dvattiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi ca asītianubyañ‍jana lakkhaṇai hi ca sampan‍nauti satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikanti thaumaitīti adhippāyau| vuttañhi buddhavaṃsaṭṭhakathāyaṃ –

‘‘satapuñ‍ña lakkhaṇauti anantaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu sabbai sattā aikaikaṃ puñ‍ñakammaṃ satakkhattuṃ karaiyyuṃ, aittakaihi janaihi katakammaṃ baudhisattau sayamaiva aikau sataguṇaṃ katvā nibbattau| tasmā satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇauti vuc‍cati| kaici pana sataina sataina puñ‍ñakammaina nibbattaṃ aikaikalakkhaṇauti vadanti| aivaṃ santai yau kauci buddhau bhavaiyyāti| taṃ aṭṭhakathāsu paṭikkhitta’’nti| pāthaiyyaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘sata puñ‍ñalakkhaṇanti sataina sataina puñ‍ñakammaina nibbattaṃ aikaikaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ| aivaṃ santai yau kauci buddhau bhavaiyyāti na raucayiṃsu| anantaisu pana cak‍kavāḷaisu sabbai sattā aikaikaṃ kammaṃ satakkhattuṃ karaiyyuṃ| aittakaihi janaihi kataṃ kammaṃ baudhisattau aikauva aikaikaṃ sataguṇaṃ katvā nibbattau| tasmā satapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇauti imamatthaṃ raucayiṃsū’’ti vuttaṃ| dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ pana upari āvibhavissatīti|

virajanti rāgarajādīnaṃ abhāvā vigatarajaṃ| rañ‍jati sañ‍jati rūpādiārammaṇaisūti rajau, rāgādirajau| ranjadhātu rāgai a| vigatau rajau assāti virajau, jinau| taṃ virajaṃ| jinau hi baudhipal‍laṅkaiyaiva sabbakilaisamāra rajassa jayitattā vigatarajauti atthau| gaganūpamadhinti aittha dhī vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| tasmā ākāsūpamasabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇavantanti atthau| gacchanti aittha vihaṅgamādayauti gaganaṃ, ākāsau| gamudhātu gatiyaṃ yu| makārassa gau| upamiyatai upamā| bhāvasādhanauyaṃ| upapubbamādhātu parimānai a| sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ dharati jānātīti dhī, sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇaṃ| dharadhātu ñāṇai k‍vi, itthiliṅgajautakaīpac‍cayau ca| gaganaina ākāsaina yuttā upamā gaganūpamā, taṃ sadisaṃ dhī sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ yassāti gaganūpamadhi, jinau| taṃ gaganūpamadhiṃ| ayaṃ pana anantādiguṇahīnattā dhammahīnaupamā nāma| vuttañhi subaudhālaṅkārai ‘‘dhammahīnāmukhambhauja, sadisaṃ muninau itī’’ti| yathā hi ākāsau anantauti vuttattā ākāsau anantau hutvā vittharati, aivaṃ bhagavatau anantaṃ buddhañāṇanti vuttattā sabbañ‍ñutañāṇassa anantārammaṇattā ca ñāṇaṃ anantaṃ hutvā vittharati| tasmā gaganūpamadhinti thaumaitīti|

dhitimairusamanti aittha dhiti vuc‍cati samādhi| sinairupabbata sadisaṃ acalasamādhivantanti atthau| dhiyati ṭhapiyati sampayuttadhammai  aikārammaṇai aitaināti dhiti, bhagavatau laukiyalaukuttarajhānasamādhi| dhidhātu dhāraṇaiti| raṃsiyā andhakāraṃ mināti hiṃsatīti mairu, pabbatarājā| mīdhātu hiṃsāyaṃ rupac‍cayau| imaṃ vacanatthañhi sinairussa pācīnapassaṃ rajatamayaṃ, dakkhiṇapassaṃ maṇimayaṃ, pacchimapassaṃ phal‍likamayaṃ, uttarapassaṃ suvaṇṇamayaṃ hautīti vuttavacanaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| atha vā mināti hiṃsati sabbai pabbatai attanau uc‍cataraṭṭhaināti mairu, mairunā samau dhiti samādhi yassāti dhitimairusamau, jinau| taṃ dhitimairu samaṃ| yathā hi mairurājā aṭṭhahi disāhi āgataihi vātaihi na kampati na calati, aivaṃ jinau kāmacchandādinīvaraṇai pajahitvā laukuttarajjhānasamādhinā saman‍nāgatattā aṭṭhahi laukadhammaihi ca micchādiṭṭhivātaihi ca na kampati na calatīti dhitimairusamanti thaumaiti|

jalajūpamasītalasīlayutanti udakajātappadumasadisaina atisītalaina asaṅkhyaiyyasīlaina saṃyuttaṃ| vuttañhi apadāna pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘sīlaṃ tassa asaṅkhyaiyya’’nti| tadaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘bhagavatau pana sīlaṃ asaṅkhyaiyyamaivā’’ti| jalati dibbatīti jalaṃ, udakaṃ| jaladhātu dittiyaṃ a| jalai udakai jātaṃ jalajaṃ, padumaṃ| jalajaina padumaina upamaṃ sadisanti jalajūpamaṃ, sīlaṃ| sukhatthikaihi saiviyatīti sītalaṃ, sīlaṃ| sidhātu saivāyaṃ talapac‍cayau| atha vā sītaguṇaṃ lāti gaṇhātīti sītalaṃ, sīlaṃ| sītasaddūpapada lādhātu gahaṇai k‍vi| udakai pana jātaṃ padumaṃ udakaina uṇhassa samitattā sītalaṃ viya| sīlaṃ pana buddhassa kilaisatā panassa samaitattā ac‍cantasītalaṃ hauti| surakkhitañhi visuddhaṃ sīlaṃ kilaisappahānaina sattānaṃ kilaisapariḷāhaṃ upasamaiti na haritacandanaṃ muttāhārau maṇi udakaṃ vātau candakiraṇau ca| tasmā sīlamaiva sītalanti ac‍cantasītalanti vuc‍cati| taina vuttaṃ visuddhimaggai –

‘‘na  taṃ sajaladā vātā, na cāpi haricandanaṃ|

naiva hārā na maṇayau, na candakiraṇaṅkurā|

samayantīdha sattānaṃ, pariḷāhaṃ surakkhitaṃ|

yaṃ samaitidaṃ ariyaṃ, sīlaṃ ac‍cantasītala’’nti||

ayaṃ pana tassa gāthādvayassatthau| surakkhitaṃ suṭṭhurakkhitaṃ ariyaṃ visuddhaṃ ac‍cantasītalaṃ idaṃ sīlaṃ idhalaukai sattānaṃ yaṃ pariḷāhaṃ samaiti, taṃ pariḷāhaṃ sajaladā udakadadaina maighaina saha pavattā vātā sītavātā na samayanti haritacandanañ‍cāpi na samayati| hārā muttāhārā naiva samayanti| maṇayau vaiḷuriyādimaṇiyau na samayanti| candakiraṇaṅkurā candassa raṃsiaṅkurā na samayantīti| sīlati samādhiyati kāyakammādīnaṃ susīlyavasaina na vippakiratīti sīlaṃ| sīladhātu samādhimhi a| atha vā sīlanti samādahanti cittaṃ aitaināti sīlaṃ, sīlainti vā aitaina kusalai dhammai upadhārainti ābhusau dhārainti sādhavauti sīlaṃ, caitanādhammau, kusalacittuppādau vā labbhati| sīladhātu upadhāraṇai a| vuttañhaitaṃ visuddhimaggaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘kainaṭṭhaina sīlanti sīlanaṭṭhaina sīlaṃ| kimidaṃ sīlaṃ nāma, samādhānaṃ vā, kāyakammādīnaṃ susīlyavasaina avippakiṇṇatāti atthau| upadhāraṇaṃ vā, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ patiṭṭhānavasaina ādhārabhāvauti atthau’’ti| jalajūpamaina padumasadisaina sītalaina ac‍cantasītalaina sīlaina asaṅkhyaiyyasīlaina yuttauti jalajūpamasītalasīlayutau, jinau| taṃ jalajūpamasītalasīlayutaṃ| yathā hi udakai jātaṃ padumaṃ jalaina sītalaṃ hauti, aivaṃ bhagavatau maggasīlaina kilaisupatāpanassa pahīnattā taṃ sītalaṃ ac‍cantasītalaṃ| taina sampayuttauti jalajūpamasītalasīla yutanti thaumaitīti vuttaṃ hauti|

pathavīsahananti pathavī viya khantisampan‍naṃ khantivantaṃ vā| pathanti patiṭṭhahanti aittha tarupabbatādayauti pathavī, bhūmi| pathadhātu patiṭṭhāyaṃ  vīpac‍cayau| atha vā puthu hutvā jātanti pathavī| jātataddhitatthai vīpac‍cayau| sahayatai khamiyatai sahanaṃ, sahati khamatīti vā sahanaṃ, adhivāsanakhanti| sahadhātu khantiyaṃ yu| pathavī viya sahanaṃ khamanaṃ yassa sauti pathavīsahanau, jinau| taṃ pathavīsahanaṃ| yathā hi pathavī attanau upari pakkhittaṃ sucimpi asucimpi sabbaṃ sahati, na paṭighānunayaṃ karauti, aivaṃ jinau iṭṭhārammaṇampi aniṭṭhārammaṇampi sabbaṃ sahati, na tattha paṭighānunayaṃ karautīti pathavīsahananti thaumaitīti adhippāyau| taina vuttaṃ apadānapāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘lābhālābhai na saj‍janti, sammānanavimānanai|

pathavīsadisā buddhā, tasmā tai na virādhiyā’’ti||

ahaṃ jinaṃ paṇamāmi vandāmīti sambandhau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| satapuñ‍ñajalakkhaṇikaṃ anaikapāramīpuñ‍ñataijaina jātaina dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaina sampan‍naṃ, virajaṃ rāgarajādīnaṃ abhāvā vigatarajaṃ, gaganūpamadhiṃ ākāsūpamasabbañ‍ñutañāṇavantaṃ, dhitimairusamaṃ sinairupabbatasadisaṃ acalasamādhivantaṃ, jalajūpamasītala sīlayutaṃ| udakajātappadumasadisaina ac‍cantaatisītalaina asaṅkhyaiyyasīlaina sampayuttaṃ, pathavīsahanaṃ pathavī viya khantisampan‍naṃ, khantivantaṃ vā, jinaṃ pañ‍camāravijayaṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ paṇamāmi vandāmīti|

dvādasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

13.

 yau buddhau sumati divai divākarauva,

saubhantau ratijananai silāsanamhi|

āsīnau sivasukhadaṃ adaisi dhammaṃ,

daivānaṃ tamasadisaṃ namāmi nic‍caṃ||

13. aivaṃ  dvādasamagāthāya jinaṃ vanditvā idāni tāvatiṃsapurai mātudaivaputtappamukhānaṃ upapattidaivānaṃ abhidhammaṃ daisaka guṇaina abhitthavitvā vanditukāmau yau buddhau sumatityādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ma na ja ra gaṇaihi ca aikagarunā ca tīhi yatīhi ca dasahi yatīhi ca racitattā tairasakkhara saṃyuttā pahassiṇīgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhaitaṃ vuttaudayai ‘‘mnā jarā gau tidasayatippahassiṇī sā’’ti|

tassatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ mnā magaṇa nagaṇā ca jarā ja gaṇa ragaṇā ca gau garu ca tidasayati tiyati ca dasayati ca cai siyā, sā gāthā pahassiṇīgāthā nāmāti| tattha sumati yau buddhau dhammaṃ daivānaṃ adaisīti sambandhau| yauti aniyamavācakasabbanāmapadaṃ| tassa vacanatthau na kātabbau| vuttañhi saddavidūhi –

‘‘ruḷhīkhyātaṃ nipātañ‍cu, pasaggālapanaṃ tathā|

sabbanāmanti aitaisu, na katau viggahau chasū’’ti||

atha vā sabbanāmabhāvaṃ yātīti yau, aniyamavācaka bhāvaṃ yātīti vā yau| sabbanāmabhāvaina yāti pavattatīti vā yau| aniyamavācakabhāvaina yāti pavattatīti vā yau| sabbanāmabhāvaina yātabbau ñātabbauti vā yau, yātabbau ñātabbau aitaina aniyamatthauti vā yau| sabbanāmasaṅkhātaṃ yaitipadaṃ labbhati| yau yādi sauti atthau| buddhauti paṭividdhacatusac‍cadhammau, paṭividdhasabbañaiyyadhammau vā| tassa pana vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| sumatīti sundarasabbañ‍ñutañāṇavātau, daisanāñāṇavātau vā| adaisi dhammaṃ daivānanti vuc‍camānattā hi idha sabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇaṃ sumatīti vuc‍cati| manati jānāti sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ daisaitabbadhammaṃ vāti mati| manadhātu ñāṇaiti| sundarā mati sumati, sabbañaiyyadhammānaṃ yathāsabhāvajānanasamatthaṃ taisaṃ daisai tabbappakārajānanasamatthaṃ  baudhaitabbapuggalānaṃ āsayādhi muttiyādivibhāvanasamatthañ‍cāti tisamatthasampan‍naṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ labbhati| sumatīti iminā hi sabbañ‍ñutañāṇamaiva abhidhammakathāya samuṭṭhānabhāvai samatthaṃ| nāñ‍ñanti imamatthaṃ dīpaintau abhidhammakathāya anañ‍ñasādhāraṇabhāvaṃ dassaiti| sundarā mati assa asmiṃ vā atthīti sumati, buddhau| samāsanta taddhitāyaṃ| tadassatthītivī cāti suttai casaddaina apac‍cayau| yathā pītisukhanti| vuttañhi pārājikaṇḍaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘ayañ‍ca pīti idañ‍ca sukhaṃ assa jhānassa asmiṃ vā jhānai atthīti idaṃ jhānaṃ pītisukhanti vuc‍catī’’ti|

divaiti divasai| sūriyaubhāsaina dibbati aitthāti divau| divudhātu jutiyaṃ a| tasmiṃ divai| divā karauvāti sūriyau iva| divādinaṃ karautīti divākarau, sūriyau| divāsaddūpa pada karadhātu karaṇai a| atha vā divā divasai karau raṃsi assāti divākarau| karasaddau hi raṃsivācakau| saubhantauti viraucamānau hutvā| subhati viraucati chabbaṇṇaraṃsiādīhīti saubhantau, buddhau| subhadhātu saubhai, dittiyaṃ vā antapac‍cayau| bhagavā hi chabbaṇṇaraṃsīhi dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi asītyānubyañ‍janalakkhaṇaihi ca daivānaṃ majjhai saubhati viraucatīti atthau| ratijananaiti daivānaṃ atappakapītisaumanassuppādanai, paimuppādanai vā| ramanaṃ rati| ramudhātu kīḷāyanti| taṃ janaiti uppādaitīti ratijananaṃ| silāsanaṃ| ratisaddūpapada janadhātu jananai yu| tasmiṃ ratijananai|

silāsanamhīti tidasālayai, pāricchattakamūlai jātai nikkhittai vā dīghatau saṭṭhiyaujanai vitthāratau paṇṇāsa yaujanai puthulatau pan‍narasayaujanai vaṇṇatau jayasumanapupphasadisai rattakampalavaṇṇai vā saṇhasukhumai paṇḍukampa lanāmakai silāsanai| sak‍kaina saiviyatīti silaṃ| pāsāṇau| sidhātu saivāyaṃ alapac‍cayau| āsiyati aitthāti āsanaṃ| atha vā āgantvā saiviyati aitthāti āsanaṃ| āpubbasidhātu saivāyaṃ yu| silaṃaiva āsanaṃ silāsanaṃ| paṇḍukampalasilāsanaṃ labbhati| tasmiṃ silāsanamhi| āsīnauti dasahi cak‍kavāḷasahassaihi āgamma san‍nisin‍nānaṃ daivānaṃ gaṇaina parivāritau hutvā nisīdantau| āsīdanaṃ nisaj‍janaṃ āsīnau| āsamantatau sīdati vasatīti vā āsīnau| abhidhammakathābhimukhaṃ sīdati nisaj‍jatīti vā āsīnau, buddhau| āpubbasīdadhātu nisaj‍jāyaṃ| visaraṇa gatyāvasānaisu vā tapac‍cayau| tassa īṇādaisau, ṇassanattaṃ, dhātvantalaupau| sivasukhadanti nibbānasukhadāyakaṃ| ariyaihi saiviyatīti sivaṃ, nibbānaṃ| sidhātu saivāyaṃ avau| atha vā rāgaggiādīhi samati upasamati aitthāti sivaṃ, nibbānaṃ| samudhātu upasamai rivau| abhidhānappadīpikaṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘sivaṃ khaimabhāvaṃ karautīti sivaṃ, saṃsārabhīrukaihi saivitabbattā vā sivaṃ| yadādinā vapac‍cayau’’ti vuttaṃ| sukhayatīti sukhaṃ| yassuppaj‍jati, taṃ sukhitaṃ karautīti atthau| atha vā suṭṭhu dukkhaṃ khanatīti sukhaṃ| supubba khanudhātu avadāraṇai k‍vi| sivassa nibbānassa sukhaṃ sivasukhaṃ, taṃ dadātīti sivasukhadaṃ, abhidhammadhammau| sivasukhasaddūpapadadādhātu a| yau hi abhidhammadhammaṃ suṇāti, sau tassa nibbānasukhaṃ dadātīti atthau|

adaisi dhammaṃ daivānanti daivānaṃ dhammaṃ adaisīti sambandhau| aittha ca dhammanti nānānayavicittaṃ gambhīraṃ saṇhasukhumaṃ duddasaṃ abhidhamma dhammaṃ| attanau sabhāvaṃ dhāraitīti dhammau, paṇḍitaihi dhāriyati na bālaihīti vā dhammau, abhidhammapiṭakaṃ| dharadhātu dhāraṇai rammapac‍cayau| taṃ dhammaṃ| daivānanti pubbai manussalaukai mātubhūtadaivaputtappamukhānaṃ upapattidaivānaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ aṭṭhasālinīaṭṭhakathāyaṃ –

‘‘mātaraṃ  pamukhaṃ katvā, tassā pañ‍ñāya taijasā|

abhidhammakathāmaggaṃ, daivānaṃ sampavattayī’’ti||

aittha ca mātarantipadassa pubbai manussalaukai mātubhūtaṃ daivaputtanti atthau daṭṭhabbau| vuttañhaitaṃ jinālaṅkārapāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘tatau puttaṃ gahaitvāna, gatā mātā sakaṃ gharaṃ|

sattamiyaṃ cutā gantvā, daivaputtattamāgamī’’ti||

taṭṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘baudhisattamātā gabbhavuṭṭhānatau sattamai divasai kālaṅkatvā tusitapurai daivaputtau hutvā nibbattī’’ti vuttaṃ| aittha ca daivaputtasaddaina vuttamaiva| kāḷudāyī thairagāthāaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘parivāritāti ca itthiliṅganiddaisau purimattabhāvasiddhaṃ itthibhāvaṃ daivatābhāvaṃ vā sandhāya katau| daivūpapatti pana purisabhāvaina jātā’’ti vuttaṃ| jātakaṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘daivaurauhanaiti mātudaivaputtassa taimāsaṃ abhidhammapiṭakaṃ daisaitvā daivaurauhanakālai’’ti mātudaivaputtasaddaina vuttaṃ| abhidhammāvatāraṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘daivānanti tadā dhammapaṭiggāhakānaṃ dasasahassacak‍kavāḷādhivāsīnaṃ mātudaivaputtappamukhānaṃ upapattidaivāna’’nti mātudaivaputta saddaina vuttaṃ| sirīmahāmāyāsatthai ca ‘‘sirīmahāmāyā daivīpi sattadivasaiyaiva vasitvā kālaṅkatvā attanau puñ‍ñānubhāvaina tusitabhavanai daivaputtau hutvā nibbattī’’ti daivaputtasaddaina vuttaṃ| maṇidīpaṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘mātaranti pubbai manussalaukai mātubhūtaṃ daivaputtanti atthau| sirīmahāmāyā hi baudhisattaṃ vijāyitvā sattāhamattaṃ ṭhatvā itau cavitvā tusitabhavanai purisabhāvainaiva nibbattau, na itthibhāvainā’’ti vuttaṃ| tasmā mātarantipadassa mātubhūtaṃ daivaputtanti atthau mukhyaina daṭṭhabbau|

madhusārattha dīpanīṭīkāyaṃ pana –

‘‘suddhaudanau nāma pitā mahaisinau,

buddhassa mātā pana māyā nāma||

baudhisattaṃ  parihariya kucchinā,

kāyassa bhaidā tidivasmiṃ maudati|

sā gautamī kālaṃkatā itau cutā,

dibbaihi kāmaihi samaṅgibhūtā|

āmaudati kāmaguṇaihi pañ‍cahi,

parivāritā daivagaṇaihi taihī’’ti| thairāpāḷi|

tadaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘parivāritāti ca itthiliṅganiddaisau purimatta bhāvasiddhaṃ itthibhāvaṃ daivatābhāvaṃ vā sandhāya katau| daivūpapatti pana purisabhāvaina jātāti vuttattā mātaranti manussattabhāvavasaina vutta’’nti vadanti| thairaṭṭhakathā pana nasaṅgahāruḷhā| añ‍ñaṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu ca tādisaṃ vacanaṃ natthi| patthanākālai ca buddhamātā hutvā pacchā purisau haumīti patthanā natthi| tiṃsadhammatāyañ‍ca buddhamātuyā purisabhāvau nāma natthi| tasmā taṃ vīmaṃsitabbantipi vidū vadantīti vuttaṃ| taṃ pana na gahaitabbaṃ| kasmā, saṃsayavacanaina vīmaṃsitabbanti vidūvadantīti ca vuttattā ca yathāvuttaṭṭhakathāṭīkāvacanaihi ujukaṃ viruddhattā ca| daivatāsaddau hi daivaputtai brahmai daivadhītāya ca vattati| tathāhaisa ‘‘atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā’’tiādīsu daivaputtai| vuttañhi khuddakapāṭhaaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘añ‍ñatarāti aniyamita niddaisau| sā hi nāmagauttatau apākaṭā| tasmā añ‍ñatarāti vuttā| daivauyaiva daivatā| itthipurisasādhāraṇamaivaitaṃ| idha pana purisauaiva| sau daivaputtau| kintusādhāraṇanāmavasaina daivatāti vuttau’’ti| sunītatthairaapadāna pāḷiyaṃ āgatāsu ‘‘tathā tā daivatā sattasatā uḷārā brahmā vimānā abhinikkhamitvā’’tiādīsu brahmai| padīpavimānavatthupāḷiyaṃ vuttāsu –

‘‘abhik‍kantaina vaṇṇaina, yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhati daivatai|

aubhāsaintī disā sabbā, ausadhī viya tārakā’’ti||

ādīsu  daivadhītāya| aivaṃ pana parivāritāsaddaupi vaiditabbau| tasmā ācariyadhammapālatthairaina katāya thairaṭṭhakathāya vuttavacanaṃ jātakaṭīkāya vuttavacanañ‍ca ācariyasumaṅgalatthairaina katāya abhidhammāvatāraṭīkāya vuttavacanaṃ pamāṇaṃ katvā mātudaivaputtauti atthauyaiva daṭṭhabbau| aitarahi pana kaici ācariyā thairāpāḷiyaṃ āgataṃ parivāritāsaddañ‍ca madhusāratthadīpanīṭīkāvacanañ‍ca paṭic‍ca mātudaivadhītāti vadanti| taṃ pana taisaṃ matimattanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| adaisīti mahākaruṇāpubbaṅgamaina sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaina kathaisi| asadisaṃ tanti sambandhau| aittha ca tanti buddhaṃ| asadisanti appaṭipuggalaṃ| natthi aitassa sadisau paṭipuggalauti asadisau, buddhau| sadaivakai laukai sīlādiguṇaina buddhaina sadisau kauci puggalau natthīti atthau| taināha vinayamahāvaggapāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘na mai ācariyau atthi, sadisau mai na vij‍jati|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, natthi mai paṭipuggalau’’ti||

nic‍cantisatataṃ samitaṃ, sabbakālaṃ nirantaranti atthau| asadisaṃ taṃ buddhaṃ nic‍caṃ namāmīti sambandhauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| sumati sundarasabbañ‍ñuta ñāṇavātau, daisanāñāṇavātau vā| yau yādisau buddhau| divai divasai divākarauva sūriyauiva, saubhantau viraucamānau hutvā, ratijananai daivānaṃ atappakapīti saumanassuppādanai, paimuppādanai vā, silāsanamhi paṇḍu kampalanāmakai silāsanai, āsīnau nisīdantau hutvā sivasukhadaṃ nibbānasukhadāyakaṃ, dhammaṃ abhidhammadhammaṃ, daivānaṃ mātu pubbadaivaputtappamukhānaṃ upapattidaivānaṃ adaisi kathaisi| asadisaṃ appaṭipuggalaṃ taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ nic‍caṃ satataṃ namāmi vandāmīti|

tairasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

14.

 yau  pādapaṅkajamuduttalarājikaihi,

laukaihi tīhivikalaihi nirākulaihi|

sampāpuṇai nirupamaiyyatamaiva nāthau,

taṃ sabbalaukamahitaṃ asamaṃ namāmi||

14. aivaṃ tairasamagāthāya buddhaṃ vanditvā idāni buddhassa pādatalai vicittappavattaihi aṭṭhasatacak‍kalakkhaṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau yau pādapaṅkajatyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ta bha ja ja gaṇaihi ca garudvayaina racitattā cuddasakkharaihi yuttā vasantatilakāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhaitaṃ vuttaudayai ‘‘vuttā vasantatilakā ta bha jā jagā gau’’ti| tassāyamatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ ta bha jā ta bha ja gaṇā ca jagā jagaṇagarukā ca gau garu ca cai bhavanti, sā gāthā vasantatilakāgāthāti chandācariyaihi vuttāti|

tattha pana yau nāthau avikalaihi nirākulaihi pādapaṅkajamuduttalaihitīhi laukaihi nirupamaiyyataṃ sampāpuṇaiaiva sabbalaukamahitaṃ asamaṃ taṃ nāthaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau| aittha pana saddak‍kamaina adassaitvā atthak‍kamaina vaṇṇanaṃ dassayissāma| yau nāthauti yau yādisau sabbasattānaṃ paṭisaraṇau buddhau| taṃ pana padaṃ sampāpuṇaiaivātipadai kattubhāvaina sambandhitabbaṃ| nāthassa pana vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| avikalaihīti kainaci avaikal‍laihi sabbapādacak‍kalakkhaṇaihi sampuṇṇaihīti atthau| virūpaṃ kalati gacchatīti vikalau| vipubbakaladhātu gatiyaṃ a| natthi kiñ‍ci vikalaṃ aitissā rājiyāti avikalā, pādarāji| pādadvayai paribyattaṃ saṇṭhānarūpaṃ kiñ‍ci vikalaṃ natthīti atthau| taihi avikalaihi|

nirākulaihīti  ākulavirahitaihi| pādacak‍kalakkhaṇānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ asaṃmissaihi asaṃsaṭṭhaihīti atthau| ābhusau kulati bandhatīti ākulaṃ, āpubba kuladhātu bandhanai a| atha vā ābhusau kulati āvarati saubhananti ākulaṃ, natthi ākulaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñamissaṃ aitissā pādarāji yāti nirākulā, pādarāji| taihi nirākulaihi| taṃ pana padadvayaṃ pādapaṅkajamuduttalarājikaihītipadai visaisanaṃ| pādapaṅkajamuduttalarājikaihīti pādasaṅkhātapadumassa saṇha talai jātaihi aṭṭhasatacak‍kalakkhaṇarājikaihi padati gacchati aitaināti pādau| padadhātu gatimhiṇa| paṅkai kaddamai jātanti paṅkajaṃ, padumaṃ| pādasaṅkhātaṃ paṅkajaṃ pādapaṅkajaṃ| paṅkajaṃiva pādau vā pādapaṅkajaṃ, pādau| saṇhabhāvaina mudati pavattatīti mudu, mudadhātu gatiyaṃ u| talati patiṭṭhati aitthāti talaṃ| taladhātu patiṭṭhāyaṃ a| mudu ca taṃ talañ‍cāti muduttalaṃ, pādapaṅkajassa muduttalaṃ pādapaṅkajamuduttalaṃ, rājati dissatīti rājikā, rājati saubhati aitaināti rājikā| rājadhātu saubhanai ṇikau| pādapaṅkaja muduttalai jātaṃ aṭṭhasatacak‍kalakkhaṇasaṅkhātaṃ rājikanti pādapaṅkajamuduttalarājikaṃ, taihi|

taṃ pana aṭṭhasatapādacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ nāmaivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| bhagavatau pādapaṅkajamudutalassa majjhai nābhi dissati| nābhipaṭicchin‍nā vaṭṭalaikhā dissati| nābhimukhaparikkhaipapaṭṭau dissati| panāḷimukhaṃ dissati| arā dissanti| araisu vaṭṭalaikhā dissanti| naimi dissati| naimimaṇikā dissanti| idaṃ tāva pāḷiāgatamaiva| sampahulavārau pana anāgatau, sau aivaṃ daṭṭhabbau| satti, sirīvacchau, nandiyāvaṭṭaṃ, sauvattikau, vaṭaṃsakau, vaḍḍhamānakaṃ, macchayugalaṃ, bhaddapīṭhaṃ, aṅkusaṃ, taumarau, pāsādau, tauraṇaṃ, saitacchattaṃ, khaggau, tālavaṇḍaṃ, maurahatthakau, vāḷabījani, uṇhīsaṃ, pattau, maṇikusumadāmaṃ, nīluppalaṃ, rattuppalaṃ, saituppalaṃ, padumaṃ, puṇḍarikaṃ, puṇṇaghaṭau, puṇṇapāti, samuddau, cak‍kavāḷaṃ, himavā, sinairu, candimasūriyā, nakkhattāni, cattārau mahādīpā, dvaiparittadīpasahassāni antamasau cak‍kavatti rañ‍ñau parisaṃ upādāya sabbau cak‍kalakkhaṇassaiva parivārauti aittakamaiva majjhimapaṇṇāsaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttanti vaiditabbaṃ|

jinālaṅkāraṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘haiṭṭhāpādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātānīti cak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ nāma dvīsu pādatalaisu dvai cak‍kāni aikaikasmiṃ sahassamattā arā yuttā haunti| naimivalayaṃ suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ| nābhi indanīlamaṇivaṇṇā| tasmiṃ majjhai ca nābhipariyantai ca nābhimukhai ca vaṭṭalaikhā majjhai chiddaṃ cak‍kapariyantai naimitau bahiddhā vaṭṭai| satti, sirīvacchau, nandiyāvaṭṭaṃ, sauvattikau, vaṭaṃsakaṃ, vaḍḍhamānakaṃ, bhaddapīṭhaṃ, aṅkusau, pāsādaṃ, tauraṇaṃ, saitacchattaṃ, khaggaṃ, tālavaṇḍaṃ, mayūrahatthaṃ, cāmaraṃ, uṇhīsaṃ, pattaṃ, maṇi, sumanadāmaṃ, nīluppalaṃ, rattuppalaṃ, rattapadumaṃ, saitapadumaṃ, puṇḍarikaṃ, puṇṇakalasaṃ, puṇṇapattaṃ, samuddau, cak‍kavāḷapabbatau, himavantapabbatau, mairupabbatau, sūriyamaṇḍalaṃ, candamaṇḍalaṃ, nakkhattaṃ, saparivārā catumahādīpā, saparivārau sattaratanasamaṅgī cak‍kavatti, dakkhiṇāvaṭṭa saitasaṅkhaṃ, suvaṇṇamacchayugaḷaṃ, cak‍kāvudhaṃ, sattamahāgaṅgā, sattakulapabbatā, sattasīdantasāgarā, suvaṇṇa haṃsarājā, saṃsumārau, dhajapaṭākau, suvaṇṇasivikā, suvaṇṇavāḷabījani, kailāsapabbatau, sīharājā, byaggharājā, valāhakaassarājā, upausathachaddantahatthirājā, vāsukīnāgarājā, haṃsarājā, usabharājā, airāvaṇa hatthirājā, suvaṇṇamakaraṃ, catumukhasuvaṇṇanāvā, savacchakā dhainu, kiṃpurisau, kin‍narī, karavīkau, mayūrarājā, kauñ‍carājā, cak‍kavākarājā, jīvañ‍jīvakarājā cha daivalaukā, sauḷasa  brahmalaukāti imai aittakā aggamaṅgalalakkhaṇā pādatalai pañ‍ñāyantī’’ti vuttaṃ| iminā ca vacanaina aṭṭhasata pādacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ vaiditabbanti|

tīhi laukaihīti sambandhau| tīhi kāmarūpārūpa laukaihīti atthau| tassa pana vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| taṃ panaitaṃ katvatthai karaṇavacanaṃ| tassa pana padassa nirupamaiyyāti padaina kattubhāvaina sambandhau| nirupamaiyyatanti appamaiyyabhāvaṃ| upamitabbanti upamaiyyaṃ| upapubba mādhātu pamānai ṇyau| upamaiyyassa bhāvau upamaiyyataṃ| natthi upamaiyyataṃ assa rājikassāti nirupamaiyyataṃ| atha vā natthi upamaiyyaṃ aitassa rājikassāti nirupamaiyyau, pādacak‍kalakkhaṇarājikau labbhati| tassa bhāvau nirupamaiyyataṃ| taṃ pana padaṃ sampāpuṇai aivātipadai kammaṃ| sampāpuṇaiaivāti sambandhau| sammā gacchatiaivāti atthau| kasmā pana tīhi laukaihi pādapaṅkajamudutalaihi nirupamaiyyataṃ pāpuṇātīti| buddhatau añ‍ñassa kassaci daivamanussassa pādatalamajjhai pādacak‍kalakkhaṇassa abhāvatau| bhagavatau hi pādapaṅkajamudutalai pāramipuñ‍ñānubhāvaina nibbattāni aṭṭhasatapādacak‍kalakkhaṇāni sampāpuṇanti, na añ‍ñaisaṃ| tasmā nirupamaiyyataṃ pāpuṇātīti|

sabbalaukamahitanti sabbalaukaina pūjitaṃ| sabbaina daivamanussalaukaina mahitabbau pūjitabbauti sabbalauka mahitau, nāthau labbhati| taṃ| uppan‍nai hi tathāgatai yai kaici mahaisakkhā daivamanussā buddhassa pūjainti, pagaiva añ‍ñā| tathā hi brahmā sahampati sinairumattaina ratana dāmaina tathāgataṃ pūjaiti| yathābalañ‍ca añ‍ñaipi daivā manussā ca bimbisārakausalarājādayau nibbutampi bhagavantaṃ uddissa chan‍navutikauṭidhanaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā asauka mahārājā  sakalajambudīpai caturāsītivihārasahassāni patiṭṭhāpaiti| kā kathā pana añ‍ñaisaṃ pūjāti vuttaṃ hauti|

asamanti sabbamanussadaivabrahmaihi asadisaṃ| appaṭipuggalanti atthau| natthi samau sadisau puggalau assāti asamau, nāthau| sau pana asītianubyañ‍janapaṭimaṇḍita dvattiṃsa mahāpurisalakkhaṇa vicitrarūpakāyau| sabbākāra parisuddha sīlakkhandhādiguṇaratanasamiddhadhammakāyau| puñ‍ñamahatta thāmamahattaiddhimahatta yasamahatta pañ‍ñāmahattānaṃ paramuk‍kaṃsa gatau ca hauti, tasmā tathāgataina samau sadisau nāma natthīti atthau| taṃ asamaṃ| tanti nāthaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| yau nāthau yau yādisau sabbasattānaṃ paṭisaraṇau buddhau, avikalaihi kaihici avaikal‍laihi, nirākulaihi ākulavirahitaihi, pādapaṅkaja muduttalarājikaihi pādasaṅkhātapadumassa saṇhatalai jātaihi aṭṭhasatacak‍kalakkhaṇarājikaihi, tīhi laukaihi kattubhūtaihi, nirupamaiyyataṃ appamaiyyabhāvaṃ appamitabba bhāvaṃ vā, sampāpuṇaiaiva sammā gacchatiaiva, sabbalaukamahitaṃ sabbalaukaina pūjitaṃ, asamaṃ sabbamanussadaivabrahmaihi asadisaṃ taṃ nāthaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti|

cuddasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

15.

 buddhaṃ narānarasamausaraṇaṃ dhitattaṃ,

pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā vihatandhakāraṃ|

atthābhikāmanaradaivahitāvahaṃ taṃ,

vandāmi kāruṇikamaggamanantañāṇaṃ||

15. aivaṃ  cuddasamagāthāya nāthaṃ natvā idāni narānarasa mausaraṇantyādīhi sattahi guṇaihi thaumitvā buddhaṃ vanditu kāmau buddhaṃ narānarasamausaraṇantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana tabhajajagaṇaihi garudvayaina ca racitattā cuddasakkharaihi yuttā vasantatilakāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| sādhakaṃ panaittha haiṭṭhā vuttamaiva|

tattha pana narānarasamausaraṇanti aittha naraanarasamaṃ ausaraṇanti padavibhāgau| manussadaivānaṃ samaṃ ausaraṇaṃ san‍nipatitaṭṭhānanti atthau| narānarasaddassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| samaṃ avasaranti san‍nipatanti aitthāti samausaraṇau, buddhau| samaavapubba saradhātu gatiyaṃ yu| narānarānaṃ manussadaivānaṃ samausaraṇau narānarasamausaraṇau, buddhau| yathā hi mahānigraudhau phalakāmānaṃ sakuṇa samūhānaṃ samausaraṇaṃ hauti, aivaṃ buddhau laukiya laukuttarasampattikāmānaṃ manussadaivānaṃ samausaraṇau san‍nipatitau paṭisaraṇau vā hauti| tasmā narānarasamausaraṇanti thaumaiti|

dhitattanti samāhitacittaṃ| aittha attasaddau attasammā paṇidhi cātiādīsu attasaddau viya cittavācakau daṭṭhabbau| dhiyati ṭhapiyati aittha aitaina vā aikārammaṇai sampayutta dhammāti dhitau, samādhi| dhādhātu patiṭṭhāyaṃ ta| ārammaṇaṃ adati ādadātīti attaṃ, cittaṃ| adadhātu ādānai ta| dhitaṃ samāhitaṃ attaṃ cittaṃ yassāti dhitattau, buddhau| taṃ dhitattaṃ|

pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyāti arahattamaggañāṇasaṅkhātapadīpaubhāsaina karaṇabhūtaina| pajānātīti pañ‍ñā, arahattamagga ñāṇaṃ| yathāsabhāvaṃ sabbappakāraihi catusac‍cadhammaṃ paṭivijjhatīti atthau| pañ‍ñāva ñaiyyāvaraṇappahānatau sabbapakāraihi  dhammasabhāvaṃ dīpaitīti pañ‍ñāpadīpau| jautaitīti juti, pañ‍ñāpadīpaubhāsau| judhātu dittiyanti| pañ‍ñā padīpauaiva juti pañ‍ñāpadīpajuti| atha vā pañ‍ñāpadīpassa juti pañ‍ñāpadīpajuti, ayaṃ pana samāsau abhaidabhaidū pacāraina daṭṭhabbau| tāya pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā|

vihatandhakāranti hanitabbaavij‍jāsaṅkhātamauhatamaṃ| savāsanakilaisappahānatau visaisaina hanitabbauti vihatau| vipubbahanadhātu hiṃsāyaṃ ta| andhaṃ tamaṃ karautīti andhakārau, avij‍jāsaṅkhātamauhau| visayasabhāvapaṭicchādanatau andhakārasarikkhakatāya mauhau andhakārauti vuc‍cati| atha vā andhaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhuandhaṃ karautīti andhakārau, mauhau| andhasaddūpapada karadhātu ṇa| vihatau andhakārau yaina sauti vihatandhakārau, buddhau| taṃ vihatandhakāraṃ| yathā hi laukai padīpau attanau jutiyā andhakāraṃ hanati, aivaṃ buddhassa arahattamaggapañ‍ñāpi avij‍jandhakāraṃ hanatīti vuttā| ayaṃ pana hīnūpamāti vuc‍cati| pañ‍ñāvatau hi aikapal‍laṅkainapi nisin‍nassa dasasahassi laukadhātu aikapaj‍jautā hauti| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘cattāraumai bhikkhavai paj‍jautā| katamai cattārau| candapaj‍jautau, sūriyapaj‍jautau, aggipaj‍jautau, pañ‍ñā paj‍jautau| imai khau bhikkhavai cattārau paj‍jautā| aitadaggaṃ bhikkhavai imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ paj‍jautānaṃ yadidaṃ pañ‍ñā paj‍jautau’’ti|

pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā vihatandhakāranti aittha pana pañ‍ñāpadīpa jutiyātiiminā buddhassa arahattamaggañāṇaṃ mukhyaina dassaiti| taṃ dassanaina pāramīñāṇañ‍ca vipassanāñāṇañ‍ca haiṭṭhimamaggaphala ñāṇañ‍ca phalūpacāraina dīpaiti| taddīpakaina avij‍jandhakārappahānaṃ paṭhamakāraṇūpacāraina pakāsaiti| tappakāsanaina arahatta phalañāṇalabhanaṃ dutiyakāraṇūpacāraina vibhāvaiti| taṃ vibhāvanaina  sabbañ‍ñutañāṇādisabbabuddhaguṇalabhanaṃ tatiya kāraṇūpacāraina ānubhāvaiti|

nanu ca sabbaisampi khīṇāsavānaṃ pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā vihatandhakāratā sambhavati, atha kasmā añ‍ñasādhāraṇāvisaisa guṇaina buddhassa thaumanā katāti| savāsanappahānaina anañ‍ñasādhāraṇavisaisatāya sambhavatau| sabbaisampi hi khīṇāsavānaṃ satipi pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā vihatandhakārattai ariyasāvakapac‍caikabuddhānaṃ savāsanāya kilaisappahānaṃ na vij‍jati| sammāsambuddhānaṃyaiva savāsanappahānaina kilaisappahānassa visaisau vij‍jatīti sātisayavasaina avij‍jandhakārappahānaina bhagavantaṃyaiva pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā vihatandhakāranti thaumaitīti|

atthābhikāmanti sattalaukassa laukiyalaukuttara saṅkhātaṃ payaujanaṃ ativiya icchantaṃ| kāraṇānurūpaṃ asati bhavatīti atthaṃ, phalaṃ| asadhātu bhuvimhi tha| taṃ abhiatiraikaṃ kāmaiti icchatīti atthābhikāmau, buddhau| atthasaddūpapada abhipubbakamudhātu icchāyaṃ ṇa| taṃ atthābhikāmaṃ| naradaivahitāvahanti manussadaivānaṃ hitaṃ dhārakaṃ| hitaṃ narati naitīti narau, manussau| atha vā nariyati sakaina kammaina niyyatīti narau, manussau| naradhātu nayanai a| vuttañhi ‘‘kammaina niyyati laukau’’ti| kāmaguṇa jhānābhiñ‍ñacittissariyādīhi dibbanti kīḷantīti daivā, taisu vā viharantīti daivā, divudhātu kīḷādīsu ṇa| narā ca daivā ca naradaivā| hinauti vaḍḍhatīti hitaṃ, phalaṃ| hidhātu vaḍḍhanai ta| naradaivānaṃ hitaṃ naradaivahitaṃ, taṃ āvahatīti naradaivahitāvahau, buddhau| naradaivahitasaddūpapadaāpubba vahadhātu haraṇai ṇa| buddhau hi sabbadaivamanussānaṃ laukiyalaukuttarasampattiatthaṃ dhammadaisanāya dhāraitīti vuttaṃ hauti, taṃ naradaivahitāvahaṃ| kāruṇikamaggamananta ñāṇanti  aittha kāruṇikaṃ aggaṃ anantañāṇantipadacchaidau| tattha kāruṇikanti sabbasattaisu mahākaruṇāya yuttaṃ, mahākaruṇavantaṃ vā| kiratīti karuṇā| kiradhātu apanayanai uṇapac‍cayau| paradukkhaṃ vikkhipati apanaitīti atthau|

atha vā kinātīti karuṇā| kidhātu hiṃsāyaṃ ruṇapac‍cayau| paradukkhai sati kāruṇikaṃ hiṃsati vibādhatīti atthau| paradukkhai sati sādhūnaṃ hadayakampanaṃ karautīti vā karuṇā| karadhātu uṇapac‍cayau| atha vā kaṃ vuc‍cati sukhaṃ, taṃ rundhatīti karuṇā| kaṃsaddūpapada rudhidhātu āvaraṇai yupac‍cayau| aisā hi paradukkhāpanayanakāmatā lakkhaṇā| attasukhanirapaikkhatāya kāruṇikānaṃ sukhaṃ rundhati vibādhatīti atthau| karuṇāya mahākaruṇāya yuttau kāruṇikau| karuṇā assa atthīti vā kāruṇikau, buddhau| taṃ kāruṇitaṃ| agganti jātipuñ‍ña guṇamahattabhāvaina sabbasattuttamaṃ saiṭṭhabhāvaṃ ajati gacchatīti aggau, buddhau| ajadhātu gatiyaṃ a| jassa gau| buddhau hi asambhin‍nakhattiyavaṃsai jātattā jātivasaina ca pūritapāramīpuñ‍ñattā puñ‍ñavasaina ca aparimitanirupamasīlādi guṇavisaisaina saman‍nāgatattā guṇavasaina ca anantalaukadhātūsu sabbasattuttamabhāvaṃ gacchatīti aggau| nibbānaṃ ajati gacchatīti vā aggau| atha vā ajaiti khaipaiti kilaisaiti aggau, buddhau| ajadhātu khaipanai a| taṃ aggaṃ| anantañāṇanti gaṇanavasaina ca sabhāvavasaina ca visayavasaina ca antavirahitañāṇaṃ| kainaci puggalaina ñāṇaina anantaṃ aparicchin‍naṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇavantaṃ vā| natthi antaṃ yassāti anantaṃ, ñāṇaṃ| sabbañaiyyadhammaṃ jānātīti ñāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| anantaṃ ñāṇaṃ yassa sauti anantañāṇau, buddhau| buddhassa hi ñāṇaṃ kauci manussau vā daivau vā brahmā vā aittakanti paricchindituṃ na sak‍kauti, tasmā anantaṃ aparimāṇaṃ hautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

vuttañhaitaṃ  sāriputtatthairaapadānai –

‘‘sak‍kā samuddai udakaṃ, pamaituṃ aḷhakaina vā|

natvaiva tava sabbañ‍ñu, ñāṇaṃ sak‍kā pamaitavai’’ti||
