kāruṇikamaggamanantañāṇanti aittha ca karuṇaggahaṇaina sammāsambaudhiyā mūlaṃ dassaiti, mahākaruṇāsañ‍cauditamānasau hi bhagavā saṃsārapaṅkatau sattānaṃ samuddharaṇatthaṃ dīpaṅkarapādamūlai katābhinīhārau kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni anupubbaina pāramiyau pūraitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ adhigatauti karuṇā sammāsambaudhiyā mūlanti| agganti aitaina bhagavatau aparimitanirupamasīlādiguṇasamaṅgītāya aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ uttamabhāvaṃ dassaiti| anantañāṇantiaitaina sammāsambaudhiṃ dassaiti| sabbañ‍ñutañāṇapadaṭṭhānañhi aggamaggañāṇaṃ, aggamaggañāṇapadaṭṭhānañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ sammāsambaudhīti vuc‍cati| tassa ca sāmatthiyaina catupaṭisambhidāñāṇaṃ catuvaisāraj‍jañāṇaṃ chaasādhāraṇañāṇāni ca chaabhiñ‍ñā aṭṭhasu parisāsu akampana ñāṇāni dasabalañāṇāni sauḷasañāṇacariyā ca catucattārīsañāṇavatthūni sattasattatiñāṇavatthūni cāti aivamādīnaṃ anaikaisaṃ pañ‍ñāpabhaidānaṃ vasaina ñāṇācāraṃ dassaitīti| taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ vippasan‍naina caitasā vandāmīti sambandhauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| narānarasamausaraṇaṃ manussadaivānaṃ samaṃ ausaraṇaṃ san‍nipatitaṭṭhānaṃ, dhitattaṃ samāhitacittaṃ, pañ‍ñāpadīpajutiyā arahattamaggañāṇasaṅkhāta padīpaubhāsaina karaṇabhūtaina vihatandhakāraṃ hanitabba avij‍jāsaṅkhātamauhatamaṃ, atthābhikāmaṃ sattalaukassa laukiyalaukuttarasaṅkhātapayaujanaṃ ativiya icchantaṃ, naradaivahitāvahaṃ manussadaivānaṃ hitaṃ dhārakaṃ, kāruṇikaṃ sabbasattaisu  mahākaruṇāya yuttaṃ mahākaruṇavantaṃ vā, aggaṃ jātipuñ‍ñaguṇamahattabhāvaina sabbasattuttamaṃ, ananta ñāṇaṃ gaṇavasaina ca sabhāvavasaina ca visayavasaina ca antavirahitañāṇaṃ, anantaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇavantaṃ vā taṃ buddhaṃ ahaṃ vippasan‍naina caitasā vandāmīti|

pan‍narasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

16.

 akhilaguṇanidhānau yau munindaupagantvā,

vanamisipatanavhaṃ sañ‍ñatānaṃ nikaitaṃ|

tahimakusalachaidaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattau,

tamatulamabhikantaṃ vandanaiyyaṃ namāmi||

16. aivaṃ pan‍narasamagāthāya buddhaṃ vanditvā idāni isipatana migadāyai dhammacak‍kapavattanādiguṇaihi thaumitvā munindaṃ vanditu kāmau akhilaguṇanidhānau tyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana nanamayayagaṇaihi racitattā pan‍narasakkharaihi yuttā mālinī gāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘nanamayayayutāyaṃ mālinībhaugisīhī’’ti| tassatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ bhaugisīhi aṭṭhasattayatīhi yuttā ca nanamayayayutā nanagaṇamagaṇa yayagaṇaihi yuttā ca cai siyuṃ, ayaṃ gāthā mālinīgāthā nāmāti| aittha ca bhaugisīhīti bhaugaisīhīti padacchaidau| bhaugasaddau aṭṭhasaṅkhyā vācakau| isisaddau sattasaṅkhyāvācakauti daṭṭhabbau|

tattha akhilaguṇanidhānauti sabbalaukiyalaukuttara saṅkhātānaṃ sīlasamādhipañ‍ñādiguṇasamūhānaṃ patiṭṭhānau| aittha ca akhilasaddau anavasaisavācakau| sabbauti atthau| vuttañhi madhuṭīkāyaṃ ‘‘khilasaddau haittha avasaisavācakau’’ti| na khilaṃ akhilaṃ| atha vā na khīyatīti akhilaṃ, sabbaṃ| napubbakhidhātu khayai lau| guṇasaddassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| akhilāni sabbāni guṇāni akhilaguṇāni, taisaṃ nidhāti tiṭṭhati aitthāti akhilaguṇanidhānau, munindau labbhati| yauti yau yādisau| munindaupagantvāti aittha munindau upagantvāti padacchaidau| munindauti agāriyamuni, anagāriyamuni, saikkhamuni, asaikkhamuni, pac‍caika munivasaina pañ‍can‍naṃ munīnaṃ indau issarauti atthau| tassa pana vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| akhilaguṇanidhānau yau munindau sañ‍ñatānaṃ nikaitaṃ isipatanaavhaṃ vanaṃ upagantvā tahiṃ akusalachaidaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattauti sambandhau|

vanamisipatanavhanti vanaṃ isipatanaavhanti padacchaidau| aittha vananti migadāyavanaṃ| tañhi vivaikakāmaihi vanitabbaṃ sambhajitabbaṃ aitthāti vananti vuc‍cati| vanadhātu sambhattiyaṃ k‍vi| isipatanavhanti isipatananāmikaṃ isīnaṃ patanaṭṭhānattā isipatananti vauharitabbaṃ vā, sīlakkhandhādikai aisanti gavaisantīti isi, pac‍caikabuddhādayau| isadhātu gavaisanai ipac‍cayau| tai patanti san‍nipatanti aitthāti isipatanaṃ, vanaṃ| isisaddūpapadapatadhātu gatiyaṃ yu| atha vā isīnaṃ patanaṃ isipatanaṃ, vanaṃ| avhaitabbaṃ vauharitabbanti avhaṃ| āpubbavhaidhātu avhayanai k‍vi| saṃyaugaparattā ākārassa rassādaisau| isipatananti avhaitabbanti isipatanavhaṃ, vanaṃ| sañ‍ñatānanti kāyavacīmanausaṃyamantānaṃ sādhūnaṃ| saṃyamantīti sañ‍ñatā, pac‍caikabuddhādayau sādhū| saṃpubbayamudhātu uparamai ta| taisaṃ sañ‍ñatānaṃ| nikaitanti vasanaṭṭhānaṃ, rammaṇaṭṭhānaṃ vā| nikitati nivasati rammati vā aitthāti nikaitaṃ, vanaṃ| nipubba kitadhātu nivāsai ṇa| upagantvāti gamitvā| tahinti tasmiṃ isipatananāmakai migadāyavanai| akusalachaidanti akusalassa samucchaidaṃ| akusalaṃ chindati samucchindati  aitaina suttaināti akusalachaidaṃ, dhammacak‍ka pavattanasuttaṃ| akusalasaddūpapadachididhātu dvidhākaraṇai ṇa|

dhammacak‍kanti dhammacak‍kapavattanasuttaṃ| aittha hi cak‍kasaddau sampattiyaṃ, lakkhaṇai, rathaṅgai, iriyāpathai, dānai, ratana dhammakhuracak‍kādīsu ca dissati| cattārimāni bhikkhavai cak‍kāni, yaihi saman‍nāgatānaṃ daivamanussānantiādīsu hi sampattiyaṃ dissati| pādatalaisu cak‍kāni jātānīti aittha lakkhaṇai| cak‍kaṃva vahatau padanti aittha rathaṅgai| catucak‍kaṃ navadvāranti aittha iriyāpathai| dadaṃ bhuñ‍ja mā ca pamādau cak‍kaṃ vattaya sabbapāṇīnanti aittha dānai| dibbacak‍karatanaṃ pāturahausīti aittha ratanacak‍kai| mayā pavattitaṃ cak‍kanti aittha dhammacak‍kai| icchāhatassa pausassa cak‍kaṃ bhamati matthakaiti aittha khuracak‍kai| khurapariyantaina cak‍kaināti aittha paharaṇacak‍kai| asanivicak‍kanti aittha asanimaṇḍalai| idha panāyaṃ dhammacak‍kai daṭṭhabbau| tañhi satipaṭṭhānādidhammaṃ cak‍kaiti pavattaitīti dhammacak‍kanti vuc‍cati| ariyasac‍cādidhammaṃ vā cak‍kaiti pavattaitīti cak‍kanti| atha vā dhammatau anapaitattā dhammañ‍ca taṃ āṇācak‍kañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ| dhammaina ñāyaina cak‍kanti dhammacak‍kaṃ, taina vuttaṃ paṭisambhidāyaṃ ‘‘dhammacak‍kanti kainaṭṭhaina dhammacak‍kaṃ| dhammañ‍ca pavattaiti cak‍kañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, cak‍kañ‍ca pavattaiti dhammañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammaina pavattaitīti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammacariyāya pavattaitīti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammai ṭhitau pavattaitīti dhammacak‍ka’’ntiādi| api ca dhammacak‍kanti paṭivaidhañāṇampi daisanāñāṇampi| tattha paṭivaidhañāṇaṃ nāma yaina ñāṇaina baudhipal‍laṅkai nisin‍nau cattāri sac‍cāni sauḷasahākāraihi saṭṭhinayasahassaihi paṭivijjhi| daisanāñāṇaṃ nāma yaina ñāṇaina tiparivaṭṭaṃ dvādasākāraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaisi| ubhayampaitaṃ dasabalassa urai jātañāṇamaiva| taisu pana dhammadaisanāñāṇaṃ adhippaitaṃ| suttaṃ pana ṭhānūpacāraina dhammacak‍kanti vuc‍cati| pavattauti pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ dvaimai bhikkhavai antā pabbajitaina na saivitabbātiādinā adaisayi|

tamatulamabhikantanti taṃ atulaṃ abhikantanti padacchaidau| aittha tanti munindaṃ| atulanti sīlādīhi guṇaihi kainaci asadisaṃ| natthi tulau sadisau puggalau aitassāti atulau, munindau| tassa hi aparimāṇāsu lauka dhātūsu sīlādīhi guṇaihi sadisau puggalau natthi| asadisaihi buddhaihi samauti atthau| abhikantanti sabbadaivamanussaihi ativiya kantaṃ kamanīyaṃ sarīravaṇṇavantaṃ| atiraikaṃ tusitabbasarīravaṇṇasampan‍naṃ vā| abhik‍kantavaṇṇanti hi vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ kakāralaupañ‍ca vaṇṇapadalaupaṃ katvā vuttasandhivasaina abhikantanti vuttaṃ| uttarapadalaupa vasaina vā| munindassa pana sarīravaṇṇaṃ sabbadaivamanussaihi ativiya atiraikaṃ vā kamiyati tausiyatīti abhikantanti vuc‍cati| abhipubbakamudhātu icchākantīsu ta| abhikantaṃ vaṇṇaṃ sarīravaṇṇaṃ yassa sauti abhikantau, munindau| tassa hi dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi ca asītyānubyañ‍jana lakkhaṇaihi ca chabbaṇṇaraṃsīhi ca suvaṇṇavaṇṇaina ca saman‍nā gatattā sarīrasaubhaggappattitau sabbadaivamanussaihi ativiya kamanīyau tuṭṭhau sarīravaṇṇau atthīti abhikantanti thaumaiti| taṃ abhikantaṃ| vandanaiyyanti daivamanussānaṃ vandanārahaṃ| taihi vanditabbaṃ vā, vandiyatai thaumiyatai vā vandanaṃ| bhāvasādhanauyaṃ| vandadhātu vadidhātu vā abhivādanathutīsu yu| taṃ arahatīti vandanaiyyau, munindau| vandapadaṃ arahatitaddhitai ṇaiyyau|

atha vā daivamanussaihi vanditabbaṃ thaumitabbanti vā vandanaiyyau, munindau| sau hi ādimajjhapariyausāna guṇaihi saman‍nāgatattā sabbadaivamanussānaṃ vandanārahau taihi vā vanditabbau hauti| tattha ca ādiguṇaṃ nāma karuṇānidānaṃ  sīlaṃ pāṇātipātādiviratippavattitau| majjha guṇaṃ nāma samādhi jhānattayayaugatau| pariyausānaguṇaṃ nāma pañ‍ñā tatau uttari karaṇīyābhāvatauti visaisaina bhagavatau vandanaiyyanti thaumaiti| ahaṃ tīhi dvāraihi namāmi vandāmīti sambandhau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| akhilanidhānau sabba laukiyalaukuttarasaṅkhātānaṃ sīlasamādhipañ‍ñādiguṇa samūhānaṃ patiṭṭhānau yau yādisau munindau pañ‍can‍naṃ munīnaṃ indau issarau bhagavā, sañ‍ñatānaṃ kāyavacīmanausaṃya mantānaṃ sādhūnaṃ, nikaitaṃ vasanaṭṭhānaṃ rammaṇaṭṭhānaṃ vā, isipatanavhaṃ isipatananāmikaṃ isipatananti vauharitabbaṃ vā, vanaṃ migadāyavanaṃ, upagantvā gamitvā tahiṃ tasmiṃ isipatananāmakai migadāya vanai, akusalassa samucchaidaṃ, dhammacak‍kaṃ dhammacak‍kappavattanasuttaṃ, pavattau pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ adaisayi, atulaṃ sīlādīhi guṇaihi kainaci asadisaṃ, abhikantaṃ sabbadaivamanussaihi ativiya kantaṃ kamanīyaṃ tuṭṭhi sarīravaṇṇavantaṃ, vandanaiyyaṃ daiva manussānaṃ vandanārahaṃ taihi vā vanditabbaṃ, taṃ munindaṃ ahaṃ tīhi dvāraihi namāmi vandāmīti|

sauḷasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

17.

 suciparivāritaṃ surucirappabhāhi rattaṃ,

sirivisarālayaṃ gupitamindriyaihupaitaṃ|

ravisasimaṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaupacittaṃ,

suranarapūjitaṃ sugatamādaraṃ namāmi||

17. aivaṃ sauḷasamagāthāya munindaṃ vanditvā idāni suciparivāritantyādīhi sattahi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau suciparivāritantyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana na jabhajaragaṇaihi ca aikagarunā ca racitattā sauḷasakkharaihi yuttāvāṇinīgāthāti  daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘najabhajarā yadā bhavati vāṇinī gayuttā’’ti| tassatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ najabhajarā nagaṇa jagaṇa bhagaṇa jagaṇa ragaṇā yadā gayuttā pādantagarunā saṃyaugā cai siyuṃ, tadā sā vuttā vāṇinī vāṇinīgāthā nāma bhavatīti|

tattha suciparivāritanti rāgādimalavisuddhāhi ariyasāvakādiparisāhi parikkhāritaṃ kilaisamalavisuddhaṃ parikkhāravantaṃ vā| rāgādimalaihi sucati sujjhatīti suci, parisā| sucadhātu saucaiyyai i| parisamantatau ariyabhikkhuādīhi daiva manussaihi vāriyati parikkhariyatīti parivārau, sugatau| paripubbavaradhātu āvaraṇai parikkhārai vā ṇa| sucīhi parisāhi parivāritau suciparivāritau, sugatau| tatiya tappurisasamāsauyaṃ| bhagavatau hi sadā rāgādimala visuddhaihi ariyabhikkhuādīhi catūhi parisāhi aṭṭhahi parisāhi vā parivāritau hautīti adhippāyau| atha vā suciparisuddhaṃ parivāritaṃ yassa sauti suciparivāritau, sugatau| surucirappabhāhīti suṭṭhu kantitasarīranikkhanta byāmamattaraṃsīhi karaṇabhūtaihi suṭṭhu ruciyati kamiyati tusiyatīti surucirā, raṃsi| supubba rucadhātu raucanai irau| pakāraina bhāti dibbatīti pabhā, raṃsi| papubba bhādhātu dibbanai k‍vi| surucirā ca sā pabhā cāti surucirappabhā, tāhi surucirappabhāhi| rattanti nic‍caṃ rañ‍jitabbakāyaṃ| rañ‍jiyatīti rattau, kāyau| ranjadhātu rāgai ta| rattau kāyau yassa sauti rattau, sugatau| uttarapadalaupasamā sauyaṃ| rūpassa bhavau rūpaṃ viya, taṃ rattaṃ| bhagavatau hi sarīratau nic‍caṃ nikkhantaihi byāmamattaraṃsīhi rañ‍jitabbau rūpakāyau atthīti vuttaṃ hauti|

sirivisarālayanti pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñasaṅkhātalakkhisamūhānaṃ patiṭṭhānaṃ| aittha ca sirīti pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| atha vā puñ‍ñanibbattā  sarīrasaubhaggādisampatti| sā hi katapuñ‍ñai siyati nissayati, katapuñ‍ñaihi vā siyati nissiyatīti sirīti vuc‍cati, pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñaṃ, issariyaṃ vā| sarīrasaubhaggādi sampatti vā labbhati| vuttañhi vimānavatthuaṭṭhakathādīsu ‘‘sirīti buddhipuñ‍ñānaṃ adhivacananti ca| sirīti issariyanti ca sirīti rūpakāyasampattī’’ti ca| sidhātu saivāyaṃ, rapac‍cayau ca, itthiliṅgajautakaīpac‍cayau ca| sirīti hi dīghavasaina vattabbai gāthābandhattā chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ rassavasaina vuttaṃ| visarālayanti visaraālayanti padacchaidau| visarauti samūhau| visanti pavisanti avayavā yasmiṃ samūhaiti visarau, samūhau| visadhātu pavaisanai arapac‍cayau| atha vā visaranti samausaranti avayavā yasminti visarau| vipubba saradhātu samausaraṇai a| pautthakaisu pana visayāla yanti yakāraina pāṭhau dissati| na sau yuj‍jati| kasmā, visarasaddassaiva samūhavācakattā| vuttañhi abhidhānappa dīpikāyaṃ –

‘‘samūhau gaṇasaṅghātā,

samudāyau ca sañ‍cayau|

sandauhau nivahau aughau,

visarau nikarau cayau’’tyādi||

ālayanti patiṭṭhānaṃ| ālayanti patiṭṭhanti aitthāti ālayau, sugatau| āpubba lidhātu al‍liyanai ṇa| sirīnaṃ pañ‍ñāsaṅkhātalakkhīnaṃ visarau samūhau sirīvisarau, taisaṃ ālayau sirīvisarālayau, sugatau| taṃ sirivisarālayaṃ| gupitamindriyaihupaitanti gaupitabbaihi saṃvaritabbaihi cakkhādīhi chahi indriyaihi upaitaṃ samupaitaṃ saman‍nāgataṃ| gupiyanti rakkhiyantīti gupitāni| gupadhātu rakkhanai ta| idanti sahajātadhammai issariyaṃ karauntīti indriyāni| ididhātu paramissariyai iyau| gupitāni indriyāni gupitamindriyāni, cakkhādiindriyāni labbhanti| taihi| upaiti sampaj‍jatīti upaitau, sugatau| upapubba idhātu gatiyaṃ ta, taṃ upaitaṃ| kiñ‍cāpi buddhatau añ‍ñaihi ariyasāvakādīhi puggalaihi cakkhādiindriyāni rakkhitāni| buddhauyaiva pana baudhipal‍laṅkai saha vāsanāya sabba kilaisānaṃ arahattamaggaina viddhaṃsitattā visiṭṭhaina cakkhādi indriyaṃ rakkhitau hautīti tassa gupitamindriyaihupaitanti thaumaiti|

ravisasimaṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaupacittanti sūriyamaṇḍala candamaṇḍalādīhi pādacak‍kalakkhaṇaihi abhivicitrapādaṃ| upasaddau caittha bhusaṭṭhavācakau| abhivicitraṃ sūriyamaṇḍala candamaṇḍalādiṃ pādacak‍kalakkhaṇavantaṃ vā| aittha ca ravīti sūriyau, sau hi ravati sīghaṃ gacchatīti ravīti vuc‍cati| rudhātu gatiyaṃ ṇi| vuttañhi pāthaiyyaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘candau ujukaṃ saṇikaṃ gacchati, sūriyassa pana ujukaṃ gamanaṃ sīgha’’nti| ṇvādi mauggal‍lānai pana ‘‘rusaddai i| au avādaisau| ravati gaj‍jatīti ravi| ādic‍cau’’ti vuttaṃ| abhidhāna ṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘ravanti aitaina sattā pabhāvitattāti ravī’’ti vuttaṃ| sasīti candau| sau hi sasati tiṇaina pāṇati jīvatīti sasau, sasapaṇḍitau| sasadhātu pāṇai a| sasarūpalakkhaṇamaittha atthīti sasīti vuc‍cati| atītai hi sasarājā brāhmaṇavaisaina āgantvā yācakassa sak‍karañ‍ñau aṅgārarāsimhi laṅghitvā jīvitaṃ paric‍cajitvā kāyaṃ adāsi| atha pana sak‍kau rājā tassa sasapaṇḍitassa jīvitaparic‍cajitassa jīvitadānaguṇau sakalakappaṃ pākaṭau hautūti pabbataṃ pīḷaitvā pabbatarasaṃ ādāya candamaṇḍalai sasarūpalakkhaṇaṃ likhitvā ṭhapaisi| tasmā candau sasīti laukaihi vauhariyati| tassa pana vitthārau sasapaṇḍitajātakai passitvā gahaitabbau|

atha  vā sasati hiṃsati uṇhaguṇanti sasīti vuc‍cati| sasudhātu hiṃsāyaṃ īpac‍cayau| maṇḍiyati vibhūsiyati paricchaidakaraṇavasaināti maṇḍalaṃ| maṇḍadhātu maḍidhātu vā vibhūsanai alau| atha vā maṇḍaṃ vibhūsanaṃ parisamantatau lāti gaṇhātīti maṇḍalaṃ| maṇḍasaddūpapada lādhātu gahaṇai k‍vi| ravinau maṇḍalaṃ ravimaṇḍalaṃ, sasinau maṇḍalaṃ sasimaṇḍalaṃ, ravimaṇḍalañ‍ca sasimaṇḍalañ‍ca ravisasimaṇḍalaṃ, maṇḍalasaddau ravisaddaipi yaujaitabbau| maṇḍalasaddassa dvandapadatau suyyamānattā| pabhutītiādi| sau hi ādyatthavācakau abyayanipātau| ravisasimaṇḍalaṃ pabhutiādi yassāti ravisasimaṇḍalappabhuti, pādacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ labbhati| lakkhiyati lakkhitabbaṃ aitaināti lakkhaṇaṃ, pādappa vattacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ, ravisasimaṇḍalappabhuti ca taṃ lakkhaṇañ‍cāti ravisasimaṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaṃ, taina upacittaṃ ativicitraṃ pādaṃ yassa sauti ravisasimaṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaupa cittau, sugatau| atha vā upacittaṃ ativicitraṃ ravisasi maṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaṃ pādacak‍kalakkhaṇaṃ assāti tathā, sugatau| taṃ|

suranarapūjitanti daivamanussaihi pūjitabbaṃ| suranti issariya kīḷādīhi dibbantīti surā, daivā| suradhātu dibbanai a| atha vā surati isati daivissariyaṃ pāpuṇāti viraucati cāti surā, daivā| suradhātu issariyadittīsu a| sundarā rāvācā aitaisanti vā surā, daivā| khandhasantānaṃ sakakammaṃ vā naranti niyantīti narā, manussā| naradhātu nayanai a| surā ca narā ca suranarā, taihi pūjitabbauti suranara pūjitau, sugatau| taṃ| sugatanti buddhaṃ| sau hi saubhanaṃ gataṃ gamanaṃ aitassāti sugatauti vuc‍cati| bhagavatau hi vainaiyyajanūpasaṅkamanaṃ aikantaina taisaṃ hitasukhanipphādanatau saubhanaṃ| tathā lakkhaṇānubyañ‍janappaṭi maṇḍitarūpakāyatāya  dutavilambitakhalitānukaḍḍhananippiḷinuk‍kuṭika kuṭilākuṭilatādi dausarahita mavahasita rājahaṃsausabha vāraṇa migarājagamanaṃ kāyagamanaṃ| ñāṇagamanañ‍ca vipula nimmala karuṇā sati vīriyādi guṇavisaisa sahita abhinīhāratau yāva mahābaudhi anavaj‍jatāya saubhanamaivāti|

atha vā sayambhūñāṇaina sakalampi laukaṃ pariñ‍ñābhi samayavasaina parijānantau ñāṇaina sammā gatau avagatauti sugatau, tathā laukasamudayaṃ pahānātisamayavasaina pajahantau anuppattidhammataṃ āpādaintau sammā gatau avagatauti sugatau, laukaniraudhaṃ nibbānaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayavasaina sammā gatau avagatauti sugatau, laukaniraudhagāminippaṭipadaṃ bhāvanābhi samayavasaina sammā gatau paṭipan‍nauti sugatau, sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti sugatautiādinā nayaina ayamatthau vibhāvaitabbau| atha vā sundaraṃ ṭhānaṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ nibbānamaiva vā gatau adhigatauti sugatau| yasmā vā bhūtaṃ tacchaṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ vainaiyyānaṃ yathārahaṃ yuttamaiva ca dhammaṃ bhāsati, tasmā sammā gadatīti sugatau dakārassa takāraṃ katvā| iti saubhana gamanatādīhi sugatau, taṃ sugataṃ| ādaranti sak‍kac‍caṃ, ādaranaṃ ādarau| āpubba daradhātu ādarai ṇa| taṃ ādaraṃ| namāmīti ahaṃ vandāmīti atthauti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| suciparivāritaṃ rāgādimala visuddhāhi ariyasāvakādiparisāhi parikkhāritaṃ kilaisamalavisuddhaṃ parikkhāravantaṃ vā, surucirappabhāhi suṭṭhu kantita sarīranikkhantabyāmamattaraṃsīhi karaṇabhūtaihi, rattaṃ nic‍caṃ rañ‍jitabbakāyaṃ, sirivisarālayaṃ pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñasaṅkhātalakkhisa mūhānaṃ  patiṭṭhānaṃ, gupitamindriyaihi gupitabbaihi saṃvaritabbaihi chahi cakkhādiindriyaihi, upaitaṃ samupaitaṃ saman‍nāgataṃ, ravisasimaṇḍalappabhutilakkhaṇaupacittaṃ sūriyamaṇḍalacandamaṇḍalādīhi pādacak‍kalakkhaṇaihi abhivicitrapādaṃ, abhivicitraṃ sūriya maṇḍalacandamaṇḍalādiṃ pādacak‍kalakkhaṇavantaṃ vā, suranarapūjitaṃ daivamanussaihi pūjitabbaṃ, sugataṃ buddhaṃ ādaraṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ ahaṃ namāmi vandāmīti|

sattarasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

18.

 maggauḷumpaina muhapaṭighāsādiul‍laulavīciṃ,

saṃsāraughaṃ tari tamabhayaṃ pārapattaṃ pajānaṃ|

tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ asamasaraṇaṃ aikatitthaṃ patiṭṭhaṃ,

puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ paramasukhadaṃ dhammarājaṃ namāmi||

18. aivaṃ sattarasamagāthāya sugataṃ vanditvā idāni saṃsāraughaṃ taritādīhi navahi guṇaihi thaumitvā vanditukāmau maggauḷumpainatyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ma bha na ta ta gaṇaihi ca garudvayaina ca racitattā sattarasakkharaihi yuttā mandak‍kantāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘mandak‍kantā ma bha na tata gā gau yugutvassa kaihī’’ti| tassā panāyamatthau| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ yugutvassakaihi catu cha sattayatīhi ma bha na ta ta gā magaṇa bhagaṇa nagaṇa tagaṇa tagaṇa garū ca gau garu ca cai yuttā, mandak‍kantāgāthānāmāti| yugutvassakaihīti aittha ca yugasaddassa maggaphalacatubbidhapurisayugaḷavācakattā yugasaddau catusaṅkhyāvācakau, utūnaṃ haimantasisira vassanta gimha vassāna sarada utuvasaina chabbidhattā utusaddau chasaṅkhyāvācakau| assasaddau sattasaṅkhyāvācakauti vaiditabbau| chappac‍cayaṭīkādīsu hi ‘‘assasaddassa sattasaṅkhyāvācakabhāvau vuttau’’ti|

tattha pana yau dhammarājā muhapaṭighāsādiul‍laulavīciṃ saṃsāraughaṃ maggauḷumpaina tari, abhayaṃ pārapattaṃ pajānaṃ tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ asamasaraṇaṃ aikatitthaṃ patiṭṭhaṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ paramasukhadaṃ taṃ dhammarājaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau| aitissaṃ pana atthak‍kamaina vaṇṇayissāmi| muhapaṭighāsādi ul‍laulavīcinti mauhadausataṇhādisaṅkhātaṃ mahātaraṅga khuddaūmikaṃ| muyhanti aitaina sampayuttadhammā, sayaṃ vā muyhati, muyhanamattamaivaitanti muhau, mauhau| muhadhātu vaikhittai ṇa| paṭihañ‍ñanti sampayuttadhammā, sayaṃ vā paṭihañ‍ñati paṭihañ‍ñanamattamaivaitanti paṭighau, dausau| paṭipubba hanadhātu hiṃsāgatīsu a| hanassa ghau| asanaṃ icchanaṃ āsā, isati icchatīti vā āsā, taṇhā| isudhātu icchāyaṃ a| issa ā| muhau ca paṭighau ca āsā ca muhapaṭighāsā, tai ādi yaisantaiti muhapaṭighāsādi, laubhādikilaisā labbhanti| aittha ca ādisaddaina māna diṭṭhi vicikicchā thina uddhac‍ca ahirika anauttappakilaisai ca akusalacittuppādai ca saṅgaṇhāti| uddhaṃ udakaṃ laulati ākulaitīti ul‍laulau, mahātaraṅgau| upubbalaula dhātu ummādanai ṇa| tīraṃ ulati gacchatīti vā ul‍laulau| uladhātu gatiyaṃ aulapac‍cayau| vimhayaṃ vicittaṃ cinautīti vīci, khuddakaūmi| vipubba cidhātu cayai k‍vi| ul‍laulau ca vīci ca ul‍laulavīci| kaisuci pautthakaisu ca kul‍laulavīcintipāṭhau dissati| na sau yuj‍jati, kasmā, kul‍lasaddassa uḷumpatthavācakattā| ul‍laulasaddauyaiva hi mahātaraṅgattha vācakau hautīti| vuttañhi abhidhānappadīpikāyaṃ ‘‘ul‍laulau tu ca kal‍laulau, mahāvīcisu kathyatai’’ti|

mūlapaṇṇāsaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘kul‍lau nāma taraṇatthāya kalāpaṃ katvā bandhau| pattharitvā baddhā pana padaracāṭi ādayau  uḷumpau’’ti vuttau| muhapaṭighāsādiaiva ul‍laulavīci| sā asmiṃ saṃsāraughai atthīti muhapaṭighāsādiul‍laulavīci| samāsantataddhitāyaṃ| pītisukhaṃ, mahāsan‍naṃ, rattidivantyādīsu viya| aivaṃ panaittha vacanatthau kātabbau| pīti ca sukhañ‍ca pītisukhaṃ, taṃ assa jhānassa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti pītisukhaṃ, jhānaṃ| mahantaṃ āsanaṃ mahāsanaṃ, tassa bhāvau mahāsan‍naṃ| ratti ca divā ca rattidivaṃ| rattidivaṃ kataṃ rattidivanti| atha vā ul‍laulavīci viya muhapaṭighāsādi ul‍laulavīci, saṃsāraughaṃ labbhati| upamā bahubbīhisamāsauyaṃ| yathā pana mahāsamuddai bhāyitabbā mahātaraṅgaūmivaigādayau ca saṃsumāra timi tipiṅgalādi macchamakārādayau ca aukiṇṇā haunti, aivaṃ mauhādikilaisā ca akusalacittuppādā ca taihi nibbattā bhayānakā bahū jātijarābyādhimaraṇadukkhādayau ca khandhādayau ca aukiṇṇā hauntīti vuttaṃ hauti| saṃsāraughanti saṃsārasaṅkhātaṃ aughaṃ samuddajalappavāhaṃ| saṃpunappunaṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ vā sarati gacchati pavattatīti saṃsārau| saṃpubbasaradhātu gatiyaṃ ṇa| khandhadhātuāyata nānaṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ pavattatīti atthau| taina vuttaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu –

‘‘khandhānañ‍ca paṭipāṭi, dhātuāyatanāna ca|

abbaucchin‍naṃ vattamānā, saṃsārauti pavuc‍catī’’ti||

sattai auttharitvā ajjhauttharitvā haiṭṭhā hanati haratīti aughau, jalappavāhau| avapubba hanadhātu haraṇai ṇa| hanassa ghau| avasaddau auttharaṇatthau| attani patitai sattai haiṭṭhā katvā hanaiti ausīdāpaitīti vā aughau, jalappavāhau| avapubba hanadhātu ausīdāpanai ṇa| avasaddau haiṭṭhābhāgatthau| aughau viyāti aughau, saṃsārau| atha  vā sattasaṅkhārai auttharitvā ajjhauttharitvā abhibhavitvā vaṭṭasmiṃ hanati haratīti aughau, auhanaiti ausīdāpaiti vaṭṭasmiṃ sattasaṅkhāraiti vā aughau, rāgādi kilaisā labbhanti| saṃsārauaiva aughau saṃsāraughau, taṃ saṃsāraughaṃ|

maggauḷumpaināti aittha maggaauḷumpaināti padacchaidau| aṭṭhamaggaṅgasaṅkhātaina kul‍laināti atthau| aittha ca maggau vuc‍cati aṭṭhaṅgikamaggau| magganti gacchanti buddhā ca ariyasāvakā ca agataṃ disaṃ nibbānanti maggau| aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| maggadhātu gatiyaṃ a| vuttañhi paṭisambhidāmaggai ‘‘aitaina maggaina buddhā ca sāvakā ca agataṃ disaṃ nibbānaṃ gacchantīti aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti| atha vā nibbānaṃ maggati gavaisatīti maggau, nibbānatthikaihi maggiyati gavaisiyatīti vā maggau, aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| ñāṇaina dassiyatīti atthau| maggadhātu gavaisanai a| vuttañhaitaṃ mahāvaggasaṃyuttaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘maggauti kainaṭṭhaina maggau, nibbānaṃ magganaṭṭhaina nibbānatthikaihi magganiyaṭṭhaina cā’’ti| atha vā maggiyati paṭipaj‍jiyatīti maggau, nibbānatthikaihi aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau paṭipaj‍jiyatīti atthau| uḷu vuc‍cati udakaṃ, tatau pāti rakkhatīti uḷupau, sauyaiva niggahitāgamavasaina uḷumpau, kul‍lau| uḷusaddūpapadapādhātu rakkhanai k‍vi| maggauaiva uḷumpau maggauḷumpau, maggasaṅkhātau vā uḷumpau tathā, maggasadisau vā uḷumpau tathā, maggau viya vā uḷumpau tathā, maggau ca uḷumpau ca maggauḷumpau| atha vā uḷumpauaiva maggau maggauḷumpau, uḷumpasaṅkhātau vā maggau tathā, uḷumpa sadisau vā maggau tathā, uḷumpau viya vā maggau tathā, uḷumpau ca maggau ca maggauḷumpauti aivaṃ rūpakasadisaupamā samupaikkhatthavasaina ca parapakkha sakapakkhatthavasaina ca vacanatthau vaiditabbau| taina maggauḷumpaina| tarīti atari| dhammarājā hi  aṭṭhamaggaṅganāvāya saṃsāraughaṃ taritvā nibbānatīraṃ pāpuṇāti| atītā buddhā pāpuṇiṃsu| anāgatā buddhā pāpuṇissantīti vuttaṃ hauti| vuttañhi mahāvaggasaṃyuttai

‘‘aikāyanaṃ jātikhayantadassī,

maggaṃ pajānāti hitānukampī|

aitaina maggaina tariṃsu pubbai,

tarissantiyaiva taranti augha’’nti| buddhavaṃsai ca

‘‘saṃsārasautaṃ chinditvā, viddhaṃsaitvā tayau bhavai|

dhammanāvaṃ samāruyha, tāraimi janataṃ bahu’’nti||

abhayanti jātijarābyādhimaraṇabhayādīnaṃ abhāvatau nibbhayaṃ| sau hi bhagavā natthi bhayaṃ aitassāti abhayauti vuc‍cati| atha vā abhayanti khaimaṃ| tañhi nibbānaṃ natthi jātiādibhayaṃ aitthāti abhayanti vuc‍cati| aitasmiṃ pana dutiyavikappai taṃ padaṃ pārantipadai visaisanaṃ| pārapattanti aittha pāranti saṃsāravaṭṭassa paratīraṭṭhaina vuc‍cati nibbānaṃ| tañhi udakaṃ pāti rakkhatīti pāraṃ, samuddapāraṃ| pādhātu rakkhanai arau| atha vā pāraiti samatthaiti taraṅgādayau vāraitunti pāraṃ| pāradhātu samatthiyaṃ a| pāraṃ viyāti pāranti vuc‍cati| nītatthaina pana aivaṃ vacanatthau kātabbau| jāti bhayādiṃ pāti rakkhati aitthāti pāraṃ, pāraiti samatthaiti aittha vā jātibhayādayau vāraitunti pāraṃ, nibbānaṃ| taṃ paj‍jittha adhigamitthāti pārapattau, dhammarājā| pārasaddūpapada padadhātu gatiyaṃ ta| atha vā muttau maucaiyyantiādinā nayaina pavattassa mahābhinīhārassa sakalassa saṃsāradukkhassa vā sabbañ‍ñutañāṇādiguṇānaṃ vā pāraṃ pariyantaṃ pattau gatauti pārapattau, dhammarājā| pārasaddau hi pariyanta vācakau| taṃ pārapattaṃ| pajānanti sabbasattānaṃ| taihi kammakilaisaihi pakāraina janiyati nibbattiyatīti pajāti vuc‍canti| taisaṃ pajānaṃ|

tāṇanti  tāyanaṭṭhaina tāṇaṃ| sattānaṃ saṃsāra bhayatau tāyati rakkhatīti tāṇau, dhammarājā| tādhātu rakkhanai yu| atha vā tāṇanti sattānaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭadukkhatau paripālanaṭṭhaina tāṇaṃ| sau hi dhammarājā sattānaṃ saṃsāravaṭṭadukkhatau dhammadaisanāya magga phalanibbānasukhaṃ dadantau tāyati pālaitīti tāṇauti vuc‍cati| taṃ tāṇaṃ| laiṇanti nilīyanaṭṭhaina laiṇaṃ, jātibhayādīhi pīḷitaihi sattaihi līyanti nilīyanti aitthāti laiṇau, dhammarājā| līdhātu nilīyanai yu| atha vā laiṇanti saṃsārabhayaṃ passantaihi nilīyanaṭṭhaina laiṇaṃ| sau hi dhammarājā saṃsāranissaraṇatthikaihi līyanti nilīyanti aitthāti laiṇauti vuc‍cati| taṃ laiṇaṃ| dhammarājā hi saṃsārabhayaihi pīḷitānaṃ sabbasattānaṃ tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ saraṇaṃ parāyanaṃ ṭhānaṃ hautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

asamasaraṇanti sabbasattānaṃ asadisaavassayanaṃ, natthi aitassa samau sadisau sīlādiguṇaināti asamau, dhammarājā| sarati gacchati avassayati aitthāti saraṇau, dhammarājā| saradhātu gatimhi yu| asamau asadisau hutvā saraṇauti asamasaraṇau, dhammarājā| sambhāva nākammadhārayasamāsauyaṃ| dhammarājā hi sabbasampattisukhānaṃ dāyakattā sabbasattānaṃ anañ‍ñasādhāraṇaṃ asamaṃ saraṇaṃ avassayanaṃ parāyanaṃ hautīti atthau| taṃ asamasaraṇaṃ| aikatitthanti nibbānapāragamanānaṃ aikatitthabhūtaṃ| taranti uttaranti aittha aitaina vā naj‍jasamuddādinti titthaṃ| naj‍jasamuddādīnaṃ pārimatīragamanatthaṃ autaraṇaṭṭhānaṃ labbhati| taradhātu autaraṇai tha| rassa tau, ikārāgamau| manussānaṃ aikaṃ titthaṃ aikatitthaṃ, taṃ viyāti aikatitthau, dhammarājā| yathā hi titthaina naj‍jādiṃ tarantā manussā pārimatīraṃ sukhaṃ pāpuṇanti, na atitthaina, aivaṃ saiṭṭhabhūtaina buddhatitthaina saṃsāraughaṃ  tarantā vainaiyyasattā nibbānapāraṃ sukhaṃ pāpuṇanti, na atitthaina añ‍ñatitthiyaina, tasmā dhammarājaṃ aikatitthauti thaumaiti| nītatthaina pana aikau saiṭṭhau hutvā nibbānapāraṃ taranti uttaranti aitthāti aika titthau, dhammarājā| khaimaṃ nibbānapāraṃ taritukāmā vainaiyyasattā aittha buddhatitthai daisitadhammaṃ sutvā daisitadhammānurūpaṃ paṭipaj‍jitvā saṃsāraughaṃ aṭṭhamaggaṅganāvāya chinditvā khaimaṃ nibbānapāraṃ sukhaṃ taranti, ititasmā aitthātipadaina niddiṭṭhau sau buddhau sabbasattānaṃ aikau saiṭṭhau titthau nāma| na añ‍ñatitthiyā aikatitthā nāma hauntīti atthau| taṃ aikatitthaṃ|

patiṭṭhanti sabbasattānaṃ mahādīpaṃ viya patiṭṭhaṃ ādhārabhūtaṃ| paṭisaraṇaṃ laiṇaṃ parāyananti atthau| patiṭṭhahanti aitthāti patiṭṭhau, dhammarājā| patipubbaṭṭhādhātu gatinivattimhi a| dhammarājā hi sabbasukhatthikānaṃ sattānaṃ mahādīpau viya patiṭṭhau paṭisaraṇau laiṇau parāyanau hautīti atthau, taṃ patiṭṭhaṃ| puñ‍ñakkhaittanti nibbānasukhatthikānaṃ puñ‍ñabījassa khaittabhūtaṃ| puñ‍ñatthikānaṃ puñ‍ñabījassa vapanaṭhānaṃ khaittabhūtaṃ vā, attanau kārakaṃ pavati saudhaitīti puñ‍ñaṃ, kusalaṃ| pudhātu saudhanai ṇyau| atha vā paraṃ puj‍jabhāvaṃ janaitīti puñ‍ñaṃ, sadā pūjitaṃ vā janaitīti puñ‍ñaṃ, kusalaṃ| pūjasaddūpapada janadhātu ṇyau| niruttinayaina saddasiddhi vaiditabbā| avasaisaṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ punāti saudhaitīti vā puñ‍ñaṃ, kusalaṃ| pudhātu saudhanai ṇyau| khittaṃ vuttaṃ bījaṃ tāyati rakkhatīti khaittaṃ, kaidārādi| khipasaddūpapada tādhātu rakkhanai k‍vi| bījāni vapantānaṃ manussānaṃ mahapphalabhāvakaraṇaina bījaṃ rakkhatīti atthau| atha vā khipanti vapanti aittha bījānīti khaittaṃ| khipadhātu pairaṇai khaipai vā ta| khaittaṃ viyāti khaittau| dhammarājā| nītatthaina pana puñ‍ñabījāni vapantānaṃ sattānaṃ mahapphala bhāvakaraṇaina  khittaṃ vuttaṃ puñ‍ñabījaṃ tāyati rakkhatīti khaittau| atha vā nibbānatthikaihi puñ‍ñabījāni khipiyanti vapiyanti aitthāti khaittau| dhammarājāti kātabbau| puñ‍ñānaṃ khaittaṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittau, dhammarājā| yathā hi rañ‍ñau vā amac‍cādīnaṃ vā sālīnaṃ vā yavānaṃ vā virūhanaṭṭhānaṃ rañ‍ñau vā amac‍cādīnaṃ vā sālikhaittaṃ yavakhaittanti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ dhammarājā sabbalaukassa puñ‍ñamahabbalabhāvakaraṇaina puñ‍ñānaṃ virūha naṭṭhānaṃ dhammarājaṃ nissāya sattalaukassa nānāppakārahita sukhasaṃvattanikāni puñ‍ñāni virūhanti, tasmā dhammarājā puñ‍ñakkhaittanti abhitthavitauti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ|

paramasukhadanti uttamaṃ nibbānasukhaṃ dadamānaṃ| vuttañhi ‘‘nibbānaṃ paramaṃ sukha’’nti| aittha ca paramasaddau uttamatthavācakau anipphan‍napāṭi padikau| atha vā paraṃ atiraikaṃ ariyaihi mānitabbanti paramaṃ, nibbānaṃ| parapubbamādhātu mānai a| pakāraina ariyaihi ramiyati aitthāti vā paramaṃ, nibbānaṃ| papubbaramudhātu ramanai ṇa| abhidhānaṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘paraṃ pac‍canīkaṃ māraitīti paramaṃ| pakaṭṭhabhāvai ramatīti paramaṃ| ṇau’’ti vuttaṃ| tassa sukhaṃ paramasukhaṃ| paramaṃ uttamaṃ vā sukhaṃ paramasukhaṃ| taṃ dadātīti paramasukhadau, dhammarājā| paramasukhasaddūpapada dādhātu dānai k‍vi| dhammarājā pana vainaiyyasattānaṃ dhammadaisanāya ca catupac‍cayappaṭiggahaṇaina ca nibbānasukhaṃ daitīti vuttaṃ hauti| taṃ paramasukhadaṃ| dhammarājanti buddhaṃ| sau hi laukiya laukuttaradhammaihi daivamanussānaṃ rañ‍jaiti tausaitīti dhammarājāti| atha vā pāramīdhammainaiva buddhattaṃ patvā rājā jātauti dhammarājāti| pāramīdhammainaiva jātau vā rājāti dhammarājāti ca vuc‍cati| taṃ dhammarājaṃ ahaṃ namāmīti sambandhau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| yau dhammarājā muha paṭighāsādiul‍laulavīciṃ mauhadausataṇhādisaṅkhātaṃ mahātaraṅga khuddakaūmikaṃ, saṃsāraughaṃ saṃsārasaṅkhātaṃ aughaṃ samuddajalappavāhaṃ| maggauḷumpaina aṭṭhamaggaṅgasaṅkhātaina kul‍laina tari atariṃ, abhayaṃ khaimaṃ, jātiādīnaṃ abhāvatau nibbhayaṃ vā, pārapattaṃ nibbānapāraṃ pattaṃ adhigataṃ, pajānaṃ sabba sattānaṃ, tāṇaṃ tāyanaṭṭhaina tāṇaṃ rakkhaṇaṃ| laiṇaṃ nilīyanaṭṭhaina laiṇaṃ nilīyanaṃ, asamasaraṇaṃ sabbasattānaṃ asadisaavassayanaṃ, aikatitthaṃ nibbānapāragamanānaṃ aikatittha bhūtaṃ, patiṭṭhaṃ sabbasattānaṃ mahādīpaṃ viya patiṭṭhaṃ ādhārabhūtaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ nibbānasukhatthikānaṃ puñ‍ñabījassa khaittabhūtaṃ, puñ‍ñatthikānaṃ puñ‍ñabījassa vapanaṭṭhānaṃ khaittabhūtaṃ vā, paramasukhaṃ uttamaṃ nibbānasukhaṃ dadamānaṃ, dhammarājaṃ buddhaṃ laukiyalaukuttara dhammaihi pajānaṃ rañ‍jāpanaṃ vā, pāramīdhammainaiva buddhabhāvaṃ jātaṃ buddhaṃ vā, ahaṃ tīhi dvāraihi namāmīti|

aṭṭhārasamavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

19.

 kaṇḍambaṃmūlai parahitakarau yau munindau nisin‍nau|

acchairaṃ sīghaṃ nayanasubhagaṃ ākulaṇṇaggijālaṃ|

duj‍jāladdhaṃsaṃ munibhijahitaṃ pāṭihairaṃ akāsi|

vandai taṃ saiṭṭhaṃ paramaratijaṃ iddhidhammaihupaitaṃ||

19. aivaṃ aṭṭhārasamagāthāya dhammarājaṃ vanditvā idāni sāvatthiyaṃ kaṇḍambamūlai iddhipāṭihairādīhi guṇaihi munindassa thaumaitvā vanditukāmau kaṇḍambaṃmūlaityādi gāthamāha| ayaṃ pana matanayayayagaṇaihi ca pañ‍ca yati chayati sattaya tīhi ca racitattā aṭṭhārasakkharaihi yuttā kusumitalatā vail‍litāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘mau tau nau yau yā kusumitalatā vail‍litākkhutvisīhī’’ti| ayaṃ panaittha yaujanā| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ akkhutvisīhi pañ‍cayati chayati sattayatīhi maugaṇau ca taugaṇau ca naugaṇau ca yaugaṇau ca yā yagaṇadvayañ‍ca cai yuttā, sā gāthā kusumitalatā vail‍litā kusumitalatā vail‍litā gāthā nāmāti| aittha ca akkhasaddassa pañ‍cindriyavācakattā akkhasaddau pañ‍casaṅkhyāvācakauti vaiditabbau| utūnaṃ haimantasisīravassantagimhavassānasaradavasaina chabbidhattā utusaddau chasaṅkhyāvācakauti| isisaddau vipassīādīnaṃ buddhānaṃ sattavidhattā sattasaṅkhyāvācakauti vaiditabbauti|

tattha pana parahitakarau yau munindau kaṇḍambamūlai nisin‍nau pāṭihairaṃ sīghaṃ akāsi, taṃ munindaṃ ahaṃ vandai vandāmīti sambandhau| atthak‍kamaina panaittha vaṇṇayissāmi| parahitakarauti attanā paraisaṃ sabbasattānaṃ hitaṃ atthaṃ karauntau| hinauti vaḍḍhati sukhaṃ aitaināti hitaṃ, atthaṃ payaujanaṃ vā| hidhātu vaḍḍhanai ta| taṃsamaṅginau laukiyalaukuttarasukhaṃ vaḍḍhatīti atthau| paraisaṃ hitaṃ parahitaṃ, taṃ karautīti parahitakarau, munindau| sau hi dīpaṅkarassa bhagavatau pādamūlai laddhabyākaraṇatau paṭṭhāya attanau sukhaṃ pajahantau sattalaukassa hitasukhatthāya kappasata sahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni samatiṃsapāramiyau pañ‍camahāparic‍cāgai ca karauntau sabbañ‍ñubuddhabhāvaṃ pattakālai ca pañ‍cacattālīsavassāni dhamma daisanāya sattalaukassa laukiyalaukuttarahita sukhaṃ karautīti parahitakarauti thaumaiti| yauti yau yādisau| munindauti buddhau| sau hi agāriya muni, anagāriyamuni, saikkhamuni, asaikkhamuni, pac‍caikamunivasaina pañ‍can‍naṃ munīnaṃ indauti munindauti vuc‍cati| kaṇḍambamūlaiti aittha kaṇḍaambamūlaiti padacchaidau| chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ bindāgamau| kaṇḍanāmikaina pasainadīkausalarañ‍ñau uyyānapālaina raupitassa saitambarukkhassa samīpaiti atthau| aittha ca kaṇḍanti tassaiva nāmaṃ| atha vā kaṃ vuc‍cati sukhaṃ, taṃ ḍaiti gacchati  pāpuṇātīti kaṇḍau, uyyānapālau| kaṃsaddūpapadaḍi dhātu gatiyaṃ ṇa| kaṃ vuc‍cati vā udakaṃ, taṃ rukkhaisu siñ‍canaṃ ṭināti jānātīti kaṇḍau, uyyānapālau uyyāna nāyakau vā| phalakāmaihi janaihi amiyati gamiyatīti ambau, saitambarukkhau| amadhātu gatiyaṃ bau| kaṇḍanāmikaina uyyānapālaina raupitau ambau kaṇḍambau, tassa mūlaṃ samīpanti kaṇḍambamūlaṃ, tasmiṃ kaṇḍambamūlai|

nisin‍nauti vasamānau| vimhāpaniyaṭṭhaina acchairaṃ| ayañhi acchairasaddau vimhayatthavācakau anipphan‍napāṭi padikau| atha vā ābhusau vimhayau carati pavattatīti acchairaṃ, pāṭihairaṃ| āpubba caradhātu gatiyaṃ a| caradhātussacchairādaisau| ākārassa rassau| andhassa pabbatārauhanaṃ viya nic‍caṃ na carati na pavattatīti acchairaṃ, pāṭihairaṃ| napubba caradhātu gatiyaṃ a| yathā hi andhassa pabbātārauhanaṃ nic‍caṃ na hauti, kadāciyaiva hauti, aivamaiva bhagavatau pāṭihāriyaṃ nic‍caṃ na hauti, kadāciyaiva hauti| tasmā taṃ acchairanti vuc‍cati| taina vuttaṃ naittiaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘yaṃ abhiṇhaṃ na pavattati, taṃ acchariya’’nti| atha vā accharaṃ paharituṃ yaugganti acchariyaṃ| yauggataddhitai ṇiyapac‍cayau| acchariyanti hi vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ iyasaddalaupaina acchairanti vuttaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ sīlakkhandhavaggaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘andhassa pabbatārauhanaṃ viya nic‍caṃ na hautīti acchariyaṃ| ayaṃ tāva saddanayau| ayaṃ pana aṭṭhakathānayau, accharāyaugganti acchariyaṃ, accharaṃ paharituṃ yuttanti atthau’’ti| nayanasubhaganti daivamanussānaṃ pasādacakkhussa saubhanapattaṃ| samavisamaṃ dassaintaṃ attabhāvaṃ naitīti nayanaṃ, pasādacakkhu| nīdhātu nayai yu| tassa subhaṃ saubhanaṃ gacchatīti nayanasubhagaṃ, pāṭihairaṃ| nayanasubha saddūpapadagamudhātu gatiyaṃ k‍vi| taṃ nayanasubhagaṃ|

ākulaṇṇaggijālanti  aittha ākulaaṇṇaaggijālanti padacchaidau| parikiṇṇa udakadhāra aggisikhāvantaṃ| atha vā parisamantatau kulaṃ pakiṇṇaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñamissakaṃ yamakaṃ yamakaṃ udakajālaaggijālavantanti atthau| ābhusau kulati udakaggijālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñamissakaṃ karautīti ākulaṃ, udakaggijālaṃ| āpubbakuladhātu karaṇai a| ābhusaṃ kulati bandhati añ‍ñamañ‍ñanti vā ākulaṃ| atha vā āsamantatau kulati yamakaṃ yamakaṃ udakaggijālaṃ pavattatīti ākulaṃ, udakaggijālaṃ| udakajālaṃ aggijālaina aggijālañ‍ca udakajālaina sammissau hautīti atthau| ananti jīvanti aitaina sattāti aṇṇaṃ, udakaṃ| anadhātu jīvāyaṃ a| nassa ṇau| tassa dvittaṃ| dvibhāvau vā| atha vā arati gacchati taṃ taṃ ṭhānanti aṇṇaṃ| aradhātu gatimhitau| tassa an‍nādaisau, dhātvantalaupau, n‍nassa ṇṇattaṃ| aṇṇassa dhāraṃ aṇṇaṃ| uttarapadalaupaina| kuṭilabhāvaina ajati gacchatīti aggi, ajadhātu gatiyaṃ i| jassa ggau| jalati dibbatīti jālaṃ, sikhaṃ| jaladhātu dittiyaṃ ṇa| agginau jālaṃ aggijālaṃ| aṇṇañ‍ca aggijālañ‍ca aṇṇaggijālaṃ, ākulaṃ parikiṇṇaṃ aṇṇaggijālaṃ assāti ākulaṇṇaggijālaṃ, yamaka pāṭihairaṃ labbhati| taṃ ākulaṇṇaggijālaṃ|

duj‍jāladdhaṃsanti duṭṭhu diṭṭhivādasaṅkhātajālaṃ viddhaṃsitaṃ| duṭṭhu jalati bandhati saṃsāravaṭṭaiti duj‍jālaṃ, diṭṭhivādajālaṃ| titthiya vādau hi natthi din‍nantiādinayapavattau saṃsāratau nissaritumappadānavasaina jālasadisattā duj‍jālanti atthau| dupubba jaladhātu bandhanai ṇau| taṃ dhaṃsati viddhaṃsatīti duj‍jāladdhaṃsaṃ, yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ| munibhijahitanti munibhi ajahitanti padacchaidau| tattha munibhīti sabbasammāsambuddhaihi| tai hi maunaṃ vuc‍cati sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ, taṃ aitaisaṃ atthīti munīti vuc‍canti| kāyamaunaiyya vacīmaunaiyya manaumaunaiyyaṃ aitaisaṃ atthīti vā  muni| assatthitaddhitai ṇīpac‍cayau| atha vā idhalauka paralaukaṃ attahita parahitaṃ hitāhitañ‍ca munanti jānantīti munīti vuc‍canti| taihi munībhi|

ajahitanti avijahitaṃ aparic‍cattaṃ vā, hātabbaṃ cajitabbanti jahitaṃ| hādhātu cāgai ta| na jahitaṃ ajahitaṃ, pāṭihairaṃ| taṃ ajahitaṃ| bhagavatau hi sāvatthinagarassa dakkhiṇadvārai kaṇḍambarukkhamūlai kataṃ pāṭihāriyañ‍ca bhagavā pana tasmiṃ pāṭihāriyāvasānai tāvatiṃsapuraṃ gatau, tasmiṃ pārichattakamūlamhi paṇḍukambalanāmakai silāsanai san‍nisin‍nau daivānaṃ abhidhammakathāya daisanā ca sabbasammāsambuddhaihi avijahitā dhammatāyaiva hautīti vuttaṃ hauti| buddhavaṃsaṭṭhakathāyañhi samatiṃsadhammatāpakāsanai sāvatthi nagaradvārai yamakapāṭihāriyakaraṇaṃ| tāvatiṃsai bhavanai abhidhammadaisanāti dhammatābhāvaina vuttaṃ| tasmā pāṭihairaṃ munībhijahitanti thaumaiti|

pāṭihairanti pac‍canīkapaṭiharaṇavasaina pāṭihāriyaṃ, paṭipakkhai titthiyai madditvā hinauti pavattatīti pāṭihairaṃ| paṭipubbahidhātu gatiyaṃ ṇau| hissa hairaṇidaisau| atha vā bhagavatau ca sāsanassa ca paṭipakkhai titthiyai harati apanaitīti pāṭihairaṃ, iddhividhañāṇaṃ| paṭipubbaharadhātu apanayanai ṇau| paṭiharati apanaiti sattānaṃ diṭṭhimānaupagatāni cittānīti vā pāṭihairaṃ| appasan‍nānaṃ pasādaṃ āharatīti vā pāṭihairaṃ| samāhitai cittai vigatūpak‍kilaisai katakic‍caina paṭipacchā haritabbaṃ pavattaitabbanti vā pāṭihairaṃ| paṭīti hi ayaṃsaddau pacchāti aitassa atthaṃ baudhaiti| tasmiṃ paṭipaviṭṭhamhi añ‍ñau āgacchi brāhmaṇautiādīsu viya| atha vā paṭipunappunaṃ udakaggijālaṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ yamakaṃ yamakaṃ hutvā harati pavattatīti pāṭihairaṃ, yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ| tañ‍ca pana iddhipāṭihāriya, ādaisanāpāṭihāriya, anusā sanī  pāṭihāriyavasaina tividhaṃ hauti| taisu pana idha iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ adhippaitanti| sīghanti khippaṃ āsuṃ lahuṃ vā| taṃ pana akāsītipadai kriyāvisaisanaṃ| saiti lahu hutvā pavattatīti sīghaṃ| sidhātu gatimhi ghapac‍cayau| dīghau| akāsīti akari|

aittha ca pāṭihāriyamaivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| bhagavā hi abhisambaudhitau sattamai saṃvaccharai sāvatthinagarassa dakkhiṇadvārai kaṇḍambarukkhamūlai daivānamindaina sak‍kaina āṇāpitaina visukammadaivaputtaina nimmitai dvādasayaujanai ratana maṇḍapai bhagavatā nimmitai yaujanappamāṇai ratanapal‍laṅkai ratanacaṅkamai ca nisīditvā tiyaujanikai dibbasaitacchattai dhārayamānai samantatau dvādasayaujanāya parisāya atthādānaparidīpanaṃ titthiyamaddanaṃ uparimakāyatau aggikkhandhau pavattati, haiṭṭhimakāyatau udakadhārā pavattati, haiṭṭhimakāyatau aggikkhandhau, uparimakāyatau udakadhārā| puratthimakāyatau aggikkhandhau, pacchimakāyatau udakadhārā| pacchimakāyatau aggikkhandhau, puratthimakāyatau udakadhārā, dakkhiṇaakkhitau aggikkhandhau, vāmaakkhitau udakadhārā| vāmaakkhitau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇaakkhitau udakadhārā| dakkhiṇakaṇṇasautatau aggikkhandhau, vāmakaṇṇasautatau udakadhārā| vāmakaṇṇasautatau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇakaṇṇasautatau udakadhārā| dakkhiṇa nāsikāsautatau aggikkhandhau, vāmanāsikāsau tatau udakadhārā| vāmanāsikāsautatau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇanāsikāsautatau udakadhārā| dakkhiṇaaṃsakūṭatau aggikkhandhau, vāmaaṃsakūṭatau udakadhārā| vāmaaṃsakūṭatau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇaaṃsakūṭatau udakadhārā| dakkhiṇahatthatau aggikkhandhau, vāmahatthatau udakadhārā| vāmahatthatau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇahatthatau udakadhārā| dakkhiṇapassatau  aggikkhandhau, vāmapassatau udakadhārā| vāmapassatau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇapassatau udakadhārā| dakkhiṇapādatau aggikkhandhau, vāmapādatau udakadhārā| vāmapādatau aggikkhandhau, dakkhiṇapādatau udakadhārā| aṅgulaṅgulaihi aggikkhandhau, aṅgulantarikāhi udakadhārā| aṅgulantarikāhi aggikkhandhau, aṅgulaṅgulaihi udakadhārā| aikaikalaumatau aggikkhandhau, aikaikalaumatau udakadhārā| laumakūpatau laumakūpatau aggikkhandhau, laumakūpatau laumakūpatau udakadhārā pavattati| chan‍naṃ vaṇṇānaṃ nīlānaṃ pītānaṃ lauhitakānaṃ audātānaṃ mañ‍jaṭṭhānaṃ pabhassarānaṃ ubbahanabhūtānaṃ yamakā yamakā vaṇṇā pavattanti| bhagavā caṅkamati, nimmitau tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| bhagavā tiṭṭhati, nimmitau caṅkamati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| bhagavā nisīdati, nimmitau caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| bhagavā saiyyaṃ kappaiti, nimmitau caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā| nimmitau caṅkamati, bhagavā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| nimmitau tiṭṭhati, bhagavā caṅkamati vā nisīdati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| nimmitau nisīdati, bhagavā caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā saiyyaṃ vā kappaiti| nimmitau saiyyaṃ kappaiti, bhagavā caṅkamati vā tiṭṭhati vā nisīdati vāti vividhaṃ yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ titthiyānaṃ maddanatthāya akāsīti| aittha ca pana yau kauci aivarūpaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ kātuṃ samatthau cai| sau āgacchatūti caudanāsadisattā vuttaṃ atthādānaparidīpananti| atthādānañhi anuyaugau, paṭipakkhassa atthassa ādānaṃ gahaṇanti katvā, tassa paridīpanaṃ atthādānaparidīpanaṃ| titthiyamaddananti pāṭihāriyaṃ karissāmāti kuhāyanavasaina pubbai uṭṭhitānaṃ titthiyānaṃ maddanaṃ| tañ‍ca tathā kātuṃ asamatthatā sampādanamaiva| tadaitaṃ padadvayaṃ yamakapāṭihāriyanti aitaina sambandhitabbanti|

saiṭṭhanti  pasatthataraṃ| tassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| paramaratijanti daivamanussānaṃ uttamaratiṃ janakaṃ| paramānaṃ uttamasādhūnaṃ cittaramanaṃ janāpaitaṃ vā, aittha ca paṭhamavikappai paramasaddau uttamavācakau anipphan‍napāṭipadikau nipātau| dutiyavikappai sādhuvācakau nipphan‍napāṭipadikau| daivamanussānaṃ paramaṃ uttamaṃ ratiṃ janaitīti paramaratijau, munindau| paramaratisaddūpapada janadhātu jananai k‍vi| atha vā pakāraina saddhammai ramantīti paramā, sādhū labbhanti| papubba ramudhātu kīḷāyaṃ a| taisaṃ cittassa ratiṃ ramanaṃ janaitīti paramaratijau, munindau| taṃ paramaratijaṃ| sau hi dhamma daisanāya ca pāṭihāriyaina iddhiyā sarīrasaubhagga sampattiādīhi ca daivamanussānaṃ cittassa abhiratiṃ abhik‍kantaṃ janaitīti paramaratijanti thaumaiti|

iddhidhammaihupaitanti iddhidhammaihi upaitanti padacchaidau| iddhi dhammaihīti acintaiyyaappamaiyyaihi buddhaiddhisaṅkhātaguṇaihi| dhammasaddau hi guṇai vattati buddhadhammātiādīsu viya| ijjhanaṃ samijjhanaṃ iddhi| ijjhati samijjhatīti vā iddhi| ijjhati samijjhati yathicchitatthau aitāyāti iddhi| idhadhātu vuddhiyaṃ i, tipac‍cayau vā| bhagavā aitāya iddhiyā yathicchitatthau iddhau vuddhau uk‍kaṃsagatau hautīti atthau| dhāraiti pakāsaiti taṃsamaṅginau dabbanti dhammau, guṇau| iddhiaiva dhammau iddhidhammau, taihi dhammaihi| upaitanti saman‍nāgataṃ| upagacchati sampaj‍jatīti upaitau, munindau| upapubbaidhātu gatiyaṃ ta| taṃ upaitaṃ|

iddhi pana adhiṭṭhānā iddhi, vikuppanā iddhi, manaumayā iddhi, ñāṇavipphārā iddhi, samādhivipphārā iddhi, ariyā iddhi, kammavipākajā iddhi, puñ‍ñavatau iddhi, vij‍jāmayā iddhi, tattha tattha sammāpayaugapac‍cayā ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhi cāti dasavidhā hauti| tattha pakatiyā aikau bahukaṃ āpaj‍jati| sataṃ  vā sahassaṃ vā āvaj‍jitvā ñāṇaina adhiṭṭhāti bahukau haumīti aivaṃ vibhajitvā dassitā iddhi adhiṭṭhāna vasaina nipphan‍nattā adhiṭṭhānā iddhi nāma| pakativaṇṇaṃ vijahitvā kumāravaṇṇaṃ vā dassaiti nāgavaṇṇaṃ vā supaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ vā yakkhavaṇṇaṃ vā…pai॰… vividhampi sainābyūhaṃ dassaitīti aivaṃ āgatā iddhi pakativaṇṇavijahanavikāravasaina pavattattā vikuppanā iddhi nāma| idha bhikkhu imamhā kāyā añ‍ñaṃ kāyaṃ abhinimmināti rūpaṃ manaumayanti iminā nayaina āgatā iddhi sarīrabbhantarai añ‍ñassaiva manaumayassa sarīrassa nipphattivasaina pavattattā manaumayā iddhi nāma| ñāṇuppattitau pubbai vā pacchā vā taṅkhaṇai vā ñāṇānubhāvanibbattā visaisā ñāṇavipphārā iddhi nāma| aittha ca āyasmatau bākulatthairassa ca āyasmatau saṃkic‍catthairassa ca āyasmatau bhūtapālatthairassa ca ñāṇavipphārā iddhi vaiditabbā| samādhitau pubbai vā pacchā vā taṅkhaṇai vā samathānubhāvaina nibbattā visaisā samādhivipphārā iddhi nāma| aittha ca āyasmatau sāriputtatthairassa ca āyasmatau sañ‍jīvatthairassa ca āyasmatau khāṇukauṇḍañ‍ñatthairassa ca uttarāya upāsikāya ca sāmāvatiyā upāsikāya ca samādhivipphārā iddhi vaiditabbā|

paṭikūlādīsu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī vihārādikā pana ariyā iddhi nāma| pakkhidaivādīnaṃ vaihāsagamanādikā kamma vipākajā iddhi nāma| yathāha ‘‘katamā vipākajā iddhi| sabbaisaṃ pakkhīnaṃ sabbaisaṃ daivānaṃ aikac‍cānaṃ manussānaṃ aikac‍cā nañ‍ca vinipātikānaṃ ayaṃ kammavipākajā iddhī’’tiādi| cak‍kavattiādīnaṃ vaihāsagamanādikā pana puñ‍ñavatau iddhi nāma| yathāha ‘‘katamā puñ‍ñavatau iddhi| rājā cak‍kavatti vaihāsaṃ gacchati saddhiṃ caturaṅginiyā saināya antamasau assabandhagaubandhapurisai  upādāya| jautikassa gahapatissa puñ‍ñavatau iddhi| jaṭilassa gahapatissa puñ‍ñavatau iddhi| ghausakassa gahapatissa puñ‍ñavatau iddhi| maiṇḍakassa gahapatissa puñ‍ñavatau iddhi| pañ‍can‍naṃ mahāpuñ‍ñānaṃ puñ‍ñavatau iddhī’’ti| vij‍jādharādīnaṃ vaihāsagamanādikā pana vij‍jāmayā iddhi nāma| aittha ca ādisaddaina ākāsai antalikkhai hatthimpi dassaiti| assampi…pai॰… vividhampi sainābyūhaṃ dassaiti tyādiṃ saṅgaṇhāti| taina taina sammāpayaugaina tassa tassa kammassa ijjhanaṃ tattha tattha sammā payaugapac‍cayā ijjhanaṭṭhaina iddhi nāma|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipau| vitthārau pana paṭisambhidāmaggai iddhikathāya ca visuddhimaggaṭṭhakathāya ca gahaitabbau| tāsu pana iddhīsu bhagavatau ṭhapaitvā vij‍jāmayiddhiṃ avasaisā atthīti vaiditabbā| atha vā bhagavatau tīhi vij‍jāhi ca aṭṭhahi vij‍jāhi ca saman‍nāgatattā sabbā buddhaiddhiyau santīti vij‍jāmayiddhipi labbhatīti daṭṭhabbā| taṃ munindaṃ ahaṃ vandai vandāmīti sambandhau|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipatthayaujanā| parahitakarau attanā paraisaṃ sabbasattānaṃ atthaṃ payaujanaṃ karauntau, yau munindau yau yādisau buddhau, kaṇḍambaṃmūlai kaṇḍanāmikaina pasainadikausalarañ‍ñau uyyānapālaina raupitassa saitambarukkhassa samīpai san‍nisin‍nau vasamānau acchairaṃ vimhāpanīyaṃ nayanasubhagaṃ daivamanussānaṃ pasāda cakkhussa saubhanapattaṃ ākulaṇṇaggijālaṃ parikiṇṇaudaka dhāraaggisikhavantaṃ parisamantatau kulaṃ parikiṇṇaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ña missakaṃ yamakaṃ yamakaṃ udakajālaaggijālavantaṃ vā, duj‍jāladdhaṃsaṃ duṭṭhuṃ micchādiṭṭhivādasaṅkhātaṃ jālaṃ viddhaṃsitaṃ, munibhijahitaṃ sabbasammāsambuddhaihi avijahitaṃ, pāṭihairaṃ pac‍canīka paṭihārakaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ udakaggiyamakaṃyamakaṃ pāṭihāriyaṃ vā, sīghaṃ khippaṃ akāsi akari, saiṭṭhaṃ pasatthataraṃ, paramaratijaṃ daivamanussānaṃ uttamaratiṃ janakaṃ uttamānaṃ sādhūnaṃ cittaramanaṃ janāpaitaṃ vā, iddhidhammaihi acintaiyyaappamaiyyaihi buddhaiddhi guṇaihi upaitaṃ saman‍nāgataṃ, taṃ munindaṃ ahaṃ sirasā vandai vandāmīti|

aikūnavīsatimavandanagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

20. munindak‍kau yvaikau dayudayaruṇau ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbau, vinaiyyappāṇaughaṃ kamalakathitaṃ dhammaraṃsīvaraihi| subaudhaisī suddhai tibhavakuharai byāpitak‍kittinañ‍ca, tilaukaikac‍cakkhuṃ dukhamasahanaṃ taṃ mahaisiṃ namāmi|

20. aivaṃ aikūnavīsatimāya gāthāya munindaṃ vanditvā idāni vinaiyyappāṇaughaṃ dhammadaisanāya catusac‍cadhammaṃ baudhanaiyyādīhi navahi guṇaihi thaumitvā munindak‍kaṃ vanditukāmau munindak‍kautyādigāthamāha| ayaṃ pana ya ma na sa ra ragaṇaihi ca aikagarunā ca chayatīhi ca sattayatīhi ca racitattā aikūnavīsatakkharā maighavipphuj‍jitāgāthāti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhi vuttaudayai ‘‘rasutvassaihi ymā na sa ra ra garū maighavipphuj‍jitā sā’’ti| ayaṃ panaittha yaujanā| yassaṃ paṭipādaṃ rasutvassaihi cha cha satta yatīhi ymā yagaṇamagaṇā ca na sa rara garū nagaṇa sagaṇa ragaṇa ragaṇa aikagaru ca cai yuttā, sā gāthā maighavipphuj‍jitā maigha vipphuj‍jitā gāthā nāmāti| aittha ca rasānaṃ kasāva titta madhuralavaṇa ambila kaṭukavasaina chabbidhattā rasasaddau chasaṅkhyāvācakauti daṭṭhabbau| utūnaṃ haimanta sisīra vasanta gimhavassānasaradavasaina chabbidhattā utusaddau chasaṅkhyāvācakauti| assasaddau sattasaṅkhyāvācakauti daṭṭhabbau| assasaddassa hi sattasaṅkhyāvācakabhāvau chandaṭīkāsu vuttauti|

tattha  pana dayudayaruṇau ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbau aikau yau munindak‍kau suddhai tibhavakuharai kamalakathitaṃ vinaiyyappāṇaughaṃ dhammaraṃsīvaraihi subaudhaisi byāpitak‍kitti nañ‍ca tilaukaikac‍cakkhuṃ dukhamasahanaṃ mahaisiṃ taṃ munindak‍kaṃ namāmīti yaujanā|

imissā gāthāya ca atthak‍kamainaiva sukhajānanatthaṃ vaṇṇayissāma, na saddak‍kamaina| dayudayaruṇautipadassa dayāudayaaruṇauti padavibhāgau kātabbau| mahākaruṇāsaṅkhātaudayapabbatuggatasūriyāruṇau| atha vā udayapabbatatau uggatasūriyāruṇau viya mahākaruṇā vāti atthau| aittha ca dayāsaddau karuṇāmaittāsu vattati| adayāti vuc‍cati nik‍karuṇatanti aittha hi karuṇāyaṃ| dayāpan‍nauti aittha maittāyaṃ vattati| maittācittataṃ āpan‍nauti atthau| idha pana karuṇā adhippaitā|

ayaṃ pana vacanatthau| dayati hiṃsati kāruṇikaṃ yāva yathādhippaitaṃ parassa hitanibbattiṃ na pāpuṇāti tāvāti dayā, mahākaruṇā| dayadhātu hiṃsāyaṃ a| dayati anuggaṇhāti pāpajanampi aitāyāti vā dayā| dayadhātu anuggahaṇai| dayati attanau sukhampi pahāya khaidaṃ gaṇhātīti vā dayā| dayadhātu gahaṇai| atha vā dayanti gaṇhanti aitāya baudhisattā saṃsāradukkhañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñutañāṇañ‍cāti dayā, mahākaruṇā| mahābaudhisattā hi buddhabhāvāya abhinīhārakaraṇakālai hatthagatampi arahatta phalaṃ chaṭṭaitvā saṃsārasāgaratau sattai samuddharitukāmā anassāsakaraṃ atibhayānakaṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ pacchimabhavai ca saha amatadhātupaṭilābhaina anaikaguṇasamalaṅkataṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇañ‍ca gaṇhantīti atthau| karuṇāmūlakā hi sabbai buddhaguṇā| taina vuttaṃ aṅguttaraṭīkāyaṃ ‘‘sabbaisañ‍ca buddhaguṇānaṃ karuṇā ādi, tan‍nidānabhāvatau’’ti|

api  ca dayanti anurakkhanti sattai aitāya, sayaṃ vā anuddayati anudayamattamaiva vā aitanti dayā| dayadhātu anurakkhaṇai apac‍cayau| udaiti vaḍḍhaitīti udayau, pabbatau| udidhātu pasavanai ṇa| āsamantatau ālaukaṃ karauntau uṇati gacchatīti aruṇau, sūriyaraṃsī visaisau| āpubbauṇadhātu gatiyaṃ ṇa| dayāsaṅkhātatau udayapabbatatau uggatasūriyassa aruṇau yassāti dayudayaruṇau| atha vā udayapabbatatau uggatasūriyāruṇau viya dayā mahākaruṇā yassāti dayudayaruṇau, munindak‍kau| yathā hi uggatasūriyassa aruṇau ālaukassa ādi hauti, aivaṃ baudhisattau dīpaṅkarabuddhassa pādamūlai karaupayātaṃ nibbānasukhamausaj‍ja mahākaruṇāya saṃsārasamuddatau sattai samuddharitukāmau buddhabhāvāya suduk‍karā dānādayau dasa pāramiyau ca pañ‍ca mahāparic‍cāgai ca kappasatasahassādhikāni cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni akāsīti karuṇā buddhabhāvāya ādi| tasmā buddhassaiva dayudayaruṇauti abhitthavituṃ arahatīti vuttaṃ hauti|

ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbauti sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasaṅkhātau vitthāra parimaṇḍalau| vitthāracak‍kavāḷamaṇḍalaṃ viya vipulārammaṇa sabbañ‍ñutañāṇavā| saṅkhatāsaṅkhatabhaidaṃ sabbaiñaiyyadhammaṃ jānātīti ñāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ| ñādhātu avabaudhanaiyu| visaisaina tanauti vitthāraitīti vitthiṇṇaṃ, ñāṇaṃ| vipubbatanudhātu vitthārai tapac‍cayau| tassa iṇṇādaisau| dhātvantassa laupau, asarūpadvittaṃ| atha vā vittharati pattharatīti vitthiṇṇaṃ| tharadhātumaivaittha visaisau| vamati parimaṇḍalākāraina gacchati pavattatīti bimbaṃ, ñāṇamaṇḍalaṃ| vamadhātu gatiyaṃ bau| assi| vassa bau|

ṇvādimauggal‍lānai pana ‘‘vama uggiraṇai ba| vamissa vassa bittaṃ| vamati uggiratīti bimbaṃ, sarīra’’nti vuttaṃ| taṃ pana bimbasaddassa sarīravācakai  yuttamaiva| na parimaṇḍalavācakai| idha pana bimbasaddassa parimaṇḍalavācakattā| ācariyā pana idha bimbasaddassa atthaṃ cak‍kamaṇḍalanti vadanti| taṃ pana vicāraitabbaṃ| kasmā, abhidhānappadīpikāyaṃ ‘‘bimbaṃ tu paṭibimbai ca, maṇḍalai bimbikāphalai’’ti ca| taṭṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘maṇḍalai paṭimaṇḍalai’’ti ca vuttattā| ñāṇaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ vitthiṇṇaṃ vitthāraṃ bimbaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ assāti ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbau| atha vā vitthiṇṇaṃ vitthāraṃ bimbaṃ cak‍kavāḷaparimaṇḍalaṃ viya vipulārammaṇaṃ ñāṇaṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ assāti ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbau, munindak‍kau| yathā hi cak‍kavāḷassa anantattā cak‍kavāḷamaṇḍalaṃ vipulaṃ mahantaṃ hauti, aiva maiva buddhassa sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ anantārammaṇattā vipulaṃ mahantaṃ| tasmā tassa ñāṇavitthiṇṇabimbauti thaumaitīti| yvaikauti yau aikauti padacchaidau| yaikautipi katthaci pāṭhau| tassa pana yau aikauti padacchaidau kātabbau| taṃ pana pāṭhadvayaṃ yuttamaiva| yvaikauti hi pāṭhai ādaisasandhi hauti| yaikauti pāṭhai pana laupasandhīti ayamaitaisaṃ visaisau| yau munindak‍kauti sambandhau|

aikauti asahāyau, adutiyakau aika buddhabhūtauti atthau| aittha ca aikasaddau añ‍ñasaiṭṭhāsahāya saṅkhyādīsu dissati| tathāhaisa sassatau attā ca laukau ca, idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ñanti itthaikai abhivadantītiādīsu añ‍ñatthai dissati| caitasau aikaudibhāvantiādīsu saiṭṭhai| aikau vūpakaṭṭhautiādīsu asahāyai| aikauva khau bhikkhavai khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyāyātiādīsu saṅkhyāyaṃ| idha pana asahāyai adhippaitau| ayaṃ panassa vacanatthau| asahāyau hutvā aiti gacchati pavattatīti aikau, adutiyakau hutvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ aiti jānāti paṭivijjhatīti vā aikau, munindak‍kau| aidhātu idhātu vā ñāṇagatīsu ṇvu| atha vā aikau bhagavā pabbaj‍jasaṅkhātaina aikau| adutiyaṭṭhaina aikau, taṇhāya pahānaṭṭhaina aikau, aikantavītarāgauti aikau, aikantavītadausauti aikau, aikantavītamauhauti aikau, aikantanik‍kilaisauti aikau, aikāya maggaṃ gatauti aikau, anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti aikautiādinā āgataina mahāniddaisanayainapaittha atthau vaiditabbau|

api ca anantacak‍kavāḷaisuyaiva aikauva buddhau aiti uppaj‍jati vikasati vāti aikau| vuttañhi aikanipāta aṅguttara pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ bhikkhavai anavakāsau, yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai arahantau sammāsambuddhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| ṭhānañ‍ca khau aitaṃ bhikkhavai vij‍jati, yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā aikauva arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau uppaj‍jaiyya, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti| tadaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘aikissā laukadhātuyāti dasasahassilaukadhātuyā| tīṇi hi khaittāni jātikkhaittaṃ āṇakkhaittaṃ visayakkhaittanti| tattha jātikkhaittaṃ nāma dasasahassilaukadhātu| sā hi tathāgatassa mātukucchiṃ auk‍kamanakālai nikkhamanakālai sambaudhikālai dhammacak‍kappavattanai āyu saṅkhāravaussaj‍janai parinibbānai ca kampati, kauṭisatasahassa cak‍kavāḷaṃ pana āṇakkhaittaṃ nāma| āṭānāṭiyaparitta mauraparittadhajaggaparitta ratanaparittādīnañhi aittha āṇā pavattati| visayakkhaittassa pana parimāṇaṃ natthi| buddhānañhi yāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tāvatakaṃ ñaiyyaṃ| yāvatakaṃ ñaiyyaṃ, tāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ñāṇapariyantikaṃ ñaiyyaṃ, ñaiyyapariyantikaṃ ñāṇanti vacanatau avisayau nāma natthi| imaisu pana tīsu khaittaisu ṭhapaitvā imaṃ cak‍kavāḷaṃ añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhā uppaj‍jantīti suttaṃ natthi| na uppaj‍jantīti pana atthi| tīṇi hi piṭakāni vinayapiṭakaṃ, suttantapiṭakaṃ, abhidhammapiṭakaṃ| tissau saṃgītiyau mahākassapatthairassa saṃgīti, yasatthairassa saṃgīti, mauggaliputtatthairassa saṃgīti, imā tissau saṃgītiyau  āruḷhai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai imaṃ cak‍kavāḷaṃ muñ‍citvā añ‍ñattha buddhā uppaj‍jantīti suttaṃ natthi| na uppaj‍jantīti pana atthī’’ti|

aittha ca aikissā laukadhātuyāti dasasahassi laukadhātuyāti idaṃ jātikkhaittaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| dasasahassilaukadhātu hi imaṃ laukadhātuṃ parivāraitvā ṭhitā hauti| tattakānaṃyaiva jātikkhaittabhāvau dhammatā vasaina vaiditabbau| imaisu pana tīsu khaittaisūti iminā dasasahassilaukadhātusaṅkhātai jātikkhaittai kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷasaṅkhātai āṇakkhaittai anantacak‍kavāḷa saṅkhātai visayakkhaittai cāti imaisu tīsu buddhakkhaittaisu itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu buddhā nuppaj‍jantīti dassaiti| tividhañhi buddhakkhaittaṃ dassaitvā imaisu pana tīsu khaittaisūti ādhārabhāvaina vuttaṃ, na niddhāraṇabhāvaina| kasmā, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ niddhāraṇīyapadassa abhāvatau| sati hi aṭṭhakathācariyau tassa padassa niddhāraṇatthai adhippaitai imaisu tīsu khaittaisu āṇakkhaittai ṭhapaitvā imaṃ cak‍kavāḷantiādi vuc‍caiyya| na| tasmā tassa padassa ādhāratthauyaiva gahaitabbau| pubbanissayā cariyaihi ca tassa padassa ādhārabhāvaina atthayaujanā katāti| tathaiva pana abhidhammañāṇavibhaṅgapāḷiyañ‍ca, tadaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca uparipaṇṇāsai bahudhātusuttapāḷiyañ‍ca, tadaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca naittipāḷiyañ‍ca tadaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca taṃ kāraṇaṃ āgatamaivāti| taṃ aṅguttaraṭīkāyaṃ pana ‘‘na uppaj‍jantīti pana atthīti na mai ācariyau atthi, sadisau mai navij‍jatītiādi| imissā laukadhātuyā ṭhatvā vadantaina bhagavatā kiṃpanāvusau sāriputta atthaitarahi añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā bhagavatā samasamau sambaudhiyanti aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ bhantai nauti vadaiyyanti vatvā tassa  kāraṇaṃ dassaituṃ aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau, yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai arahantau sammāsambuddhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyunti imaṃ suttaṃ āharantaina dhamma saināpatinā ca buddhakkhaittabhūtaṃ imaṃ laukadhātuṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñattha anuppatti vuttā hautīti adhippāyau’’ti vuttaṃ| tathaiva pana ñāṇavibhaṅgamūlaṭīkāyaṃ, uparipaṇṇāsabahudhātu suttaṭīkāyañ‍ca vuttaṃ| aittha ca imaṃ laukadhātuṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñattha anuppatti vuttā hautīti iminā buddhakkhaittabhūtatau itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu buddhānaṃ anuppatti dassitā hauti| añ‍ñatthāti hi sāmañ‍ñaina vuttaipi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaina imaisu tīsu khaittaisūti saddantarasan‍nidhānaina anantacak‍kavāḷabhūtau visaisatthau gahaitabbau| sāmañ‍ñavacanaupi hi saddau saddantarasan‍nidhānaina visaisavisayauti|

ayaṃ panaittha aparau nayau| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttai buddhā hi uppaj‍jamānā imasmiṃyaiva cak‍kavāḷai uppaj‍janti| uppaj‍janaṭṭhānai pana vāritai itau añ‍ñaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu nuppaj‍jantīti vāritamaiva hautītiaittha vākyai imasmiṃ yaivātipadai samabhiniviṭṭhau aivasaddau nivattāpanāvadhāraṇatthau| aitaina añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñaisaṃ buddhānaṃ uppattiṃ nivattaiti| yadipi hi añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñai buddhā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aivaṃ santaisu saṅkhatāsaṅkhatabhaidaṃ sabbadhammaṃ jānantau passantau amhākaṃ bhagavā añ‍ñacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñai buddhā uppaj‍jantīti aivaṃ vuc‍caiyya| na| taṇhaṅkarādayau hi aṭṭhavīsati sambuddhā itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu uppaj‍jantītivacanaṃ taipiṭakai buddhavacanai natthi, tathā aṭṭhakathā ṭīkāsu ca| tasmā itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu ananta cak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñai buddhā nuppaj‍jantīti daṭṭhabbā| vuttañhaitaṃ suttantamahāvaggaṭṭhakathāyaṃ mahāsamayasuttavaṇṇanāyaṃ –

‘‘athassa  aitadahausi mādisau buddhauyaiva sak‍kuṇaiyya| atthi pana katthaci añ‍ñau buddhauti anantāsu laukadhātūsu anantañāṇaṃ pattharitvā aulaukaintau añ‍ñaṃ buddhaṃ na addasa| anacchariyañ‍caitaṃ, yaṃ idāni attanā samaṃ na passaiyya| yau jātadivasaipi brahmajālasuttavaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttanayaina attanā samaṃ apassantau aggauhamasmi laukassāti appaṭivattiyaṃ sīhanādaṃ nadi| aivaṃ añ‍ñaṃ attanā samaṃ apassitvā cintaisī’’ti| tathaivaitaṃ| purābhaidasutta mahāniddaisaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca, taṃ mahāsamayasuttaṭīkāyañ‍ca aikissā laukadhātuyāti suttai āgatanayaina sabbatthaiva pana apubbaṃ acarimaṃ dvai buddhā nāma na hauntaiva| tainaivāha ‘‘anantāsu lauka dhātūsu…pai॰… na addasā’’ti saṃvaṇṇaiti|

apaṇṇakajātakaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘satthā upāsakā haiṭṭhā avīciṃ upari bhavaggaṃ paricchaidaṃ katvā aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu sīlādīhi guṇaihi buddhaina sadisau nāma natthi| kutau adhikatarau…pai॰… āhā’’ti vuttaṃ| imāsu dvīsu aṭṭhakathāsu ca anantāsu laukadhātūsūti ca aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsūti ca anantaaparimāṇa saddaina vuttaṃ| taihi dvīhi saddaihi ṭīkāvacanaina ca anantāsu laukadhātūsu añ‍ñaisaṃ buddhānaṃ anuppattiṃ dassaiti|

sammauhavinaudanīaṭṭhakathāya yaujanāyaṃ pana imaisu panātiādinā kauṭisatasahassa cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhā na uppaj‍jantīti ñāpaiti| mayaṃ pana aivaṃ vitak‍kayāma| añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷaiti itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu sabbaisupi tīsu buddhakkhaittaisūti atthai gayhamānai sati kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhā na uppaj‍jantīti viñ‍ñāyati| yadi pana añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷaiti itau añ‍ñasmiṃ jātiāṇākhaittabhūtai cak‍kavāḷaiti atthai  gahitai kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhānaṃ uppatti bhavaiyyāti viñ‍ñāyati| ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau, yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai arahantau sammāsambuddhā’’tiādipāḷiyā ca| ‘‘aṭṭhānamaitaṃ anavakāsau, yaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā dvai rājānau cak‍kavattī’’tiādipāḷiyā saddhiṃ kathanatau kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷatau buddhānaṃ anuppatti viya añ‍ñissā laukadhātuyā cak‍kavattirañ‍ñau anuppatti bhavaiyya| kathāvatthuppakaraṇai ca kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhānaṃ uppatti na paṭisaidhitā| tasmā āṇakkhaittabhūtatau kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhassa bhavaiyyāti ayaṃ amhākaṃ khanti| vīmaṃsaitvā gahaitabbanti vuttaṃ|

aittha ca yaṃ kathāvatthuppakaraṇai ca…pai॰… na paṭisaidhitāti vuttaṃ| taṃ pana kathāvatthupāḷiyaṃ natthaiva| imissā pana sabbadisākathāyaṃ paravādīnaṃ parikappavacanamattamaiva atthi| taina vuttaṃ tassaṃ pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘sabbadisā buddhā tiṭṭhantīti| āmantā| puratthimāya disāya buddhau tiṭṭhatīti| nahaivaṃ vattabbai…pai॰… puratthimāya disāya buddhau tiṭṭhatīti| āmantā| kin‍nāmau sau bhagavā kiṃjac‍cau kiṃgauttau, kin‍nāmā tassa bhagavatau mātāpitarau, kin‍nāmaṃ tassa bhagavatau sāvakayugaṃ, kau nāmau tassa bhagavatau upaṭṭhakau, kiṃdisaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraiti, kiṃdisaṃ pattaṃ dhāraiti| katarasmiṃ gāmai vā nigamai vā nagarai vā raṭṭhai vā janapadai vāti| nahaivaṃ vattabbai’’tyādi|

tadaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘idāni sabbadisākathā nāma hauti| tattha catūsu disāsu haiṭṭhā uparīti samantatau laukadhātusan‍nivaisaṃ sabbalaukadhātūsu ca buddhau atthīti attanau vikappasippaṃ uppādaitvā sabbadisāsu buddhā tiṭṭhantīti yaisaṃ…pai॰…  saiyyathāpi mahāsaṅghikānaṃ, tai sandhāya pucchā sakavādissa| paṭiñ‍ñā itarassa| puratthimāyāti puṭṭhau sakyamuniṃ sandhāya paṭikkhipati| puna puṭṭhau laddhivasaina añ‍ñalaukadhātuyaṃ ṭhitaṃ sandhāya paṭijānāti| kin‍nāmau sau bhagavātiādi sacai tvaṃ jānāsi| nāmādivasaina naṃ kathaihīti caudanatthaṃ vuttaṃ| iminā upāyaina sabbattha atthau vaiditabbau’’ti vuttaṃ| aittha ca catūsu disāsu…pai॰… saiyyathāpi mahāsaṅghikānanti iminā paravādīnaṃ laddhiṃ dassaiti| tattha pana catūsu disāsu ca haiṭṭhā ca upari ca iti aivaṃ samantatau laukadhātusan‍nivaisaṃ atthīti yaujanā| yaisaṃ…pai॰… mahāsaṅghikānanti yaisaṃ paravādīnaṃ laddhi hauti| kaisaṃ laddhi viya| mahāsaṅghikānaṃ laddhi saiyyathāpīti atthau| tai sandhāyāti tai paravādīpuggalai sandhāya ñāṇaina dhāraitvā sal‍lakkhaitvāti atthau| pucchā sakavādissāti sakavādissa sabbadisāsu buddhā tiṭṭhantīti pucchā hauti| paṭiñ‍ñā itarassāti paravādissa paṭijānanā hauti| puratthimāyāti puṭṭhauti sakavādinā puratthi māyātiādinā pucchitai sati| sakyamuninti sakyavaṃsa khattiyajātibhūtaṃ amhākaṃ gautamabuddhaṃ sandhāya| paṭikkhipatīti paravādīpuggalau apanaiti| puna puṭṭhauti sakavādinā puna puratthimāya buddhau tiṭṭhatīti pucchitai sati| laddhivasaināti attanau añ‍ñalaukadhātuyaṃ buddhau atthi ca uppaj‍jati cāti micchāladdhivasaina paṭijānātīti paravādī puggalau paṭijānāti| yadi pana añ‍ñalaukadhātuyaṃ buddhau tiṭṭhati| aivaṃ sati tassa nāmagauttādiṃ tvaṃ kathaihīti sakavādissa caudanāvacanaṃ dassaituṃ kin‍nāmau sau bhagavātiādimāha| iminā ca itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñaisaṃ buddhānaṃ abhāvaṃ dassaiti| taina vuttaṃ kathāvatthupāḷiyaṃ ‘‘kin‍nāmau sau bhagavā kiṃjac‍cau…pai॰… janapadaivāti| nahaivaṃ vattabbai’’ti|

aittha  ca pana sakavādinā kin‍nāmautyādinā pucchitai paravādīpuggalau attanau laddhiyā sabbalaukadhātūsu uppaj‍jamānānaṃ buddhānaṃ nāmādiṃ ajānantau apassantau na haivaṃ vattabbaiti paṭikkhipati| tasmā panaittha sakavādī puggalassa laddhiyā añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñaisaṃ buddhānaṃ uppaj‍janābhāvau viñ‍ñāyati| yadipi hi añ‍ñānantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñabuddhā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ| aivaṃ santaisu buddhassa matiṃ yathābhūtatau jānantau passantau mahāpañ‍ñau iddhimā dibbacakkhuādīhi chaḷabhiñ‍ñaihi sampan‍nau paṭisambhidā pattau satthārā din‍namātikānayaina kathāvatthupāḷiṃ daisaintau āyasmā mahāmauggaliputtatthairau añ‍ñānantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñabuddhānaṃ uppattiṃ kathaiyya ca| nāti| tasmā ayaṃ kathāvatthupāḷi ca tadaṭṭhakathāvacanañ‍ca añ‍ñānantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñabuddhānaṃ abhāvadīpanai sabbaṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu vuttavacanaihi daḷhataraṃ padhānavacanamaiva hautīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

na pana buddhāaiva añ‍ñānantacak‍kavāḷaisu na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ, atha khau laddhabyākaraṇā mahābaudhisattā ca pac‍caikabaudhi sattā aggasāvakamahāsāvakabaudhisattā paripākindriyā sāvakabaudhisattā ca buddhakkhaittabhūtai imasmiṃyaiva cak‍kavāḷai uppaj‍janti, na añ‍ñattha| kasmā, taisu buddhānaṃ anuppan‍nattā, taisaṃ pana abhinīhārau pāramīsambharaṇaṃ ñāṇaparipācanañ‍ca tasmiṃaiva samijjhati, na añ‍ñattha| taina vuttaṃ mahāpadāna suttaṭīkāyaṃ –

‘‘purimabuddhānaṃ mahābaudhisattānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānañ‍ca nibbattiyā sāvakabaudhisattānaṃ sāvakabaudhiyā abhinīhārau sāvakapāramiyā sambharaṇaṃ paripācanañ‍ca buddhakkhaittabhūtai imasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai jambudīpaiaiva ijjhati, na añ‍ñatthā’’ti| tathaivaitaṃ aikanipātaaṅguttaraṭīkāyañ‍cāti|

aitarahi  pana aparai ācariyā ‘‘āṇakkhaittabhūtatau kauṭisatasahassacak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu buddhā uppaj‍jantī’’ti vadanti| taisaṃ pana vacanaṃ ayuttamaiva| kasmā, aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttai añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷaiti aittha añ‍ñasaddassa ṭhapaitvā imaṃ cak‍kavāḷantipadatau añ‍ñassa apādānassa asambhavatau ca anantacak‍kavāḷaisu bahūsu buddhaisu uppan‍naisu buddhassa anacchariyattā ca| acchariyamanussau hi sammāsambuddhau| vuttañhi aikanipāta aṅguttarapāḷiyaṃ

‘‘aikapuggalau bhikkhavai laukai uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati acchariyamanussau| katamau aikapuggalau, tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| ayaṃ khau bhikkhavai aikapuggalau laukai uppaj‍jati acchariyamanussau’’ti| añ‍ñaṃ pana kāraṇaṃ mayā haiṭṭhā vuttanayainapi viñ‍ñūhi viñ‍ñātabbanti| añ‍ñai pana ācariyā buddhaapadānapāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘disā dasavidhā laukai, yāyatau natthi antakaṃ|

tasmiñ‍ca disābhāgamhi, buddhakkhaittai asaṅkhayā|

pabhā pakittitā mayhaṃ, yamakā raṃsivāhanā|

aitthantarai raṃsijālaṃ, ālaukau vipulau bhavai’’ti||

vuttāsu dvīsu gāthāsu paṭhamagāthāya atthaṃ buddhassa anādhippāyaina gahaitvā micchāñāṇaina añ‍ñānantacak‍kavāḷaisu uppaj‍jamānā buddhā asaṅkhayāti vadanti| taisaṃ panaitaṃ ayuttamaiva asundaramaiva| kasmā, pāḷiyā ca aṭṭhakathāṭīkāvacanaihi ca viruddhattā ca tassā gāthāya atthaggahaṇassa micchattā ca| imāya hi pāḷiyā gāthāya vā añ‍ñānantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñai buddhā uppaj‍jantīti atthai gayhamānai aṭṭhakathāsu imaisu pana tīsu khaittaisu ṭhapaitvā imaṃ cak‍kavāḷaṃ añ‍ñasmiṃ cak‍kavāḷai buddhā uppaj‍jantīti suttaṃ natthi, na uppaj‍jantīti pana atthīti vuttavacanaina ca, tīṇi hi  piṭakāni…pai॰… imā tissau saṃgītiyau āruḷhai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai…pai॰… suttaṃ natthi| na uppaj‍jantīti pana atthīti vuttavacanaina ca ujukaṃ virujjhati ca| bhaddantamahā buddhaghausādayau aṭṭhakathācariyā taṃ pāḷiṃ na passantīti attanau duggahitaina aṭṭhakathācariyānaṃ abbhakkhānaṃ āpaj‍jati ca| kiṃ panaitai tipiṭakadharā tikkhavisadajavana gambhīrādiñāṇikā aṭṭhakathācariyā na passaiyyuṃ| passaiyyuṃ aiva| tasmā ayuttamaiva| aṭṭhakathāsu pana āgataṃ suttaṃ natthītiādivacanamaiva yuttataraṃ hautīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aittha ca buddhakkhaittaitipadaṃ tasmiñ‍ca disābhāgamhītipadai visaisanaṃ, asaṅkhayātipadaṃ dutiyagāthāya pabhātipadassa visaisananti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ayaṃ panaittha yaujanā| laukai cak‍kavāḷalaukai dasavidhā dasa kauṭṭhāsā disā haunti| tattha kauṭṭhāsai yāyatau yāyantassa gacchantassa mai antakaṃ pariyausānaṃ natthi| buddhakkhaittai buddhassa dasasahassisaṅkhātajātikkhaittabhūtai tasmiñ‍ca disābhāgamhi mayhaṃ asaṅkhayā saṅkhārahitā yamakā yugaḷā yugaḷā hutvā raṃsivāhanā raṃsivahantā raṃsimuñ‍camānā vā pabhā cak‍karatanamaṇiratanādīnaṃ ālaukā pakittitā pākaṭā pattharitā vā| aitthantarai dasasahassacak‍kavāḷantarai raṃsijālaṃ raṃsisamūhaina vipulau bahutarau ālaukau bhavai ahausinti| ayamatthau cak‍kindābhisirīsaddhamma dhajamahādhammarājādhirājagurūtiladdhalañchitaina ācariyabudhaina likhitauti daṭṭhabbau|

bhagavā hi imāhi gāthāhi cak‍kavattirājakālai mayhaṃcak‍karatanamaṇīratanādīnaṃ asaṅkhayā pabhā buddhakkhaittabhūtai dasasahassicak‍kavāḷai pākaṭā pattharitāti dassaiti| na anantacak‍kavāḷaisu uppaj‍janabuddhānaṃ asaṅkhayabhāvanti|

vuttañhaitaṃ  buddhaapadānaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘disā dasavidhā laukaiti cak‍kavāḷalaukai dasavidhā dasakauṭṭhāsā disā haunti| tattha kauṭṭhāsai yāyatau yāyantassa gacchantassa antakaṃ natthīti atthau| cak‍kavattikālai tasmiṃ mayā gataṭṭhānai disābhāgai vā buddhakkhaittai buddhavisayā asaṅkhayā saṅkhārahitā| pabhā pakittitāti tadā cak‍kavatti rājakālai mayhaṃ pabhā cak‍karatanamaṇiratanādīnaṃ pabhā ālaukā yamakā yugaḷā yugaḷā hutvā raṃsivāhanā raṃsimuñ‍camānā pakittitā pākaṭā| aitthantarai dasasahassacak‍kavāḷantarai raṃsijālaṃ raṃsisamūhaṃ ālaukau vipulau bahutarau bhavai ahausinti atthau’’ti| aittha ca paṭhamagāthāya pubbaḍḍhassa aikavākyabhāvatau disā dasavidhā laukaiti cak‍kavāḷalaukai…pai॰… antakaṃ natthīti atthauti vuttaṃ| aparaḍḍhassa dutiyagāthāyaṃ antaugadhattā cak‍kavattikālai…pai॰… asaṅkhayā saṅkhārahitā pabhā pakittitāti tadā cak‍kavattikālai…pai॰… bhavai ahausinti atthauti vuttaṃ| tasmā asaṅkhayātipadaṃ dutiyagāthāya pabhātipadassa visaisananti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

jinālaṅkāraṭīkāyaṃ pana aivamassa sabbadhammaisu appaṭihata ñāṇācāraṃ dassaitvā idāni laukai tādisassa añ‍ñassa abhāvaṃ dassaintau āha|

‘‘anantasattaisu ca laukadhātusu,

aikauva sabbaipi samā na tainā’’ti||

tattha anantalaukadhātūsu aikaikissaṃ laukadhātuyā anantasattaisu taina tathāgataina samau aikaupi natthi| sacai aikau pana samau na bhavaiyya| sabbai sattā aikajjhaṃ hutvā samā bhavaiyyunti cai| tathāpi natthi, sabbai aikajjhaṃ hutvāpi iminā aikakaina samā na hauntīti adhippāyauti  vuttaṃ| aittha ca anantalaukadhātusūtiādinā itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñaisu anantacak‍kavāḷaisu añ‍ñaisaṃ buddhānaṃ abhāvaṃ dassaiti| aivaṃ pana mayā pathavaujaṃ uddharitvā dassitau viya dassitaihi atidaḷhataraihi sādhakavacanaihi itau cak‍kavāḷatau añ‍ñacak‍kavāḷaisu buddhā uppaj‍jantīti micchāvādau sūriyau andhakāraṃ paṭihanati viya paṭihaniyati apaniyatīti saddhahakaṃ daṭṭhabbauti|

munindak‍kauti muninda ak‍kauti padacchaidau| buddhādic‍cauti atthau| sabbadhammai manati jānātīti muni| manadhātu ñāṇai i| makārai assuttaṃ| atha vā munāti sabbadhammai saka sāmañ‍ñalakkhaṇādinā paṭivijjhatīti muni| munati hitāhitaṃ paricchindatīti vā muni, bhagavā| munadhātu paṭivijjhanai, catusac‍cadhammai munāti jānātīti vā maunaṃ| catumaggañāṇaṃ| vuttañhi saṃyuttaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘maunanti catumaggañāṇaṃ| tañhi munātīti maunaṃ, catusac‍cadhammai jānātīti atthau’’ti| maunaṃ assa atthīti muni, bhagavā| muni ca sau indati vainaiyyasattai dhammaina abhibhavati indau cāti munindau, bhagavā| atha vā munīnaṃ pañ‍camunīnaṃ indauti munindau, bhagavā| na kamati na gacchati pādaina attanau puñ‍ñajavimānaina gacchatīti ak‍kau, ādic‍cau| napubba kamudhātu gatiyaṃ k‍vi| dhātvantalaupau| kassa dvittaṃ| atha vā yugandharubbaidhappamāṇai ākāsai arati gacchatīti ak‍kau| aradhātu gamanai kapac‍cayau| kassa dvittaṃ| taṃ taṃ rāsitau taṃ taṃ rāsiṃ arati yātīti vā ak‍kau| atha vā mahantajutatāya ak‍kiyati abhitthaviyati tappasan‍naihi janaihīti ak‍kau, ādic‍cau| ak‍kadhātu thavanai a| munindau ca sau ak‍kau cāti munindak‍kau, ak‍kau ādic‍cau viya vā munindauti munindak‍kau, buddhau| yathā hi sūriyau attanau  sahassaraṃsiyā aikacak‍kavāḷai timirandhakāraṃ vidhamaitvā dinai dinai navayaujanasatasahassappadaisaṃ kauṭisahassappadaisaṃ vā dasadisaṃ vā aubhāsaitvā viraucamānau carati, aivamaiva bhagavāpi attanau saddhammaraṃ siyā buddhakkhaittabhūtaisu dasasahassacak‍kavāḷaisu vinaiyyānaṃ avij‍jandhakāraṃ vidhamaitvā dinai dinai tasmiṃ tasmiṃ disābhāgai vainaiyyasattasamūhaṃ catusac‍cadhammaubhāsaina jautayamānau viraucanaṃ caratīti ṭhānānurūpaṃ munindak‍kauti thaumaiti| ayaṃ samāsaupamā hīnūpamāti daṭṭhabbā|

suddhaiti rāgādimalaihi visuddhai| sujjhati visujjhatīti suddhau, tibhavau| sudhadhātu saucaiyyaiti| tibhava kuharaiti tibhavasaṅkhātakamalasarai| kamalasaraṃ viya tibhavasmiṃ vā bhavanti sattā aitthāti bhavau| bhūdhātu sattāyaṃ ṇa| tayau bhavā tibhavā, kāmarūpārūpabhūmi labbhati| kuṃ vuc‍cati udakaṃ, taṃ haratīti kuharau| kupubbaharadhātu haraṇai a| tayau bhavā ca tai kuharau cāti tibhavakuharau, kuharau viya tayau bhavāti vā tathā| tasmiṃ tibhavakuharai| kamala kathitanti uppalanti kathaitabbaṃ kaṃ jalaṃ alayati vibhūsayatīti kamalaṃ, uppalaṃ| kaṃsaddūpapada aladhātu vibhūsanai a| atha vā kamiyati icchiyatīti kamalaṃ| kamudhātu icchāyaṃ alapac‍cayau| kamalanti uppalanti kathiyatīti kamalakathitaṃ| vinaiyyappāṇaughanti vinaiyyapāṇaaughanti padavibhāgau| vinaitabbasattasamūhanti atthau| vinaitabbau damaitabbau satthārāti vinaiyyau, sattau| vipubbanidhātu damanai ṇyau| paṇanti jīvanti sattā anaināti pāṇaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ| paṇadhātu jīvāyaṃ ṇa| taṃ assa atthi tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti pāṇau, sattau| assatthitaddhitai ṇapac‍cayau| vinaiyyau ca sau pāṇau cāti vinaiyyappāṇau| avayavaina  saha hanati gacchati pavattatīti aughau, samūhau| avapubba hanadhātu gatiyaṃ ṇau| avassa au| hanassa ghau| vinaiyyappāṇānaṃ aughau samūhauti vinaiyyappāṇaughau, taṃ|

dhammaraṃsivaraihīti uttamadhammasaṅkhātaraṃsīhi| aittha ca dhammauti yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānai sattai catūsu apāyaisu vaṭṭadukkhaisu ca apatamānai katvā dhāraitīti dhammau| dharadhātu dhāraṇai rammapac‍cayau| dhāraiti ca apāyādinibbattakakilaisaṃ viddhaṃsaitīti atthau| caturiyamaggau ca nibbānañ‍ca nippariyāyatau labbhati| kasmā, ariyamaggassa kilaisānaṃ samucchaidappahānavasaina samucchaidakattā, nibbānassa ca nissaraṇappahānavasaina tassa ārammaṇabhāvaṃ patvā tadatthasiddhihaituttā| caturiyaphalañ‍ca pariyattidhammau ca pariyāyatau labbhati| kasmā, phalassa maggaina viddhaṃsi tabbānaṃ kilaisānaṃ paṭippassambhanappahānavasaina maggānukūlappa vattattā| pariyattidhammassa ca tadaṅgappahānavasaina tadadhigama haituttā| atha vā kilaisai dhunāti kampati calati viddhaṃsaitīti dhammau, caturiyamaggau| dhunadhātu kampanai rammau| ariyaihi dhāriyati paṭivijjhiyati sacchikariyati ārammaṇa karaṇavasaināti dhammau, nibbānaṃ| dharadhātu ñāṇai| dhaṃsaiti paṭippassambhaiti maggappahīnakilaisavāsananti dhammau, ariyaphalaṃ| dhaṃsadhātu viddhaṃsanai| attanau sabhāvaṃ dhāraiti ca dhāriyati ca paṇḍitaihi na bālaihīti dhammau, pariyattidhammau| dhammau ca dhammau ca dhammau ca dhammau cāti dhammau aika saisanayaina, dasavidhau dhammau labbhati| vuttañhaitaṃ chattavimānai –

‘‘rāgavirāgamanaijamasaukaṃ,

dhammamasaṅkhatamappaṭikūlaṃ|

madhuramimaṃ paguṇaṃ suvibhattaṃ,

dhammamimaṃ saraṇattamupaihī’’ti||

ayaṃ  panaittha yaujanā| māṇava rāgavirāgaṃ rāgavigataṃ imaṃ dhammaṃ maggadhammañ‍ca anaijaṃ aijāsaṅkhātataṇhāvirahitaṃ asaukaṃ saukavirahitaṃ imaṃ dhammaṃ phaladhammañ‍ca, asaṅkhataṃ saṅkhatavirahitaṃ imaṃ dhammaṃ nibbānadhammañ‍ca| appaṭikūlaṃ aviraudhanaṃ madhuraṃ atthabyañ‍jana sampan‍nattā madhuraṃ, paguṇaṃ pakaṭṭhaguṇaṃ suvibhattaṃ suṭṭhu vibhajitabbaṃ, imaṃ dhammaṃ pariyattidhammañ‍ca saraṇattaṃ saraṇabhāvaṃ upaihi upagacchāhīti|

aittha hi kāmarāgādibhaidau sabbaupi rāgau viraj‍jati pahiyati aitaināti rāgavirāgauti maggau kathitau| aijāsaṅkhātāya taṇhāya antaunijjhānalakkhaṇassa ca saukassa taduppattiyā sabbasau parikkhīṇattā anaijama saukanti phalaṃ kathitaṃ| kainaci pac‍cayaina asaṅkhatattā dhammamasaṅkhatanti nibbānaṃ kathitaṃ| aviraudhadīpanatau pana atthabyañ‍janassa sampan‍natāya pakaṭṭhaguṇavibhāvanatau suṭṭhu vibhajitattā ca appaṭikūlantiādinā sabbaupi pariyatti dhammau kathitau| vuttañhi tadaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘aittha hi rāgavirāganti maggau kathitau| anaijamasaukanti phalaṃ| dhammamasaṅkhatanti nibbānaṃ| appaṭikūlaṃ madhuramimaṃ paguṇaṃ suvibhattanti piṭakattayaina vibhattā sabbadhammakkhandhā’’ti| idha pana sāmañ‍ñatau dasavidhau dhammau| visaisatau pana pariyatti dhammau adhippaitau| kasmā, subaudhaisīti vuc‍camānattā| raṃsīti rasiyanti assādiyantīti raṃsī, rasanti assādainti sattā aitāyāti vā raṃsī, rasadhātu assādanai a ca ī ca| atha vā rasiyati sinaihiyati taṃ janaihīti raṃsī| rasadhātu sinaihai| niggahītāgamau|

varauti uttamau, varitabbau patthitabbauti varau, varati nivāraiti hīnanti vā varau| varadhātu patthanai nivāraṇai vā a| dhammau ca sau raṃsi cāti dhammaraṃsi, dhammassa vā raṃsi dhammaraṃsi| ayaṃ pana samāsau abhaidūpacāraina vuttau| varau ca sau dhammaraṃsi cāti dhammaraṃsivarau, taihi dhammaraṃsivaraihi| subaudhaisīti catusac‍cadhammaṃ paṭivijjhayamānaina suṭṭhu pabaudhaisi vikāsaitīti atthau| yathā hi sūriyau attanau raṃsiyā phusayitvā kuhara saṅkhātai sarai jātaṃ uppalaṃ vikāsaiti, aivamaiva bhagavāpi attanau dhammadaisanāsaṅkhātaraṃsiyā kamalasarasaṅkhātai bhavattayai vasantaṃ puñ‍ñūpanissayaṃ paṭivijjhārahaṃ kamala saṅkhātaṃ vinaiyyappāṇaughaṃ catusac‍cadhammaṃ paṭivijjhayamānau phaladhammaina pabaudhaisi vikāsaitīti vuttaṃ hauti|

byāpitak‍kittinañ‍cāti tilaukai patthaṭaparikittanaṃ| sīlādiguṇaihi patthaṭathutighausikanti atthau| casaddau caittha padapūraṇamattauyaiva| visaisaina vividhaṃ vā āpati vaḍḍhatīti byāpitau| vipubbaāpadhātu byāpanai vaḍḍhanai vā ta| byāpanaṃ vā byāpitaṃ| kittiyatai abhitthaviyatai kittinaṃ| bhāvasādhanauyaṃ| thutikriyā labbhati| kittiyati abhitthaviyati daivamanussaihīti kittinaṃ, thutighausau| kittadhātu abhitthavanai yu| byāpitau patthaṭau kittanau thutighausau yassa sauti byāpitak‍kittinau, munindak‍kau| taṃ| bhagavatau hi tilaukai daivamanussaihi sīlādiguṇaihi ca arahaṃ sammāsambuddhautyādīhi guṇaihi ca thaumitau kittisaddau yāva bhavaggaṃ abbhuggacchatīti byāpitak‍kittinanti thaumaitīti vuttaṃ hauti|

tilaukaikac‍cakkhunti tilaukai aikacakkhūti padacchaidau| kāmarūpa arūpasaṅkhātānaṃ tilaukānaṃ aikaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhubhūtanti atthau| tayau laukā tilaukaṃ| tassa cakkhu viya aika bhūtaṃpañ‍ñācakkhu yassa sauti tilaukaikac‍cakkhu, munindak‍kau| aittha ca aikacakkhusaddassa laupau hauti brahmassarautiādīsu viya| atha vā tilaukassa aikaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhuṃ bhūtau pattauti tilaukaikac‍cakkhu, munindak‍kau| tilaukaikac‍cakkhubhūtanti vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ bhūtasaddalaupaṃ katvā  tilaukaikac‍cakkhunti vuttaṃ| atha vā tilaukassa aikabhūtau pañ‍ñācakkhu hutvā sabbadhammai cakkhati passatīti tilaukaikac‍cakkhu, munindak‍kau| taṃ| bhagavā hi pañ‍ñācakkhu mayattā sattaisu ca taduppādanatau tilaukassa cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau hauti| vuttañhi madhupiṇḍikasuttai ‘‘sau hāvusau bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau vattā pavattā atthassa nin‍naitā amatassa dātā dhammassāmī tathāgatau’’ti| tasmā tilaukaikac‍cakkhunti thaumaiti|

dukhamasahananti puthuj‍janaihi atidukkhamitaṭṭhānaṃ khamanaṃ| puthuj‍janaihi dukkhaina kasiraina khamitabbanti dukhamaṃ, iṭṭhāniṭṭhādi ārammaṇaṃ| dukkhamanti vattabbai gaj‍jabandhattā chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ kakāralaupaṃ katvā dukhamanti vuttaṃ| taṃ sahati khamatīti dukhamasahanau, munindak‍kau| sahadhātu parisahanai yu| taṃ dukhamasahanaṃ| bhagavā hi puthuj‍janaihi sudukkhamitaṃ iṭṭhā niṭṭhādiārammaṇaṃ sītuṇhādiṃ lābhālābhayasāyasa nindapasaṃsasukhadukkhasaṅkhātaṃ laukadhammañ‍ca ativiya khamati adhivāsaiti, tasmā dukhamasahananti thaumaiti|

mahaisinti mahantaṃ sīlakkhandhādiṃ pariyaisamānaṃ mahantaṃ nibbānaṃ gavaisamānaṃ vā mahantai sīlakkhandhādikai aisati gavaisati pariyaisatīti mahaisī, munindak‍kau| mahantasaddūpa pada aisadhātu gavaisanai ī| vuttañhi mahāniddaisai ‘‘mahaisīti mahaisī| bhagavā mahantaṃ sīlakkhandhaṃ aisi gavaisi pariyaisīti mahaisī| mahantaṃ samādhikkhandhaṃ mahantaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ mahantaṃ vimuttikkhandhaṃ mahantaṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ aisī gavaisī pariyaisīti mahaisī’’ti| mahantai sattatiṃsabaudhipakkhiya dhammai aisati gavaisatīti vā mahaisī, mahantaṃ nibbānaṃ aisati gavaisatīti vā mahaisī| vuttañhi mahāniddaisai ‘‘mahantai satipaṭṭhānai mahantai sammappadhānai mahantai iddhipādai mahantāni indriyāni  mahantāni balāni mahantai baujjhaṅgai mahantaṃ ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ mahantaṃ paramatthaṃ amataṃ nibbānaṃ aisī gavaisī pariyaisīti mahaisī’’ti| atha vā mahaisakkhaihi sattaihi aisiyati gavaisiyati pariyaisiyati amhākaṃ kahaṃ buddhauti mahaisī| vuttañhi mahāniddaisai ‘‘mahaisakkhaihi vā sattaihi aisitau gavaisitau kahaṃ buddhau’’tyādi| taṃ mahaisiṃ| tanti munindak‍kaṃ| ahaṃ sirasā namāmīti sambandhau|
