tikkhauti tikkhappañ‍ñavā ca nisānapañ‍ñavā vā| tijanaṃ nisanaṃ tikkhā, pañ‍ñā| tijadhātu nisānai kha| jassa kau| tikkhā pañ‍ñā mai assa atthīti tikkhau| assathyatthai ṇau| tikkha pañ‍ñāya sampan‍nau cāti atthau| pañ‍ñāya ca sabba kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ saiṭṭhattā mūlakāraṇattā ca tikkhauti patthanaṃ karauti| vuttañhi milindapañhai –

‘‘pañ‍ñā pasaṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, katā saddhammaṭṭhitiyā|

pañ‍ñāya vimatiṃ hantvā, santiṃ pappauti paṇḍitau’’ti||

sūrauti catūsu parisāsu aṭṭhasu parisāsu ca vīrau ca| sattivantau nibbhayauti atthau| sūrayatai virayatai sūrau| bhāvasādhanauyaṃ| sūradhātu vik‍kantiyaṃ vīrai vā a| atha vā abhiruachambhianutrāsabhāvaina sūrati gacchati pavattatīti sūrau| sūradhātu gatiyaṃ a| sīhau viya parisamajjhai sūravīrasampan‍nau cāti atthau| sāsani kaihi pana saddhammavidūhi sūrasaddassa nibbacanaṃ na dassitaṃ| kaivalaṃ pana sūrauti visiṭṭhasūrauti atthavivaraṇamattamaiva dassitaṃ| dhitattauti aittha dhita attauti padacchaidau| dhitauti samādhi| attanti cittaṃ| aikaggacittasampan‍nau cāti atthau| dhāti tiṭṭhati aikārammaṇaihi dhitau, aikārammaṇai sampayuttadhammai dhāraitīti vā dhitau, samādhi| dhādhātu dhāraṇai ta| ārammaṇaṃ arati jānātīti attaṃ, cittaṃ| aradhātu gatiyaṃ ta| rassa tau| dhitau samādhi attaṃ cittaṃ assa maiti dhitattau|

saparahitacarauti attahitaparahitacarau, attanau ca parassa ca atthaṃ paṭipan‍nakau cāti atthau| attanau ca parassa ca hitaṃ atthaṃ payaujanaṃ carati paṭipaj‍jatīti saparahitacarau| saparahitasaddūpapada caradhātu caraṇai a| dīghajīvīti dīghāyukau ca| dīghaina jīvitindriyaina sampan‍nau vā| dīghau jīvau jīvitaṃ assa maiti dīghajīvi| āyukappampi dīghajīvitasampan‍nau cāti atthau| araugauti raugavirahau ca āraugyaṃ vā| ruj‍jati bhañ‍jati aṅgapac‍caṅgānīti raugau, byādhi| rujadhātu bhaṅgai ṇa| atha vā ruj‍jati hiṃsatīti raugau| rujadhātu hiṃsāyaṃ ṇa| natthi raugau tassa maiti araugau, chan‍navutiraugānaṃ abhāvau bhavaiyyanti atthau| dhañ‍ñauti sirīpuñ‍ñapañ‍ñālakkhaṇasampan‍nau ca| dhañ‍ñasaddau hi sirīpuñ‍ñapañ‍ñālakkhaṇavācakauti daṭṭhabbau| vuttañhi vaissantarajātakai ‘‘dhañ‍ñaṃ maṅgala sammata’’nti ca| taimiyajātakai ‘‘dhañ‍ñapuñ‍ñalakkhaṇasampan‍naṃ puttaṃ vijāyī’’ti ca| idaṃ pana nibbacanaṃ| dhananaṃ sampaj‍janaṃ dhañ‍ñaṃ, sirīpañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñalakkhaṇānaṃ sampan‍nanti atthau| dhanadhātu sampan‍nai| dhātūnaṃ anaikatthattā ṇyapac‍cayau| ṇakārānubandhai laupai katai yapac‍cayaina saddhiṃ nakārassa ñakāraṃ katvā dhañ‍ñanti rūpasiddhi vaiditabbā| atha vā dhanaṃ sirīpañ‍ñā puñ‍ñalakkhaṇasampadaṃ assa mai atthīti dhañ‍ñau| assathyatthai ṇyau|

vaṇṇauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca| aittha ca vaṇṇau duvidhau hauti sarīravaṇṇaguṇavaṇṇavasaina| tattha sarīra vaṇṇau nāma rūpakāyassa pāsādikarūpasampan‍natā| guṇa vaṇṇau nāma sīlasamādhipañ‍ñādiguṇaina ca dhutaṅgādipaṭipadāguṇaina ca sampan‍natā| idaṃ pana nibbacanaṃ| vaṇṇaiti vikāramāpan‍nānaṃ hadayaṅgatabhāvaṃ pakāsaitīti vaṇṇau, sarīrarūpaṃ| vaṇṇaiti attanau dabbaṃ pakāsaitīti vaṇṇau, guṇau| vaṇṇadhātu pakāsanai a| idha pana tadubhayampi yuj‍jati| vaṇṇaṃ assa mai atthīti vaṇṇau| assathyatthai a| yasassīti parivārasampan‍nau kittivantau vā| aittha ca  duvidhau hauti yasau parivārakittiyasavasaina| tattha yajiyati pūjiyati aitaina parivāraināti yasau, parivārau| yajadhātu pūjāyaṃ a| jassa sau| atha vā yāti gacchati pāpuṇāti sāmikassa parivāranti yasau, parivārau| yādhātu gatiyaṃ saupac‍cayau| yajiyati pūjiyati aitaināti yasau, kittisaddau| yajadhātu daivapūjāsaṅgahakaraṇadānadhammaisu a| jassa sau| atha vā yāti gacchati pāpuṇāti sabbattha byāpananti yasau, kittighausau thutisaddau vā| yādhātu gatiyaṃ saupac‍cayau| yasau ca yasau ca yasau aikasaisanayaina| sau assa mai atthīti yasassī, assathyatthai sīpac‍cayau|

atibalavadharauti atibalavantadhārakau ca ativiya kāyabalañāṇabalavantaṃ dhāraitīti atibalavadharau| ajātasatturājādayau viya pañ‍cahatthi dasahatthibalassa dhāraṇasamatthau ca, sāriputtatthairādayau viya ñāṇabalassa dhāraṇasamatthau ca bhavaiyyanti atthau| kittimāti kittighausavantau ca| kittiyatai kathiyatai guṇanti kitti, thutighausau| kittadhātu saddai i| kittighausau assa mai atthīti kittimā| assathyatthai mantupac‍cayau| khantupaitauti khantiyā upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatau ca| khamanaṃ sahanaṃ khanti| khamudhātu sahanai tapac‍cayau| tassanti tāya upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatauti khantupaitau| khantivādatāpasau viya khantupaitau ca bhavaiyyanti atthau| khantiyā hi duk‍karattā sabbakusala dhammānaṃ padhānattā saiṭṭhatapattā ca khantupaitauti patthanaṃ karauti| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘khantī paramaṃ tapau titikkhā’’ti ca| ‘‘khantyā bhiyyau na vij‍jatī’’ti ca| ‘‘khantībalaṃ balānīka’’nti ca|

‘‘sīlasamādhipañ‍ñānaṃ, khantippadhānakāraṇaṃ|

sabbaipi kusalā dhammā, khantyāyattāva vaḍḍharai’’ti ca||

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| sauhaṃ sau ahaṃ, aitaina puñ‍ñaina iminā buddhavandanapuñ‍ñakammaina samparāyai paralaukai, nipuṇamati ca saṇhasukhumapañ‍ñavā ca, satau satimā ca tittau tappanau yathāladdhavatthunā santuṭṭhau ca, dakkhau taṃtaṃkic‍cai chaikau ca, diṭṭhuj‍jupañ‍ñau ujudiṭṭhi pañ‍ñāya sampan‍nau ca avīkalavīriyau anūnavīriyau ca paripuṇṇavīriyau vā, bhaugavābhuñ‍jitabbasampattisukhaina sampan‍nau ca saṃvibhāgī saṃvibhajanasīlau ca tikkhau tikkhapañ‍ñavā ca, sūrauti sattivantau nibbhayau ca, dhitattau samāhita cittau ca aikaggacittau vā, saparahitacarau attahita parahitapaṭipan‍nakau ca dīghajīvī dīghāyukau ca araugau raugavirahau ca dhañ‍ñau sirīpuñ‍ñapañ‍ñālakkhaṇasampan‍nau ca vaṇṇau vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca yasassī parivārasampan‍nau ca atibalavadharau atibalavantadhārakau ca kittimā kitti ghausavantau ca khantupaitau khantiyā upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatau ca bhavaiyyanti|

sattavīsatimagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

28. aivaṃ puñ‍ñainaitainatyādigāthāya samparāyai paṇidhiṃ katvā idāni diṭṭhadhammai patthanaṃ kattukāmau saddhau dātaṅgupaitau tyādigāthamāha| tattha saddhauti kammādi saddahanalakkhaṇāya saddhāya sampan‍nau ca| saddahanaṃ saddhā, saddahatīti vā saddhā| saddahanti sampayuttadhammā aitāyāti saddhā| saṃpubbadhādhātu dhāraṇai ta| sā pana kammādīsu saddahanalakkhaṇā| aukappanalakkhaṇā vā| pasādarasā| udakappasādakamaṇi viya| pakkhandanarasā vā| aughuttaraṇau viya| akālussiyapac‍cupaṭṭhānā adhimuttipac‍cupaṭṭhānā vā, saddhaiyyavatthupadaṭṭhānā sautāpattiyaṅgapadaṭṭhānā vā| sā hatthavittabījāni viya daṭṭhabbā| bhagavatā hi sā saddhā ‘‘saddhāhatthau mahānāgau’’ti ca| ‘‘saddhīdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭha’’nti ca| ‘‘saddhābījaṃ tapau vuṭṭhī’’ti ca ‘‘saddhāya tarati augha’’nti ca ‘‘saddhā bandhati pāthaiyyaṃ, saddhā dutiyā purisassa hautī’’ti ca, ‘‘saddhaisikau bhikkhavai ariyasāvakau’’ti ca, ‘‘saddhādhana’’nti ca|

‘‘yaisaṃ atthi saddhā buddhai, dhammai ca saṅgharatanai|

tai maṃ ativiraucanti, āyunā yasasā siriyā’’ti||

ca| ‘‘saddhaṃ upanissāya dānaṃ daiti, sīlaṃ samādiyati, upausathakammaṃ karauti, jhānaṃ uppādaiti, vipassanaṃ uppādaiti, maggaṃ uppādaiti, abhiñ‍ñaṃ uppādaiti, samāpattiṃ uppādaitī’’ti ca| ‘‘dul‍labhā saddhāsampattī’’ti ca vuttā| tasmā saddhau ca bhavaiyyanti patthanaṃ karauti|

sā ca pana catubbidhā hauti āgamasaddhā, adhigamasaddhā, aukappanasaddhā, pasādasaddhā cāti| tattha sabbañ‍ñubaudhisattānaṃ abhinīhāratau āgatattā āgamasaddhā nāma| ariyasāvakānaṃ sac‍capaṭivaidhaina adhigatattā adhigamasaddhā nāma| buddhau dhammau saṅghauti vuttai acalabhāvaina aukappanaṃ aukappanasaddhā nāma| buddhādīsu pasāduppattimattā pasādasaddhā nāma| idha pana paṇidhānaṭṭhānattā sabbasaddhā adhippaitā| saddhā assa mai atthīti saddhau| assathyatthai ṇa|

dātaṅgupaitauti dātassa aṅgāya kāraṇabhūtāya dhanasampattiyā upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatau ca| dātabbavatthu ca, tappaṭiggāhakapuggalau ca, saddhā cāti tīhi aṅgaihi upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatau ca vā| diyatai dātaṃ| bhāvasādhanauyaṃ| dādhātu dānai a| aṅgīyati  ñāyati aitaināti aṅgau, dhanasampatti| dātassa aṅgau dātaṅgau, taina upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatauti dātaṅgupaitau, atha vā diyatīti dātaṃ, dānavatthu| dātassa aṅgau dātaṅgau, taina upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatauti dātaṅgupaitau, dātabbavatthu ca, tappaṭiggāhaka puggalau ca, saddhā cāti tīhi aṅgaihi sampaj‍jāmīti patthanaṃ karautīti vuttaṃ hauti| vuttañhi itivuttaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘saddhā daiyyadhammau paṭiggāhakau cāti imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ samukhībhūtakālaiyaiva hi dānaṃ sambhavatī’’ti|

paramasiridharauti uttamasirīdhārakau ca| uttamasiriṃ dhāraṇasamatthau ca vā| aittha ca sirīti pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñasarīrasaubhaggasampattīnamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| katapuñ‍ñaihi saiviyatīti sirī, katapuñ‍ñai puggalai saivati nissayatīti vā sirī, pañ‍ñāpuñ‍ñasarīrasaubhaggasampatti labbhati| sidhātu saivāyaṃ irau ī ca| paramā uttamā sirī paramasirī, taṃ dhāraitīti paramasirīdharau, paramasiriyā dhāraṇasamatthauti atthau|

diṭṭhadhammaiti pac‍cakkhabhūtai attabhāvai| aittha ca diṭṭhadhammau vuc‍cati pac‍cakkhabhūtau attabhāvau| sau hi passitabbauti diṭṭhau, attabhāvau| diṭṭhau passitabbau dhammau sabhāvau assāti diṭṭhadhammauti vuc‍cati| tasmiṃ diṭṭhadhammai| virattauti kāmaguṇaisu vigatarattau ca, raj‍janaṃ rattau| ranjadhātu rāgai| vigatau rattau assa maiti virattau| laj‍jīti pāpahirivantau ca, pāpajigucchanalakkhaṇāya laj‍jāya sampan‍nau ca vā| atha vā laj‍jīti hirauttappasampan‍nau ca| laj‍jīsaddaina hi lakkhaṇāhāranayaina auttappampi gahaitabbaṃ| kasmā, kusala lakkhaṇasabhāvaina samānattā| vuttañhaitaṃ naittipāḷiyaṃ –

‘‘vuttamhi aikadhammai, yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā kaici|

vuttā bhavanti sabbau, sauhārau lakkhaṇau nāmā’’ti||

laj‍jatīti  laj‍jā, hiri| laj‍jadhātu hiriyaṃ| laj‍jā assa mai atthīti laj‍jā, kāyaduc‍caritāditau hirauttappasaman‍nāgatau cāti atthau| kalyāṇamittauti sundaramitta sampan‍nau ca| kalyāṇasaddau hi sundaravācakau anipphan‍na pāṭipadikau| atha vā kalyaṃ hitaṃ aṇayati pāpayatīti kalyāṇau, sumittau| kalyasaddūpapadaaṇadhātu gatiyaṃ ṇa| mij‍jati sinaihatīti mittau, mij‍jiyati sinaihiyatīti vā mittau, midadhātu sinaihai ta| kalyāṇau mittau assa maiti kalyāṇamittau| hitadharasumittasampan‍nau ca bhavaiyyanti atthau| idha pana saddhāsīlasutacāgavīriyasati samādhipañ‍ñāguṇaihi saman‍nāgatau kalyāṇamittau adhippaitau| taṃ pana sandhāya bhagavatā siṅgālasuttai –

‘‘upakārau ca yau mittau, sukhadukkhai ca yau sakhā|

atthakkhāyī ca yau mittau, yau ca mittānukampakau|

aitaipi mittai cattārau, iti viñ‍ñāya paṇḍitau| sak‍kac‍caṃ

payirupāsaiyya, mātā puttaṃva aurasa’’nti vuttau||

visuddhimaggai ca –

‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau| gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, nau cāṭṭhānai niyaujayai’’ti vuttau| iti aivarūpassa ca kalyāṇamittassa dul‍labhattā kalyāṇamittaina sampaj‍jāmīti patthanaṃ karautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

abhiratakusalauti dānādipuñ‍ñakusaladhammaisu abhiramaṇau ca| kucchitaṃ apāyadvāraṃ salanti saṃvaranti pidahanti sādhavau aitaināti kusalaṃ, dānādipuñ‍ñadhammā| kupubba saladhātu saṃvaraṇai a| taisu abhiatiraikaṃ ramatīti abhiratakusalau, kitantasamāsauyaṃ| kusalābhiratauti vattabbai chandānurakkhaṇatthaṃ padavipariyāyaina aivaṃ vuttanti| atha vā abhiatiraikaṃ ramanaṃ abhiratau, kusalaisu abhiratau yassa maiti abhiratakusalau, nic‍caṃ kusalapuñ‍ñassa karaṇai abhiratacittasampan‍nau cāti atthau|

pañ‍casīlādirakkhauti pañ‍casīlādīnaṃ rakkhituṃ samatthau ca| aittha ca ādisaddaina gihīnaṃ aṭṭhaṅgupausathasīlaṃ, sāmaṇairānaṃ dasaṅgasīlañ‍ca, bhikkhūnaṃ catupārisuddhisīlañ‍ca saṅgaṇhāti| pañ‍casīlaṃ ādi yaisaṃ taiti pañ‍casīlādi, taṃ rakkhati rakkhituṃ samatthaitīti pañ‍casīlādirakkhau| gahaṭṭhānaṃ nic‍casīlavasaina pañ‍casīlaṃ sati ussāhasamatthai upausathaṅgavasaina aṭṭhaṅgasīlaṃ dasaṅgasīlañ‍ca akhaṇḍaṃ acchiddaṃ asabalaṃ akammāsaṃ katvā rakkhituṃ sāmaṇairasīlavasaina dasasīlañ‍ca, bhikkhusīlavasaina catupārisuddhisīlañ‍ca rakkhituṃ samatthau cāti atthau| bhagavatā hi ‘‘sīlavipattihaitu vā bhikkhavai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti ca| ‘‘sīlasampadāhaitu vā bhikkhavai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti ca| ‘‘sīlasampan‍nau mahantaṃ bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati| catuparisaṃ visāradau upasaṅkamati amaṅkubhūtau| asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti ca vuttattā ca| aṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘sīlālaṅkārasadisau alaṅkārau natthi, sīlasadisaṃ pupphaṃ natthi, sīlagandhasadisau gandhau natthī’’ti ca| ‘‘sīla sampattiñhi nissāya tissau vij‍jā pāpuṇātī’’ti ca| ‘‘ādimhi sīlaṃ visaudhaiyyā’’ti ca| ‘‘sattā sīlaina visujjhantī’’ti ca| ‘‘sīlaṃ sāsanassa ādī’’ti ca| ‘‘sattasu visuddhīsu  sīlavisuddhi sarīraṃ nāmā’’ti ca| ‘‘sīlaṃ nāmaitaṃ avassayau patiṭṭhā ārammaṇaṃ tāṇaṃ laiṇaṃ gati parāyana’’nti ca vuttattā pañ‍casīlādirakkhauti patthanaṃ karautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

appicchauti anicchau ca, rūpādikāmaguṇaisu anicchantauti atthau| abhāvatthauhaittha appasaddau| appaḍaṃsamakasavā tātapātiādīsu viya icchanaṃ icchā, rūpādikāmaguṇaṃ icchatīti vā icchā, kāmataṇhā| isudhātu icchākantīsu a| dhātvantassacchau| natthi icchā yassa maiti appicchau, rūpādīsu kāmaisu anicchantau cāti atthau| appicchau pana catubbidhau hauti pac‍cayaappicchau, pariyattiappicchau, dhutaṅgaappicchau, paṭivaidhaappicchau cāti| tattha catūsu pac‍cayaisu appicchau pac‍cayaappicchau| yau pana pac‍cayai bahuṃ daintai appaṃ gaṇhati, appaṃ daintai appataraṃ vā gaṇhati na vā gaṇhati| na anavasaisagāhī hauti| ayaṃ pac‍cayaappicchau nāma| yau pana taipiṭakaupi samānau na bahussutabhāvaṃ jānāpaitukāmau hauti, ayaṃ pariyattiappicchau nāma| dhutaṅgasamādānassa pana dhutaṅgapariharaṇabhāvaṃ añ‍ñaisaṃ jānituṃ na daiti, ayaṃ dhutaṅgaappicchau nāma| yau pana sautāpan‍nādīsu añ‍ñatarau hutvā sautāpan‍nādibhāvaṃ jānāpaituṃ na icchati, ayaṃ adhigamaappicchau nāma| imaihi appicchaguṇaihi sampan‍nau ca ahaṃ bhavaiyyanti vuttaṃ hauti|

appakaudhauti dussanalakkhaṇadausatau virahitau ca| sau hi kujjhatīti kaudhauti vuc‍cati| dausassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| natthi kaudhau assa maiti appakaudhau|

kuddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, kuddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ kaudhau sahatai naranti||

vuttadaisanaṃ manasikaritvā appakaudhauti patthanaṃ karautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

ativujuhadayauti  atiaiva ujucittasaman‍nāgatau ca| arati gacchati pavattati akuṭilabhāvaināti uju, aj‍javaṃ| aradhātu gatiyaṃ ju| arissa u| ārammaṇaṃ haratīti hadayaṃ, cittaṃ| haradhātu haraṇai aya| rassa dau| atha vā ārammaṇaṃ harantaṃ ayati gacchati pavattatīti hadayaṃ, cittaṃ| harasaddūpapada ayadhātu gatiyaṃ a| uju ca taṃ hadayañ‍cāti ujuhadayaṃ| atiaiva uju hadayaṃ assa maiti ativujuhadayau| gaumuttavaṅkau ca candalaikhāvaṅkau ca naṅgalakauṭi vaṅkau cāti tīhi vaṅkaihi vigatau hutvā ativiya ujucittasaman‍nāgatau ca bhavaiyyanti atthau| tattha ca yau pāpaṃ katvāva na karaumīti bhāsati| sau gantvā pac‍causak‍kanatāya gaumuttavaṅkau nāma| yau pāpaṃ karauntauva bhāyāmahaṃ pāpassāti bhāsati| sau yaibhuyyaina kuṭilatāya candalaikhā vaṅkau nāma| yau pāpaṃ karauntauva kau pāpassa na bhāyaiyyāti bhāsati, sau nātikuṭilatāya naṅgalakauṭivaṅkau nāma hauti| yassa vā tīṇi kammadvārāni asuddhāni| sau gaumuttavaṅkau nāma hauti| yassa yāni kānici dvai| sau candalaikhā vaṅkau nāma| yassa yaṃkiñ‍ci aikaṃ| sau naṅgalakauṭivaṅkau nāmāti vaiditabbau|

iddhimāti iddhiyā saman‍nāgatau ca, iddhisaddassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| iddhi assa mai atthīti iddhimā| assathyatthai mantupac‍cayau| mānussikālai manussiddhiyā daivuppattikālai daividdhiyā samaṇuppattikālai samaṇiddhiyā ca sampuṇṇaiyyanti atthau|

appamaiyyauti appamaitabbaihi sīlādiguṇaihi sampan‍nau ca| pamaitabbauti pamaiyyau| papubbamādhātu pamāṇai ṇya| na pamaiyyau appamaiyyau, appamaiyyau sīlādiguṇau mai assa atthīti appamaiyyau| assathyatthai ṇa| appamaiyyaihi  sīlādiguṇaihi ca bhaugasampattīhi ca sampaj‍jāmīti patthanaṃ karautīti vuttaṃ hauti|

pāsaṃsauti sīlādiguṇaihi buddhādisappurisānaṃ pasaṃsārahau ca, pasaṃsiyatai thaviyatai pasaṃsau| bhāva sādhanauyaṃ| papubbasaṃsadhātu thutiyaṃ, taṃ arahatīti pāsaṃsau| arahatitaddhitai ṇa| buddhādisappurisaihi pasaṃsi tabbaihi dānasīlādiguṇaihi sampan‍nau ca bhavaiyyanti vuttaṃ hauti|

paimavācauti atthabyañ‍janamadhuratāya buddhādisappurisaihi paimitabbavācāya sampan‍nau ca| piyataiti paimā, vācā| pīdhātu tappanakantīsu imau| vacitabbāti vācā| vacadhātu viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ ṇa| paimā vācā assa maiti paima vācau| buddhādisappurisaihi paimiyā caturaṅgasaman‍nāgata vācāya sampan‍nau cāti atthau| sappurisānaṃ pana vācā caturaṅgasaman‍nāgatā hauti| subhāsitavācā ca dhammavācā piyavācā sac‍cavācāti| tathāhaitaṃ vuttaṃ sagāthāvagga saṃyuttai –

subhāsitaṃ uttamamāhu santau,

dhammaṃ bhaṇai nādhammaṃ taṃ dutiyaṃ|

piyaṃ bhaṇai nāppiyaṃ taṃ tatiyaṃ,

sac‍caṃ bhaṇai nālikaṃ taṃ catutthanti||

ayaṃ panaittha yaujanā| santau sappurisā uttamaṃ subhāsitaṃ suṭṭhu bhāsitaṃ vācaṃ āhu, dhammaṃ bhaṇai bhaṇaiyya adhammaṃ na bhaṇai na bhaṇaiyya, dutiyaṃ taṃ vācaṃ āhu, piyaṃ bhaṇai bhaṇaiyya, appiyaṃ na bhaṇai na bhaṇaiyya, tatiyaṃ taṃ vācaṃ āhu, sac‍caṃ bhaṇai bhaṇaiyya alikaṃ na bhaṇai na bhaṇaiyya, catutthaṃ taṃ vācaṃ āhūti|

sujanaguṇavidūti  buddhādisappurisānaṃ sīlādiguṇaṃ vijānakau ca| sundarau janau sujanau| guṇati pakāsaiti sīlādiguṇasamaṅgitaṃ puggalanti guṇaṃ, sīlādiguṇaṃ| guṇadhātu pakāsanai a| sujanānaṃ buddhādisappurisānaṃ guṇau sujanaguṇau, taṃ vidati jānātīti sujanaguṇavidū| sujana guṇasaddūpapadavidadhātu ñāṇai ū| buddhādisappurisānaṃ sīlasamādhipañ‍ñādiguṇaṃ jānituṃ samatthau ca bhavaiyyanti atthau|

māmakauti buddhādisaj‍janānaṃ māmakau ca| mamāyatīti māmakau| mamadhātu mānanai paimai vā ṇvu| tassa akau| buddhādiratanattayañ‍ca saddhammañ‍ca mānantau ca bhavaiyyanti atthau| sauti sau ahaṃ| bhavaiyyanti bhavāmīti|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipayaujanā| sau ahaṃ diṭṭhadhammai pac‍cakkhabhūtai attabhāvai, saddhau saddhāya saman‍nāgatau ca, dātaṅgupaitau dātassa aṅgāya kāraṇabhūtāya dhana sampattiyā upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatau ca, dātabba vatthu ca, tappaṭiggāhakapuggalau ca, saddhā cāti tīhi aṅgaihi upaitau samupaitau saman‍nāgatau ca vā, paramasiridharau uttamasirīdhārakau ca, uttamasiriṃ dhāraṇasamatthau ca vā, virattau kāmaguṇaisu vigatarattau ca, laj‍jī pāpahirivantau ca, pāpajigucchanalakkhaṇāya laj‍jāya sampan‍nau ca vā, hirauttappasaman‍nāgatau ca vā, kalyāṇamittau sundaramitta sampan‍nau ca, abhiratakusalau dānādipuñ‍ñakusaladhammaisu abhiramaṇau ca, pañ‍casīlādirakkhau pañ‍casīlādīni rakkhituṃ samatthau ca, appicchau anicchau ca, rūpādikāmaguṇaisu anicchantau ca vā, appakaudhau dussanalakkhaṇadausatau virahitau ca, ativujuhadayau atiaiva ujucittasaman‍nāgatau ca, iddhiyā saman‍nāgatau ca, appamaiyyau appamaiyyaihi sīlādiguṇaihi sampan‍nau ca, pāsaṃsau sīlādiguṇaihi buddhādisappurisānaṃ pasaṃsārahau ca, paimavācau atthabyañ‍janamadhuratāya  buddhādisappurisaihi paimitabbavācāya sampan‍nau ca, sujanaguṇavidū buddhādisappurisānaṃ sīlādiguṇaṃ vijānakau ca, māmakau buddhādisaj‍janānaṃ māmakau ca, buddhādiratanattayañ‍ca saddhammañ‍ca mānantau ca vā, bhavaiyyaṃ bhavāmīti|

aṭṭhavīsatimagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

29.

 itthaṃ asaṅkhayai nātha, guṇai lakkhaṇadīpitai|

gāthāsu sūcakāsvaika, gāthampi saratai budhau||

30.

 caturāpāyamuttau sau, sādhakatthadvayassa ca|

hatūpaddavajālau ca, lābhī hitasukhassa ca||

31.

 adhipau naradaivānaṃ, catudīpissaraupi vā|

bhavaiyya antimai daihai, tamañ‍ñaṃ saitachattakaṃ||

32.

 bhāvanāyānamāruyha, samamaissati subbatau|

imasmiṃ attabhāvaipi, araugau dīghajīvikau||

33.

 pūjitau sabbalaukaihi, bhāvanābhiratīmanau|

janappiyau manāpau ca, kā kathākhiladhāraṇai||

29. aivaṃ puñ‍ñainaitainatyādi gāthādvayaina paṇidhiṃ katvā idāni buddhavandanassa phalānisaṃsaṃ dassaintau itthantyādi gāthāpañ‍cakaṃ āha| taṃ pana mūlācariyaihi na vuttaṃ, pacchā pana hitakāmaihi paṇḍitaihi pakkhittaṃ gāthāpañ‍cakanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| tā pana gāthāyau pathyāvattagāthāti daṭṭhabbā|

tāsu pana paṭhamagāthāyaṃ itthaṃ asaṅkhayai lakkhaṇadīpitai nāthaguṇai sūcakāsu gāthāsu aikagāthampi yau budhau sarataiti sambandhau| itthanti iminā sugatantiādinā vuttappakāraina| asaṅkhayaiti aittakā buddhaguṇāti gaṇaituṃ asamatthai| saṅkhiyatai saṅkhayā| saṅkhiyati gaṇiyatīti vā saṅkhayā| sakhidhātu saṅkhyāyaṃ yapac‍cayau| atha vā saṅkhituṃ na sak‍kauntīti asaṅkhayā| aittakā buddhaguṇā aittakā buddhaguṇāti niravasaisatau aikabuddhaupi anaikavassa satasahassakauṭiyāpi gaṇaituṃ asamatthauva| pagaiva pana añ‍ñaiti atthau| vuttampi caitaṃ apadānai –

‘‘buddhaupi buddhassa bhaṇaiyya vaṇṇaṃ,

kappampi cai añ‍ñamabhāsamānau|

khīyaitha kappau ciradīghamantarai,

vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassā’’ti ca||

‘‘sak‍kā samuddai udakaṃ, pamaituṃ āḷhakaina vā|

natvaiva tava sabbañ‍ñu, ñāṇaṃ sak‍kā pamaitavai’’ti ca||

mahāvaggasaṃyuttaṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘appamāṇā hi buddhā taisaṃ buddhāpi anavasaisatau vaṇṇaṃ vattuṃ asamatthā, pagaiva itarā pajā’’ti ca| lakkhaṇadīpitaiti upalakkhaṇa nayaina pakāsitabbai| aittha ca padhānanayau nidassana nayau ca labbhati| nāthaguṇaiti buddhassa sīlasamādhi pañ‍ñādiguṇai| tassa pana padassa vacanatthau haiṭṭhā vuttauyaiva| sūcakāsvaikagāthampīti sūcakāsu aikagāthampīti padacchaidau| sūcakāsūti pakāsakāsu| gāthāsūti sugatantyādinā vuttāsu chabbīsativandanagāthāsu| niddhāraṇai caitaṃ bhummavacanaṃ| kavīhi gāyati kathiyatīti gāthā| paj‍javisaisau| gaidhātu saddai tha| gaidhātussa gādaisau| atha vā gaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ| taṃ tiṭṭhati aitthāti gāthā| gaṃsaddūpapadathādhātu gatinivattiyaṃ| gaṃ vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ, taina kavīhi bandhitvā thiyati thapiyatīti vā gāthā, tāsu gāthāsu| aikagāthampīti aikapaj‍jampi| aittha ca pisaddau garahatthau| taina anaikagāthāya buddhaguṇasaraṇai kā kathāvāti sambhāvaiti| budhauti yau paṇḍitau| sau  hi atthānatthaṃ budhati jānātītibudhauti vuc‍cati| sarataiti sarati| chandānurakkhaṇatthañhi parassapadativibhattiyā attanaupadataivibhattādaisau|

30. sauti sau budhau bhavaiyyāti sambandhau| caturāpāyamuttauti nirayatiracchānapaitaasurakāyasaṅkhātaihi catūhi apāyaihi vimuttau ca| puñ‍ñasammatā ayāyaibhuyyaina apagatauti apāyau| ayatau sukhatau apagatauti vā apāyau| abyayībhāvasamāsauyaṃ| atha vā tissau sampattiyau ayanti gacchanti pavattanti aitaināti ayau, puñ‍ñaṃ| ayadhātu gatiyaṃ a| puñ‍ñatau ayati gacchati pavattatīti vā, puñ‍ñakārīhi ayitabbau pavattaitabbauti vā ayau, sukhaṃ| tatau apagatauti apāyau| caturau apāyau caturāpāyau, taihi muttau vimuttauti caturāpāyamuttau| sādhakatthadvayassa cāti sādhakau atthadvayassa cāti padacchaidau| atthadvayassāti diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikasaṅkhātassa atthadvayassa| aittha casaddau aṭṭhānayaugau| sau pana sādhakau tyādīsu padaisu yuttau| sādhakauti nipphādakau ca| sau hi sādhaiti nipphādaitīti sādhakauti vuc‍cati|

hatūpaddavajālau cāti hatau upaddavasaṅkhātajālau ca| hanayittha apanayitthāti hatau| hanadhātu hiṃsāyaṃ ta| upagantvā davati hiṃsatīti upaddavau, jātibhayādi sauḷasaupaddavau| upapubbadudhātu hiṃsāyaṃ, upaddavā nāma pana sauḷasavidhā haunti jātibhayaṃ, jarābhayaṃ, byādhibhayaṃ, maraṇabhayaṃ, rājabhayaṃ, caurabhayaṃ, aggibhayaṃ, udakabhayaṃ, ūmibhayaṃ, kumbhilabhayaṃ, āvaṭṭabhayaṃ, susukābhayaṃ, attānuvādabhayaṃ, parānuvādabhayaṃ, daṇḍabhayaṃ, duggatibhayañ‍cāti| jalati hiṃsati macchai aitaināti jālaṃ, macchajālaṃ| jālaṃ  viyāti jālau, upaddavau| nitatthaina pana jalati hiṃsati sattai aitaināti jālau| upaddavau| jaladhātu hiṃsāyaṃ ṇa| hatau upaddavasaṅkhātajālau yaina sauti hatūpaddavajālau| buddhapaṇāmacaitanāya pahitau upaddavasaṅkhātajālau bhavaiyyāti atthau| lābhī hitasukhassa cāti hitassa ca sukhassa ca lābhī ca| labhanaṃ lābhau, taṃ assa atthīti lābhī, hinauti vaḍḍhatīti hitaṃ, atthaṃ| sukhayatīti sukhaṃ| yassuppaj‍jati, taṃ sukhitaṃ karautīti atthau| atha vā suṭṭhu dukkhaṃ khanatīti sukhaṃ| supubba khanudhātu avadhāraṇai k‍vi| hitañ‍ca sukhañ‍ca hitasukhaṃ, tassa lābhī bhavaiyyāti atthau|

31-32. naradaivānaṃ adhipau ca catudīpissaraupi vā bhavaiyyāti sambandhau| naradaivānanti narā ca daivā ca naradaivā, taisaṃ| kammatthai caitaṃ chaṭṭhīvacanaṃ| adhipauti issarau ca| sau hi attādhīnaṃ pāti rakkhati dhammaina vā adhiabhibhavitvā pāti rakkhatīti adhipauti vuc‍cati| adhipubbapādhātu rakkhanai a, narānaṃ adhipau issarau manussarājā ca daivānaṃ adhipau issarau daivarājā ca bhavaiyyāti atthau| catudīpissaraupi vāti aittha pisaddau sambhāvanatthai nipātau| taina pagaiva aikadīpissararājabhāvau ca, padai sissararājabhāvau cāti sambhāvaiti| vā saddau padapūraṇa mattauyaiva| catudīpissarauti pubbavidaihau, aparagau yānau, jambudīpau, uttarakuru cāti catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ issarau cak‍kavattirājāpi bhavaiyyāti atthau| tattha pubbavidaihauti sinairunau purai bhavaṃ pubbaṃ, vaidaina pañ‍ñāya īhanti vāyamanti aitthāti vaidaihau, vidasaddūpapada īhadhātu vāyāmai ṇa| sauyaiva vidaihau| imaṃ dīpamupādāya sinairunau pubbadisabhāgattā pubbau ca sau vidaihau cāti pubbavidaihau, pubbadīpau|

aparagauyānauti  sinairunau aparai pacchimai bhavau aparau| gavaina yanti gacchanti aitthāti gauyānau| gausaddūpapada yādhātu gamanai yu| sinairunau pacchima disabhāgattā aparau ca sau gauyānau cāti aparagauyānau, pacchimadīpau| jambudīpauti jāyatīti jambū| yaujanasatubbaidhau rukkhau| janadhātu jananai ū| nassa mau| bakārāgamau| jalamajjhai dippatīti dīpau, dippanti jautanti aittha saddhammāti vā dīpau| dīpadhātu dittiyaṃ a| tassa pana dviāpauti padacchaidaṃ katvā dvidhā āpau sandati aitthāti dīpauti bahubbīhisamāsaupi yuj‍jati| jambuyā lakkhitau pañ‍ñātau vā dīpau jambudīpau, dakkhiṇadīpau| uttarakurūti sinairunau uttarai bhavā uttarā| dhammatāsiddhassa pañ‍casīlassa ānubhāvaina kaṃ sukhaṃ urumahantaṃ aitthāti kuru| bahubbīhisamāsauyaṃ| kuṃ vuc‍cati pāpaṃ| taṃ rundhanti aitthāti vā kuru| kupubbarudhidhātu āvaraṇai k‍vi| dhātvantassa laupau| sinairunau uttaradisabhāgattā uttarau ca sau kuru cāti uttarakuru| uttaradīpauti catun‍naṃ dīpānaṃ issarau catudīpissarau, cak‍kavattirājā labbhati|

aivaṃ laukiyahitaṃ dassaitvā idāni laukuttarahitaṃ dassaintau āha antimai daihai…pai॰… subbatauti antimai daihai subbatau bhāvanāyānaṃ āruyha añ‍ñaṃ taṃ saitachattakaṃ dhāraintau samaṃ aissatīti sambandhau| tattha pana atthak‍kamaina vaṇṇayissāma| antimaiti carimai pacchimai vā| antai pariyausānai bhavau antimau, pacchimasarīrau| tasmiṃ| daihaiti sarīrai| dihati upaciyati vaḍḍhatīti daihau, sarīrau| dihadhātu upacayai ṇa| atha vā dihati upacayati aitaina puñ‍ñāpuñ‍ñanti daihau, pacchimabhavabhūtai bhabbakulaputtabhūtai vā sarīraiti atthau| subbatauti suṭṭhu paṭipan‍nakau hutvā| suṭṭhu vataṃ paṭipan‍naṃ assāti subbatau| vakārassabbau| bhāvanāyānamāruyhāti bhāvanāyānaṃ āruyhāti padacchaidau| vipassanābhāvanāsaṅkhātayānaṃ āruhitvāti atthau| bhāvaitabbā vaḍḍhaitabbāti bhāvanā| taibhūmaka saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ| atha vā tadārammaṇaṃ bhāvaiti vaḍḍhaitīti bhāvanā, vipassanāpañ‍ñā| bhūdhātu sattāyaṃ vaḍḍhanai vā yu| sā ca samathavipassanā bhāvanāvasaina duvidhā hauti| tattha idha pana añ‍ñaṃ saitachattakanti saddantarasan‍niṭṭhānaina vipassanābhāvanā adhippaitā| yanti pāpuṇanti aitaina nibbānanti yānaṃ, anic‍cādivipassanāñāṇaṃ| yādhātu gatiyaṃ yu| bhāvanāsaṅkhātaṃ yānaṃ bhāvanāyānaṃ, bhāvanā ca sā yānañ‍cāti vā bhāvanāyānaṃ|

āruyhāti ārauhitvā| pakatiyānasadisaṃ anic‍ca dukkhaanattayānaṃ āruhitvāti atthau| anic‍cādilakkhaṇaina hi vinā maggaphalanibbānaṃ pattau nāma natthi| tasmā bhāvanāyānamāruyhāti vuttaṃ| añ‍ñanti arahattaphalaṃ| tañhi paṭhamamaggaina ādiṭṭhamariyādaṃ anatik‍kamitvā catusac‍cadhammaṃ avabujjhatīti añ‍ñanti vuc‍cati| āpubbañādhātu avabaudhanai k‍vi| āsaddau mariyādatthau| saitachattakanti audātātapattaṃ| saiviyatīti saitaṃ, dhavalaṃ| sidhātu saivāyaṃ ta| kilaisatapaṃ chādaitīti chattaṃ| chadadhātu saṃvaraṇai ta| taṃyaiva chattakaṃ| saitañ‍ca taṃ chattakañ‍cāti saitachattakaṃ| arahattaphalaātapattaṃ labbhati| añ‍ñaṃ arahattaphalasaṅkhātaṃ saitachattakaṃ dhāraintau hutvāti sambandhau| samanti nibbānaṃ| tañhi sabbaṃ vaṭṭadukkhaṃ samati vūpasamati nirujjhati aitthāti samanti vuc‍cati| aissatīti pāpuṇissatīti|

idāni diṭṭhadhammikatthaṃ dassaintau imasmiṃ attabhāvaipītiādimāha| tattha imasmiṃ attabhāvaipi araugau ca dīghāyukau ca sabbalaukaihi pūjitau ca bhāvanābhi ratimanau  ca janappiyau ca manāpau ca bhavaiyyāti yaujanā| attabhāvaipīti aittha pisaddau sampiṇḍanatthau| taina samparāyikatthaṃ sampiṇḍaiti| attāti bhavati aitaina abhidhānaṃ buddhi cāti attabhāvau| khandhapañ‍cakassaitamadhivacanaṃ| araugauti sabbabyādhivirahau ca| sau hi natthi raugau assāti araugauti vuc‍cati| dīghajīvikauti dīghāyukau ca| sau hi dīghā jīvi āyu assāti dīghajīvikauti vuc‍cati|

33. sabbalaukaihīti sabbadaivamanussaihi| pūjitauti pūjitabbau ca| bhāvanābhiratimanauti samathavipassanā saṅkhātāya bhāvanāya abhiramaṇacittau ca| janappiyauti sabbajanaihi piyāyitabbau ca| manāpauti sabbasattānaṃ cittavaḍḍhanakau ca bhavaiyyāti sambandhau|

aivaṃ chabbīsatiyā vandanagāthāsu aikagāthāmattampi buddhaguṇānussaraṇai phalānisaṃsaṃ dassaitvā idāni sabbagāthāyau vacasā dhāraitvā buddhavandanai anaikāni phalānisaṃsāni dassaituṃ kā kathākhiladhāraṇaiti āha| tattha kā kathākhiladhāraṇaiti kā kathā akhiladhāraṇaiti padacchaidau| sabbabuddhavandanagāthāya vacasā dhāraṇai kiṃ vattabbā yaivāti atthau|

aittha ca akhilasaddau sabbavācakau anipphan‍napāṭipadikau| atha vā khiyanaṃ khilaṃ| khidhātu khayai lapac‍cayau| khayaṃ lāti gaṇhātīti vā khilaṃ, appakaṃ| khayasaddūpapadalādhātu gahaṇai a| na khilaṃ akhilaṃ, sabbagāthāyau| dhāriyatai dhāraṇaṃ, akhilāya sabbāya gāthāya dhāraṇaṃ akhiladhāraṇaṃ, tasmiṃ| chabbīsatiyā buddhavandanagāthāsu aikaṃ gāthaṃ vacasā dhārayitvā buddhavandanai yathāvuttaphalānisaṃsāni labbhanti, pagaiva pana sabbabuddhavandanagāthāyau vacasā dhārayitvā dinai dinai buddhavandanaiti  atthauti| iminā pana gāthāpañ‍cakaina yathāvutta phalānisaṃsaṃ ñatvā saddahakau sammādiṭṭhikau kulaputtau sabbabuddhavandanagāthāyau vacasā dhārayitvā rattindivaṃ pubbaṇhakālai ca sāyanhakālai ca rattiyā paṭhamayāmai ca pacchimayāmai ca satataṃ samitaṃ nirantaraṃ buddhaṃ vandaiyyāti uyyaujanaṃ dassaitīti|

pañ‍cakagāthāvaṇṇanā samattā|

iti paurāṇācariyaihi vanditassa namak‍kārassa saddatthanayaihi chandālaṅkāranayaihi ca saṇhasukhumasududdasa gambhīrassa buddhassa duppaṭividhaguṇaparidīpakassa sappurisānaṃ cittapasan‍najanakassa sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai asaṃhira visāradañāṇacārinā sāsanasaudhakaina bhaddantaraiva tatthairavaraina racitā buddhaguṇappadīpikanāmikā vaṇṇanā|

nigamana

idaṃ pana puraṃ nimmitaina sirīpavaravijayānantayasapaṇḍita tribhavanādityādhipatimahādhammarājādhirājāti lañchitanāmikaina piṭakattayapāḷiṃ silārūḷhaṃ kārayitvā pañ‍cama saṅgītikaraṇaina timahādhammarājaina ratanāpuñ‍janti sammatai rājadhānībhūtai nānākulasaṃkiṇṇai puñ‍ñadhañ‍ñavantānaṃ sādhūnañ‍ca sīlasamādhipañ‍ñavantānaṃ bhikkhūnañ‍ca nivāsabhūtai buddhadhammasaṅgharatanākarai candimasūriyā viya buddhasāsanuj‍jautanai mantalai nagarai iti parikittitai mahārāmai daisai vasantaina upāsaka guṇasampan‍naina nāmikaina upāsakaina ca tassupaṭṭhākāya nāmikāya upāsikāya ca samakusalacchandaṃ katvā nibbānassa sacchikaraṇatthāya kāritai manauramai suvaṇṇaina limpitattā suvaṇṇavatī nāmikai  dvibhummikai mahiṭṭhakavihārai aṭṭhahi disāhi āgatānaṃ sutaisanabhikkhūnaṃ sāṭṭhakathaṃ piṭakattayaṃ dinai dinai satataṃ nirantaraṃ vācaintaina adhivasantaina sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai asaṃhiravisāradañāṇacārinā mantalai purai pariyattisāsanahitadhammācariyagaṇassa jaiṭṭhuk‍kaṭṭhabhūtaina raivatābhipaṇḍitadhajasāsanavaṃsamahādhammarājagurūti ca agga mahāpaṇḍitāti ca laddhalañchitaina mūladvādasasāsana karamahāgaṇassa jaiṭṭhamahānāyakabhūtaina sāsanāpruñ‍ñau ācariyauti vissutaina sāsanasaudhaka mahānāyakatthairavaraina paurāṇaihi tattha tattha pāḷiyaṃ bhagavatā vuttaihi buddhaguṇappakāsakaihi buddhaguṇapadaihi thaumitvā vanditassa atthabyañ‍janaina chandālaṅkāranayaina ca pathavīsandhārakassa udakakkhandhassa viya gambhīrassa pabbataina paṭicchādaitvā ṭhapitassa vatthussa viya atiduddasassa satadhā bhin‍nassa vālassa aggakauṭiyā viya atisaṇha sukhumassa puthuj‍janañāṇaina buddhassa duppaṭividhaguṇaparidīpakassa namak‍kārassa līnatthappakāsanā buddhaguṇappadīpikanāmikā saṃvaṇṇanā tāsu tāsu aṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu āgataṃ saṃvaṇṇanānayaṃ nissāya pañ‍ñāpāṭavatthāya sāsanavuddhiyā ca katā|

aittāvatā ca –

sampattai cak‍kavassai sā, dvisahassai catūsatai|

navādhikāsitīyā ca, sak‍karājai ca sampattai||

sattatisatasahassa, vassai phussamāsassaiva|

kālapakkhasattamai ca, buddhadinai suniṭṭhitā| sāmañ‍ña||

aisā ca saṅkharauntaina, yaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ mayā|

añ‍ñampi taina puñ‍ñaina, patvā sambaudhimuttamaṃ||

saṃsāratau  bahū sattai, tārayitvā taṇhakkhayaṃ|

nibbānaṃ sacchikāraiyyaṃ, maggañāṇuttaraina ca||

sadā rakkhantu rājānau, dhammainaiva pajaṃ imaṃ|

sabbai sattā sukhī hauntu, jautaintu jinasāsanaṃ||

mrammaraṭṭhai manussā ca, sabbadā nirupaddavā|

nic‍caṃ kalyāṇasaṅkappā, pappauntu amataṃ padanti||

buddhaguṇappadīpikanāmikā

namak‍kāraṭīkā samattā|
